# Terror Infinity — Canon and Translation Reference

This is the authoritative living reference for the English translation of `wuxiankongbu.txt`. The source is GB18030/GBK-encoded and must remain untouched. Translation reads from the ordered UTF-8 batches in `decoded/`; `decoded/manifest.json` is the batching authority. Entries marked **locked** must be used consistently; entries marked **provisional** require later-source confirmation.

## Project conventions

- English title: *Terror Infinity* (**locked**)
- Author credit: zhttty
- Chinese personal names use pinyin without tone marks, family name first.
- Narrative tense: past.
- Dialogue: curly double quotation marks.
- Movie titles and characters use official English release names.
- `主神` → **Lord God**; `主神空间` → **Lord God's Dimension** (**locked**, established genre terminology)
- `恐怖片` → **horror movie** when discussing a film; **scenario** when referring to the lived mission as a game/system unit.
- System-facing terms use concise game language, without adding UI formatting absent from the source.

## Voice and style

- Zheng Zha's early narration should feel emotionally numb rather than melodramatically florid; action prose becomes faster and more visceral as his will to live returns.
- Zhang Jie speaks bluntly, cynically, and with the authority of traumatic experience.
- Chu Xuan's dialogue should be exact, emotionally spare, and logically structured.
- Preserve the novel's escalating survival-horror pace, but split overloaded Chinese sentences when necessary for clean English action.
- Do not introduce fandom explanations into the prose. Ambiguous system lore must remain ambiguous until revealed.
- Explicit sexual material involving Luo Li while she is under eighteen is not reproduced. Preserve plot-relevant facts and emotional consequences non-graphically and mark the omitted explicit passage; never silently change her stated age.
- Apply the same treatment to sexualized description or contact involving Zhao Yingkong while she is approximately sixteen: retain necessary facts and interpersonal context, omit explicit detail, and insert a concise editorial marker.

## Continuity ledger

- Entry prompt: “Do you want to understand the meaning of life? Do you want to truly… live?” (**locked**)
- Base completion reward stated in the opening: 1,000 reward points per survived scenario.
- Return to the real world is initially stated to cost 50,000 reward points.
- Return to Origin permanently preserves redeemed items, attribute enhancements, created life-forms, summoned life-forms, and bodily enhancement mutations.
- Six ordinary-human base attributes are initially stated to average 100 each.
- In the opening *Resident Evil* scenario, participants must remain within 100 meters of the designated plot character.
- Explaining the system rules to newcomers earns Zhang Jie 100 reward points.
- Zheng Zha first unlocks the genetic lock involuntarily while rescuing Zhan Lan from a Xenomorph. The state heightens perception, precision, survival-oriented combat instinct, and physical control, but ends in a severe toxic backlash involving systemic convulsions, organ pain, bleeding, and respiratory failure.
- Zheng Zha's first adult-Xenomorph kill awards 500 reward points. His enhanced cell vitality, immune strength, and vampire genes provide unusually fast healing and resistance to Xenomorph acid, but do not make him immune to injury or exhaustion.
- In the merchant ship's control room, the seven survivors vote to fight rather than enter cryogenic hibernation and carry the Xenomorphs back to Earth. Chu Xuan's first isolation plan assigns Li Shuaixi as bait; five vote in favor and two against.
- Four surviving newcomers are observed being herded alive toward the Queen as hosts. If implantation succeeds, Chu Xuan projects that the group will face nine Xenomorphs.
- The bait plan contains a concealed contingency: one bundled grenade charge detonates a few seconds after arming, while the other detonates immediately when its pin is pulled. Li Shuaixi discovers that Chu Xuan lied about reopening the control-room bulkhead, turns the explosives on the group, and is killed by the instant-fuse charge.
- After Li Shuaixi's death, the survivors split ideologically. Chu Xuan, Zero, and Overlord accept utilitarian sacrifice for the team's survival; Zheng Zha rejects abandoning acknowledged companions, with Zhang Jie and Zhan Lan aligned with him.
- Zheng Zha's group masks its scent in the showers at room twenty-two using water and food odors, then ambushes an escaped adult Xenomorph through the steel door with spiral steel spikes.
- During his second deliberate genetic-lock battle, Zheng Zha instinctively routes internal energy from the base of his spine across his scalp and into his right hand; an unidentified force from deep in his mind mixes with it, foreshadowed as a possible use of vampire energy. The empowered throw kills an adult Xenomorph but leaves his right arm with a comminuted fracture and triggers the usual toxic backlash.
- Spiral spikes can carry grenades into a Xenomorph before detonation, inflicting severe internal damage. Zheng Zha is right-handed; throws made with his left hand are explicitly weaker and less accurate.
- At the room-seventeen armory battle, Zhang Jie kills a wounded adult Xenomorph by holding it back with a spiral spike, losing both forearms to acid in the process. Overlord suffers a near-fatal tail wound to the chest. The two factions then form an alliance of independent teams rather than reunifying as companions.
- The Queen battle is initially estimated at a thirty-percent chance of success, conditional on Zheng Zha landing an explosive spiral spike. The Queen is about three times an ordinary Xenomorph's size; ordinary bullets cannot penetrate its shell, and hull breach from its acidic blood is a lethal risk.
- Xenomorph nest secretions corrode steel but oxidize over time to roughly weak-acid strength. Fresh layers are slick and cover floors, walls, ceilings, and lighting. Newly laid eggs have unusually soft shells; Facehuggers can emerge and leap rapidly, while the Queen is believed to be weakened shortly after laying.
- The Queen encountered in cargo hold one stands roughly seven to eight meters tall and exceeds ten meters including her tail. She deliberately uses four implanted captives as bait above a corroded false floor, demonstrating tactical intelligence.
- Under a deeper genetic-lock activation, Zheng Zha can suppress pain, calculate remaining survival time, and circulate vampire energy throughout his body to sustain vitality. Vampire energy first displays its own corrosive property when concentrated in his left hand, burning through the Queen's armor; it is distinct from Xenomorph acid resistance.
- Killing the Queen awards Zheng Zha 3,000 reward points. His two adult-Xenomorph kills award 1,000 total, and scenario plus mission completion add 2,000 and one D-rank side-story reward, for 6,000 points gross. Full restoration costs him over 2,000, leaving a stated balance of 4,170.
- After restoration, Zheng Zha receives the unexplained system line “Surpassed the genetic critical threshold...” (`突破基因临界值`). Preserve its ambiguity until the system explains it later.
- Overlord does not return from *Alien* and is confirmed dead. The returning survivors are Zheng Zha, Zhang Jie, Zhan Lan, Chu Xuan, and Zero.
- 阿诺 | Arnold | Chu Xuan's created human bodyguard: male, age twenty-two, approximately 2.4–2.5 meters tall, about twice ordinary-human physical ability, and assigned special-forces experience. Created beings can receive later enhancements.
- Chu Xuan's proposed standard loadout has three roles: a close-combat anti-spirit or legendary-magic weapon, a rapid-fire weapon for massed enemies, and a heavy weapon. He recommends a Gauss-ion sniper rifle for Zero and depleted-uranium ammunition for ordinary rapid-fire weapons.
- Materials and manufacturing equipment are much cheaper than finished products in the exchange catalog. Chu Xuan proposes constructing an ammunition workshop rather than purchasing unlimited-ammunition firearms; one reward point is stated to buy one metric ton of high-quality yellow explosive powder.
- A recorded world can be unlocked with one D-rank side-story reward. Time in an unlocked world costs fifty reward points per day. A temporary return to the real world follows this mechanism; Zheng Zha purchases thirty days for 1,500 points.
- Temporary return imposes strict conditions: the traveler cannot reveal the Lord God's Dimension by any means; must return to the original arrival point at the deadline; and must physically touch every person or object being brought back. Violations or failure to return erase the traveler and applicable possessions.
- Before the temporary return, Zheng Zha spends 2,000 reward points to raise neural reaction speed and muscle density by 100 attribute points each, plus 10 points on one cubic meter of platinum. His resulting stated attributes are intelligence 187, mental capacity 232, cellular vitality 321, neural reaction speed 387, muscle density 402, and immune-system strength 331, with 660 points remaining.
- Chu Xuan's concealed locator-message unit transmits intermittently whenever Zheng Zha removes the dagger from the Na ring. Its stored message includes advanced designs for a stable hydrogen-fusion reactor, a Gauss ultra-long-range cannon, and a high-efficiency battery, prompting the authorities to recall the pursuit.
- After returning from reality, Zheng Zha spends more than 400 reward points restoring his own gunshot wounds and severed left leg; Zhang Jie pays separately for Luo Li's restoration so Zheng Zha's balance will not become negative.
- Zero loses one arm while tackling Chu Xuan away from Zheng Zha's dagger strike and immediately undergoes restoration. No death penalty is charged because Zheng Zha does not kill him.
- Chu Xuan argues that the Lord God is a rule-bound supercomputer without independent consciousness: the concealed locator discloses the Dimension without erasing either him or the unaware returnee. Treat this as Chu Xuan's current inference, not confirmed omniscient narration.
- Zheng Zha formally terminates his alliance with Chu Xuan over the concealed experiment and vows to kill him if Chu Xuan schemes against him again.
- The *Ju-On: The Grudge* scenario has the maximum twenty participants: five returning humans, Arnold, and fifteen newcomers. The primary mission is to survive seven days. Killing the grudge's core, Kayako, once awards one B-rank horror side story and 5,000 reward points.
- Chu Xuan manufactures communicators with a fifty-kilometer range that function without satellite signals. Before entering *Ju-On*, the veterans spend nearly all remaining points on anti-spirit ammunition and survival supplies.
- Zheng Zha can sense the grudge as an extreme, deathly cold before the others notice anything. At the scenario's opening he fires into the attic, and the presence retreats, but faint traces of the curse remain attached to everyone.
- Chu Xuan and Arnold leave the group at the opening of *Ju-On*. The remaining party consists of Zheng Zha, Zhang Jie, Zhan Lan, Zero, Zhao Yingkong, Qi Tengyi, Ming Yanwei, Lu Renjia, Xiao Bingyi, and Cun Zhongding; the other ten participants' whereabouts are initially unknown.
- Zheng Zha is the acting team leader. Zhang Jie describes himself as a partial heavy-weapons specialist; Zhan Lan covers logistics, first aid, and analysis; Zero is a sniper; Zhao Yingkong is an assassin; Qi Tengyi is an artifact appraiser.
- At dawn on day two of *Ju-On*, two of the participants who left the group are found dead: one without internal organs and one impossibly impaled on a utility pole. Seven of their companions were reportedly still with them beforehand.
- The ten-person group stays in four suites at the Sunshine Hotel: Zhao Yingkong alone on floor 11; Zheng Zha, Qi Tengyi, and Lu Renjia on 12; Zhang Jie, Zero, Xiao Bingyi, and Cun Zhongding on 14; Zhan Lan and Ming Yanwei on 16.
- On day two, the child ghost grabs Zheng Zha's left ankle near the third temple. His spirit ward burns completely and forces the hand away; crossing the mountain gate removes the surrounding supernatural pressure because the ashes of an accomplished Tang monk were scattered there.
- The temple abbot gives the party several crumbling, gold-script sutra pages copied by that monk. Their warmth reduces the grudge's mark to a nearly imperceptible trace.
- Zhao Yingkong can unlock the genetic lock before joining China Team; in that state her fingers cut like blades and she can threaten Zheng Zha at close range. Zero interrupts their first fight with a suppressed shot. She accepts Zheng Zha's authority until she judges him unfit and challenges him.
- On day three, Xiao Bingyi is found compressed inside a narrow shopping-mall waste pipe. Four more deserters are also found dead, leaving three newcomers with unknown whereabouts on their side in addition to Chu Xuan and Arnold.
- The hotel group consolidates into Zheng Zha's suite and guards the gold-script sutra in rotating three-person, three-hour shifts through the night.
- Zero identifies Zhao Yingkong's barehanded techniques as evidence that she may be an inner member of one of two surviving assassin clans, one Asian and one European. This remains his informed conjecture.
- Ming Yanwei says a gang attacked her after her boyfriend's car broke down in the countryside and that he abandoned her to escape alone. Preserve her trauma and resulting distrust of men without inventing unstated details.
- During the second sutra watch, Lu Renjia and Cun Zhongding strike Qi Tengyi with an ashtray and steal the scripture. Ming Yanwei appears to join their escape but leaves a timed `DANGER` message on her phone. Zheng Zha sends Zero to locate them from the roof with his Gauss-ion sniper rifle.
- Zero shoots off Cun Zhongding's left leg and then his arms during the recovery attempt. Cun Zhongding throws the gold-script sutra into traffic, where a car destroys it; Zheng Zha beheads him and incurs the stated 1,000-point team-kill penalty.
- Lu Renjia accidentally shoots Ming Yanwei in the head while using her as a hostage. Zhao Yingkong disables and mutilates him without killing him; Zheng Zha applies hemostatic medicine before the group escapes through the sewer.
- Chu Xuan's communicator is the primary unit and can monitor the team's secondary units even while they appear switched off.
- In his final call from a high-rise roof, Chu Xuan urges Zheng Zha to distinguish viable companions from people he cannot save and to think as a leader responsible for the whole team. He suggests that the mission's number seven may indicate how many times Kayako's core must be killed. Kayako appears behind him as the call ends.
- Chu Xuan was the sole viable subject of a decade-long, enormously expensive genetic-engineering program. From birth he possessed research-level knowledge, exceptional intelligence and health, but lacked pain, touch, smell, taste, ordinary emotional chemistry, and even the desire to survive. A white-haired Director Chu who cared for him later recognized this as a profound failure.
- Chu Xuan can unlock the genetic lock with skill comparable to Zheng Zha and Zhao Yingkong. He determines that ordinary bullets pass through the grudge, sustained anti-spirit fire can disperse an apparition, contact can paralyze a victim, and every part of an attached spirit must be damaged. He kills Arnold along with the spirit holding him when no other option remains.
- Destroying Kayako apparitions yields no points. Each defeated wave returns stronger; after Chu Xuan destroys three waves, dozens or hundreds of apparitions climb onto the roof as the apparent fourth wave.
- Chu Xuan defeats the fourth wave and discovers the fifth can be harmed only during the instant it attacks; he sacrifices an arm to destroy its male apparition. The sixth wave attacks his organs from inside his body, leading him to fire into his own abdomen.
- The Lord God cannot restore Chu Xuan's missing senses or emotions because his genes were deliberately altered rather than damaged. His father, Director Chu, bound him to life with a dying request to complete the stable fusion reactor, Gauss cannon, and high-efficiency battery.
- A ten-meter manifestation identified as Kayako's core rises after the sixth wave. Exhausted and seeking genuine rest, Chu Xuan closes his eyes rather than resisting; keep his exact fate provisional until subsequent narration confirms it.
- Chu Xuan's prerecorded message plays twelve hours after his death, confirming that he died on the rooftop. It preserves his spoken observations from waves one through six for the team.
- China Team infers a seven-wave structure: wave one approaches visibly from a distance; waves two and three seize victims from behind; wave four attacks as a large horde; wave five can be harmed only during its attack and transfers struck flesh into the victim's intestines; wave six attacks internal organs; wave seven is provisionally identified as Kayako's core.
- The countermeasure plan calls for five bulletproof vests, six steel helmets, and open ground with sightlines beyond one hundred meters. Zheng Zha volunteers as bait for wave five because his unlocked danger sense and the Na ring give him the best chance to counter at contact range.
- The six survivors abandon the hotel for an open plaza. They use paired three-hour watches with two spirit wards at the center: Zhang Jie/Qi Tengyi, Zheng Zha/Zhan Lan, then Zero/Zhao Yingkong.
- Zhan Lan carries grief connected to a selfless man who died after intervening to help someone; the source has not yet supplied his identity or the full event. She tells Zheng Zha that his refusal to abandon accepted teammates is what holds the group together.
- On the evening of day seven, Zhan Lan vanishes from the crowded plaza without either central ward reacting and wakes alone in the former Sunshine Hotel suite. Her personal spirit ward is already burning.
- Zheng Zha deliberately activates the genetic lock for the first time while racing up the Sunshine Hotel stairs without a direct external attack. The activation causes vomiting, severe muscular convulsions, abdominal distress, and shifting bones, but grants greater speed, instinct, and danger perception than his involuntary uses.
- After Zheng Zha disperses the second-wave apparition holding Zhan Lan, the third attacks his back. He destroys it in one punch using internal energy through the Na ring's spirit-damaging field.
- During the fourth wave, at least eight hundred to one thousand Kayako apparitions mass outside the hotel while more enter through the hallway and windows. Zheng Zha manifests the second stage of the genetic lock, enlarging his right arm to roughly three times normal thickness, binds Zhan Lan to himself with the bulletproof vest, and jumps from the twelfth floor using the particle-cutting dagger to brake against the wall.
- The dagger's vibration fails twice during the descent; Zheng Zha's enlarged arm absorbs the first violent stop and is left swollen with widespread capillary damage. He and Zhan Lan escape by having her steer while he operates the accelerator.
- The fourth-wave horde can crawl faster than a speeding sedan while pursuing, but slows to an ordinary woman's crawling pace after the car stops. Ordinary pedestrians, traffic, and police appear to vanish from the attack area. Zhang Jie and Qi Tengyi destroy most of the horde with a compact heavy machine gun and a belt of anti-spirit ammunition.
- Immediately after the fourth wave is cleared, Japanese paper screens and the argument depicting Kayako's murder appear before Zheng Zha, Zhan Lan, Zhang Jie, and Qi Tengyi, signaling the fifth wave.
- Wave five manifests both Kayako and her husband. Damage inflicted during one ghost's attack is real, but either spirit regenerates unless both are destroyed simultaneously. Zheng Zha imitates Chu Xuan's detached reasoning, sacrifices his right arm to their simultaneous attacks, and destroys both with a single Na-powered left-handed strike.
- Zhan Lan's Wind Spirit spell increases Zheng Zha's speed by about thirty percent, enabling his first fifth-wave evasion. Immediately after the fifth wave, the sixth attacks Zhan Lan internally; Zheng Zha responds by striking her abdomen with his internal-energy-charged left fist.
- The Na ring's field destroys the sixth-wave presence inside Zhan Lan without the physical force of Zheng Zha's punch entering her body. He performs emergency surgery: her intestines and other organs are badly damaged, and he uses hemostatic spray and compression bandaging to slow the bleeding.
- The seventh wave is a Kayako manifestation more than ten meters tall. Ordinary people reappear and cannot see it; physical contact kills them, apparently allowing it to absorb their souls and grow. Anti-spirit Gauss rounds inflict major damage, ordinary anti-spirit rounds slow regeneration, and the Na ring erases limbs on contact.
- While Zheng Zha fights the giant manifestation, a separate blue-white female apparition emerges against his back and paralyzes him, draining physical strength, internal energy, and vitality through contact.
- Under the draining attack, Zheng Zha consciously releases vampire energy throughout his body for the first time. It is violently hot and corrosive to spirits, boosts vitality but not stamina, creates bloodred vision in which ghosts appear pale blue, and is consumed as it burns them.
- The giant seventh-wave manifestation is a collective curse made from the souls of its victims; Zheng Zha recognizes the northeastern newcomer and Xiao Bingyi within it. Kayako's smaller core body is embedded at the center of its chest.
- A back-attacking apparition also drains Zero after his anti-spirit Gauss shots. Zhao Yingkong saves him, but he needs about ten minutes before he can snipe again. Zheng Zha moves toward the plaza's roughly ten-meter clock structure to reach the core's height.
- Zhao Yingkong improvises anti-spirit melee claws by removing the propellant from cartridges and fitting five empty inscribed casings over her fingers. Combined with her unlocked state and assassin training, they let her defend the rooftop while Zero recovers.
- Zheng Zha's first sustained voluntary genetic-lock activation approaches the critical point of genetic collapse by the time he reaches the clock tower summit. With internal energy exhausted and only vampire energy remaining, he leaps directly toward the Kayako core embedded in the giant's chest.
- Vampire energy burns a human-shaped tunnel through the giant spirit but loses more than forty percent of its remaining supply within seconds. Chu Xuan's uncorrupted-looking soul appears inside, does not attack Zheng Zha, and instead holds back other victim souls; whether this is truly Chu Xuan or an illusion remains unresolved.
- Zero uses his final strength for one anti-spirit Gauss shot through Zheng Zha's left shoulder and arm into the core after vampire energy shrinks Kayako below one-fifth size. The round destroys the core, pulverizes Zheng Zha's arm, disperses the collective spirit, and triggers the survivors' return to the Lord God's Dimension.
- The six survivors of *Ju-On* are Zheng Zha, Zhang Jie, Zhan Lan, Zero, Zhao Yingkong, and Qi Tengyi. Zheng Zha's post-restoration balance is exactly 4,177 points and two C-rank side-story rewards after the 1,000-point team-kill penalty and 1,000-point survival reward cancel out.
- Zero receives 2,300 points and one C-rank side story for eliminating part of the grudge; Zhang Jie reports 1,800 points and two D-rank side stories; another speaker reports 1,500 points and two D-rank side stories; Zhan Lan receives two D-rank side stories; Qi Tengyi receives one D-rank side story. Do not assign the unattributed 1,500-point line to a character without later confirmation.
- Zhan Lan infers that optional-objective rewards are distributed according to contribution, unlike a primary-mission kill. The team plans a ten-day group trip to Hawaii, costing 100 reward points per traveler at the stated time rate.
- A golden stele permanently appears once a team has cumulatively produced at least three people who unlocked the genetic lock, counting deceased members. Its creators state that the fifth stage is the final stage and produces a higher life-form capable of advancing or devastating humanity.
- After the three-unlocker threshold, teams from different continental Lord God's Dimensions may encounter one another in scenarios. Killing an ordinary opposing member awards 2,000 points and one C-rank side story; killing an opposing unlocked member awards 7,000 points and one B-rank side story; eliminating the opposing team awards every survivor one C-rank side story.
- The stele claims that ultimately only one team can survive, but Zhan Lan notes that ordinary scenario completion or time-limit conditions may still allow disengagement. The large kill rewards incentivize preemptive conflict without explicitly making combat immediately mandatory.
- Zero purchases Permanent Druid Hawk-Eye to specialize further as a sniper; Zhao Yingkong purchases Netherfire Fang; Zheng Zha purchases Red Flame. Zheng Zha's first brief Red Flame test consumes nearly half his vampire energy, so it must be reserved for emergencies.
- Zero, Zhang Jie, Zheng Zha, Zhan Lan, and eventually Zhao Yingkong agree to the Hawaii trip; Qi Tengyi declines because he has the fewest rewards and greatest need for practical enhancement.
- Zhao Yingkong challenges Zheng Zha to a controlled genetic-lock duel to measure herself against future opposing teams. At first stage, Zheng Zha has superior raw attributes and deeper activation, while Zhao Yingkong has much better trained technique; the exchange pressures him into invoking the second stage.
- In the duel, Zheng Zha's second stage enlarges one arm to roughly twice normal size, letting him overpower Zhao Yingkong and knock her unconscious. Releasing it causes extreme convulsions and sweating. Zhao Yingkong warns that mercy during an active fight can be fatal, particularly once opposing human teams appear.
- The team shortens its Hawaii vacation from ten days to five at Zhao Yingkong's urging. Time spent revisiting a scenario world passes only as an instant in the Lord God's Dimension. Each traveler carries a compact submachine gun, two spirit wards, and one of six Lord God communicators; the communicators cost fifty points each and indicate the approximate direction of the other units.
- Zero creates or brings a boy of eleven or twelve whom he identifies as his younger brother. The narration suggests the boy was modeled on Zero's real brother, but does not yet confirm the details.
- Zero's created companion has a female body but represents the younger brother he killed in reality. Zero was born to a Chinese-American criminal family in the United States; his father was murdered when Zero was fifteen, and he trained under an assumed identity for ten years before taking revenge. A diary revealed too late that the child was his father's son and that Zero had misunderstood his mother's apparent betrayal. Zero now treats the created companion as his reason to survive. Preserve the sibling's gender ambiguity as stated rather than resolving it editorially.
- Zhang Jie says he entered the Dimension after leaving the military, losing a corporate job following conflict with his supervisor, and withdrawing into internet cafés. His account may be incomplete, but no contradiction is yet established.
- In Hawaii, Zhao Yingkong uses a hotel computer to arrange business visas for the party and a transfer exceeding one million US dollars from an American multinational company. Zhan Lan privately asks Zheng Zha to meet her in the lobby at nine.
- Zhan Lan's enhanced mental capacity and two learned spells make her sense an oppressive presence monitoring the team. She distinguishes it from the Lord God's familiar attention but cannot yet identify its source. She asks Zheng Zha, if she dies, to beware whichever teammate shows the greatest contrast between their behavior before and after joining the group.
- Zhan Lan notes that neither of the paired spirit wards burned before she was displaced to the Sunshine Hotel during the final day of *Ju-On*, casting doubt on the assumption that the grudge transported her. Zheng Zha considers Chu Xuan, Zero, and Zhao Yingkong possible subjects of her warning but reaches no conclusion.
- Before dawn in Hawaii, an unexplained sense of imminent danger wakes Zheng Zha and leads him to the beach, where he finds Zhan Lan unconscious and nearly drowned offshore.
- Zhan Lan says her body moved independently while she slept, walking from the hotel into the sea as she called to Zheng Zha mentally. She and Zheng Zha identify hypnosis as the likely cause and name Zhang Jie as their principal suspect, though they have no evidence and long-range hypnosis appears implausible to them.
- Zhan Lan senses strong psychic pressure near Zhang Jie and argues that his decline from a forceful veteran into Zheng Zha's apparently ordinary subordinate may conceal both his power and his desire to avoid responsibility. Treat this as an unproven theory; Zheng Zha withholds their suspicion from the team.
- The group ends its Hawaiian recreation early, consolidates into one suite, and restores paired watches. Zheng Zha pairs himself with Zhang Jie and Zhan Lan with Zhao Yingkong. No second attack occurs before the fifth-day return.
- During the nine remaining days, Zero teaches firearms, Zhao Yingkong teaches close combat, and Zheng Zha trains his vampire abilities and second-stage genetic lock. Zhang Jie outwardly studies like an inexperienced newcomer, including asking Zheng Zha about the genetic lock.
- The next scenario is *The Mummy*. Its transfer verse foreshadows two teams fighting in a pyramid tomb and associates gold with the power of life and black with the terror of death.
- The *Mummy* scenario begins in the Cairo prison shortly before Evelyn rescues Rick O'Connell. Use established film forms for Imhotep, Anck-su-namun, Seti I, Hamunaptra, Rick O'Connell, Evelyn, Beni, the Book of the Dead, the Book of Amun-Ra, and Hom-Dai.
- China Team has eleven participants in *The Mummy*: the six returning survivors and five newcomers. The objective is to eliminate the undead Imhotep, awarding every team member one D-rank side-story reward. They must remain within five thousand meters of O'Connell or be erased.
- India Team enters only after Imhotep is resurrected. Killing an ordinary opposing member awards 2,000 points and one C-rank side story; killing an unlocked opponent awards 7,000 points and one B-rank side story. Every allied death scores negative one and every enemy death positive one; the final score times 2,000 becomes an additional reward for each surviving member of the corresponding team.
- The five *Mummy* newcomers are Gao Hongliang, a computer programmer; Zhang Heng, a third-year university student and Olympic archery alternate proficient with compound bows; Xiao Honglu, a twelve-year-old psychiatric-hospital research subject who claims strong precognition; Qin Zhuiyu, a film actress and singer; and an unnamed violent bald criminal.
- Zheng Zha concludes that once either team makes the first kill, the score penalty will pressure the victimized team to retaliate to avoid each survivor losing 2,000 points. He treats this as creating a likely fight to the death, though it is his inference rather than an additional explicit mission rule.
- When the unnamed criminal attempts to assault Qin Zhuiyu, Zheng Zha throws him aside, Zhao Yingkong dislocates both arms, and Zheng Zha severs both legs before stopping the bleeding. Zheng Zha refuses him a weapon and warns the newcomers that anyone threatening the team will receive similar treatment.
- Zhao Yingkong and Zero initially favor disabling the other four newcomers and leaving them safely inside the prison, while Zhang Jie, Qi Tengyi, and Zhan Lan oppose it. Xiao Honglu instead volunteers the four as bait to test India Team's intentions. Zheng Zha accepts Xiao Honglu as a protected team member immediately while leaving Gao Hongliang, Zhang Heng, and Qin Zhuiyu under observation.
- Xiao Honglu describes his precognition specifically as sensing an approaching death. He deduces that the plot may diverge once the team arrives and predicts O'Connell might die at the gallows. When the movie's rescue fails to occur on schedule, Zero covertly uses a silenced pistol and Permanent Druid Hawk-Eye to sever the hanging rope.
- Xiao Honglu explains that he perceives unconscious human premonitions of death as shades of gray: deeper gray means a higher probability, not certain death. He attributes both this perception and his reasoning ability to unusually developed central nerves and cortical folds. At this point Zheng Zha appears grayest, while Qin Zhuiyu is judged the most likely actual casualty.
- Xiao Honglu reasons that teams cannot safely collude by exchanging newcomers for equal kills: a betrayed newcomer could commit suicide first, penalizing their own team without awarding the other side a point and thereby igniting genuine conflict. He compares Lord God's teams to venomous creatures forced to devour one another inside a sealed jar.
- The unnamed criminal is erased after the party moves roughly five thousand meters away from O'Connell. China Team receives negative one score and a negative 2,000-point running balance without India Team gaining a kill point, confirming that any negative final reward balance causes erasure.
- Xiao Honglu infers that the team placed into a shared scenario first is the weaker one because advance time grants battlefield familiarity, ambush opportunities, and initiative. He estimates a seventy-percent chance that the stronger India Team specializes in magical/legendary enhancements and recommends avoiding direct battle while exploiting Zero's long-range advantage.
- China Team's current composition is described as sniper, assassin, direct-combat specialist, and support personnel, lacking primarily a combat engineer for mines and similar weapons. Xiao Honglu treats Zero as the main combatant against India Team because of his extreme range.
- Lord God inserts the team into the scenario's local history as O'Connell's fellow prisoners. Zheng Zha offers protection in exchange for passage to Hamunaptra; Zero demonstrates their power by using his futuristic silenced pistol to snap a steel rooftop ornament from nearly one kilometer away, winning O'Connell's wary cooperation.
- Zheng Zha gives O'Connell three gold bars for weapons, supplies, and passage to Hamunaptra. Because of the five-kilometer constraint, the team accompanies him to Cairo's black market before staying at his inn and arranging to meet Evelyn and Jonathan at Giza Port the next morning.
- O'Connell recounts that his unit found Hamunaptra three years earlier, was destroyed by its black-robed guardians, and that he survived both an apparition rising from the ground and a five-day desert crossing without water or a mount. He agrees to guide and travel with the team.
- Zhan Lan identifies cats as the best readily available protection to purchase before entering Hamunaptra; the reason has not yet been explained in narration.
- At Giza Port, Zheng Zha presents China Team as an expedition privately funded by a wealthy American, offering to cover expenses and protect O'Connell, Evelyn, and Jonathan in exchange for their cooperation and restraint. Evelyn claims the Book of the Dead and Book of Amun-Ra as irreplaceable cultural treasures and rejects any attempt to remove them.
- The planned route follows the river until noon the next day, then travels half a day west to the route's only major oasis, where the expedition will buy camels and water, followed by three days and nights southwest to Hamunaptra.
- Before the expected nighttime attack on the riverboat, Zheng Zha sends Evelyn to retrieve the map and disk with Zero and Zhang Jie as guards; he accompanies O'Connell to find Jonathan among the American gamblers. Zhan Lan, Qi Tengyi, and Zhao Yingkong share command of those remaining behind.
- China Team's presence appears to scale up the riverboat ambush from roughly ten black-robed guardians in the film to dozens. Zheng Zha uses the miniature submachine gun to clear attackers from the rails and the particle-cutting dagger to deflect two stray bullets; Zhao Yingkong kills nearly ten attackers barehanded with fingertip strikes through the heart.
- Evelyn, Zero, and Zhang Jie recover the Hamunaptra map and disk before the cabins burn. The party escapes by jumping into the river and reaches the planned bank together. Zhan Lan preserves a docile black cat throughout the swim. Beni and the American expedition land on the opposite bank with the horses.
- Xiao Honglu estimates only a seven-percent chance that China Team can complete the mission without any deaths and predicts at least half the team may die. He recommends following the film plot and using the Book of Amun-Ra to lay Imhotep to rest rather than inventing an untested solution.
- The ten-person team and O'Connell's three-person party purchase thirteen camels at the oasis, then travel three days and nights southwest. At dawn, Hamunaptra becomes visible as sunlight reveals the ruins on an apparently empty plain; Beni and the American expedition arrive simultaneously.
- At Hamunaptra, Zheng Zha lets the film trio explore the roughly hundred-meter-deep tombs while China Team reconnoiters the surface. Zero selects long-range sniper positions; Zhang Jie, Qi Tengyi, and Xiao Honglu identify and record close-combat ground; the remainder guard the perimeter under Zheng Zha.
- Zheng Zha deliberately limits his relationship with Zhan Lan to mutual protection and support as companions, despite her asking to retain their earlier friendship and reaffirming that she will not interfere with his relationship with Luo Li.
- The film expedition finds Imhotep's still-wet but unrevived mummy underground; three American guides are killed by a concentrated-hydrochloric-acid tomb trap. That night, dozens of black-robed riders attack and burn the camp, but Zheng Zha, Zhang Jie, and Zero repel them with modern firearms after killing more than thirty attackers in the stated waves.
- After the first camp attack, the Americans emerge unaware of the battle carrying ancient glass jars and a thick black book, provisionally identifiable as the Book of the Dead pending its immediate confirmation in the next source part.
- The black book is confirmed as the Book of the Dead. Evelyn opens it with the key-disk and reads a resurrection incantation at midnight, reviving Imhotep. Hom-Dai is said to make a resurrected victim immensely powerful and bring the Ten Plagues upon Egypt.
- Imhotep's resurrection produces two simultaneous danger signatures sensed by Zheng Zha, Zero, and Zhao Yingkong: one below Hamunaptra and one on the western dunes, the latter indicating India Team's mission-triggered arrival. An unseen opponent throws a pebble hard enough to numb Zheng Zha's arm through his blocking dagger and knock him from a ten-meter-plus pillar.
- A vast locust swarm, the first displayed plague, drives the camp into the underground tombs. The hostile presence on the western dunes withdraws rather than immediately pursuing China Team.
- India Team initially appears with eleven members, including three hypnotized newcomers and eight apparent veterans. Its unnamed young-monk captain directs Imani, Lamb, Xuenai, and Arot; preserve the captain as unnamed until the source identifies him.
- Imani possesses the B-rank eighty-percent Toguro Younger Muscle Enhancement, one of the cheapest B-rank options. He throws the pebble while Lamb controls its accuracy telekinetically. The captain judges Zheng Zha's block and landing response evidence that China Team may warrant the equal-strength custom of mutual distance until scenario completion.
- Xuenai hypnotizes India Team's three newcomers, while Arot evaluates them. The captain plans to awaken the one with slightly better mental potential after returning and feed any useless newcomers to his nearly advancing summoned beast.
- Rather than risk an unfamiliar customary first contact with the newly awakened China Team, India Team withdraws to Cairo to seek either negotiations or an opportunity for complete annihilation.
- Inside the tomb, Zheng Zha is separated with Zero, Zhang Jie, Zhang Heng, Qi Tengyi, and Qin Zhuiyu. During a mass scarab pursuit, Qin Zhuiyu falls; Zheng Zha doubles back, lifts her, and suspends them from a ceiling seam with the particle-cutting dagger until the swarm passes.
- After rescuing Qin Zhuiyu, Zheng Zha strikes and threatens her at gunpoint when she lashes out, invokes the team's death penalty, and threatens suicide. She confirms that she wants to live but privately retains hostility toward him.
- Ordinary bullets inflict damage on newly revived Imhotep that regenerates almost instantly like flowing sand. Anti-spirit rounds leave persistent wounds until the bullets blacken and fall out, after which regeneration is slower. Zheng Zha judges the effect real but weak and expects it to diminish after Imhotep drains the Americans and gains sandstorm-level power.
- Zero rejects the hope that every companion can survive but explicitly trusts Zheng Zha never to abandon the team and flee alone. Zheng Zha reaffirms that principle regardless of whether they live or die.
- Hamunaptra's tattoo-faced black-robed leader orders both expeditions to leave and threatens death if they return. Keep him unnamed until the source supplies a name. Once Imhotep is resurrected and the plot fully begins, China Team's five-kilometer O'Connell constraint disappears from their watches.
- The Book of the Dead is a Lord God-recognized plot item that can teach necromantic/dark incantations after return to the Dimension. Readers of ancient Egyptian can learn without paying points; others pay 1,000 points per spell. No side-story reward is required. Qi Tengyi can read the text and therefore qualifies for free learning.
- China Team intercepts the Americans while leaving Hamunaptra and offers ten, then agrees to fifteen, gold bricks for the Book of the Dead. Before the exchange is completed, one greedy American shoots the elderly professor and takes the bloodstained book himself.
- The Book of the Dead absorbs the professor's fresh blood without visible change and feels unnaturally cold. Its spells require a compatible source such as vampire energy, mental energy, mana, psychokinetic power, true essence, immortal spiritual energy, or divine covenant power.
- Book of the Dead spells may be learned independently by interpreting the ancient text or through Lord God after returning, at 1,000 points per spell. Before that return, the physical book must be held to cast its skills. This full notice supersedes any earlier shorthand; readers such as Qi Tengyi avoid assisted-learning cost only by successfully interpreting the spell themselves.
- Xiao Honglu identifies possession of the Book of the Dead as a double-edged sword: Imhotep will pursue it, India Team may know its value and monitor the plot characters, and China Team's early acquisition may destroy its chance of a surprise attack. He also warns that India Team may have a different objective, such as protecting Imhotep or killing the protagonists, which could end the scenario while China Team still holds a fatal negative score.
- Qi Tengyi identifies Book of the Dead incantations including one that mixes human ashes with sand to create several mummy guards and another that gathers wandering spirits into a tornado. A translated recitation does nothing; Zheng Zha must reproduce the original ancient-Egyptian sounds for the spell to engage his vampire energy.
- Zheng Zha's first tornado casting uncontrollably drains all his vampire energy into the book, knocks Qi Tengyi away, makes the book painfully hot while it clings to Zheng Zha's hands, and leaves him unconscious for a day and night. The spell forms a tornado one kilometer ahead and expands into a sandstorm that nearly buries the party; only the camels guide them through.
- After the casting, the Book of the Dead's cover emblem—the symbol of the Egyptian god of death—appears intermittently on Zheng Zha's palm. Xiao Honglu believes controlled output could make the book powerful enough to annihilate India Team.
- Over the following two days, Zheng Zha shortens his post-casting unconsciousness and begins controlling vampire-energy output. Demonstrated environmental spells include sandstorm, quicksand, and rapid plant decay. Evelyn's ancient-Egyptian recitation produces no effect because she lacks a compatible energy source.
- The palm emblem releases a chill during casting that reduces the painful heat produced when vampire energy enters the Book of the Dead. No other function is yet known.
- Most identified Book of the Dead magic manipulates desert terrain or affects groups of ordinary enemies. The single-target Wraith Corrosion spell consumes heavy vampire energy, needs over twenty seconds of chanting, and reaches only 500–1,000 meters, making it inferior to Zero's Gauss-ion rifle until mastered.
- Support spells include summoning an undead spirit to make one person invisible for thirty seconds and creating several tomb guardians from sand mixed with human ashes. The group plans to obtain museum mummies for the required ashes.
- O'Connell, Evelyn, and Jonathan agree to help find the Book of Amun-Ra and supply mummies; in exchange, China Team will attempt to lay Imhotep to rest. O'Connell plans to return to Cairo by riverboat in about two days and then escape Egypt by ocean-going steamer.
- Zhang Jie hints that China Team's weakness despite its enormous potential recruitment population has a hidden cause. He promises to reveal everything if Zheng Zha survives the fight with India Team, then says there will be no “after that.” Zheng Zha tells him that distrusting nearby companions is more exhausting than facing death.
- On reaching Cairo, China Team separates from O'Connell, Evelyn, and Jonathan to avoid drawing them into the interteam battle, while promising distant protection. The trio will obtain museum mummies and seek the Book of Amun-Ra; Zheng Zha advises them to keep a cat nearby.
- An unidentified India Team girl can locate China Team remotely with closed eyes. The young-monk captain orders nearby Imani and Muhammad Yorif to conduct the customary strength test, with Arot sent as medical support. Imani's strength and Muhammad Yorif's advanced weapons are considered too conspicuous for unrestricted urban fighting.
- China Team's urban combat formation has three veteran pairs: Zero snipes with Zhang Jie as protection; Zhao Yingkong assassinates with Zhan Lan providing Wind Spirit and Endurance; Zheng Zha fights close-range while Qi Tengyi translates unmemorized Book of the Dead spells. Xiao Honglu remains with Gao Hongliang, Zhang Heng, and Qin Zhuiyu under rotating protection.
- Because captured communicators reveal friendly positions, Zheng Zha establishes an internal password and plans to trap any approaching carrier unable to provide it. Nonmembers are excluded from the password. He also orders that monitored newcomers be killed if their actions would place the whole team in a hopeless position.
- Imani and Muhammad Yorif make first contact inside Cairo. Yorif manifests a crimson submachine gun in a flash of red light and receives Zhao Yingkong's thrown knife in its body. The two wager a 1,000-point redemption on who kills an opponent first.
- Imani's raw force lets him shatter the street into projectile-like fragments that kill more than ten civilians, leap several meters while lifting a boulder several cubic meters in volume, and bring it down toward Zheng Zha.
- At eighty-percent transformation, Imani approaches three meters in height; his muscles stop ordinary submachine-gun rounds and expel embedded bullets as they enlarge. He uses chained tiger-claw gauntlets with barbs and hooks, but his unaided thrown-stone accuracy is extremely poor without Lamb's guidance.
- The particle-cutting dagger severs roughly four-fifths of Imani's left arm through steel-like muscle, after which he tears away the remainder himself. Red Flame then consumes the residual arm and spreads into his shoulder, reducing removed flesh and bone to ash almost instantly.
- Imani's punch sends Zheng Zha through two houses, but Zheng plants both feet against Imani's abdomen at impact and absorbs only about thirty percent of its force. Arot halts the pursuit, disables Imani's right arm with a precise silver-edged cut, and surgically removes burning tissue from his shoulder.
- Arot recognizes Zheng Zha's vampire enhancement and Red Flame from a prior battle with the provisionally rendered South Atlantic Team. He orders the probe ended and reports that Muhammad Yorif has been captured by China Team.
- Muhammad Yorif is a heavily modified cyborg. His left arm contains a hollow steel cannon for miniature surface-to-surface guided missiles; the five fingers of his other hand function as gun barrels; his right eye supplies thermal-spectrum vision while his left sees ordinary color. He also has an artificial spine and a self-destruct mechanism.
- Zhao Yingkong's assassin-clan stealth suppresses breathing, sound, and killing intent so completely that even unlocked opponents struggle to sense her. Her clan's anti-cyborg footwork uses tiny uniform steps at more than ten times ordinary movement speed to leave multiple residual heat images and defeat thermal vision.
- Zhao Yingkong cuts both of Yorif's arm tendons, but his mechanical limbs remain functional. Netherfire Fang disables his shoulder joints and conducts its soul-burning effect especially efficiently through the machinery. She captures him by stabbing the artificial spine below his neck, rendering him unconscious before he can beg or self-destruct.
- Zheng Zha's defensive kick reduces Imani's clean shoulder punch to roughly thirty percent force, yet still launches him through two houses and dislocates the joint. He resets it manually and retains usable arm movement. Imani's poor control and unfocused danger signal do not force Zheng Zha into the genetic-lock state.
- Arot can approach without Zheng Zha sensing him and exerts a much stronger deathly pressure than Imani. He offers a ceasefire lasting through the scenario if China Team releases Muhammad Yorif and neither side loses a member; at this point, neither team has formally accepted it.
- Xiao Honglu scatters spare communicators in a wide ring around Cairo's central plaza while the team keeps one at its actual hideout. The decoys create mutually observable approaches suited to Zero's sniper coverage. Zheng Zha crosses the plaza under Zhao Yingkong's surveillance to verify he has no tail before receiving the clock-tower location.
- At the clock-tower hideout, Xiao Honglu opens Yorif's abdomen and removes shells and miniature missiles while Netherfire Fang remains embedded in the artificial spine, preventing movement or self-destruction. The exact scope and outcome of this disarmament continue in part 101.
- Xiao Honglu and Zhao Yingkong sever Yorif's limb joints and control lines, disconnect his brain and body circuits, and remove weapons and possible self-destruct devices from his chest and abdomen. Yorif's bones, nerves, some muscle, most organs, and spine have been replaced by metal, high-strength plastic, or electronics; limited organic structural muscle remains.
- During interrogation, Zhao Yingkong demonstrates Netherfire Fang's soul-burning pain. Before Yorif can divulge India Team's composition, captain Shiva Ganten apparently takes remote control of his expression and speech, offers to sacrifice Yorif and erase his memories, and proposes peace while reserving scenario completion for India Team.
- Imhotep's arrival in Cairo is announced by burning stones falling across the city. India Team's mission then appears: recover the Book of the Dead and resurrect Anck-su-namun; because a non-plot person has already used the book, India Team must kill the bearer of the god-of-death mark—Zheng Zha—to reclaim usage rights. This makes the proposed peace incompatible with the revealed objective.
- Zhao Yingkong kills the immobilized Muhammad Yorif with Netherfire Fang through the skull before Shiva Ganten can identify Zheng Zha as the marked user. The enemy kill raises China Team from negative one to a neutral score, removing the immediate 2,000-point erasure deficit.
- China Team rejects Qin Zhuiyu's suggestion to trade the marked Zheng Zha for peace: he is both captain and a principal fighter, and the team reasons India Team would destroy the weakened survivors afterward. Zheng Zha warns that he will kill anyone who tries to abandon him.
- The team decides to protect O'Connell, Evelyn, and Jonathan, obtain the Book of Amun-Ra as quickly as possible, avoid unnecessary battle with India Team, and end the scenario. Xiao Honglu predicts India Team may preemptively kill the protagonists so the golden book cannot be found.
- Zero and Zhang Jie are assigned high positions around the Cairo museum; Zheng Zha/Qi Tengyi and Zhao Yingkong/Zhan Lan enter it. Xiao Honglu remains at the clock tower with Gao Hongliang, Zhang Heng, and Qin Zhuiyu, armed with two pistols shared between Xiao and Zhang Heng; they plan suicide rather than becoming enemy rewards if all six veterans die.
- Imhotep's arrival brings additional plagues: Egypt's rivers and drinking water turn bloodred, while the earlier burning stones fell over Cairo. After draining or recovering two bodies, he attacks the last two Americans at their hotel but flees when a cat crosses the piano.
- Cats are guardians of the underworld and can temporarily repel Imhotep only before his body is fully restored. This explains Zhan Lan's earlier insistence on carrying a black cat. After full restoration, cats will no longer restrain him.
- O'Connell initially argues that Evelyn alone bears responsibility for releasing Imhotep and proposes fleeing Egypt, but Evelyn resolves to stay and find the Book of Amun-Ra. At the museum, the curator is revealed to be allied with the bearded black-robed tomb guardian.
- The curator and black-robed guardian belong to a secret order that has protected Hamunaptra and prevented Imhotep's return for three thousand years, killing intruding people and organizations when necessary. They are descendants of the pharaoh's guards and normally consider entry into the tombs forbidden desecration.
- Imhotep targets those who opened the sacred chest, gaining magical power with every victim. He must recover all sacred canopic jars and the Book of the Dead to resurrect Anck-su-namun, and mistakes Evelyn for Anck-su-namun's reincarnation and intended living sacrifice.
- Use of the Book of the Dead places an exclusive god-of-death mark on the caster. Until that bearer dies, nobody else—including Imhotep—can use the book, even for the basic resurrection incantation. This confirms why India Team's mission requires Zheng Zha's death.
- Evelyn deduces that earlier archaeologists reversed the books' locations: the Book of the Dead was inside the death god's statue, so the Book of Amun-Ra must be inside the sun god's statue at Hamunaptra. An unidentified gaunt, dark-skinned India Team fighter attacks her with a scimitar before China Team shoots him through museum bookcases.
- Imani reappears at the museum with his destroyed left arm fully restored, though its skin remains new and pale, demonstrating India Team's rapid medical capability. His downward tiger-claw strike and Zheng Zha's dagger block collapse the concrete floor, sending both into a basement storing documents, broken tablets, and mummy fragments.
- The gaunt India Team attacker shows no wounds after sustained submachine-gun fire and wields two scimitars surrounded by cold mist. He recognizes Zhao Yingkong as another assassin-clan member after her Wind Spirit-enhanced knife and Netherfire Fang attacks force him to drop one weapon.
- Arot intervenes against Zhao Yingkong with transparent crystal scalpels before she can finish the gaunt assassin. Shiva Ganten and several India Team members then appear at the museum's second-floor window.
- Arot's blood-induced frenzy escalates into transformation as an upright werewolf more than two meters tall, with crystal surgical blades for claws, immense strength, and skin tough enough that a silver throwing knife barely enters his neck. Explicit sexualized sadism toward underage Zhao Yingkong is omitted with an editorial marker while preserving the combat threat.
- Zhao Yingkong recognizes him as Jackal Doctor Arot, whom the European assassin clan supposedly killed. His pressure makes her voluntarily unlock the genetic lock before a direct mortal strike lands.
- Xuenai cannot mentally control Zhao Yingkong because of assassin-clan mind training and finds Zhan Lan difficult because of her mental-capacity enhancements, but she takes control of Qi Tengyi and forces him to hold a pistol against Zhan Lan's head.
- Zheng Zha returns from the basement in second-stage form after breaking Imani's neck but leaving him briefly alive. Holding the dagger at Imani's throat, Zheng demands Qi Tengyi's release and safe withdrawal for his companions under threat of mutual destruction.
- In the basement, poor darkness-adapted senses and power control prevent Imani from hitting accurately. After Imani accepts severe intestinal damage to seize and crush him, Zheng Zha bites his shoulder, develops an intense thirst for blood, enters second stage, and breaks both arms and thighs before taking him hostage; his neck is also broken by the time they emerge.
- Shiva Ganten can descend through open air from the museum's second floor. He partially reveals that the books contain abilities unavailable for direct Lord God redemption and that the Book of Amun-Ra grants something each person can access only once, but his sentence is interrupted before the effect is named.
- When teams of similar power meet, the customary practice is to exchange survival intelligence and altered-scenario escape methods. India Team learned the two books' secret from another team that had already experienced *The Mummy*; Shiva does not finish explaining the custom for unequal teams.
- At Shiva's command, Xuenai releases the collapsing Qi Tengyi and takes control of Zheng Zha instead. Shiva manifests a faint two-headed cobra more than one meter thick and over ten meters long, with differently colored heads, and sends one head to swallow the immobilized Zheng Zha.
- Xuenai's control ability is named Mind Chain. Zheng Zha applies second-stage control to his nervous system, raising neural reactions enough to break the chain, evade the cobra at contact range, and destroy roughly half one head with an internal-energy Na-ring punch.
- An unseen India Team attacker pierces Qi Tengyi's heart with a remotely controlled steel needle. Dying, Qi insists on translating the original Egyptian pronunciation for the tomb-guardian spell and mourns that fellow tomb robbers deceived him and sold Chinese artifacts overseas. The needle withdraws itself into Shiva's smoke after Qi dies.
- Using mummy fragments and sand-encrusted stone from the museum basement, Zheng Zha casts Qi's final incantation and summons four skeletal mummy guards armed with scimitars and shields. They display strange speed and immediately engage India Team inside the smoke.
- Arot defeats and gravely wounds Zhao Yingkong, including severe shoulder damage and loss of left-arm function, then throws her at Zheng Zha and impales him through the left back as he shields her. Zero's Gauss-ion sniper shot enters Arot's right lung, crosses the whole chest, and retains enough force to demolish the far museum wall.
- Manavia's full-strength transparent barrier cannot stop an anti-spirit Gauss round, but reduces its power by about fifty percent before shattering. Zero requires approximately seven seconds between shots at this point.
- Shiva uses a regenerating cobra head to draw Zero's fire, while Xuenai enters Shiva's mental system and transmits his visual field to Lamb. Shiva locates Zero's eastern rooftop in six seconds; Lamb sends the same remotely controlled steel needle toward him while Zero's return shot tears off Shiva's left arm at the shoulder.
- Lamb's needle wounds Zero but misses a vital point because an unidentified mental interference disrupts Lamb's thoughts at impact, resembling Xuenai's control. India Team mistakenly treats Zero as no immediate threat. Lamb is sent to confirm his death, Shiva pursues Zheng Zha, and Xuenai remotely tracks and transmits China Team's positions.
- Zhan Lan reunites with Zheng Zha and the gravely injured Zhao Yingkong in Cairo's alleys. She carries Zhao while Zheng applies hemostatic spray; O'Connell leads the film party and curator toward the central plaza by a shortcut while danger continues following them.
- Zheng Zha summons another group of mummy guards from fragments and sandy stone to delay Shiva. The fully regenerated two-headed cobra destroys them from beyond ten meters, becomes invisible while carrying Shiva through buildings at high speed, and produces a lightning strike beneath him.
- Xuenai's mental tracking is invaded and falsified, showing China Team motionless in an empty clearing before all targets disappear from her senses. This also prevents India Team from maintaining pursuit. The source has not yet explicitly identified the intruder.
- Imani dies from his accumulated injuries, while Arot reverts from werewolf form and is reportedly close to death. Lamb fails to confirm Zero's death; Zhang Jie later reaches the clock tower carrying Zero with a blood-covered chest.
- Lamb's needle enters through a gap between Zero's lung lobes, missing lung tissue and the heart but leaving pooled blood in the chest cavity. Without thoracic surgery, he will suffer chest pain during movement for several days but is otherwise stable.
- Zhao Yingkong's bitten shoulder has severed blood vessels and tendons with possible bone damage. The left arm is unusable and cannot be restored by contemporary medicine, though her general mobility returns rapidly; she requests surgical supplies, disinfectant, gauze, local Egyptian healing medicine, and lean meat.
- China Team's system score is confirmed neutral after Qi Tengyi's death produces negative one and Imani's death produces positive one. Zheng sends Gao Hongliang and Zhang Heng into Cairo with O'Connell for supplies, consciously accepting risk to newcomers to preserve veteran combat strength.
- Xiao Honglu assigns a seventy-percent probability that Imani died and thirty percent that Arot died. Because Arot restored Imani's lost arm, Xiao warns that Arot's survival would erase roughly half the value of China Team's battle gains. Zheng judges a duel with werewolf Arot most likely to end in mutual destruction.
- Xiao identifies Arot, Shiva's likely hidden trump card, and Lamb's long-range needle as the direct combat threats; Manavia provides strong barrier support. He ranks Xuenai's mental control as the greatest threat because she can convert China Team members into attackers and concludes India Team cannot be defeated while she lives.
- Xiao Honglu chooses Hamunaptra as China Team's battlefield because its indestructible narrow passages favor Zheng Zha's close combat and its mummy guards can be controlled with the Book of the Dead. Zero accepts a solo, roughly fifty-fifty mission to snipe Xuenai from extreme range while the others race to retrieve the Book of Amun-Ra and strip Imhotep of his magic.
- Zhan Lan infers that Xuenai tracked the fleeing party psychically; Zheng Zha and Zhan Lan suspect the exhausted Zhang Jie secretly shielded them. Before the team can depart, O'Connell reports that Cairo's dead—and some people who suddenly die—are rising as living corpses.
- In the altered plot, roughly three or four of every ten risen corpses shed their flesh and become armored mummy guards. The black-robed guardian clan musters more than ten thousand cavalry along the desert route, echoing its battle against Anubis's army in the second *Mummy* film; Xiao positions Zero near them so their interception can conceal his sniper attack. Keep the clan and its bearded leader unnamed until the source supplies names.
- Arot's bite carries a lesser werewolf virus that numbs one side of Zhao Yingkong's body and blackens the flesh around her shoulder wound. She directs Zheng Zha, using the genetic lock for surgical precision, to excise the infected tissue. She previously killed an assassin partner who became corrupted by killing and brutal training and judges Arot by the same code.
- Zhao Yingkong cauterizes the debrided werewolf bite with gunpowder ignited by the Netherfire Fang, then has Zheng Zha clean and bandage it. Zhang Heng acquires a genuine hardwood English longbow and several arrows from Cairo's black market.
- Zero finds mummy guards concentrated on the routes to Cairo's gates and harbor, with several hundred near the harbor. O'Connell proposes traveling west to a parking lot, stealing vehicles, and driving around the horde; because Imhotep has absorbed only two victims and is not yet complete, the party plans to deter him with its captive black cat.
- Before leaving the clock tower, Zheng Zha uses the Na ring's stores to equip the entire party with cheap anti-spirit ammunition and summons four final mummy guards from his remaining fragments. Zhang Jie uses his compact heavy machine gun to drive a path through thousands of living corpses, grieving Qi Tengyi, whose body the bearded guardian carries for burial outside Cairo.
- The party steals a minibus from the western parking lot. Zheng Zha hot-wires it by smashing the ignition, while O'Connell drives directly through the gathering undead toward the harbor.
- Zheng Zha invokes a small tornado through the Book of the Dead, instantly exhausting all his blood energy but clearing at least ten thousand living corpses and hundreds of mummy guards from the minibus's path. His Na ring holds only 1.5 cubic meters, and less than half his anti-spirit ammunition remains afterward.
- Xuenai reacquires China Team after its mental shielding vanishes. Shiva suspects their hidden mental controller might be a Guide, but rejects the idea because Zheng Zha has independently reached the genetic lock's second stage while the supposed Guide still exists. Arot reports his pierced lung seventy-percent healed and his transformed strength and speed reduced to seventy percent.
- Imhotep attacks the moving minibus as a sandstorm, against which ordinary anti-spirit rounds have no effect. He seizes and drains one of the two remaining Americans, restoring his outwardly living appearance, then attacks the final American despite the party's attempt to deploy its black cat.
- The Na ring's force field repels sandstorm-form Imhotep and burns his shoulder, while the Netherfire Fang frightens both him and Zheng Zha's summoned mummy guards. Imhotep summons a vision-obscuring fly swarm, which the party kills with smoke grenades, and amasses at least a thousand mummy guards before the harbor.
- Lamb kills the black cat under Xuenai's mental guidance, allowing Imhotep to resume his attack; China Team's unexplained mental shielding reappears immediately afterward. Arot's werewolf mind descends into madness beyond Xuenai's control, and his attack causes Imhotep's mummy guards to turn against India Team as well.
- In second-stage combat, Arot repeatedly pierces Zheng Zha's torso, while Zheng severs one of Arot's arms at the shoulder with the particle-cutting dagger. Zhao Yingkong drives the Netherfire Fang through Imhotep's heart, rescues the last American, then wounds Arot in the side of the chest and is knocked away before Arot retreats.
- At the harbor, no passenger vessel remains and the nearest freighter is over ten meters offshore. Zheng Zha uses Qi Tengyi's stone-control incantation from the Book of the Dead to raise the road into a launch ramp; Zhao Yingkong carries the exhausted Zheng and hooks their chain to the minibus as it leaps toward the ship.
- The minibus lands safely in a freighter's loosely covered cardboard cargo. Zhao Yingkong corrects a displacement in Zheng Zha's spine, despite having torn open her own shoulder wound by carrying him. Mummy guards refuse to enter the river, and Zero's anti-spirit Gauss round collapses sandstorm-form Imhotep and sends his burning body toward the water.
- Zheng Zha develops a high fever overnight but heals wounds deep enough to expose bone in roughly one day, including damaged intestines that rapidly close around Zhao Yingkong's simple stitches. He attributes the regeneration to his vampire enhancement, second-stage bodily control, or both, while privately fearing that his body and mind are becoming less human.
- At the desert market, Zero will separate for the Xuenai assassination; Xiao Honglu and Zhang Heng will remain with the cavalry; Zheng Zha, Zhang Jie, Zhan Lan, Zhao Yingkong, O'Connell, Evelyn, Jonathan, and the bearded guardian will enter Hamunaptra. The curator is to stay with the main force.
- Qin Zhuiyu and Gao Hongliang choose to remain aboard the freighter and sail out to sea. Xiao Honglu deliberately leaves them a shared communicator and later reveals them as bait: once beyond the team's unexplained psychic protection, Xuenai controls them, halts the vessel, and then keeps it crawling within her six-hour remaining control radius.
- Xiao infers that India Team will retrieve the controlled newcomers and use the airplane from the original film to reach Hamunaptra first. Shiva indeed sends Arot and Lamb to locate an airfield, keeps the freighter moving slowly, allies with Imhotep, and asks his mummy army to engage the black-robed clan's more than ten thousand cavalry.
- At the desert market, the group buys horses and supplies and accesses the black-robed clan's underground arsenal. Xiao Honglu gives two uninformed clansmen subsets of the communicators: one goes to the main cavalry and the other travels toward Hamunaptra at half speed, while the actual party rotates three horses apiece and covers the multi-day journey in one day and night.
- Qi Tengyi is buried on a slope outside the desert market; Zheng Zha takes his remaining high-explosive grenades. Zero's Permanent Druid Hawk-Eye detects thousands of fast, tireless mummy guards and nearly ten thousand black-robed riders converging in the predawn desert.
- More than ten aircraft carry India Team toward Hamunaptra, preventing Zero from eliminating them all during the Gauss rifle's slow reload. Zero and Zhang Heng form a two-man sniper unit targeting Xuenai; the others are to search the tomb for the sun-god statue. Imhotep ambushes Zheng Zha at the entrance just as Zero fires his first shot.
- Xiao Honglu's hidden plan sends one thousand black-robed riders behind China Team with three mounts apiece. Zero's first gunshot is their signal to kill anyone approaching Hamunaptra; the communicators and absence of visible cavalry lure India Team into landing and advancing on foot.
- Zero's first shot kills a dark-skinned India Team swordsman, raising China Team to positive one. His second kills the mentally controlled Gao Hongliang, immediately returning the score to zero and making China Team initially fear Zero has died. Gao had been disguised in Indian women's clothing; two veiled women remain as possible Xuenai targets.
- Xuenai concludes the hidden mental defender is the Guide and weakening under the Lord God's punitive backlash. Guided to Zero's position, Lamb drives his electrified steel needle directly through Zero's heart as Zero fires a third Gauss shot. The needle escapes Zheng Zha's grip, and Zero dies after promising to fight beside him “until death.”
- After draining the last American, Imhotep becomes physically complete, buries the thousand-rider ambush beneath a sand wave, abducts Evelyn and Zhan Lan, and demands the Book of the Dead within approximately one hour or he will turn both women into mummies.
- Zhang Jie suffers apparent Lord God backlash: he collapses, his skin splits, and capillaries burst across his body. At the same time, a person Xuenai identifies as the Guide invades her mind during her scan and forcibly erases her consciousness and even autonomic functions; the juxtaposition strongly implies Zhang Jie, but the narration has not yet stated this outright. Arot cannot heal psychic destruction, so Shiva has his summoned cobra consume Xuenai to deny China Team the kill point.
- Zero's final Gauss shot misses Lamb's heart only because Lamb's needle hits first; the passing round still tears off Lamb's left arm at the shoulder, leaving him coughing blood and unable to walk unaided. Xuenai's death releases Qin Zhuiyu from control amid the enraged India Team.
- O'Connell, Jonathan, the bearded guardian, and the curator are sent to find the Book of Amun-Ra. With Zhang Jie incapacitated and Zhao Yingkong injured, Zhang Heng volunteers to hide in the tomb and protect Zhang Jie and Xiao Honglu.
- Zheng Zha accepts Zhang Heng's offer, prioritizing the team's overall fighting chance over the near-certain survival of Zhang Jie and Xiao Honglu if found. Zhao Yingkong claims Arot as her target, arguing that a true assassin stakes everything on one attack rather than brute force and that injury therefore does not change her method.
- India Team's four remaining veterans are Shiva Ganten, Arot, Lamb, and Manavia. Lamb's concentration is impaired and his needle range reduced to roughly fifty meters; Manavia can maintain her barrier for about ten minutes. Shiva assigns her to keep Lamb alive and orders the group to remain together while seeking Imhotep.
- Zhao Yingkong teaches Zheng Zha the assassin's foundational concealment: self-hypnosis, identification with darkness, and elimination of emotion and killing intent—the state of no-mind. Zheng Zha uses second-stage control to achieve it within seconds, then bets that India Team is bound by force rather than loyalty: he ambushes Shiva and drives him through the wall into the hidden treasure chamber while Zhao targets Arot.
- Lamb cannot target what he cannot see unless a target has already been locked, regardless of range. He and Manavia therefore abandon Shiva's battle in the pitch-black treasure chamber and carry the captive Qin Zhuiyu while seeking Imhotep; each covets the reward for killing her.
- Shiva's A-rank ability is Ancient Yamata no Orochi Summoning. It evolves by consuming people of sufficient quality; Shiva expects eating a second-stage unlocked fighter to produce a third head. The current two heads alternately breathe illuminating fire and discharge lightning, with a small pause between attacks and roughly thirty seconds before the next fire burst.
- Shiva lies about explicit sexualized torture of Qin Zhuiyu to provoke Zheng Zha; the details are omitted under the translation safety rule and are not evidence of her actual condition. Zheng instead uses the state of no-mind to crawl across the ceiling, then drops into the cobra's coils and activates Red Flame, instantly burning away both supernatural serpent heads.
- Shiva's golden prayer wheel projects a Buddhist-light barrier that stops the particle-cutting dagger, but every contact with Red Flame weakens it. Zheng Zha severs Shiva's left arm, overwhelms the barrier with dagger, Na-ring, and Red-Flame strikes, then bites off Shiva's left ear and much of the skin on that side of his face, driving him into terrified flight.
- Arot has killed forty-seven assassins and seventeen members—stated as one-third of the fighting strength—of a European assassin clan. His explicit sexual torture and murder of the clan leader's twelve-year-old daughter is represented only by an editorial omission marker. He locates Zhao Yingkong by the blood scent from her wound but charges into a narrow rock gap that traps his werewolf body; she kills him with one Netherfire Fang thrust through the mouth.
- Zhang Heng demonstrates extraordinary archery talent: he fires two initially inaccurate arrows, then has one strike the other's tail in flight and redirect it into a torch. Xiao Honglu still judges the English longbow slower and less powerful than firearms without enhancement or a legendary magical bow.
- Xiao Honglu permits Zhang Heng to fight under three restrictions: do not attack Shiva or Arot except when cornered; do not actively seek enemies; and strike other targets only from concealment. Xiao assesses the dark-skinned scimitar fighter as vulnerable to one ambush arrow, Lamb as sight-limited without Xuenai's psychic lock, and barrier-user Manavia as Zhang Heng's natural counter.
- Xiao deduces Xuenai is dead because China Team remains uncontrolled and undiscovered. He believes Zero's final shot gravely wounded her and India Team then killed her to deny the score; this causal detail is Xiao's deduction and differs from the concurrent narration, which strongly implies the Guide's psychic attack erased her mind.
- Zhang Heng attributes his involuntary fear of fighting, injury, and blood to childhood abuse by his stepfather beginning at age six. He once fled from a wanted murderer and accompanying thugs, leaving Ming Yanwei behind; although he forced himself back one minute later, they had abducted her, and his insistence on fighting now comes from guilt and a desire to prove he is not a coward.
- Part 127 opens after Manavia has been shot dead without narrating the shot itself; do not invent its mechanics. Lamb survives several pistol rounds from Xiao Honglu and escapes deeper into the tomb, while Zhang Heng is too physically sick and terrified by combat to fire a second arrow.
- Zhao Yingkong returns without visible blood and confirms by implication that Arot is dead. Xiao Honglu hints that the tomb may contain something as valuable as their lives and that Zhang Heng may have another chance involving Ming Yanwei, but does not yet state what it is.
- Shiva uses a regenerated cobra head to flee Zheng Zha, then destroys the ledge beneath Jonathan instead of directly attacking the film party. Zheng abandons the chase, leaps more than eight meters without a running start, catches the falling Jonathan, and anchors them by punching his left fist into the opposite stone wall.
- O'Connell, the curator, and the bearded guardian make a climbing rope from their clothes. The Book of Amun-Ra lies beneath the sun-god statue's solid-gold base behind a combination-like sliding mechanism; forcing it may destroy the book, and the group has moved it approximately two-thirds of the way.
- Imhotep's altar is guarded by seven or eight five-meter sphinxes made of regenerating sand, creatures absent from the original film. He has placed Anck-su-namun's mummy between the captive Zhan Lan and Evelyn and waits for Zheng Zha to bring the Book of the Dead.
- Shiva's golden prayer wheel is a B-rank Buddhist artifact requiring a B-rank side story and many reward points. It can heal and project defensive Buddhist light, regenerates its finite energy, and is currently too depleted for Shiva to heal Lamb without sacrificing his own protection.
- Lamb reaches the altar after taking a Forced-March Pill but has a wounded lung and no remaining strength. Shiva refuses to spend prayer-wheel energy and feeds him alive to the two-headed cobra to deny China Team a kill point.
