Zheng Zha and Zhao Yingkong then enter from opposite passages and jointly punch through the nearest regenerating sphinx.
- The Na ring and Netherfire Fang reduce a sphinx's regeneration to roughly one-hundredth its normal speed. Imhotep offers to release Zhan Lan in exchange for the Book of the Dead while retaining Evelyn as Anck-su-namun's sacrifice; Zheng counters by threatening to drop the book into a mire pit, so Imhotep sends the sphinxes after the Book of Amun-Ra party instead.
- Qin Zhuiyu has genuinely been subjected to the mutilation Shiva earlier claimed, is still alive, and begs Zheng Zha to kill her. Shiva feeds her to his cobra before Zheng; this is an adult victim and the scene is retained without the underage-content omission used in parts 124–125.
- Zhao Yingkong severs both mummy guards' weapon hands with silver throwing knives and pins Imhotep to a pillar with the Netherfire Fang, freeing the hostage standoff. Zheng Zha burns away the cobra heads, exhausts Shiva's Buddhist-light barrier with Red Flame, then pursues, mauls, blinds, and kills Shiva inside a dark passage.
- The provisional English title for 神鬼秘宝 is “Divine and Demonic Treasures”; keep it consistent unless later context establishes a better title.
- Zheng Zha gives Imhotep the Book of the Dead in return for releasing Zhao Yingkong, Zhan Lan, and Evelyn, but Imhotep immediately betrays the agreement and summons elite mummy guards plus new sphinxes. Physical high-explosive grenades pulverize the sphinxes but only delay their regeneration.
- Zheng Zha retrieves the Netherfire Fang and holds his Na-ring fist over Anck-su-namun's mummy, forcing Imhotep to let all three women cross the monster ranks. As they retreat, Jonathan unexpectedly collides with them while displaying the newly extracted Book of Amun-Ra, allowing Imhotep to seize Zheng's left fist and protect the mummy despite his hands burning under its spirit attack.
- Evelyn uses the Book of Amun-Ra to command the mummy guards against Imhotep and the sphinxes. With the shared key, she recites the banishment incantation; an otherworldly chariot extracts Imhotep's spirit, after which Zheng Zha beheads his mortal body and all his summoned guardians dissolve.
- The Lord God changes the mission to escaping the collapsing City of the Dead while keeping Evelyn, O'Connell, and Jonathan alive; each death would deduct 5,000 reward points.
- The Book of Amun-Ra contains light and restoration spells. Use requires a compatible energy such as internal, psychic, magical, psychokinetic, cultivation, immortal-spiritual, or divine-contract energy. Its text may be deciphered directly or learned in the Lord God's Dimension for 1,000 points; before return, the physical book must be held to use its abilities.
- The Book of Amun-Ra can resurrect any reincarnation-team member personally seen by its holder, but each individual can be resurrected only once. The subject returns to the state immediately before death, at a cost equal to twice all reward points and side stories that person ever possessed or spent. Zheng Zha learns these rules on touching the book, but a falling rock knocks it into the chasm during the collapse.
- Everyone escapes Hamunaptra, including O'Connell, the bearded guardian, and the curator. Zheng Zha recovers the shared book key and gives Jonathan only five of the promised six gold bars before returning to the Lord God's Dimension. The fourth scenario, *Divine and Demonic Treasures*, ends; the next source heading is Arc 7, *Death God's Desperate Realm (I)*.
- Six members return from *The Mummy*: Zheng Zha, Zhan Lan, Zhang Jie, Zhao Yingkong, Xiao Honglu, and Zhang Heng. Zheng orders full restoration at his expense; Zhao's shoulder requires the longest treatment, while Zhang Jie wakes with only a faint beam but looks mentally devastated and withdraws to his room.
- Each member's private room adopts any imagined environment when its doorknob is held. Each person may also create one living being free of charge, choosing sex, age, size, and appearance; its muscles, reactions, and comparable attributes may be set to twice ordinary human strength, allowing it to serve as a bodyguard.
- Only one day passes in the Lord God's Dimension while the team experiences a scenario. Zero's boyish-looking young female companion disappears after his death, causing Luo Li to fear that she too will vanish if Zheng Zha dies.
- Zheng Zha's post-*Mummy* total is 13,770 reward points, one B-rank side story, two C-rank side stories, and one D-rank side story. Xiao Honglu immediately investigates the Lord God's exchange catalog and independently infers that the deceased Chu Xuan taught Zheng about using the free creation as a bodyguard.
- Xiao Honglu theorizes that reward size scales with scenario lethality. He judges China Team extraordinarily lucky because its unplanned abilities happened to counter India Team: an unidentified member neutralized Xuenai, Zero dominated the open battlefield, and the Lord God supplied the weaker team with the Book of the Dead while Qi Tengyi could read it.
- Xiao identifies a missing team function: a captain who coordinates members' enhancement paths into complementary combinations. He also infers that India Team could preview its next scenario from its desert-adapted clothing, while China Team failed to trigger or obtain some system option; India Team's easier past scenarios produced greater raw strength but less experience fighting to the death.
- Zheng Zha plans to upgrade the mutated vampire bloodline to the B-rank Vampire Count bloodline to expand blood energy. Xiao Honglu proposes a 2,000-point miniature surface-to-surface missile capable of destroying most or all living creatures within a one-kilometer radius.
- The Lord God sells very cheap theoretical reports, including Gauss-propulsion miniaturization, a human-genome manual, and analysis of bodily electrical currents versus internal energy; Xiao buys many for just over thirty points. Private rooms can freely create and erase non-legendary-magical weapons for testing, including tactical nuclear devices, and can support a basement at least ten square kilometers in area.
- The B-rank Mutated Troll Bloodline can regenerate within one hour despite destruction of the brain and heart provided more than half the body remains, but it grants little strength or agility and cannot evolve. Xiao recommends progressive D-to-B agility enhancement for Zhao Yingkong and the Vampire Count bloodline for Zheng because its greater blood energy also fuels the Book of the Dead.
- Zhan Lan is compatible with psychic energy and is designated China Team's future mental controller. Psychic Scan costs one C-rank side story and 4,000 points and increases range with mental capacity. Incompatibility messages demand thresholds of 2,500 for Xiao Honglu, 3,000 for Zheng Zha, 3,500 for Zhao Yingkong, and 5,000 for Zhang Heng, implying compatible people may be one in ten thousand.
- Reward points can already pay for another member's full restoration. Xiao now hypothesizes that members can also spend their own points and side stories to purchase items or attribute enhancements directly for teammates.
- Cross-member enhancement purchase is confirmed after a Lord God scan finds no coercion or coerced memory. After gifting one enhancement, the purchaser cannot buy another enhancement for anyone else until completion of the next horror scenario.
- Zhan Lan receives Psychic Scan from Zheng Zha. At her current mental capacity above 200 but below 300, it vaguely detects presence within about a twenty-kilometer radius or along a two-hundred-kilometer line, and distinguishes detailed actions only within about two kilometers. Formula: per 100 mental-capacity points, broad radius 10 km, linear range 100 km, detailed radius 1 km; Xiao recommends at least 500 mental capacity. Each person's psychic signature becomes as recognizable as a face.
- Mind Chain is the cheapest direct mental-communication ability identified, costing two B-rank side stories and 8,000 points.
- Zhang Heng purchases the rating-74 C-rank Elven Wind Archer bloodline for one C-rank side story and 2,000 points. Its Arrow of Wind concentrates all bodily strength into a shot exceeding ordinary range and power by more than fivefold, then leaves him weak for ten minutes. The path evolves through B-rank Elven Lightning Archer to A-rank Elven Dream Archer.
- Zhang Heng also purchases Ancient Bow: Sirius, a bronze-colored recurved bow costing one D-rank side story and 1,500 points. It greatly increases arrow speed and range, imbues arrows with evil-dispelling celestial-wolf killing intent, and renders struck targets unconscious through psychic shock unless their mental fortitude is exceptionally strong.
- After gifting Psychic Scan, Zheng Zha retains 9,770 points plus one B-rank and one D-rank side story; he plans the Vampire Count upgrade and a D-rank high-tech heavy weapon.
- The rating-90 mutated Vampire Count bloodline costs one B-rank side story and 3,000 points and requires the mutated Vampire Viscount prerequisite. It regenerates continuously while brain and heart remain intact, enables vampire abilities, and retains the mutation's immunity to sunlight and silver. During enhancement, temporary black fleshy wings tear through Zheng Zha's clothing and then vanish.
- Vampire Count gives Zheng Zha more than ten times his former raw vampire energy and improves its density from a gaseous sensation to a bloodlike liquid; measured at the former quality, the usable amount is twenty to thirty times greater. Red Flame rises roughly one meter, contains apparently stronger golden threads, and can now last over thirty minutes rather than a few minutes.
- Zheng Zha's stated post-upgrade attributes are intelligence 277, mental capacity 332, cellular vitality 521, neural reaction speed 487, muscle density 602, and immune-system strength 431. The source first omits his remaining C-rank side story, then states after the Count purchase that he has one C, one D, and 6,770 points; preserve this discrepancy.
- The increased vampire energy breaks its former equilibrium with internal energy and spontaneously attacks the dantian, causing qi deviation, burning meridians, and bloody vomiting. Zhang Jie instructs Zheng Zha to enter the second stage and consciously bind vampire energy inside his mind, explaining that the genetic lock is necessary to master powerful enhancements and prevent fatal backlash.
- Zheng Zha needs more than ten hours under second-stage control to force vampire energy back into his mind and bind it there. Some vampire power permanently merges into his internal energy, turning it faint red and highly corrosive: a strike using one-tenth of his internal energy silently penetrates slightly over twenty centimeters of granite, more than ten times his former destructive power.
- A basic catalog entry simply called Lightness Skill costs one C-rank side story and 2,000 points. Similar introductory entries apparently exist for palm, fist, finger, and sword techniques.
- An ordinary person can express at most about 10% of an A-rank enhancement; genetic-lock stage one allows 20–30%, stage two 40–50%, and stage four 100%. Five stages are known. Stage five permits altering, optimizing, and adapting the enhancement itself, while higher-stage users created many catalog bloodlines and abilities through genetic or energy control.
- Zheng Zha's hybrid corrosive internal energy resembles the A-rank Xuan Yin Art but is an original, much cheaper shortcut. Zhang Jie says higher genetic-lock stages are about creating one's own strength, then agrees that some truths must wait for the next scenario; Zheng Zha directly asks who he really is.
- Zhao Yingkong purchases the five-stage Shining Enhancement Gene path, progressing from D to double-A rank. It greatly increases battle speed; its Shining ability multiplies speed severalfold or more for ten seconds, limited by her neural reactions, and reinforces fractures in her mind enough to resist ordinary mental control. She gives more than 2,000 surplus points to raise Zhan Lan's mental capacity, expanding broad Psychic Scan to a forty-kilometer radius.
- Xiao Honglu purchases a protective jade pendant with his D-rank side story. It automatically blocks lethal attacks up to thirty activations or until its stored energy is depleted. The source compares it to Shiva's prayer wheel but here calls that wheel C rank, conflicting with the earlier B-rank description; preserve the discrepancy.
- Zheng Zha purchases Lightness Skill for one C and 2,000 points, and the Air Cannon for one D and 2,000 points. The palm-sized Air Cannon, based on *Doraemon*, consumes internal or vampire energy rather than ammunition, charges for about two seconds, pulverizes steel armor into sand, and can reduce an unobstructed living target to pulp within fifty meters; it has no special anti-spirit effect.
- After also buying large quantities of anti-spirit ammunition, Zheng Zha retains 1,270 points. Lightness Skill permits six-meter-plus leaps and repeated rebounding from branches but exhausts his current internal energy within several minutes.
- With three days remaining, China Team begins a friendly match: Zheng Zha and Zhan Lan versus Zhang Jie, Zhao Yingkong, Xiao Honglu, and Zhang Heng; high-powered weapons and vital strikes are forbidden, and any injury eliminates a participant. Zhan Lan hides and supplies positions by Psychic Scan while Zheng fights alone.
- Zhang Jie's private basement can expand across an area one hundred kilometers wide and reproduce forests, lakes, and grasslands. During training, Zhang Heng's best practical range with Ancient Bow: Sirius is 1,500–2,000 meters; Arrow of Wind can approach 10,000 meters but exceeds his realistic aiming ability. His rear arrow can strike and explosively accelerate a leading arrow, whose displaced air alone cuts Zheng Zha and wins the match.
- An accidental sexualized contact with underage Zhao Yingkong during the spar is omitted with an editorial marker; tactically, Zheng pins her unharmed against a tree and she concedes, praising his formless unlocked fighting style.
- China Team adopts five provisional rules: (1) the current six are members; newcomers are evaluated by occupation and fitness, then voted into provisional status and aided when veterans are not endangered; (2) unrecognized newcomers receive a weapon and gold bar, or water and food without civilization, and may be killed or incapacitated if they threaten members, the team, or scenario requirements; (3) rewards are jointly recorded and enhancements must serve specialized team roles, with three ignored warnings punished by collective execution; (4) the captain holds special scenario items unless a 70% vote transfers one to collective custody, and a failed challenger loses the right to another vote on that item; (5) mental controllers, strategists, and special-ability members receive priority protection and captain-directed support capped at one-quarter of a combatant's rewards.
- Zhang Heng's family archery manual contains Curving Arrow and Explosive Arrow. Explosive Arrow fires at least two shafts in succession so that a more forceful rear shaft strikes and accelerates the leading one to more than twice its normal speed and power. Its legendary nine-shaft culmination is called Nine Arrows Shooting Down the Sun. Zhang Heng can currently perform only the two-arrow form and repeatedly uses full restoration to repair training damage to his hands.
- Zhan Lan's private diary reveals unresolved grief for a dead former lover and her growing feelings for Zheng Zha. After rereading entries from her earliest scenarios, she writes that she will abandon romantic love and apologize to the dead man in hell.
- Two minor-related sexualized passages in part 140—one involving Luo Li and one involving Xiao Honglu's adult created companion, Sister Lan—are replaced with editorial omission markers while their surrounding relationship and character beats are retained.
- Zhao Yingkong trains to navigate a randomly packed obstacle course in total darkness without sound, then attempts to attack a scarred human-shaped iron post. Her remaining weakness is the killing intent and mental fluctuation that surface at the instant of attack. The source's explicitly fetishizing aside about the underage character is omitted.
- Zhang Jie asks his created companion to vanish from the world with him—neither life nor death—and she promises to follow him anywhere. He calls the next scenario the final stretch of their journey and unexpectedly takes her into the Lord God's beam.
- Zheng Zha's Na ring has 1.5 cubic meters of storage and is now completely filled by gold, weapons, ammunition, explosives, the Book of the Dead, food, water, and medical supplies.
- The next scenario is *Final Destination 2*. Retain that official English film title. Luo Li has watched the franchise and warns Zheng that apparently unrelated circumstances can combine into sudden death immediately before the Lord God announces it.
- In *Final Destination 2*, Zhang Jie openly reveals himself as a mental controller, controls Zhan Lan, Zhao Yingkong, Xiao Honglu, and Zhang Heng, and leaves with them and his created companion. He challenges Zheng Zha to incapacitate the four without killing them or bypass them and defeat Zhang Jie; each defeated teammate earns part of the truth, while defeating Zhang Jie reveals everything and makes Zheng captain.
- Zhang Jie states that a previous *Final Destination* scenario wiped out everyone except him and calls the franchise one of the Lord God's hardest possible series. He guarantees the four controlled veterans will survive unless Zheng kills them, but abandons the newcomers.
- The *Final Destination 2* mission is to survive five days. Each surviving film character grants every team member 500 points; preserving all of them grants every member one B-rank side story and 5,000 points. A reincarnation-team member who kills a film character loses 5,000 points per death.
- The source calls this a fourteen-person scenario although seven returnees/companions awaken with eight newcomers aboard the bus; Zhang Jie's created companion may be excluded from the difficulty count, but do not silently resolve the arithmetic.
- Wang Renjia, commonly called Wang Xia, is a tall, strongly built Chinese reconnaissance soldier. He reacts intelligently to Zheng's threat by blocking the submachine gun trigger and attempting a wrist lock, then accepts the situation after Zheng crushes the gun with pale-red internal energy. Use “Wang Xia” after his introduction.
- Zheng tells the newcomers that 1,000 points are awarded for each survived scenario and that accumulating 50,000 permits a return to reality. He distributes one gold bar to each of the eight newcomers and three communicators among their groups but withholds guns because he lacks enough for everyone.
- The eight newcomers initially divide into two groups: a delinquent Sichuan girl of seventeen or eighteen with a muscular, handsome dyed-haired man; the remaining newcomers follow Wang Xia. One exceptionally beautiful young woman with a meter-long braid keeps her eyes closed because she is blind; Zheng refuses her request to follow him and asks Wang Xia to protect her from deliberate harm.
- The *Final Destination 2* protagonist is Kimberly. The Lord God alters her opening premonition so that the bus explosion and the team's collective death fall between the original mother and son's deaths. Because everyone aboard dies in the same explosion, Zheng cannot determine an individual death order for the team.
- Zheng hypothesizes that Zhang Jie's group left before being incorporated into Kimberly's premonition and may therefore stand outside Death's attack sequence. Rather than pursue them, he decides to wait for them to attack him or settle matters after the five-day return.
- Zheng seeks false identification through the bartender at the Waves Bar, following advice he remembers from Zero, because police detention and isolation in a room would be especially dangerous under *Final Destination* rules.
- At the Waves Bar, Zheng pays the bartender two gold bars and the document maker four to obtain false identification by noon the next day. A camera flash masks a dagger ambush, but Zheng catches the weapon and leaves fingerprints pressed into its metal.
- Death's first apparent attack upon Zheng begins when a motorcycle skids on the wet road, then strikes a utility pole and explodes. A stone fragment severs a high-voltage line, which lands in Zheng's puddle and paralyzes him. He survives by enduring the Air Cannon's two-second charge and firing downward so its recoil throws him several meters clear, after which he loses consciousness.
- Zheng recovers motor control roughly two minutes after the electrocution, defeats more than a dozen gang members, and recruits their wider network of two to three hundred motorcyclists to locate Zhang Jie's six-person party: three Asian men, three women, including Xiao Honglu, an eleven- or twelve-year-old boy. He offers two gold bars per person or thirty for locating all six, plus expenses.
- This is Zheng's first deliberate attempt to imitate Chu Xuan and Xiao Honglu's strategic thinking: he uses the horror-film world's local population and environment to offset Zhang Jie's control of four enhanced teammates. Increased intelligence improves memory and processing, but the narrative explicitly distinguishes purchasable intelligence from learned wisdom and modes of thought.
- The dyed-haired male newcomer is Yang Le; the delinquent Sichuan girl is Lu Chichuan, stated to be seventeen or eighteen. Their explicit sexual conversation is omitted with an editorial marker while preserving that Yang propositions her and they remain paired.
- Lu Chichuan's apparent death chain begins when stolen-cigar smoke triggers a bank's fire sprinklers; she collides with the smokers, skids from the wet pavement into the roadway, and faces an intoxicated driver coming blindly around a curve. At the same instant, sleeping Zheng believes he hears a scream.
- Zhao Yingkong infiltrates Zheng's room concealed beneath an unusually heavy food cart, then opens her attack with Netherfire Fang aimed at his heart. Her perfectly suppressed presence becomes perceptible only when killing intent emerges at the instant of attack.
- Zhao's Netherfire Fang pierces Zheng's left arm and enters his chest by roughly half an inch; he kicks her away before she can draw throwing knives. Zhang Heng then uses a two-shaft Explosive Arrow that passes between Zheng's lungs, emerges a few centimeters beside his spine, and carries him several meters into an alley.
- Zheng deliberately accepts the torso hit to feign grave injury and lure Zhao into close range, but his unlocked body's automatic use of Lightness Skill slows his fall. Xiao Honglu notices the unnatural descent and orders Zhao to withdraw.
- Vampire Count regeneration causes the pierced arm to scab over within minutes, repairs even the damaged arm bone, and visibly closes an explosive chest wound almost three coins wide; Zheng expects complete healing by the next day. The recovery produces extreme hunger, implying a major nutritional cost.
- Zheng reasons that a frontline captain can still command effectively once Zhan Lan's Mind Chain and Psychic Scan connect the battlefield, allowing immediate tactical adjustment while he directly measures enemy strength.
- More than a dozen local gang leaders collectively control over two hundred people. Zheng offers escalating rewards—two gold bars for the first teammate found, three for the second, and so on—then melts a bar with Red Flame to deter betrayal. He separately seeks a corruptible police contact and an arms dealer.
- Local gang members mistake Zheng for an aspiring underworld boss and compare him favorably with the city's current leader, Black Czech. Zheng accepts the misunderstanding only as a temporary tool for finding Zhang Jie's group within five days.
- Wang Xia served in a special detachment of a field-army division under the code name Greedy Wolf. He specializes in reconnaissance, counter-reconnaissance, mine emplacement, and trap construction; Zheng judges his starting combat value potentially comparable to Zhao Yingkong when she first entered.
- Six newcomers staying with Zheng: Wang Xia; Huang Lilin, a magazine editor/freelance illustrator; Li Laoshi, nicknamed Honest Li, a plumber and electrician with twenty years' experience since age fourteen; Zheng Xue, a lawyer seeking work in Hong Kong; unemployed, timid Wang Xiaoxiao; and Zhu Wen, a professional fortune-teller with abnormal vision.
- Zhu Wen is not blind. A childhood vehicle collision mutated her optic nerves, allowing her to see a death aura or instinctive premonition gathering around people before they die. Her open eyes show only slightly protruding whites. In the Waves Bar room, she sees everyone surrounded by darkness like smoke from burning corpses and judges all six equally liable to die at any instant.
- Yang Le contacts Zheng in panic to report that Lu Chichuan has died; his plea is cut off by a hiss followed by the sound of spraying blood, implying another immediate death attack.
- Lu Chichuan is confirmed killed by a car at a street bend. Yang Le steals a motorcycle and is decapitated—along with the left hand holding his communicator—by nearly transparent fishing line accidentally stretched across a narrow street from second-floor fishing tackle.
- The team's insertion changes *Final Destination 2*'s reverse death sequence into apparently random targeting: Death attacks Zheng before Lu and Yang die while the film's mother and son remain alive. Zheng rejects the psychiatric hospital as an absolute refuge because the Lord God raises difficulty when participants exploit apparent safe loopholes.
- Zheng realizes his informal rise to leadership after *Alien* may not have made him the true captain, because Zhang Jie explicitly promises the title only after being defeated. Zheng maps Zhang Jie to the four controlled teammates, theorizes they act under hypnotic suggestion rather than complete mind control, adds psychic attack to Zhang Jie's profile, and wonders whether Zhang Jie is seeking death.
- Wang Xiaoxiao selects a room near the front of the hourly hotel. After the window closes without intervention, an inexplicable breeze continues circulating inside, signaling the next Death attack.
- Wang Xiaoxiao's death chain: he leaves a bathtub running and falls asleep; an inexplicably blocked drain floods the room; water shorts a floor-standing air conditioner and becomes electrified; both door and window jam; he breaks the window; the air conditioner explodes and throws him almost ten meters from the second floor into the path of a truck, which crushes him beyond visual identification.
- Police investigating Wang Xiaoxiao connect an outside criminal organization involving Asians with inquiries about an arms dealer. After three newcomer deaths by the second day—and Zheng's own near-fatal attack—the survivors leave the hourly hotel and watch the police from a nearby French restaurant.
- Zheng's forged U.S. identity is Stephen Chow, inserted into the city's immigration database; the name prompts a joke about Stephen Chow's film *The God of Cookery*.
- Three controlled teammates—Xiao Honglu, Zhan Lan, and Zhang Heng—are traced to the central television-broadcasting building at about 2:00 a.m. Zhao Yingkong later leaves the building with Zhan Lan and Zhang Heng while Xiao remains inside.
- Using sand, bone fragments, the Book of the Dead, and vampire energy, Zheng summons four Tomb Guardians stronger than ordinary humans. He assigns one to each of four gang surveillance teams around the broadcasting building, with orders to defend the watchers while they telephone him.
- An arms dealer named Old Crab operates in the western district and accepts real gold. Wang Xia, Honest Li, and Zheng Xue agree to arm themselves and fight beside Zheng in exchange for full China Team membership if they return alive. Zheng asks Wang Xia to identify Zhang Heng's firing position within five seconds after Zheng is hit.
- Death traps Zheng, Wang Xia, Honest Li, and Zheng Xue inside a bulletproof stretch limousine beside a building demolition. Zheng cuts through the armored door, but a blast launches a wrecking ball that crushes Zheng Xue and the limousine, then nearly crushes Zheng against a bus.
- Zhang Heng's Explosive Arrow pierces Zheng's right shoulder, damages the bone and right lung, and pins him to a bus. Zheng enters genetic-lock stage two, tears it out, and escapes the wrecking ball by less than one second.
- Zheng begins involuntarily modeling his opponents through the recurring thought “if I were...” He anticipates Zhao Yingkong hidden upon the bus roof and mirrors her movement, cutting from her left chest across the arm, then predicts Zhang Heng's follow-up shot and halts his own leap by trapping the embedded particle-cutting dagger's handle between his feet. This is the first explicit sign that he is nearing genetic-lock stage three.
- After Zheng knocks Zhan Lan unconscious, Zhang Jie contacts him in a timeless shared-consciousness space and confirms that the task is one-quarter complete. He warns that around day four Death will attack without regard for nearby ordinary lives, but defeating Zhang Jie at the broadcasting tower will end the scenario successfully.
- Zhang Jie identifies himself as both the Guide and China Team's captain. A Guide cannot truly die until a first team member ordinarily unlocks genetic-lock stage one; the Guide tests that candidate, and success grants the captain's office and raises the candidate's genetic-lock level by one stage. A Guide instinctively cannot attack other teammates and receives direct Lord God punishment for doing so, except when attacking the candidate during the trial.
- Zhang Jie explicitly confirms that Zheng is close to genetic-lock stage three. Zheng steals an old sedan in exchange for two gold bars, revives Zhan Lan, and asks her to scan the remaining four before preparing a counterattack after acquiring weapons.
- Zhan Lan confirms that Zhang Jie's gaze left her conscious but compelled to regard Zheng as the scenario's greatest monster and kill him, supporting Zheng's hypnotic-suggestion theory and confirming Zhang Jie caused her earlier walk into the sea.
- Zhan Lan's Psychic Scan cannot penetrate within five kilometers of the television-broadcasting building, implying mental shielding like that which previously concealed China Team from India Team.
- Wang Xia judges ordinary Zhang Heng arrows comparable to an anti-materiel sniper rifle and Arrow of Wind potentially close to a Gauss sniper rifle. Because the broadcasting building requires crossing roughly one kilometer of open ground, he recommends an armored cash-transport truck; Zheng decides to rob one the next day.
- Old Crab is the western district's principal arms dealer, feared even by Black Czech. He agrees to sell weapons for five times normal price in physical gold. Wang Xia selects a compact Delta Force-style submachine gun, grenades, and a semiautomatic precision rifle effective within three hundred yards; he can convert explosives into contact-triggered traps by the following day.
- The burning label ignites Old Crab's ammunition warehouse. Exploding cartridges kill the guards, Old Crab, and John; Wang Xia is swallowed by flame, though his status is not yet confirmed. Zheng escapes carrying Zhan Lan by entering the unlocked state and using Lightness Skill so the blast wave propels rather than crushes him, but bullets and fragments shred his back without reaching the spine.
- Zhan Lan focuses Psychic Scan into a one-meter surgical field and enters genetic-lock stage one for the first time while removing every fragment with the particle-cutting dagger. She subsequently suffers the standard first-release convulsions and respiratory paralysis; Zheng keeps her breathing through mouth-to-mouth ventilation.
- At the hourly hotel, Zhu Wen sees Death as a drifting skull face accompanying an unnatural wind. A ground-floor fire traps her and Huang Lilin behind jammed doors and windows. Huang breaks the glass and helps Zhu jump safely, but an explosion launches a burning timber through Huang's chest as she follows, burning her to death above the street.
- Zhan Lan's first genetic-lock release produces mainly mental agony, respiratory paralysis, and a headache rather than Zheng's full physical extremity, establishing that initial symptoms vary. Zheng warns that repeated early activation can cause genetic collapse and death unless the Lord God restores her quickly.
- Zhu Wen is taken toward the police station. Her death aura intensifies behind a bamboo-laden construction truck; strands of its restraining rope snap, and when the driver opens the partition in response to her unheard pounding, a falling bamboo pole penetrates the windshield and enters Zhu's mouth through the opening.
- After a night in an open plaza more than one hundred meters from tall buildings, Zheng's Count bloodline largely heals his shrapnel-shredded back and nearly repairs the bone-deep shoulder wound. Zhan Lan spends the night maintaining Psychic Scan while they prepare to repeat their two-person practice-match strategy against the controlled team.
- At about 9:00 a.m., Zheng incapacitates six cash-transport guards, severs two arms, and steals an armored cash truck while Zhan Lan draws Zhao Yingkong away by taxi. The uncontrolled rear compartment scatters cash and causes a crowd to trample the guards severely.
- Death sends a driverless truck into the armored vehicle at an intersection. Zheng uses the Air Cannon to pulverize his own armored door and the other truck's front end, narrowly avoiding a building column. At roughly one kilometer from the tower, Zhang Heng fires two ordinary arrows less than one second apart into the same point, penetrating and then shattering the bulletproof windshield.
- The armored cash truck crashes through the broadcasting building's reception desk without striking anyone. Psychic Scan remains completely blocked inside. Zhan Lan estimates Zhao will catch her in ten minutes, and Zheng swears to defeat Zhang Jie before then.
- Inside the broadcasting building, Zheng avoids elevators and uses Lightness Skill to climb over twenty floors in under a minute, but his intermediate internal energy sustains the technique for only several minutes.
- Zhang Heng uses angled glass reflections and Curving Arrow: two arrows collide in flight to change the leading shaft's trajectory. Zheng's opponent-modeling predicts the shot, and he brakes his leap by catching the deactivated particle-cutting dagger between his feet; without vibration, its edge is stated to be inferior to an ordinary kitchen knife.
- In a fifty-meter corridor below the roof, Zheng charges before Zhang Heng's paired shafts can combine, deliberately allowing each to pass separately through the same left-center chest wound. They graze the heart and damage a lung, but bring him within slightly more than ten meters before Zhang Heng prepares Arrow of Wind.
- At three to four meters, Arrow of Wind exerts overwhelming perceived pressure. Zheng imitates Chu Xuan's reasoning, tears open his chest wound, and splashes Zhang Heng with blood; Zhang's combat trauma disrupts his unlocked state and makes the arrow graze Zheng's scalp. Its displaced air cuts skin, and the shaft penetrates then fractures an entire concrete wall, demonstrating power comparable to a Gauss sniper round.
- Zhang Heng enters genetic-lock stage one for the first time while firing Arrow of Wind, then collapses with foam and convulsions. Zheng knocks him unconscious; Xiao Honglu voluntarily incapacitates himself with a flowerpot.
- Zhang Jie's abilities are the Eye of Suggestion and double-A-rank telekinesis. His hypnosis is a strong suggestion rather than direct control and therefore preserves the targets' full combat power; defeating its source will release Zhao Yingkong. His telekinesis stops a submachine-gun burst two meters away, reverses it base-first, and attacks nearly the entire rooftop at once.
- A Guide is an anthropomorphic consciousness created by the Lord God, living like a teammate but respawning as a later newcomer if killed. Guides cannot serve as the primary force in clearing scenarios, cannot attack anyone except the recognized captain candidate, and disappear after a passed trial while granting captain authority and raising the candidate one genetic-lock stage.
- During *A Nightmare on Elm Street*, Zhang Jie's captain candidate passed the trial but died during the merger. Zhang Jie absorbed the candidate's body and memories and partially escaped the Lord God's restrictions, becoming both China Team captain and Guide: unable to act as the scenario's primary combat force, but able to attack any teammate.
- The original Zhang Jie was himself the first China Team member to unlock the genetic lock and passed his Guide's trial during *A Nightmare on Elm Street*. The nightmare's main enemy crossed from the dream and killed his original body halfway through their merger. The resulting being retains both memory sets but remains primarily Guide, cannot disclose his identity, can buy enhancements and express them at 100%, and cannot resurrect if killed.
- The Lord God repeatedly ordered Zhang Jie to test Zheng after Zheng unlocked the genetic lock and threatened erasure for delay. Zhang Jie's attack on a non-candidate in the previous scenario triggered a final ultimatum: Zheng fails and dies while Zhang lives, or Zhang dies and Zheng receives China Team's captaincy.
- While apparently threatening Zhan Lan's imminent assassination to provoke Zheng, Zhang Jie has actually ended Zhao Yingkong's suggestion from the moment his duel with Zheng begins. After Zheng breaks telekinetic restraint and beats him down, Zhang invites his “good brother” to share one final cigarette.
- Horror-film difficulty categories, from lower upward: technology, science fiction (such as *Alien*), fantasy, supernatural, with science-fiction warfare and “inexplicable” scenarios the hardest; *Final Destination* is inexplicable. Later franchise installments are ordinarily harder. A randomly generated plot-alteration mode makes a total wipe almost certain; *Alien* contained such an alteration.
- Because Zheng independently reached stage two and kept progressing, the Lord God copied his genes into Devil Team. It ordered Zhang Jie to kill Zhan Lan after she endangered Zheng in *Ju-On*, but Zhang preserved enough autonomy to send Zheng the warning that let him save her. His illegal attack in *The Mummy* contributed to China Team being forced into *Final Destination 2*.
- Zhang Jie dies in his companion's arms and completes the merger. The Lord God grants Zheng China Team captain authority and advances him from genetic-lock stage two to stage three. A failing Boeing would have destroyed the roof, but mission completion returns the team immediately before impact; Zhang Jie and his companion disappear together.
- Captain privileges: (1) fast, relevance-filtered catalog search showing enhancements, weapons, and techniques suited to each member; (2) advance knowledge of the next horror film; (3) hidden system information unavailable to ordinary members.
- Seventeen regional reincarnation teams are named broadly for ancient continental regions. Two special teams also exist: Devil Team, made from genetic copies of powerful members and described as the strongest; and Celestial Team, composed of captains who accept transfer, all genetic-lock users. Both are Lord God experiments in evolutionary conditions. Zheng refuses the invitation to Celestial Team.
- Excluding Devil Team, only four known people across all regional teams and Celestial Team have reached genetic-lock stage three; Zheng is one. He refuses Celestial Team because abandoning China Team would remove its captain and betray the memories of Zhang Jie, Chu Xuan, Zero, and Qi Tengyi.
- Completing *Final Destination 2* one day early leaves four film characters alive: 2,000 points plus the fixed 1,000 per member. Zheng's prior 1,270 becomes 4,270; group restoration costs him 560, leaving 3,710.
- A one-hundred-year team monument costs each member 200 points. An eternal monument comparable to the existing external message costs 10,000 per member and can appear at a specified time or under specified conditions.
- The next scenario is *Alien Resurrection* (the fourth *Alien* film). Once a team has a captain, every created companion must enter future horror films unless one D-rank side story is paid to exempt that person; Luo Li and Xiao Honglu's companion are therefore currently required to participate.
- For spacecraft combat, Xiao prioritizes energy, vibration/wave, and highly corrosive weapons while rejecting high explosives and high penetration that might breach the hull. Laser guns use high-fission batteries at fifty shots each; neutron guns use high-density batteries at three thousand shots each and reportedly possess rocket-launcher power. Even the cheapest models require a D-rank side story.
- The Fusion Gun costs 1,000 points, reaches fifty meters, and fires basketball-sized thermal-energy spheres at several thousand degrees or higher, intended to melt almost any enemy. Each 200-point high-fission battery supplies twenty shots.
- Enchanted +3 Sharpness Arrows cost 100 points per ten, +4 cost 100 each, and +5 cost 1,000 each. They increase destructive power without reducing speed or range and can penetrate exceptionally hard materials.
- Memory-Metal Filament costs 2,500 points with no side story, is over eight meters long and hair-fine, highly tough and corrosion-resistant, and remembers its first user's temperature to several thousandths of a degree. It is silk-soft at that temperature and extremely rigid at any other, making it suited to Zhao Yingkong.
- A Psychic Amplification Plate costs 1,000 points and works for about thirty seconds, briefly strengthening a psychic user enough to disrupt or control weak-minded beings. Non-psychic users suffer mental withering. Zheng recommends three for Zhan Lan.
- Genetic-lock stage three is simulation: dormant cerebral regions reproduce brain waves from familiar people encountered after the user's first release, allowing imitation of their thoughts, movements, and some abilities. Zheng currently achieves 30–40% fidelity with Chu Xuan, Zero, Qi Tengyi, Zhang Jie, and the current team. Stage four permits 100% simulation and imitation of observed creatures under the ability name genetic control; stage five is named energy control. No regional-team member is known to have broken through stage four, while Devil and Celestial Team data are withheld.
- Private rooms can generate high-fission batteries, possibly because Chu Xuan's recorder helped introduce their technical data to reality. This permits unlimited in-room testing of the Fusion Gun.
- A complete Protective Shield costs 1,500 points plus one D-rank side story, but its blueprints, manual, theory, and components cost fewer than 500 points total. Powered by a high-fission battery, the palm-sized metal plate blocks physically harmful attacks recognized by its owner's mind but not supernatural damage. Xiao needs seven days to understand and prototype enough units for the whole team.
- An explicit sexual scene involving underage Luo Li in part 166 is replaced with an editorial omission marker. Afterward, Zheng demonstrates stage-three simulation: Zero's exact sniper shot, Chu Xuan's acrobatic dual-pistol fire, Qi Tengyi's dagger placement, and Zhang Jie's ancient-Egyptian Book-of-the-Dead spell raising stone spikes.
- Stage-three simulation normally reproduces a target's greatest specialty but cannot be maintained long without crippling headaches; regulating its duration is essential in combat. After his first release, Zhang Heng can perform a three-shaft Explosive Arrow with immense power but less accuracy than the two-shaft form.
- A two-shaft Explosive Arrow using Enchanted +3 Sharpness Arrows produces such extreme effect at one thousand meters that Zhang Heng judges a single enchanted shot sufficient against Xenomorphs. Zhao Yingkong's practiced Memory-Metal Filament cleanly bisects a thick iron post with a mirror-smooth cut.
- Zhan Lan's diary records her belief that Zheng developed genuine feeling during the life-saving kiss, followed by guilt over briefly wishing Luo Li dead. She rejects the thought and affirms that Zheng and Luo Li deserve one another.
- Part 167's sexualized clothing and intimacy involving underage Xiao Honglu and his adult created companion are replaced by an editorial marker; the retained plot fact is that Xiao gives her his protective jade pendant and requires her to wear it against her body.
- Zheng's *Alien Resurrection* inventory: Fusion Gun and high-fission batteries; hemostatic spray; high-strength bandages; limited anti-spirit ammunition and a submachine gun; an acid-resistant military machete replacing the lost particle-cutting dagger; about ten gold bars; protective talismans; the Book of the Dead and key; and one Protective Shield. Luo Li must remain conscious for her shield to function and stay within three meters of Zheng.
- *Alien Resurrection* begins with the five veterans, two created companions, and six newcomers restrained in glass implantation chambers before Xenomorph eggs. Red Flame becomes effective when the scenario starts, incinerating Zheng's Facehugger and melting his restraints and thick glass. Protective Shields defend the conscious veterans, while Xiao's jade pendant automatically protects his unconscious companion; unconscious Luo Li's Protective Shield fails and she is implanted along with all six newcomers.
- Mission: after one hour the escape-pod hatch opens, and reaching it completes an individual's scenario and returns them to the Lord God's Dimension. One Xenomorph kill gives 100 points; one Queen gives 1,000 plus one D-rank side story; one evolved Xenomorph gives 5,000 plus one B-rank side story.
- A handsome dyed-haired newcomer with medical knowledge detects a second internal pulse accelerating toward synchronization with the hosts' hearts in about thirty minutes, implying Chestburster emergence on that timetable rather than the usual one or two days. The plot has therefore undergone another major alteration.
- The dyed-haired newcomer is Cheng Xiao, twenty-one and unmarried, a descendant of an impoverished traditional-Chinese-medicine family who has also learned practical surgery. Zheng provisionally keeps him because the team needs a doctor and abandons the other five newcomers after they ignore the thirty-second introduction order.
- The *Alien Resurrection* military ship has six large decks. The escape pods are on the top deck, the team begins on the fifth, the researchers are reported in Bay Fourteen on the deck above, and the medical bay is in Bay Seventeen. Escaped Xenomorphs attack the researchers during the team's approach, leaving only four alive at the end of part 169.
- The surviving researchers seal themselves in Bay Thirteen and remotely control the ship's central computer. From the upper stairwell's Bay One exit, the medical bay is roughly five minutes away. At the second-deck concourse, Xenomorph acid is already restructuring the ship's interior in the manner of the first *Alien*.
- Cheng Xiao says his traditional-medicine family fed him medicines and trained him in martial practices from childhood, explaining his ability to keep pace without breathlessness. His calmness toward death reminds Zheng of Chu Xuan, Zero, and Zhao Yingkong despite Cheng lacking the aura of a veteran killer.
- Zhan Lan's **Endurance Mantra** (`真言术·耐`) is a stamina-enhancing spell. In part 170 it helps the group run for several minutes without anyone gasping; she also supplies Zheng with a speed-enhancing spell before he races ahead.
- The enhanced *Alien Resurrection* Xenomorphs attack faster than Zheng's first-stage genetic-lock reflexes can fully track. His Protective Shield stops two adult inner-jaw strikes but nearly exhausts itself, then shatters against a tail strike; even after the shield breaks, the tail only gives his arm a shallow wound. Xenomorph acid now dissolves his clothing but no longer burns his skin.
- The military ship's self-destruct sequence is irrevocably set for one hour because otherwise its contingency programming would return the escaped Xenomorphs to Earth. The researchers estimate more than forty minutes from Bay Thirteen to the escape pods and thirty minutes for parasite-removal surgery.
- Facing incompatible deadlines, Zheng imitates Chu Xuan's detached probabilistic reasoning: the married researcher pair must choose one spouse to remain for surgery while the other receives the team's protection to the escape pods. Zheng estimates the team can cover the route in a little over thirty minutes and he can do so in roughly half that time with Lightness Skill.
- Cheng Xiao's blood is lethally poisonous and dissolved his implanted larva, explaining his early awakening. His mother is described as a grandmaster of **Gu sorcery** (`蛊术`), but without Gu medicines or insects he cannot remove Luo Li's parasite. Cheng recognizes the male researcher's surgical calluses as proof of far greater operative experience.
- The male researcher knocks out his wife and remains in Bay Seventeen to operate, allowing her to leave under Team China's protection. A rare synthetic gas can sedate a Xenomorph larva for about five minutes; within that window the surgeon severs its nutrient channel to stop its growth, after which complete extraction takes roughly thirty minutes.
- Luo Li's implanted larva is a **Queen**, said to occur in only one of several hundred hosts. Like a queen bee or ant, it shares a connection with surrounding Xenomorphs; forcibly extracting it will trigger them to converge upon the medical bay and defend it.
- Xenomorphs coat parts of the ship with a thick acidic secretion that blocks Zhan Lan's psyche scan, creating expanding blind zones. At the surgical midpoint she can still detect about eleven: six converging on the medical-bay deck and two trailing Xiao's group at a distance.
- After muscular separation, severing the infant Queen's neural connections requires roughly ten uninterrupted minutes and triggers its danger response. The surgeon regards saving Luo Li in exchange for his wife's protection as an equal trade and asks for a pistol with one bullet after completing the procedure.
- The main elevator's principal lines are protected by high-voltage grids: ordinary Xenomorphs have little defense against the discharge, though a Queen's carapace can withstand standard high voltage. After the elevator arrives carrying remains chewed by jaws estimated at three times ordinary size, Xiao concludes a Queen is above them.
- Zhao Yingkong can cast Memory-Metal Filament to bisect three human targets simultaneously at about seven meters, then rewind it around her wrist like soft silk. Against the suspected Queen, Xiao assigns Zhao to restrict its movement and Zhang Heng to deliver a three-shaft Explosive Arrow using an Enchanted +3 Sharpness Arrow.
- Zhang Heng rates relative accuracy at roughly 100 for a normal shot, 90 for an ordinary two-shaft Explosive Arrow, and 50 for the three-shaft form. Under Xiao's provocation he unlocks the genetic lock and destroys a large decoy Xenomorph with three enchanted shafts, after which the early-stage backlash leaves him convulsing and profoundly weak.
- A true Queen then appears, nearly ten meters tall and more than ten meters long. Cheng Xiao's Fusion Gun only melts a hollow in its thigh armor. Zhao Yingkong uses Memory-Metal Filament to sever its leg and her flame-wreathed, acid-resistant dagger to burn through its brain, but the Queen's earlier tail strike shatters bones from her left shoulder toward her abdomen and causes critical internal bleeding.
- At the genetic lock's second stage, Zheng can visually track adult Xenomorph inner jaws and tails, suppress pain, and kill four enhanced adults alone in close combat. A jaw strike still opens his left arm to the bone, while his corrosive internal energy can punch through a skull and Red Flame can burn a Xenomorph apart from within.
- **Evolved Xenomorphs** (`异形进化体`) are roughly five-meter humanoid hybrids born after the altered Queen reproduces enough ordinary defenders. They carry both human and Xenomorph genes, lack the normal creature's silhouette, and are valued at 5,000 points plus one B-rank side story each. Two approach Bay Seventeen while Zheng is occupied.
- Evolved Xenomorphs lack ordinary tail and inner-jaw attacks here but possess overwhelming physical strength and near-indestructible bodies; they can smash one another through large steel walls without lasting injury. Their psychic strength is extremely weak.
- Each Psychic Amplification Plate is consumed by one control attempt and success is not guaranteed. Zhan Lan first controls the weaker evolved Xenomorph, then expends her final two plates to control both simultaneously and make them impale one another through the head with torn steel. The load exhausts her psychic power and leaves her clinically near death without physical wounds.
- After the Queen is removed safely, the surgeon repeats that his wife's life and Luo Li's are an equal exchange, accepts Zheng's submachine gun and magazine, and shoots himself. Zheng secures Luo Li to his chest and Zhan Lan to his back with high-strength bandages before racing for the escape pods.
- While navigating upward without the unconscious Zhan Lan, Zheng meets the five surviving film characters, including Ellen Ripley. Ripley identifies Luo Li's extracted parasite as “the fourth Queen”; the following part has Zheng ask how she knows of “three Queens.” Preserve this source-level numerical discrepancy rather than reconciling it.
- The ship unlocks the escape-pod entrance only at the final ten-minute self-destruct warning; this is why the Lord God's individual mission specifies a one-hour wait. Ripley's five surviving film characters know the route, including an emotionally capable female android who previously interfaced with the central computer.
- Cheng Xiao knows the **Tendon-Severing, Bone-Dislocating Hand** (`分筋错骨手`), which twists joints and separates bones while deliberately keeping a victim conscious. He reveals himself as a candidate medic for a special operations unit and the second person to enter the Lord God's realm using information and an entry method provided by Colonel Chu Xuan.
- The other known experimental entrant is **Wang Renjia**, also called **Wang Xia**, a candidate scout for the special operations unit. Cheng says their experiments suggest only one specialist from any given field can enter: after Wang Xia vanished from the laboratory, no other scout responded, so they moved on to candidates such as military medics.
- In the altered climax, the newly born evolved Xenomorph kills Ripley rather than recognizing her as its mother, then crushes the remaining three men and female android. It runs faster than Zheng under Lightness Skill but turns poorly, repeatedly crashing into walls at corners.
- Zheng combines Lightness Skill with a third-stage simulation of Zhao Yingkong's extreme-speed footwork. On the final straightaway he switches to Chu Xuan's detached calculation, angles his leap to reduce an evolved Xenomorph's palm strike, and uses the remaining force to propel himself through the escape-pod entrance.
- Completing the final *Alien* film at highest difficulty gives 5,000 points and one B-rank side story; the Lord God announces that the *Alien* film series will never appear again.
- Zheng returns with nearly one-third of his body missing from the right shoulder to flank and is the last to finish restoration. Zhan Lan alone receives a violet healing pillar, reflecting her psychic depletion. Zheng's settlement totals 6,300 points and one B-rank side story.
- Cheng Xiao creates a pretty young woman with faint freckles and a single long braid, regarding her with unexpected tenderness. Zhang Heng, Zhan Lan, and Zhao Yingkong remain the three members without created companions.
- Zheng's rough strength scale: ordinary human 1; Zheng normally 3–4; with internal energy 5–7; genetic lock stage two 10; evolved Xenomorph at least 100. Xiao infers that high-difficulty scenarios still contain discoverable solutions tailored to specialist abilities rather than requiring players to follow the Lord God's apparent route.
- **Resurrection Cross** is the working English term for `重生十字章`; its mechanics are introduced in part 181.
- Zhan Lan receives 15,000 points and one A-rank side story (automatically formed from three B ranks) for the two evolved Xenomorph kills. **Mind Chain** costs one B rank plus 5,000 points in part 181, connects Zhan Lan with up to three others for four-way shared vision, and permits limited domination of weak psyches at the risk of backlash against stronger ones. This price conflicts with the earlier two-B/8,000 quote and must be preserved as a source discrepancy.
- Zhan Lan's Psychic Scan currently covers a roughly 70-kilometer area radius or 700 kilometers in a straight line. Mind Chain functions anywhere within that range, allowing separated groups to communicate without detectable radios.
- **Resurrection Cross** costs two B-rank side stories plus 7,000 points. Its holder revives five minutes after death at half of their optimal physical condition and may elect to leave the current film, forfeiting its standard 1,000-point completion reward. Zhan Lan hides one in a handmade sachet and gives it to Zheng without revealing its contents.
- Cheng's account of translated ancient records: genetic-lock users led early humanity against powerful creatures; after reaching stage five they created bloodlines, weapons, and abilities, exterminated those enemies, and built the continent-based Lord God's Dimensions to reenact the evolutionary struggle through culturally inspired horror films. The team that ultimately escapes alive is said to transform humanity.
- The team memorial now bears Chu Xuan, Zero, Qi Tengyi, Zhang Jie, and Wang Xia, followed by Zheng's inscription: “We fought side by side. Through our companions' strength and sacrifice, we survived. And so we must continue living—carrying their strength and sacrifice with us, living on together!”
- The next film is *Resident Evil 2*, with an opposing team selected randomly and revealed only after entry. Xiao argues the Lord God prevents simple 50,000-point escape through random or enhancement-scaled difficulty, making genetic-lock growth—especially stage-five energy control—the path beyond purchased power.
- **Magic Network Contact Constitution** progression: Initial (D, spell levels 0–3), Intermediate (C, 4–5), Advanced (B, 6–7), Super (A, 8–9), and Magus Constitution (S, 10–11). Xiao acquires Initial and Intermediate for 2,500 total points after splitting one B rank into C/D components.
- Xiao buys **Ray of Frost** (level-zero cantrip, seven uses daily) and **Summon Monster I** (level one, five uses daily) for two D ranks. The former frosts roughly one square meter weakly; the latter produces a fierce, horned hound approximately 1.8 meters tall that obeys mental commands and can carry him.
- **Focused Mind** (`心神凝聚`) has rating 79 and costs one B rank plus 2,500 points. It grants intense focus, calm caution, and immunity to psychic control for a duration inversely related to the user's cowardice; its passive **Mind Remodeling** permanently improves composure with repeated use. It does not erase underlying trauma, as Zhang Heng's dangerous self-cutting test demonstrates.
- **Enhanced Shining** costs one B rank plus 3,000 points and extends Zhao Yingkong's Shining burst from ten seconds to one minute. Her movement exceeds Zheng's Lightness Skill, but initially disrupts distance judgment and makes speech unintelligibly fast.
- **Wind Walk** costs one A rank plus 8,000 points: light refraction makes the user invisible for three minutes and triples movement speed, but also blinds the user, a weakness Mind Chain can bypass. **Shining Symbiosis** costs one A rank plus 10,000 points and creates a soul-like guardian that blocks physical and supernatural attacks for one minute, though sufficient force can destroy it.
- Zheng recommends that Cheng Xiao postpone attribute enhancement until he unlocks the genetic lock, instead spending three C ranks on a legendary-magical defense, a close-to-medium-range high-tech weapon, and one medical path: Gu sorcery, Western surgical equipment, or **Immortal-Energy Golden Needles** (`仙力金针`).
- Cheng buys twenty **Immortal-Energy Golden Needles** in an antique jade case for one C rank. Even without precise acupoint placement they accelerate recovery; to an expert acupuncturist they feel cool like icy jade and seem worth an A-rank exchange. Cheng retains two D ranks rather than buying personal defense.
- The **Zaku Heat Hawk** (`扎古电光斧`) is a snow-white, roughly ten-kilogram axe made from Gundamium Alloy, with an intangible-but-substantial ten-meter gravity chain. Its microcomputer triggers an electrical explosion beyond five meters; a test throw at about ten meters creates a one-meter crater.
- **One Kill in Ten Steps** (`十步一杀`) is a double-A weapon technique described only as “At ten paces, kill one man.” Zheng does not purchase it.
- **Advanced Qigong Enhancement** has rating 87, costs one B rank plus 2,500 points, requires Intermediate Qigong, and grants an Advanced Internal-Energy Circulation Route that greatly increases internal energy and can boost physical attributes temporarily. After purchase, Zheng's basic Lightness Skill jump rises from a little over two meters to four.
- For 50 points, the Lord God converts Zheng's basement into a realistic created world with weather and a day/night cycle. His approximately hundred-kilometer Alpine landscape contains snowy peaks, meadow, stream, lake, rare freshwater fish, and freshwater crocodiles; only the staircase back upstairs looks artificial.
- Colonel Chu Xuan instructed the real-world program to recruit from special forces and people with unusual specialties rather than the broader military. Cheng knew and admired Chu in childhood because Cheng's uncle was a military researcher with access to the base's outer compound.
- Zhang Heng refuses to create a copy of his former lover because created companions embody the creator's own memory and conception; the woman in his mind hates him, and a copy would not be the real person. He hopes she forgets him and finds peace in the outside world.
- Xiao Honglu was abandoned at birth with neural tissue protruding through a fissure in his forehead. A neurological institute raised and studied him; when he was a child, his twenty-one-year-old nurse used a half-empty oxygen cylinder to save him during a fire and died from toxic smoke herself. His beautiful created companion reflects the lost caregiver but is not literally her.
- Team China enters *Resident Evil 2* after ten days: approximately five devoted to testing enhancements and five to rest, personal interests, and light continued preparation.
- Volume X is **Resident Evil Reunion — Part I**. Team China begins *Resident Evil 2* in a police-station office with six veterans and two newcomers; ordinary life and policing persist around the earliest T-virus infections.
- Mission: find the doctor's daughter and escort her beyond the Raccoon City evacuation checkpoint for one D-rank side story per member. Locating her triggers Devil Team's entry at a random point. Killing an unlocked opponent gives 7,000 points plus one B rank; killing a non-unlocked opponent gives 2,000 plus one C. Each enemy death scores +1, each ally death −1, and the final net score times 2,000 is awarded to every survivor.
- Team China enters before Devil Team, proving it is weaker under the established team-battle rule. Xiao views Devil Team as ultimately more dangerous than Celestial Team because all members are copied for exceptional latent growth, even though a newly formed Celestial Team of second-stage users would initially be stronger.
- The altered Raccoon City extends roughly 300 kilometers from Team China's apparent central position to one edge, implying about 600 kilometers in diameter. Xiao infers the expansion exists to prevent a rapid checkpoint escape and guarantee Devil Team time to intercept after the girl is found.
- Xiao estimates an 80–90% chance that the Lord God prevents Psychic Scan from identifying the unseen doctor's daughter (called “Angie” here), because otherwise Team China could delay contact until the film characters nearly reach the checkpoint. His preferred escape is to seize an Umbrella helicopter after locating her.
- Xiao estimates Team China's current chance against Devil Team below 10%. He nevertheless argues that only one team can ultimately escape and Devil Team's selected high-potential members will outgrow random teams unless confronted or surpassed.
- The two newcomers are **Jiang Zhe**, a roughly twenty-five-year-old lawyer whose blunt integrity made his outside life miserable, and **Miao Ruoling**, a quiet painter around twenty whose work did not sell. Both awaken unusually calm; Jiang challenges an absolutist reading of Team China's no-harm rule, and Zheng affirms captain responsibility plus newcomers' right to reject protection.
- Team China meets **Jill Valentine** at the police station and joins her prisoner-transport van toward the evacuation checkpoint, expected around 7 p.m. after a roughly 9 a.m. departure. A noon supermarket stop reveals that sealed food and water have largely been looted.
- Equipment entering *Resident Evil 2*: Zhang Heng—Ancient Bow: Sirius and Enchanted +3 Sharpness Arrows; Zhao—flame dagger, silver throwing knives, Memory-Metal Filament, Protective Shield; Xiao—two **Magic Missile Wands**, forty charges each; Zhan Lan—compact submachine gun; Cheng—Zaku Heat Hawk; Zheng—compact heavy machine gun, ammunition, anti-materiel sniper rifle, submachine gun, over a dozen ultra-high-explosive bombs, military machete, and Na ring storage. Jiang and Miao receive one Desert Eagle and two magazines each.
- If forced into direct battle, Xiao recommends splitting Team China into several groups, compelling Devil Team to divide and sacrificing more of its coordination advantage than Team China's Mind Chain-supported teamwork; the cost is that a defeated subgroup cannot be rescued.
- Jill receives enough concentrated food and solidified water to sustain one person for three months. Her van reaches the checkpoint crowd after nightfall; Umbrella has erected a nearly ten-meter steel perimeter wall in about a dozen hours, with an approximately one-meter-thick armored gate that ultra-high-explosive bombs may breach faster than Red Flame.
- Contingency checkpoint breach: Zhang Heng uses Arrow of Wind plus an enchanted arrow against the gate, Cheng supplies suppressing fire with Zheng's heavy machine gun, Xiao adds Magic Missile Wand fire, Zhang provides precision fire after the breach, Zheng and Zhao assault inside, and Zhan Lan scans/manipulates defenders. They must avoid killing central film characters lest the plot path to the doctor's daughter disappear.
- Umbrella closes the gate and threatens the crowd. Jill's prisoner van is stolen during the panic, so the wounded Black officer guides the party toward a cathedral six hours away; after one hour on foot, Zheng tears open a repair garage and obtains a small cargo truck.
- Each Licker kill awards 50 points. The altered cathedral contains more than a dozen Lickers over five meters long rather than the original plot's four. They are slightly stronger than ordinary Xenomorphs in melee but less agile and vulnerable to sufficient ranged heavy fire.
- Mind Chain currently links Zhan Lan with Zheng, Zhao, and Zhang Heng, giving shared scan-marked targets. An evolved Licker eight to nine meters long kills and consumes another Licker, then visibly grows larger, echoing the first film's flesh-driven genetic evolution.
- **Alice** remembers Zheng and Zhan Lan from Team China's first *Resident Evil*, proving this sequel continues their altered timeline. Three people escaped the Hive: Alice, Matt, and **Matthew Addison**, whose chest wound led Umbrella to take him alongside Matt. Xiao proposes recruiting the altered **Nemesis** if he remembers the team, or even using Umbrella forces against Devil Team.
- Cheng's Immortal-Energy Golden Needles stimulate detoxifying acupoints without manual rotation. Seven or eight needles activate the bitten officer's liver, clear roughly 80% of the T-virus within three minutes, and leave the remaining 20% for his body to eliminate over several hours; a cross-cut over the bite drains black blood.
- Alice's T-virus mutation is fully expressed rather than limited like purchased enhancements, placing her below only one or two current Team China members in combat. She and Zheng both enter an instinctive unlocked state during the graveyard fight.
- One fist-sized ultra-high-explosive bomb annihilates the graveyard zombies, creates a deep crater, damages surrounding buildings, and has an approximately 100-meter blast radius.
- **Dr. Ashford** contacts the group by public telephone: find his daughter Angie at a city-center school and he will evacuate everyone before Umbrella destroys Raccoon City by airstrike at dusk the next day. Zhan Lan locates an intact medium bus; the route remains drivable for 60 kilometers until a vehicle-blocked bridge associated with the Nemesis encounter.
- At the bridge, a ricochet from **Nemesis One** kills the bitten Black officer. Zheng publicly claims to carry a super-evolved T-virus and challenges Umbrella to bargain as equals, intending to trade perceived genetic value and anti–Devil Team service for evacuation and military support.
- Under advanced internal energy, each Lightness Skill step covers 7–8 meters. Umbrella measures Zheng against Nemesis One at over seven times its speed, over three times its strength, and over eight times its total combat ability; after left-arm and rib fractures, its projected win probability falls below 5%. Zheng believes the creature is Matt and disables rather than kills it.
- Zheng's bridge punch opens an approximately two-cubic-meter cavity and nearly severs the structure; about 80% of its effect comes from corrosive internal energy, which became stronger rather than disappearing after the advanced upgrade.
- An Umbrella supervisor orders the next day's airstrike canceled, sends seven mercenary teams into Raccoon City, and deploys **Nemesis Two**, described as an experimental subject with extraordinary physical attributes. Umbrella intends to capture Zheng if he defeats it decisively.
- Umbrella identifies Zheng as a missing former Hive security officer and interprets his invented super-evolved T-virus claim as a natural post-leak mutation, provisionally naming it the **G-virus**. Senior leadership cancels the nuclear strike, authorizes live capture, Nemesis Two, and temporary acceptance of his demands.
- Zheng orders a four-hour rendezvous before dawn at Umbrella's city-center headquarters, demanding helicopters and at least seven mercenary teams against a supposed rival corporation (Devil Team). Alice accepts his promise that he will not assist Umbrella's bioweapons program.
- A special wristwatch costing one D-rank side story plus a large but unspecified point total can take a consenting film character who knows about the Lord God's Dimension out of their scenario; afterward the person functions much like a created companion.
- **Jason Ray**, who asks to be called **LJ** in formal settings, joins the bus after Jill previously freed him at the station. He reports a zombie Tibetan mastiff larger than a lion that decapitates other zombie dogs, plus the armed Nemesis.
- At the school, Psychic Scan cannot detect Angie. Zheng, Alice, and Zhao search inside while Cheng, Zhang Heng, and Jill protect the others. Zheng uses the Book of the Dead with bone fragments and sand to summon four armored mummy warriors capable of running along walls and ceilings; a startled mercenary destroys one before Angie reveals herself.
- Devil Team enters on a high-rise 93 kilometers northwest with ten members—eight men and two women—in standardized black bodysuits. Known roster: scarred clone Zheng Zha (captain), clone Chu Xuan (battlefield commander), Zhao Zhuikong, Ming Yanwei, Tom, Amyia, Frodo, Dechai, Norshad, and François.
- Tom and a female partner perform joint psychic work; Tom is slightly stronger than Zhan Lan, while Amyia is approximately equal. Zhan Lan compresses her scan from broad range to a seven-kilometer radius to create a concentrated field that blocks Devil Team's detection.
- Clone Chu's assessment: Team China has four primary fighters; Zheng is one of four known third-stage users with melee and limited magic, Zhao's movement resembles Zhao Zhuikong's assassin habits by 70%, Zhang Heng has weak will but trusted longbow power, and Cheng possesses mysterious naturally trained internal energy that makes him stronger in melee than ordinary first-stage users.
- Devil Team mission: protect Jill, Angie, Alice, Jason, and Carlos until they leave the city. Assignments: Zhao Zhuikong targets Zhao Yingkong; Ming Yanwei targets Zhang Heng; clone Chu, Tom, Amyia, and Frodo target Cheng, the newcomers, and especially Zhan Lan; clone Zheng, Dechai, Norshad, and François target Zheng. Clone Chu coordinates everyone except the captain.
- Because both teams exceed the *Resident Evil 2* difficulty threshold, the Lord God announces that T-virus evolution will complete in one minute. Evolved zombies grow regenerative tendrils and survive ordinary headshots unless the skull is completely destroyed; the giant zombie mastiff regenerates similarly until Zheng burns it to ash with Red Flame.
- **Carlos** is the surviving school mercenary, infected for slightly over two hours and contacted by Dr. Ashford. Alice uses antidote from Angie's concealed backpack compartment on Carlos and Zheng.
- Angie's father developed a T-virus prototype code-named **Survivor** to treat her hereditary immune weakness. It enhances physique, reflexes, learning, and other faculties without changing body or consciousness; Angie can briefly enter an unlocked state and memorize a book in one reading. Team China identifies it as a potential technological method for mass-producing first-stage genetic-lock users.
- Team China theorizes that Survivor regulates the otherwise lethal hormone reaction associated with technological first-stage unlocking while stimulating muscles and nerves. An unlocked newcomer using ordinary firearms may possess more than ten times an ordinary person's combat ability.
- Culturing Survivor requires a trace element nearly absent on Earth and mainly carried by meteorites, but Lord God can supply raw materials cheaply. The required prototype and genetic/cultivation data are held by Umbrella.
- Acquisition options: finish the scenario and pay to revisit later, or have Zheng feign capture, infiltrate Umbrella from within, steal the data and cultivation method, then fight back to the checkpoint after the team returns. Xiao considers the second plan extraordinarily risky, especially if Devil Team pursues Zheng.
- A transfer pillar can hold about three ordinary male bodies, suggesting each member might create two armed bodyguards; if created people can receive Survivor and unlock stage one, this could transform team combat. The compatibility remains explicitly unproven.
- The evolved city horde includes stronger tendril-regenerating zombies whose appendages entangle bus tires, plus Lickers covered in bloodred bone spikes. Zhan Lan drives by closed-eye Psychic Scan while Zhang Heng and Alice intercept Lickers, Cheng clears climbing zombies, Zhao protects passengers, and the others fire; Zheng opens the path with an airburst ultra-high-explosive bomb thrown over one hundred meters.
- Focused Mind removes Zhang Heng's fear of the evolved horde, while Mind Chain gives him scan-guided accuracy close to one hundred percent. After the initial breakthrough he has sixty-four arrows remaining, Carlos has two magazines, and Xiao Honglu has exhausted one Magic Missile Wand.
- Some tendril mutants consume zombies whole and grow with each victim. One more-than-three-meter specimen lands on Team China's bus and begins absorbing Jiang Zhe's arm; Zheng amputates the arm to save him, Zhang Heng kills the creature through the roof, Zhao Yingkong uses Memory-Metal Filament against further attackers, and the Book of the Dead raises stone spikes from the street.
- Clone Zheng can manifest black membranous wings from bones extending behind his shoulders and wields a huge two-handed sword wreathed in black flame. He single-handedly eradicates a mixed group of zombies, Lickers, and tendril mutants before ordering Devil Team to catch and kill Team China.
- Cheng Xiao stabilizes Jiang Zhe after Zheng's emergency amputation; the T-virus has not spread, and clean severance might permit modern surgical reattachment if equipment and the arm were available. Jiang remains sedated until Lord God restoration.
- Umbrella air-drops a satellite phone and threatens Team China's bus with seven armed helicopters. Zheng persuades the board to send six against Devil Team while one escorts the bus, then secures a military transport, checkpoint inspection and release for his companions, sealed food, and his own later transfer to headquarters. Umbrella requires him to fight Nemesis Two after reaching its rooftop.
- Clone Zheng can dissolve into an agile cloud of bats and reassemble beside a target; combined with flight and his black-flame greatsword, this lets him destroy six armed helicopters in two minutes without apparent injury. He deliberately refuses to retain an aircraft, preferring to prolong his hunt for his original.
- After reaching Umbrella's city-center building in barely over twenty minutes by driving with the genetic lock open, Team China rejects explosive restraints. Zhao Yingkong activates Shining and kills nearly all surrounding guards with flame-edged strikes and Memory-Metal Filament; Zhang Heng uses a two-arrow Explosive Arrow to destroy the remaining helicopter. Zheng frames this as proof that Umbrella must bargain as an equal.
- Carlos can pilot the military transport helicopter and is assigned to fly the party from Umbrella's rooftop to the checkpoint.
- Xiao Honglu's crash contingency depends on distance: beyond one hundred kilometers from the checkpoint, Team China should remain together in a fortified position and use Umbrella's probable nuclear strike as leverage against Devil Team; near the checkpoint, it should divide and flee so Devil Team cannot erase everyone with one large-area weapon. Zheng's sensed killing intent rules out relying on peaceful coexistence.
- Tom's **psychic metal manipulation** can alter a distant helicopter's direction and drag it into a crash when Amyia supplies Psychic Scan and psychic amplification, but at the present range he cannot deform it enough to make it explode.
- Umbrella calls Alice its sole **Survivor**, a bioweapon whose T-virus acts as a secondary agent stimulating innate potential and genetic evolution; this is contrasted with **Nemesis**, which uses the T-virus as its primary foundation for forced bodily evolution.
