This usage overlaps with the Survivor prototype name associated with Angie and must not be silently conflated or corrected.
- Clone Chu predicts Team China will either fortify and use the nuclear deadline to negotiate, or split into a larger noncombat group plus three separate rear guards—Zheng, Zhao Yingkong, and Zhang Heng. He identifies seventy kilometers from the city boundary as the ideal interception point. Clone Zheng breaches the concentrated psychic screen long enough to speak through Jill and uses Tom's power to force the transport down.
- The transport and its three escorts crash nearly seventy kilometers from the checkpoint; Team China survives the transport's slide and subsequent explosion, though Jason's arm is dislocated. Zhan Lan locates vehicles in an underground garage three kilometers ahead.
- Team China divides into four groups: Zheng snipes and delays while simulating Zero; Zhao Yingkong hides nearby to ambush anyone attacking his nest; Zhang Heng covers both from greater range with Arrow of Wind and Focused Mind; Xiao Honglu, Cheng Xiao, Zhan Lan, the two newcomers, and six film characters race for the checkpoint. The three rear guards coordinate across complex urban terrain.
- Miao Ruoleng kills an evolved zombie dog with her Desert Eagle. In the underground garage, tendril mutants feed on corpses and a Licker from the ceiling; Zheng's timed airburst bomb destroys the nest while preserving several cars. The party selects sports cars, with Zheng, Zhao, and Zhang Heng sharing one before separating.
- Devil Team uses **Green Goblin gliders**, exchanged from the Lord God for a B-rank side story or manufactured by clone Chu at high material and time cost. The available supply requires two riders per glider, though clone Chu rides alone.
- Devil Team previously completed a non-conflicting mission in ***The Legend of Zu*** and obtained cultivation texts. Clone Chu estimates that unaided practice by their method would require about fifty years to reach **Foundation Establishment**.
- Team China's final urban strongpoint lies a little over thirty kilometers from the checkpoint. Zhao hides within the alleys, Zheng occupies a high-rise with his anti-materiel rifle, and Zhang Heng takes a clock tower roughly three kilometers from Zheng. Zheng preserves Zhang Jie's cigarette butt in the Na ring alongside Zhan Lan's handmade scented pouch.
- Zhang Heng begins the ambush with thirty-seven Enchanted +3 Sharpness Arrows, enough for more than twelve three-arrow attacks; Zheng uses stage-three simulation of Zero to snipe. Zhan Lan refuses Zheng's demand to reveal them, preserving surprise even though this risks Devil Team flying past toward the convoy.
- The first anti-materiel hit knocks François from his Green Goblin glider and drains eighty percent of his defensive shield energy without wounding him. Clone Zheng blocks Zhang Heng's opening Explosive Arrow with his black-flame greatsword.
- Devil Team's local assignments are Zhao Zhuikong versus Zhao Yingkong, Ming Yanwei versus Zhang Heng, clone Zheng/Dechai/François in close combat, and Norshad providing Gauss sniper support. Clone Zheng carries his own black-covered ancient Egyptian spellbook and casts **Darkness Cage** after evading a rifle shot through bat transformation.
- **Darkness Cage** spreads black smoke across several kilometers in dozens of seconds and obstructs everyone indiscriminately. Visibility falls to about 200 meters for Zheng with the genetic lock open and roughly 50 meters otherwise; the spell is primarily an escape/support technique that prevents long-range sniping.
- **Dechai** is Thai, proficient in Muay Thai, enhanced with a two-headed/four-armed form, and at genetic-lock stage two. **François** is European, can innately communicate with some animals, has Druid shapeshifting, and is at stage one; his physical ability and enhancement make his melee strength comparable to Dechai's.
- Ming Yanwei flies on luminous featherlike wings and carries a small silver heart-shaped bow. Her appearance breaks Zhang Heng's Focused Mind before he releases Arrow of Wind; the stored force rebounds into him, causing severe internal injury, and her first toy-sized silver arrow pierces his right arm and the bronze bell behind him.
- Ming Yanwei's B-rank magical weapon is **Cupid's Bow** (`爱神之弓`). Its arrows can impose painful, conflicting emotions described as the “taste of love,” and can also carry silver flame hot enough to melt bronze. Zhang Heng wounds himself further to resist the effect, then fires an Explosive Arrow past Ming and leaves the clock tower rather than abandon his delaying mission.
- Zhao Yingkong accuses Zhao Zhuikong of killing **Yukong** (`御空`), **Mingkong** (`茗空`), and **Nikong** (`妮空`), all members of their assassin clan; these readings remain provisional until fuller names appear. Zhuikong says he killed them to test his resolve before leaving the clan. His apparent body behaves like an intangible illusion during Zhao's first two attacks.
- An as-yet unidentified humanoid more than three meters tall is deployed from an Umbrella gunship several kilometers from the battlefield. It has ironlike musculature, a gigantic feline eye embedded in its chest, strength sufficient to punch a car more than ten meters, and carries a helicopter rotary cannon plus a shoulder-fired multiple rocket launcher. Do not identify it as Nemesis Two until the source does so explicitly.
- Part 210 replaces a sexualized taunt directed at underage Zhao Yingkong with the standard concise editorial omission marker; the taunt's hostile function and Zhao's recognition of the speaker remain intact.
- François currently transforms into a black-brown bear more than five meters tall. His strongest projected Druid form is a Western dragon more than ten meters long. Dechai's battle aura remains elementary; clone Zheng projects a three-headed/six-armed enhancement combined with stronger aura as his mature melee path.
- Clone Zheng states that only he, clone Chu Xuan, and Zhao Zhuikong presently deserve to be called true Devil Team members. During a prior encounter, Devil Team entered before Celestial Team as the weaker side and escaped without combat through clone Chu's planning.
- Clone Zheng confirms the Book of Amun-Ra can revive Devil Team members with collectively paid points and side stories, but each person's one-resurrection limit still applies. He treats the original Zheng as training and permits Dechai/François to die if they cannot win.
- Zheng escapes the rooftop by accepting François's blow, falling, and using the Air Cannon's recoil near the ground. At genetic-lock stage three he simulates Zhao Yingkong's stealth, suppresses sound and killing intent inside Darkness Cage, and follows Dechai while seeking to seize a Green Goblin glider and return to the convoy.
- Dechai's elementary battle aura manifests as faint luminous sheaths around fists, feet, and knees, sharply amplifying impact. With four rotating arms and Muay Thai, he pulverizes an ordinary Licker's metal-hard claws and destroys it in under one second.
- Zheng's stage-three state passively strengthens the power and reactions of the first two stages even without active personality simulation. He exploits Dechai's relaxed instant after killing the Licker, drives him through a concrete wall, bites into his back with vampire fangs, and crushes him with arms enlarged to more than four times normal thickness.
- Zhang Heng and Ming Yanwei were childhood neighbors. Zhang's Olympic-champion archer father became abusive after the crash that killed Zhang's mother and permanently ruined his arm; years of beatings created Zhang's involuntary flight response. He reunited with Ming more than a decade later, then abandoned her during the assault, killed every attacker in revenge, and entered the Lord God's Dimension in despair.
- Ming can intuit arrow paths and targets without sight. She pursues Zhang with deliberately survivable punctures, then burns off one foot with silver flame. Zhang opens the genetic lock while fleeing and resolves to kill one Devil Team member before surrendering himself to her judgment so his death will not erase his teammates through the score.
- At the end of part 213, Zhang draws Arrow of Wind at Ming but sends it past her cheek toward an unseen target, telling her he loves her and to live. Her silver-flame arrow strikes the location of his heart while he smiles. Preserve the immediate outcome as unresolved until the next source confirms it.
- To distinguish two assassin-clan members whose toneless pinyin would both be `Mingkong`, use **Mǐngkong** for `茗空` and **Míngkong** for `冥空`; this is a deliberate tone-mark exception to the general name style. Other named dead clan members are **Yukong** (`御空`), **Nikong** (`妮空`), and **Xuekong** (`血空`).
- The assassin clan begins forced combat training at age five, progressively reducing supplies until ten children share one ration. Zhao Yingkong, the leader's daughter and a once-in-a-century prodigy, formed deep bonds despite the clan doctrine that assassins must discard affection. She, Zhao Zhuikong, and their peers returned from the training ground at thirteen to begin missions.
- On an African mission to kill a Nazi remnant for a Jewish client, Zhao Zhuikong allegedly betrayed ten deployed assassins and killed eight; one adult escaped. Zhuikong says he later lived happily in America and entered the Lord God's Dimension accidentally after opening an emailed commission rather than from despair.
- Zhao Zhuikong's **Mirror Image** duplicates his apparent body and its visible injuries; he shifts killing intent and presence away at the last instant so attacks pass through an image. He also uses nearly invisible metal filament capable of mirror-smooth cuts. Zhao Yingkong wounds his true position by redirecting her dagger during Shining, but rage makes her pursue head-on and collide with a filament stretched across the alley. Her condition remains unresolved at the end of part 214.
- Zheng kills Dechai by tearing off and pulverizing his head, then uses Red Flame to burn away his blood scent before hiding. François's Green Goblin glider carries miniature rockets; an outside rocket destroys it before he can force Zheng from cover.
- The chest-eyed giant attacks Zheng and François indiscriminately with a rotary cannon and multiple rocket launcher. Its large-caliber round lodges in Zheng's left-arm muscle without striking bone or nerves; he extracts it and heals with hemostatic spray. Zheng provisionally identifies the creature as Matthew Addison but assigns only a thirty-percent chance that it will remember him; preserve this as inference, not confirmation.
- Zheng calculates a sixty-percent chance of killing François as the wounded bear flees the giant. He ambushes it at a wall, uses the military machete to tear away part of its face, and kicks it through another wall while pursuing with the Air Cannon.
- François's bear form can hammer both green-lit paws into the ground to release a paralyzing shock wave. The Air Cannon overpowers and partially redirects it. François's transformation remains extremely resilient, but Zheng ultimately corrodes his jaw, opens his abdomen, severs his spine, and executes his returned human form with a point-blank Air Cannon blast after a two-second charge.
- The chest-eyed giant hesitates when Zheng calls it **Matthew Addison**, lowers its launcher, and then drives François back toward Zheng. It later spares the exhausted Zheng at point-blank range. This strongly supports Zheng's identification and recovered memory, but the narration still phrases the conclusion as inference.
- Clone Zheng arrives immediately after François's death and interprets the execution as proof that his original possesses the same hidden cruelty; he had previously believed the Lord God added that trait during copying.
- Clone Zheng's black flame autonomously intercepts and vaporizes rotary-cannon rounds, absorbs three rocket explosions, and vitrifies soil on contact. It is far more powerful and finely controlled than original Zheng's Red Flame; the clone remains unharmed within it and can extend a greatsword strike dozens of meters.
- Matthew Addison's raw strength exceeds original Zheng's severalfold, but clone Zheng overpowers him, tears off one arm, and uses body-covering black flame to vaporize sprayed blood and tendrils. Original Zheng intervenes with Red Flame, but the clone twists his right arm apart and severs it completely.
- Part 217 establishes `the clone` as the narrative label for Devil Team's Zheng whenever both versions appear. The clone displays irreconcilable hatred toward his original's surviving Luo Li and reacts with uncontrollable fury when asked what happened to his own version; the explanation remains pending.
- Clone Zheng was copied immediately after original Zheng created Luo Li following *Resident Evil*, because that act sharply raised his survival potential. His copied state included points, enhancements, possessions, the Na ring, and Luo Li. The then-seventeen-member Devil Team was controlled by a white captain and followers who exploited most Asian and Black members for rewards while avoiding Zhao Zhuikong.
- Part 218 omits explicit sexual violence against the underage Luo Li with the standard editorial marker. Canonically, the old Devil Team assaulted her in front of the restrained clone, then murdered and dismembered her. Clone Zheng hid rewards through *Friday the 13th Part 2*, killed the responsible members during *Predator*, reached genetic-lock stage three, inherited captaincy, and advanced to early stage four.
- Clone Zheng states optional side objectives can dwarf assigned missions: the Scorpion King's treasure during the first *Mummy* film grants an Anubis army summon, one A rank, and 15,000 points; destroying Cairo yields one B rank and 5,000 points. Preserve these as in-world claims.
- The Book of Amun-Ra offers two distinct resurrection mechanisms: Lord God-space resurrection costs twice a person's cumulative rewards, while resurrection at the source-world altar using the body and five vital organs costs one B rank plus 7,000 points regardless of enhancements. Each person still has only one resurrection.
- Clone Zheng kills original Zheng by cutting his head in half. The Resurrection Cross automatically substitutes a giant cross for the corpse and offers immediate return to the Lord God's Dimension; Zheng refuses, returns with his right arm restored, partial energy, retained wounds, and a new diagonal facial scar. Matthew Addison carries the corpse away and conceals the revival, dying from the clone's black-flame wound afterward.
- Zheng retrieves a Green Goblin glider and reconnects through Mind Chain in time to witness Xiao Honglu, Jiang Zhe, Cheng Xiao, Miao Ruoleng, and Zhan Lan die; the camcorder woman is also dead, while Devil Team carries the unconscious film characters across the checkpoint. Back in the Lord God's Dimension, Zheng vows to resurrect everyone, take revenge, and make Team China the strongest reincarnation team. This ends the novel's **First Half: The Beginning**; the second half is **The Deluge**.
- After deductions and restoration, Zheng has slightly more than 8,500 points and two B-rank side stories. He deliberately retains his new right-eyebrow-to-left-mouth scar until Devil Team is defeated. Reopening the last scenario costs 10 points per day; an older scenario costs 50 points per day plus one D rank to unlock.
- Zheng estimates two to three months and roughly 4,000–5,000 points to excavate Hamunaptra for the Book of Amun-Ra. His resurrection plan is to create an empty duplicate body for 500 points, supply its five organs at the altar, and pay one B rank plus 7,000 points to restore the dead teammate.
- Lord God exchange Green Goblin glider: one B rank plus 4,500 points, under-one-ton payload, 12 miniature air-to-ground missiles, 5,000 miniature-machine-gun rounds, lights, flamethrower, and nearly 1,000 km/h speed. Clone Chu's manufactured version has eight missiles, lights, no gun or flamethrower, and roughly 700 km/h speed.
- Zheng decides to resurrect Chu Xuan first because stage-three simulation shows that Chu's strategy and manufacturing are essential for pursuing optional side missions. Luo Li accompanies Zheng on the planned return to *The Mummy* but agrees to remain safe during dangerous work.
- Zheng's equipment doctrine holds that massed technology remains stronger than personal enhancements below A rank; only A, double-A, and S-rank development begins to exceed it. His proposed reserve weapon is a single-use ultra-miniature nuclear rocket launcher: 10,000-meter range, 5,000-meter blast radius, one C rank plus 3,000 points.
- New purchases before returning to *The Mummy*: two stronger magical protective jade pendants for two D ranks plus 1,000 total points; a 30-cubic-meter nonliving-only **Dimensional Bag** for one C rank and no points; and an unlimited-ammunition 25 mm compact aircraft autocannon for 2,700 points, with 3,000-meter range and 3,500 rounds per minute.
- Zheng spends 4,500 points and one D rank for ninety days in *The Mummy*, secretly leaving Luo Li behind because purchased return time passes as one instant in the Lord God's Dimension. His active goal is to excavate the Book of Amun-Ra and test whether independently obtaining the Scorpion King's treasure and army still awards one A rank plus 15,000 points.
- After a team accumulates three unlocked members, revealing the Lord God's Dimension to film-world inhabitants no longer incurs the earlier point penalty. Zheng tells the Cairo Museum curator that he and his companions are living people from another world.
- The guardian clan refuses to permit awakening the Scorpion King because defeat would unleash his army of Anubis against the world. It agrees to protect Hamunaptra's excavation and supply route while monitoring the work, on condition that recovered gold and Imhotep's corpse belong to the guardians.
- O'Connell and Evelyn are married, recently returned from an African honeymoon, and own an English villa purchased with Zheng's gold. Evelyn is actively seeking further supernatural treasures; the curator estimates about two weeks for them to reach Cairo after a telegram.
- One cubic meter of Zheng's stored gold weighs more than nineteen metric tons. He chooses Citibank to broker its sale and uses the Dimensional Bag to produce the gold privately in the museum storeroom.
- The nineteen-ton sale is divided among British, American, and French banks under a guardian-controlled Egyptian civil organization. Zheng separately donates another cubic meter of gold to the guardian clan, securing excavation and supply-line protection; recovered Hamunaptra gold remains theirs.
- The guardian clan knows the concealed tomb holding the Scorpion King's golden bracelet but had lost the circular key, which Zheng possesses. The clan may approve a challenge only if Zheng proves his strength and obtains the **Spear of Judgment**, missing for over two thousand years since an Egyptian pharaoh killed an invading African chieftain with it.
- More than 3,000 laborers, over 1,000 camels, tools, and limited machinery depart for Hamunaptra after one week of organization. Zheng supplies another three cubic meters of gold for continuous desert logistics and imposes a strict three-month excavation deadline.
- Guardian reports place the Spear of Judgment recently in several small African nations before it was stolen. Zheng forms an unspecified inference from “Africa” and “stolen”; preserve it until revealed.
- Zheng concludes Lord God abilities are prototypes that must be adapted. Increasing vampire-energy output darkens Red Flame; he infers the clone's black fire is ordinary Red Flame driven at vastly greater intensity. Mixing internal and vampire energy produces threads of apparently stronger golden flame inside Zheng's red fire.
- Channeling corrosive internal energy through weapons works only with the Zaku Heat Hawk; other weapons and iron tools corrode or shatter, and energy dissipates rapidly into the air. The technique is powerful but prohibitively expensive in energy.
- Jonathan unknowingly brings the **Spear of Judgment** from Africa as a compact golden staff. Twisting its handle extends it into a throwing spear. Its power scales with the user's energy and requires internal, vampire, magical, psychokinetic, cultivation, immortal-spiritual, or another compatible energy.
- Evelyn is less than two months pregnant. O'Connell refuses to let her join the Scorpion King expedition but volunteers himself after learning that Zheng's companions died and that the Book of Amun-Ra is needed to resurrect them; Evelyn privately appears to plan around his refusal.
- With three passengers in a suspended basket, the captured Green Goblin glider drains energy at twice its ordinary rate and retains roughly thirty hours of flight. Replacement solid-state energy cells cost 300 points each; Zheng considers the glider the best transport short of a double-B flying magical artifact or A-rank flying sword.
- The guardian elders condition Scorpion King information on possession of the Spear of Judgment plus Zheng defeating ten armed elite warriors barehanded. Jonathan stole the missing spear during the group's African honeymoon, resolving Zheng's “Africa/stolen” inference.
- When Zheng entwines internal and vampire energy inside the Spear of Judgment, it emits golden light, travels roughly 1,500 meters, and silently creates a crater 7–8 meters wide and 2–3 meters deep. A strong ordinary guardian throws it only several dozen meters with no effect, supporting that plot items reveal special power only to compatible reincarnation-team users.
- The guardian prophecy says a warrior bearing the Spear of Judgment will kill the Scorpion King and end his threat. Once repeated throws prove only Zheng can activate it, the elders waive the ten-warrior trial, return the spear, and send four tomb guides; the bearded guardian joins as a comrade.
- The ten-person bracelet expedition consists of Zheng, O'Connell, Evelyn, Jonathan, the bearded guardian, four guardian guides, and airship pilot **Izzy** (who remains outside after landing). Evelyn joins despite being pregnant; O'Connell's objections fail.
- Entering the hidden bracelet tomb before its scheduled plot raises difficulty to maximum and makes completion rewards double. Immediate mission: obtain the bracelet within ten minutes for 4,000 points and two C ranks; failure collapses the tomb and deducts 5,000 points.
- The bracelet tomb contains nearly three-meter black jackal-headed **Anubis warriors**, faster and stronger than ordinary mummy guards and armed with giant metal blades. Anti-spirit ammunition and the Zaku Heat Hawk reduce them to ash; Zheng's unlimited-ammunition 25 mm autocannon clears groups of roughly ten.
- The four guardian guides disappear after taking a side passage during the ten-minute rush. Evelyn's past-life intuition locates a concealed chamber with four minutes remaining, but it contains seven or eight scorpions more than three meters long.
- Zheng uses two ultra-high-explosive bombs: the first destroys the giant scorpions, and the second collapses the pursuit corridor, trapping the expedition inside. Combined internal/vampire energy lets the Zaku Heat Hawk corrode and hack through a solid granite barrier in more than ten blows, at extreme energy cost.
- The acquired plot item is locked as the **Bracelet of Anubis** (`死神手镯`; earlier “Scorpion King's bracelet”). After the Scorpion Emperor is killed, it can summon fifty times as many Anubis warriors as the holder can summon mummy guards. Failure to kill him within sixty days erases the holder. Acquisition awards 4,000 points and two C ranks.
- Removing the Bracelet of Anubis opens a gate to the Nile and floods the chamber, as warned by its inscription. The ten-minute acquisition mission is complete, but the flood escape remains unresolved.
- The opened floodgate also reveals an escape passage. Zheng kills another giant scorpion with gold-threaded Red Flame after its sting paralyzes and grotesquely swells his left arm; antivenom plus drainage of black poisoned blood saves him. He spends more combined energy on a second Spear of Judgment throw to breach the wall into the tomb's entrance hall, allowing the flood to carry everyone out.
- Zheng believes advancing the plot of *The Mummy Returns* will raise the Scorpion King hunt to maximum difficulty. Despite his attempt to go alone, the bearded guardian, Jonathan, and O'Connell insist on joining him; Evelyn promises only to observe from the airship. The bracelet's sixty-day deadline leaves Zheng no option to abandon the challenge.
- A C-rank Lord God support item called the **arcanite arrow retriever** (`奥金箭矢回收器`) may solve the Spear of Judgment's need for manual retrieval; retain this provisional rendering unless later mechanics establish a different referent.
- Zheng can currently summon at most six mummy guards. Recasting replaces the existing six rather than accumulating more. Once activated by killing the Scorpion King, the Bracelet of Anubis would therefore let him summon 300 stronger Anubis warriors at once.
- Both plot items bear mysterious symbols that conduct internal and vampire energy like luminous streams. Zheng hypothesizes that ancient evolved humans manufactured such items through a nontechnological inscription system, making mass production theoretically possible, but he cannot read or reproduce the markings and needs Qi Tengyi's knowledge plus Chu Xuan's analysis and manufacturing expertise.
- Fastening the Bracelet of Anubis to his right wrist activates the advance plot of *The Mummy Returns*: kill the Scorpion King and obtain his army of Anubis for 10,000 points and two B ranks, at maximum difficulty with double rewards. The holder is erased on failure; the announced time remaining is forty-seven days. Preserve the source's loose timing against the nearby statement that roughly one month of a ninety-day return has passed.
- The bracelet guides its wearer through projected images of Egyptian temples. At the Temple of Osiris, a seven- or eight-meter stone statue animates and targets Zheng; its punches shatter granite, but it is slow enough for him to lure into quicksand and cross safely with his lightness technique. The next destination is Abu Simbel, which Evelyn says has three roughly eight-meter seated statues.
- Lock Book of the Dead spell references introduced here: **sandstorm**, **quicksand**, mummy-guard summoning, return of the dead, and resurrection of the last undead creature killed by the Book of Amun-Ra. Evelyn recites the resurrection spell while searching, but its immediate significance remains unnoticed because she soon finds the quicksand spell.
- A clandestine faction infiltrates the Hamunaptra excavation, removes Imhotep's remains while the guardians focus on upper-level gold, and plans to steal the Book of the Dead to resurrect its “master.” A desiccated hand breaks from the camel-borne sack at the end of part 231, implying that Evelyn's recitation has already begun his return; preserve this as implication until confirmed.
- At Abu Simbel, Zheng creates several traps more than ten meters deep and over twenty meters wide by detonating ultra-high-explosive bombs in spear-bored shafts. The three statues are nearly ten meters tall, about thirty percent faster than the earlier Osiris statue, and can jump from the pits. O'Connell's group uses TNT to keep them down for roughly three seconds while Zheng's quicksand spell consumes vampire energy and buries them.
- The bracelet's final projection shows a water-filled canyon leading through forest to a golden pyramid, establishing the route to the Scorpion King's treasure. Before the party can return to the airship and rest for the night, a sandstorm approaches from the horizon.
- The sandstorm is Imhotep's magic and takes the form of his enormous head. Izzy's airship has a brief flame-propelled boost, but not unlimited fuel. Flying into the river canyon causes the sand form to shrink and dissolve into the water; the damaged airship then crashes into the forest canopy at the **Ahm Shere oasis** (`阿姆谢绿洲`).
- Imhotep is now openly resurrected and accompanied by a beautiful unnamed woman, the old black-robed priest, a powerful Black man, and mounted gunmen. Zheng states that Imhotep is presently unkillable without the Book of Amun-Ra but will lose his magic inside the Scorpion King's pyramid. Izzy remains behind to repair the torn gas-envelope airship while the expedition enters the jungle.
- Zheng theorizes that the Lord God calibrates mission difficulty from four measurable categories: attribute points, enhancements, exchanged abilities, and exchanged weapons. He infers that a self-created hidden ability may escape this calibration. This remains his stage-three simulation of Chu Xuan, not confirmed system fact.
- Lock Zheng's self-created technique as **Explosion** (`爆炸`). He releases all internal and vampire energy simultaneously and drives them into violent conflict through his body. With genetic-lock stage-three reactions, this produces at least tenfold strength and speed for ten seconds. Afterward he can barely move for thirty minutes; capillaries and some larger vessels rupture throughout his body. Hemostatic spray and vampire regeneration close the wounds within about ten minutes, making the technique survivable for him but likely fatal to an ordinary body.
- The jungle guardians are small ape-like creatures using rifles and crew-served machine guns. One ultra-high-explosive bomb kills 264 and awards exactly 264 points. The Lord God has also enlarged the film's modest jungle into a vast primeval forest.
- The giant diamond atop the golden pyramid focuses sunlight into a long-range beam. It pierces Zheng's left lung while he surveys from roughly twenty meters up; glider-balancing movement saves his heart, and his vampire-count constitution begins regenerating the wound once pooled blood is expelled. Jonathan discovers a large canyon that Zheng plans to use with his explosives against massed guardians.
- Under Explosion, Zheng counts down ten seconds while covering more than 200 meters, scaling the canyon wall, rescuing four hostages, killing or maiming their captors, bisecting Imhotep with the Heat Hawk, and escaping on the glider while the autocannon tears apart Imhotep's regenerating body. Overuse with a preexisting chest wound ruptures major vessels, partially dislocates bones, reopens the lung wound, and nearly kills him.
- Izzy lashes the Green Goblin glider beneath the damaged airship, allowing the remaining lifting gas plus the glider's thrust to carry everyone back to Hamunaptra by dusk. After medical care and more than thirty hours of sleep, vampire regeneration restores Zheng to ordinary mobility.
- Correct the altar resurrection mechanics: with the actual corpse present, resurrection costs one B rank plus 7,000 points. With no corpse, or a body manufactured by the Lord God, it costs one B rank plus 8,000 points and the altar replicates the body in its pre-death condition. This supersedes Zheng's earlier untested 500-point duplicate-body theory.
- Chu Xuan is resurrected without a true corpse for 8,000 points and one B rank. The resurrection vision reveals that pain, taste, touch, and smell were genetically removed from him; he can only perceive food visually. Zheng secures his cooperation by explaining that genetic-lock stage four may let him rewrite those genes and regain ordinary sensation.
- Lock the five-stage explanation stated here: stage one releases ancient combat instinct; stage two controls more than ninety percent of bodily functions; stage three develops additional brain regions and enables simulation; stage four alters and ultimately reorganizes genetic chains; stage five controls energy and may underlie internal arts, cultivation, vampire energy, and magic. Treat the ancient-human origins as the characters' theory.
- Chu Xuan infers that Team China's exceptional survival against Team India, *Final Destination 2*, and altered *Alien Resurrection* repeatedly raised its Lord God evaluation, leading to the Devil Team matchup; defeat reset that evaluation to zero, so he expects the next film to have ordinary team difficulty. He accepts Zheng's requirement that all former Team China members eventually be resurrected, subject to capability standards.
- Chu Xuan's immediate Scorpion King plan is to avoid spending Zheng's combat strength on the jungle guardians and import incendiary bombs to burn the dry desert-valley forest within about three days. He predicts indirect fire deaths will award no points.
- Chu Xuan states the Lord God creates persistent worlds rather than mere assignments and summarizes its economy as **threat equals reward**. His model predicts repeated easy kills raise the team/individual evaluation until enemies receive increasingly modern weapons. Self-created abilities are excluded from difficulty calibration, while intelligence and successful shortcuts count as evolution without reducing the separate Scorpion King reward. Preserve these as Chu Xuan's high-confidence deductions, not confirmed system rules.
- One hundred incendiary bombs are imported from Europe. A limited probe confirms the guardians now wield anti-materiel rifles capable of penetrating tank armor; the first shot shatters Zheng's last cracked protective jade pendant and knocks him several meters, forcing an immediate Explosion retreat.
- Chu Xuan maps the forest as an unequal-sided triangle and uses four simultaneous ignition zones, each dispersing incendiaries over roughly one hundred square meters. His survival estimate rises from 70% to 85% once the stated deadline leaves time for three-to-four-day burning and cooling phases. Preserve the source's arithmetic claim that fifteen days minus the eight-day wait leaves eight days.
- The fire produces no reward notifications. After eight days, the route contains at least tens of thousands of burned guardians and weapons ranging from pistols to sniper rifles and rocket-launcher remnants. The party reaches the golden pyramid safely.
- Entering the pyramid seals Zheng inside behind a transparent barrier. The Scorpion King revives; the challenger loses all enhanced attributes and exchanged abilities, while the army of Anubis activates and must kill every living creature within fifty kilometers until the king dies.
- Zheng retains the genetic-lock stages, a small quantity of energy cultivated beyond his exchanged reserves, and therefore access to Explosion. He loses his enhanced physique, vampire bloodline power, lightness technique, and normal high-output plot-item use. Drawing the Heat Hawk from the Na ring alone consumes about one fifth of his remaining internal energy.
- Outside, Chu Xuan collapses the narrow entrance corridor with O'Connell's dynamite, buying an estimated six to seven minutes before the Anubis warriors cut through and eight to nine minutes of total survival with remaining ammunition. Inside, the similarly depowered Imhotep and **Anck-su-namun** (`安苏娜`) confront Zheng as ordinary human combatants before a door opens on two enormous green eyes.
- The Scorpion King is a nearly six-meter half-man, half-scorpion who regenerates even after the rotary autocannon tears him apart. Stage-two muscle control lets depowered Zheng fire two short bursts but splits his hands and leaves his arm muscles trembling. Giant two-to-three-meter scorpions also emerge from the king's chamber.
- Imhotep expends his last remnant of sand magic to save Zheng and states the king is immortal through Anubis's blessing and curse; only the sun-god-gifted Spear of Judgment can kill him. Imhotep bargains never to harm Zheng or his friends and to leave for America with Anck-su-namun if spared. Zheng agrees.
- The Scorpion King catches Zheng's ordinary spear throw between his pincers. Zheng then uses the tiny retained energies to activate Explosion and physically drives the held Spear of Judgment into the king's chest. The result remains unresolved until part 243.
- The spear's golden light disintegrates the Scorpion King completely. Completion awards 10,000 points and two B ranks and unseals the Bracelet of Anubis; the pyramid begins collapsing after thirty seconds. Imhotep's restored sandstorm carries Anck-su-namun and Zheng out, honoring the agreement.
- The pyramid and oasis return to desert as Anubis's power fades. Jonathan retrieves the flawless summit diamond, about one quarter smaller than a football, during the glider-and-rope evacuation. The guardian accepts Imhotep's foreign exile as release from the clan's burden rather than a betrayal of duty.
- A full Bracelet of Anubis summon produces exactly 300 warriors and drains nearly all of Zheng's vampire energy even at vampire-count rank. Warriors persist indefinitely unless killed or dismissed. The cost blocks Explosion and energy-powered Spear of Judgment use but does not consume stamina or internal energy, leaving ordinary close combat available.
- Chu Xuan wants to reverse-engineer the Green Goblin glider so every teammate can have one, and proposes later instrumented testing of Explosion after Zheng extends it from ten seconds to at least thirty. His first attempt at leisure is a fishing rod wired to the glider's power supply for a 100,000-volt bite shock; Zheng forbids it as contrary to the point of fishing.
- After returning to Cairo, O'Connell, Evelyn, and Jonathan share one cubic meter of gold; the bearded guardian receives the remainder and promises permanent protection of Hamunaptra's resurrection altar. The film-world party separates on friendly terms.
- On his first ordinary Nile fishing trip, Chu Xuan repeatedly catches more and larger fish than Zheng, including an enormous Nile perch. He says the uncertainty of whether or what he will catch makes him want to continue and judges fishing “acceptable.” Treat this as a meaningful early sign of interest, not proof that his missing emotions or senses have returned.
- Resurrection in the Lord God's Dimension excludes rewards earned during the scenario in which the person died. Chu Xuan therefore predicts Zero will be comparatively cheap later. He delays both Zero and Zhan Lan: current points must equip the free-to-resurrect first-scenario casualties Bawang and Wang Xia, while psychically gifted but combat-weak Zhan Lan should not be restored until the team can reliably protect her from an irreversible second death.
- Zheng requires Chu Xuan to treat members as companions rather than pieces or tools; Chu Xuan agrees to try to account for companion bonds in his plans. Their development doctrine balances personal enhancements with technology in the early and middle stages.
- Lock Chu Xuan's mid-stage weapon goal as two Gauss pistols plus **Gun Kata** (`枪斗术`), a B-rank theoretical skill claimed to cover nearby blind angles through shooting postures and increase bullet power by twenty percent.
- **Bawang** and **Wang Xia** are resurrected free because neither completed a horror film before dying. Bawang is a displaced post-Soviet special-forces mercenary whose company avenged his murdered partner; Wang Xia is a Chinese lieutenant and explosives specialist who entered under a state research program to acquire horror-world technology for his country. Both have exhausted their single resurrection.
- Bawang receives an unlimited-ammunition **tubular pulse accelerator** (`管状脉冲弹射枪`) for one C rank plus 2,000 points. It vents gas downward to reduce recoil and fires 70,000 needle-sized physical rounds per second with a deep-blue pulse glow; less than one second penetrates a ten-meter alloy-steel wall and destroys the target behind it. A standard magazine lasts only three seconds.
- Wang Xia receives fifty ultra-high-explosive mines, ten timed ultra-high-explosive plastic charges, and three **high-yield gravity mines** (`重力高威地雷`), plus 100 points each in muscle density and neural reaction speed through Zheng's once-per-scenario assisted enhancement. Chu Xuan receives two unlimited-ammunition Gauss pistols, each costing one D rank plus 1,500 points.
- A gravity mine can be keyed to triggering weight and survives a point-blank timed ultra-high-explosive blast. Once triggered at a one-kilogram threshold, it makes bodies heavy, exerts a lethal inward pull, and rapidly crumples the surrounding alloy floor. Zheng requires Explosion to escape; the final effect is revealed in part 249.
- The tested gravity mine compresses all alloy across roughly 200 square meters into a suspended, irregular metal sphere and is considered universally lethal within range; its pull may affect targets beyond 200 meters. The S-rank, 40,000-point scaled version is the **intercontinental gravitational annihilation bomb** (`洲际重力毁灭弹`), whose exact yield remains untested.
- Chu Xuan proposes pushing Explosion past the sound barrier and adapting concepts from *One Piece*'s CP9 **Six Powers**: Moonwalk for air steps, Shave for burst movement, vacuum attacks comparable to Finger Pistol, and a concentrated fist cannon. Zheng's first compressed trial steps through the air and produces a vacuum kick that cuts a gash more than two meters deep into the alloy floor, but empties both energies and ruptures almost all minor vessels and skin.
- Lock Explosion's extreme compressed evolution as **Destruction** (`毁灭`). It lasts only three to four seconds on current reserves, violently ruptures Zheng's body, and consumes both energies completely, but breaks the sound barrier and magnifies his Explosion-state combat effectiveness by at least another tenfold in Chu Xuan's estimate.
- Destruction-derived techniques: **Moonwalk** (`月步`) steps on air and changes direction aloft; **Shave** (`剃`) accelerates beyond naked sight; **Tempest Kick** (`岚脚`) launches a vacuum blade; **Fist Gun** (`拳枪`) concentrates close-range striking force; **Swift Axe** (`迅斧`) hurls the Zaku Heat Hawk with a localized impact explosion.
- Target long-term energy upgrades: A-rank mutated vampire-marquis bloodline, followed by double-A vampire prince and S-rank vampire emperor. A-rank internal energy branches into named arts rather than one linear upgrade. Chu Xuan notes resurrection costs can be pooled across members, explaining the strategy of restoring more free or cheap contributors first.
- The Green Goblin glider's power module uses a legendary-magical structure resembling ancient runes; even with plans, Chu Xuan cannot manufacture it. The component exchange costs one D rank plus more than 1,000 points, so the team enters *The Lost World: Jurassic Park* with only Zheng's glider.
- *The Lost World* begins with seven participants in a clean city warehouse rather than on Isla Sorna. Main mission: travel to Isla Sorna, obtain three Tyrannosaurus rex eggs, and return them safely to the same warehouse; no completion reward is stated. Kill values: Stegosaurus 5 points, Velociraptor 5, Brontosaurus 10, Tyrannosaurus rex 30.
- Chu Xuan infers a possible twenty-member team cap from the twenty room doors and fears exceeding it through resurrection might erase a member or the resurrector. He proposes combining rapid side-plot-funded resurrection with killing useless members; Zheng categorically rejects killing innocents merely because their existence obstructs the plan. Preserve the cap and penalty as unconfirmed inference.
- Newcomers: **Luo Gandao** (`罗甘道`), a sixteen- or seventeen-year-old final-year high-school student claiming exceptional simulated driving talent; **Wang Ai'ai** (`王艾艾`), a twenty-four- or twenty-five-year-old corporate-finance professional skilled at money management; and an unnamed slightly chubby domestic helper who can cook. All three entered after an online YES/NO prompt.
- Chu Xuan gives a seventy-percent estimate that Luo Gandao's driving intuition could eventually make him a fighter comparable to Zheng through a technological pilot path: C-rank **miniature combat robot** (`战斗微缩机器人`), a 1.8–1.9-meter upright chibi-bear unit; B-rank **Macross three-form variable robot** (`超时空要塞三形态机器人`); A-rank **prototype Gundam** (`高达原型机`). These require finite energy and separate weapons and leave a weak-minded pilot vulnerable to psychic or nonphysical attacks.
- In the coastal city, the party deals with the east-side **Anthony family** under an international-mercenary cover, offering fifteen gold bars at seventy percent of market value. Zheng's corrosive internal energy leaves finger impressions and a torn edge in a bar, convincing the young blond family boss that he possesses authentic Chinese kung fu.
- The harbor contains small naval destroyers, and Isla Sorna is under navy blockade. Reports say not only dinosaurs but also the island's plants are reverting toward prehistoric forms and its insects are becoming gigantic, contradicting the initial assumption of an ordinary low-difficulty retrieval mission.
- Online and naval records place the story after *The Lost World*: a lone mad scientist entered Isla Sorna for unknown experiments, followed by a sharp dinosaur population increase, prehistoric reversion of vegetation, giant insects, and aquatic dinosaurs that forced military intervention. Finding the scientist alive or dead, or discovering the cause, is the first inferred side plot.
- The island from the first *Jurassic Park* has no public reports but is guarded by a carrier battle group while the government secretly assembles researchers, implying a second, potentially richer side plot. The main mission itself has an easy route: naval forces already recovered exactly three T. rex eggs for museum delivery; their transport sails the next day and reaches the city museum the day after.
- The blond Anthony-family heir becomes Zheng's self-declared kung-fu disciple. In exchange for Zheng explaining the internal-energy circulation route and briefly guiding energy through his meridians, he arranges museum-expert credentials and passage on the egg transport ship.
- Zheng teaches the Anthony heir the circulation route and guides a trace of internal energy through one complete circuit; this is a practical bargain, not evidence that the youth can generate internal energy himself.
- Chu Xuan's naval strike remains conditional: if the marines force a battle, Team China will maximize damage and sink at least a destroyer to draw the carrier group away from the first island. A precisely positioned high-yield gravity mine can disable a large warship, while the Green Goblin glider can evade radar by flying low over the sea at night and benefits from radar-absorbent paint.
- The Isla Sorna field team is Zheng Zha, Chu Xuan, Bawang, and Wang Xia. Luo Gandao, Wang Ai'ai, and the unnamed domestic helper remain in the Anthony building with a communicator. Their transport sails shortly after 2 p.m. and arrives around 8 p.m.; several cruisers, several destroyers, smaller escorts, and researcher-soldier boats form the blockade.
- A fortified beachhead on Isla Sorna is protected by a pesticide-treated wire perimeter; taking it required prolonged naval bombardment and still cost dozens of soldiers. Even the navy avoids the interior.
- Giant insects began growing and multiplying months earlier, but have died en masse since the current month. Chu Xuan infers that a reversion-inducing substance created conditions favorable to prehistoric reptiles and giant ferns, while modern atmospheric oxygen cannot sustain insects beyond a certain size. A four-to-five-meter **Dunkleosteus** (`恐鱼`) near the island provides further evidence of broad prehistoric reversion.
- After ten at night, Zheng's four-person field team knocks out two sentries and leaves the fort undetected. Zheng carries the rotary autocannon and Spear of Judgment, Wang Xia a submachine gun plus mines/explosives, Bawang the tubular pulse accelerator, and Chu Xuan his two concealed unlimited-ammunition Gauss pistols; the Green Goblin glider tows the other three by reinforced ropes.
- Isla Sorna has bowl-shaped terrain: dense outer jungle, a middle grassland, and low marsh around a long-abandoned research institute at the center. The team lands after one kilometer to conserve glider energy and avoid pterosaurs, aircraft, missiles, and premature naval detection; they expect to reach the institute around daybreak.
- At least some giant insects survive the die-off: Zheng finds an arm-length American cockroach still moving after impalement, and Chu Xuan identifies cockroaches as unusually tolerant of low oxygen. Red Flame burns it to ash, but it yields no reward points.
- On the plain, several seven-meter Stegosauruses sleep near the treeline. More than a dozen feathered, nearly two-meter velociraptors chase a wounded, medium-sized Triceratops toward the team. Zheng's energy-charged Spear of Judgment pierces the Triceratops's skull from about one hundred meters, while Chu Xuan enters the genetic-lock state and engages the velociraptors with both Gauss pistols and his eyes closed.
- Chu Xuan's first Gauss-pistol demonstration is only an experiment in **Gun Kata** principles, not the exchanged skill: he fires through a fixed, repeating spatial pattern with his eyes closed, kills seven or eight velociraptors, then opens his eyes to finish the rest. The silent pistols shatter heads and leave bowl-wide torso wounds.
- Chu Xuan raises the possibility of taming and transporting film creatures between worlds, naming *Starship Troopers* Arachnids, *The Lord of the Rings* dragon, giant eagles, and colossal elephant as examples; both entry into the Lord God's Dimension and fitting through later transfer beams remain unresolved.
- Zheng's fully depleted vampire energy currently recovers in about five to six hours. He summons the full 300-strong Army of Anubis in six units of fifty to hunt herbivores and small dinosaurs while the team advances; the army cannot die while its warriors' heads remain intact.
- The easy dinosaur hunt could yield each member at least 3,000 points without side stories. Precisely because the seven-person main-mission difficulty is so low, Chu Xuan suspects both optional objectives may be team-wipe threats and identifies surviving prehistoric insects as the likely danger behind the mad-scientist side plot.
- Dinosaur kills by the Army of Anubis have earned Zheng at least 1,000 unannounced points before reaching the island center. A nearly ten-meter-tall T. rex destroys over half a fifty-warrior unit before Zheng's charged Spear of Judgment erases its head; the spear's output is compared to a small missile and scales with supplied internal and vampire energy, but must be physically retrieved after throwing.
- Multiple T. rexes and increasingly common large carnivores appear near the center. The team takes flight; Chu Xuan's pistols silently kill an eleven-to-twelve-meter-wingspan pterosaur, while Bawang's tubular pulse accelerator destroys another from seven to eight kilometers away and damages a second, confirming extreme range and through-penetration.
- Near the abandoned institute, dinosaurs dwindle, bones increase, grass gives way to mud, low shrubs, mushrooms, and fungi, and giant insects become numerous. Once the institute is in sight, Chu Xuan permits unrestricted gunfire despite inevitable naval detection.
- Isla Sorna's first explicit side plot has a one-hour limit: retrieve the institute's research notes and kill the queen bee. Success gives each participant two C-rank side stories and 4,000 points; failure to remove the notes within an hour deducts 4,000 points each, while failure to kill the queen deducts two C-rank side stories.
- The institute has become a giant hive coated in mud mixed with dinosaur flesh. Prehistoric bees larger than a palm ignore targets beyond fifty meters but can dismantle a multi-ton Stegosaurus in a swarm numbering tens or hundreds of thousands.
- Chu Xuan's plan uses dried shrubs and fungi as improvised smoke charges to displace rather than kill the bees. Zheng must carry Chu Xuan inside within one minute without Explosion; Chu needs 1.5–2 minutes to identify the notes, loses clear thought after 2.5 minutes without air, and can hold his breath for at most three minutes. Zheng estimates his own limit near five minutes with internal energy and must kill the queen during the search.
- The team expects the surviving bees to pursue them after the queen's death, enabling a combined glider escape, naval strike, and transfer toward the first-film island. By the time the synchronized smoke charges and explosives are prepared, fewer than ten minutes remain.
- Wang Xia converts timed explosives into remotely triggered igniters for the smoke charges. Zheng carries Chu Xuan through the hive while Chu's patterned Gauss fire keeps hundreds of bees away; they reach the central hall in roughly twenty seconds after entry.
- The institute interior is coated in blood plasma and dark-red hexagonal combs holding ostrich-egg-sized eggs and giant grubs. A buried, corroded safe in a ruined archive contains hundreds of documents; Chu searches them while Zheng hunts the queen.
- Giant male drones are nearly half a human body in size, unaffected by smoke, faster than Zheng in the first-stage lock, and possess at least ten times ordinary human strength. Zheng enters the second-stage genetic lock and can then evade and bisect them with the Zaku Heat Hawk. Chu Xuan states that the queen is the hive's strongest combatant, guarded by a dozen or more such drones.
- Drones can tear off and launch their own venom-sac-equipped abdomens; one stinger numbs Zheng's left thigh. The queen is larger than Zheng, faster than his second-stage lock, intelligent enough to retreat from Red Flame, and can pierce several centimeters of Gundamium Alloy with its stinger.
- Zheng activates Explosion for three seconds, traps and bends the queen's stinger against the Zaku Heat Hawk, breaks the queen in half with two punches, and crushes its upper body and the alloy floor beneath it. Chu Xuan finds the needed file only after uncovering two additional safes and approaches suffocation before Zheng carries him out while transferring internal energy.
- Completing the side plot gives every participant on the island 4,000 points and two C-rank side stories, plus the plot item **Biological Gene Remapping Schematics** (`生物重拟基因图文件档`).
- Wang Xia drains black blood from Zheng's shoulder and thigh and administers two strong antivenom injections. Chu Xuan uses the first-stage genetic lock to pilot the Green Goblin glider at maximum speed, with control comparable to Zheng's.
- Bawang's tubular pulse accelerator fires needle-shaped, pulse-wave-accelerated rounds with less recoil and more power than analogous Gauss weapons. Wang Xia deploys an aerial charge containing the explosive from three timed ultra-high-explosive bombs; its blast nearly clears the closest pursuing bees.
- The Biological Gene Remapping Schematics describe a formula for an atavism-inducing genetic drug, methods to reconstruct prehistoric gene fragments, and methods to remove those fragments; they do not enable free personal gene recombination.
- The main mission has no stated deadline because prehistoric insects are still adapting to modern oxygen. The giant bees have already adapted; once other species adapt and multiply, they may destroy human civilization. Chu Xuan infers a third side plot: give the schematics to human governments and change that future.
- Zheng and Chu Xuan are framed as Team China's complementary core: Zheng is its unifying leader and martial spear, while Chu Xuan is its analytical shield. Zheng commits to all three side plots despite their escalating danger.
- The Green Goblin glider is waterproof because its power source uses a magical energy system. Satellite radar eventually fixes its position despite the radar-absorbent coating; Chu Xuan wants to integrate counter-detection and electronic jamming later.
- The navy launches three thermobaric cruise missiles, described in-world as high-density incendiaries capable of consuming the air and igniting a ten-kilometer area. Zheng enters the third-stage genetic lock and emulates Zero's vision and sniper intuition to locate them; Bawang intercepts all three at roughly ten-kilometer range before Chu Xuan dives the glider into the sea.
- A gigantic starfish-like marine creature with a circular, densely toothed mouth ambushes the glider near the surface. Zheng's danger sense prompts an emergency climb before it can bite the team.
- The first attacked vessel is identified as a battlecruiser. Zheng's ultra-high-explosive bomb destroys its main gun; Bawang's accelerator tears through the deck and detonates raised missiles, leaving the port side destroyed and the vessel listing. Its loss awards each team member 1,000 points.
- Sustained fire makes the tubular pulse accelerator's muzzle glow white. Its original magazine changes provided cooling intervals, which the unlimited-ammunition modification removes; Chu Xuan orders Bawang not to fire for thirty minutes to prevent fatal metal fatigue.
- Chu Xuan immediately attacks a second battlecruiser to make the naval losses unmistakable. Its helicopters, antiaircraft barrage, surface-to-air missiles, and air-to-air missiles converge while unlocked Chu pilots evasively and Zheng calculates each missile trajectory with the rotary autocannon.
- Zheng's rotary autocannon destroys the incoming missiles but cannot penetrate the second battlecruiser's deck. Wang Xia's timed explosive and two mines devastate only the surface, so a five-kilogram-trigger high-yield gravity mine is activated by an unarmed ultra-high-explosive bomb dropped onto it. Most of the ship and a mass of seawater collapse into an exploding sphere that sinks.
- The giant bees overwhelm the surrounding fleet, reducing sailors and dinosaurs alike to skeletons. Chu Xuan did not predict total fleet destruction, but says he would deliberately cause it if forewarned because it both draws the carrier group and proves the insect threat to governments for the third side plot.
- Zheng asks to minimize unnecessary deaths. Chu Xuan argues this becomes possible only when Zheng or Team China is powerful enough to achieve objectives without sacrificial stratagems, citing clone Zheng as the benchmark; Zheng resolves to pursue that strength.
- The tubular pulse accelerator is estimated as technology roughly 150 years beyond the real world, one tier above Gauss weapons and comparable to energy-laser arms. Eliminating its cooling limitation would raise its exchange value to at least B rank. More than ten jets arrive over the possibly annihilated Isla Sorna fleet.
- The first-film island is stated to lie six hundred nautical miles away. Four adult men reduce the Green Goblin glider's maximum speed by ten to twenty percent, but the trip is still expected to take only several dozen minutes.
- Causing prehistoric insects to escape Isla Sorna early imposes a 4,000-point penalty on every Team China member. Combined with 4,000 points and two C ranks from the hive plus 2,000 points from two sunk ships, each veteran's known net is two C ranks and 2,000 points before dinosaur kills; the newcomers' negative totals may erase them on return.
- Chu Xuan's three provisional side-plot rules: larger downstream story changes cause larger positive or negative rewards; greater danger gives greater rewards; and more inherently necessary collaborators allow more reward recipients, while adding unnecessary participants does not.
- The third side plot likely rewards only one participant. Taking all newcomers to the first-film island might expand the second plot's recipient count, but would reduce current glider speed to sixty or seventy percent and compromise maneuverability. Zheng prioritizes the irreplaceable veterans, keeps the four-person team, and assigns Luo Gandao to deliver the schematics for the third plot.
- Chu Xuan's provisional difficulty scale: D is easy, double-D somewhat harder, C difficult, double-C very difficult but safely solvable with the right method, B carries an estimated eighty-percent participant-wipe risk, double-B at least ninety percent, and A or above may threaten the whole team. Team China's reliable present ceiling is double-C.
- The mad scientist sought externally controlled human evolution. His substance makes organisms revert to ancient forms stored in their gene chains; in some subjects, spontaneous fusion and recombination instead created a novel life-form whose juvenile he released on the first-film island, describing only “dragon” as adequate for its appearance.
- The carrier battle group sails toward Isla Sorna. The four land on the first-film island at dawn and cool the homemade glider for forty to ninety minutes. Zheng scouts alone; Bawang guards the beach, and Wang Xia lays a two-hundred-meter mine perimeter with thresholds above three hundred kilograms.
- Zheng abandoned the Zaku Heat Hawk in the hive because the queen's stinger remained hooked through it. The first island shows no genetic contamination: insects remain ordinary, but no dinosaurs, remains, or other large life are visible, making the quiet resemble the edge of an apex predator's emptied lair.
- Zheng finds a fresh giant shoulder blade pierced by tooth tips thicker than his arm, then sees a genuine thirty-meter Western dragon with horns, scales, wings, and a low lizard-like body bite off most of a T. rex's head and tear its front half away.
- The second explicit side plot requires killing the **dinosaur mutant** within twenty-four hours. The killer earns one B rank and 5,000 points; assisting team members earn one C rank and 4,000 points each. The creature is classified as legendary magical and immune to high-tech ranged weapons; every team member who participates in attacking it is erased on failure.
- The Spear of Judgment is not high-tech, and Chu Xuan thinks a gravity mine may qualify as close-range explosive technology. He plans to test the glider's miniature air-to-surface missile; if missiles work, the dragon can be lured to the carrier group, otherwise Zheng must fight with the spear.
- Zheng tries to undertake the activated mission alone and offers the Book of Amun-Ra and Book of the Dead so the others can resurrect him. Bawang, Wang Xia, and Chu Xuan refuse: uncertain reward pooling at Hamunaptra could leave them unable to revive him, so all four choose to share the B-rank risk.
- At closer observation the black Western dragon measures roughly thirty-seven to thirty-eight meters long and nearly twenty meters high, moves without Brontosaurus-like sluggishness, and is assumed able to fly and use a lethal elemental breath. Chu Xuan assigns a ten-percent chance of spellcasting, which he judges unsurvivable.
- Bawang's Dungeons & Dragons model says an ancient dragon of this scale could destroy a small city with magic and oppose an army with its breath, while Zheng cites *Eragon* as a weaker flight/fire/melee model. Chu Xuan caps expectations by the B-rank mission difficulty but treats supernatural perception and technological immunity as unresolved.
- The intended attack order is a buried gravity mine first, then a miniature air-to-surface missile test. The dragon's broken-tree paths, bone piles, and branch-built sleeping nest are mapped; three timed explosives at five minutes, five minutes thirty seconds, and six minutes are arranged to draw it through a canyon, with the last gravity mine between the latter two charges.
- The canyon constrains the dragon within the gravity mine's radius, but Chu Xuan estimates only a thirty-percent chance it will step on the trap and judges this multi-recipient B-rank mission harder than Zheng's solo Scorpion King mission.
- Three miniature glider-launched air-to-surface missiles explode against the sleeping dragon's head without leaving even soot, confirming total immunity to remotely delivered high-tech attacks. The dragon runs at roughly 120–150 km/h.
- The dragon's fire breath instantly consumes a forest strip about ten meters wide and one hundred meters long without leaving the trees burning. The Green Goblin glider can accelerate from over 100 to over 300 km/h within one second to evade it.
- Timed explosives detonating at close range leave smoke marks but no injury, showing some interaction unlike the remote missiles. They enrage the dragon and draw it into the canyon to within ten meters of the buried gravity mine.
- The dragon detects the buried gravity mine by scent, stops with its foot directly above it, then turns and leaves despite the final lure charge. It flies initially at 300–400 km/h and targets Chu Xuan, Bawang, and Wang Xia on a mountainside.
- A Spear of Judgment throw charged with only twenty to thirty percent of Zheng's two energies pierces from the back of the dragon's neck through its mouth and interrupts its breath. The wound regenerates visibly; its hot black blood ignites trees. The spear is lost on the ground during the rescue.
- In sustained pursuit the dragon flies around 600 km/h, only slightly slower than the four-loaded glider. Wang Xia proposes detonating explosives inside the creature, where their full yield would act instead of less than one tenth from an external blast.
- More than a dozen fighter-launched air-to-air missiles leave the dragon unharmed. It follows the glider underwater and breathes fire after surfacing, boiling the sea in its path. The team races toward the carrier; Chu Xuan plans to exploit the dragon's greater momentum, while Zheng takes a modified five-second charge and prepares Destruction.
- High-speed naval footage previously revealed a translucent energy barrier forming just before bullets or missiles touch the dragon; its existence motivated the carrier blockade. The dragon is also implacably vindictive toward anything that attacks it.
- The glider clears the carrier's island by meters, while the dragon's momentum carries it into the superstructure, demolishing it and violently tilting the vessel. Zheng leaves the Book of the Dead and Book of Amun-Ra with the others and stays behind.
- Zheng can now sustain Destruction for four seconds, after training through repeated Lord God restoration. Each use leaves his body half destroyed and requires five to six days of vampire regeneration before combat recovery.
- During Destruction, Moonwalk carries Zheng through the air; Tempest Kick caves in the dragon's lower jaw and suppresses its breath; Shave plus Fist Gun opens an arm-wide temple wound. The energy barrier admits Zheng's body but rejects his technological bombs.
- Unable to place the five-second bombs through the barrier, Zheng shatters the lower jaw, uses Shave to penetrate the residual fire stream, and carries the explosives down the dragon's throat. The internal detonation bursts apart its entire body, leaving only the enormous head; Zheng is presumed killed.
- Bawang shoots down multiple jets while Wang Xia searches the exploded flesh and finds no trace of Zheng. The dragon head's fading barrier still blocks accelerator rounds; Chu Xuan orders Wang Xia to enter through its eye and recover a forehead **dragon crystal** (`龙晶`).
- Bathing in dragon blood at roughly 50–60°C gives Wang Xia, Chu Xuan, and Bawang +100 each to cellular vitality, neural reaction speed, muscle density, and immunity; absorption causes severe itching. Dragon flesh, bone, sinew, and blood are all presumed valuable magical materials.
- The dragon crystal accepts internal, vampire, mental, magical, psychokinetic, cultivation true essence, or immortal spiritual energy. When charged, it blocks ranged high-tech weapons; it senses energy fluctuations and grows hot when an energy fluctuation threatens the holder's life.
- After the dragon mission, each surviving veteran receives one C rank and 4,000 points, combining with the prior totals into one B rank and 6,000 points each. Hamunaptra resurrection costs one B rank and 8,000 points, plus one D rank to open *The Mummy* world and enough points for a safe ten-day trip; Chu Xuan needs at least 2,500 more points and one D rank.
- Newcomers who have never completed a scenario can be resurrected without points or side stories, but doing so consumes their one resurrection opportunity. Chu Xuan overrides Zheng's assignment to Luo Gandao and claims the third side plot to ensure Zheng can be restored before the next scenario.
- Chu Xuan values the rechargeable dragon crystal at A rank or above and proposes splitting it into at least twenty pieces for defensive rings, with Zheng supplying energy after resurrection. Because each human is far smaller than one twentieth of the dragon, each fragment may still create an exceptionally strong barrier. He also suspects the island may contain a dragon egg.
- On the carrier, severed dragon tissue remains alive: larger pieces writhe while smaller fragments attach and regrow. Scientists identify a powerful novel enzyme and name the tissue **X-cells**.
- X-cells grant intense regeneration, an exceptional energy-production system, freedom from most disease, and two-to-threefold ordinary physical capability. In every tested animal, their initial benefit eventually turns into a self-consuming reaction likened to cancer magnified one hundredfold unless the host has cells capable of opposing them.
- Zheng is found embedded in regenerative tissue attached to the dragon head. His lower body and right side are largely destroyed, with only brain and heart barely active, but dragon flesh is repairing or assimilating him. He is the only human subject to survive X-cell fusion.
- The previously unnamed domestic helper is revealed as **Wang Xue** (`汪雪`). Wang Ai'ai knows only basic English; both women spend their time shopping under Anthony-family protection, while Luo Gandao bonds with the blond heir over vehicles and wants to bring a motorcycle home.
- Chu Xuan conceals the newcomers' likely erasure and assigns three tasks: obtain a top computer and penetrate military networks; have Wang Xia locate and steal the three museum T. rex eggs; and have Bawang patrol the coast for migrating insects, which he can barely do on the glider after his four +100 attribute gains.
- By the second afternoon, four new queen bees lead four swarms toward different coastal areas, killing hundreds under a military news blackout. Officials still classify the event as an ecological disaster and reject thermobaric or nuclear strikes; Chu predicts geometric multiplication and a need to nuke half the United States within a month if a city becomes a hive.
- Chu intends to contact senior government only once the catastrophe makes the schematics indispensable. During dinner he reveals military reports that Zheng survived and is regenerating aboard the carrier, then abruptly loses consciousness.
- General **Lewis** (`刘易斯`) attends a classified carrier briefing. X-cells are ten to twenty times as resilient as human cells, but safe integration would increase overall human physical capability only two- to threefold while preserving evolved biological balance.
- The insect-occupied naval port has lost over 1,000 soldiers, preventing the damaged carrier's return. Researchers have tested cultures from more than 100 sailors and many animals; all fail. Even a whale injected with few X-cells falls comatose and is predicted to die within three days.
- Zheng fully regrows his lower body, including bone, nerves, vessels, muscle, and skin; the dragon tissue providing nutrients then dies. Researchers wrongly diagnose cerebral necrosis and a vegetative state before he awakens.
- After awakening, Zheng shatters his glass tank with a punch through liquid, whose pressure dents a wall; his speed surprises even himself, light taps knock out three guards, and a punch opens a fist-wide hole at least ten centimeters into the carrier's metal floor. He estimates strength doubled from roughly five to ten times ordinary human level, with major reflex gains.
- Destruction preserved Zheng long enough for the internal explosion to drive his upper body from the dragon's trachea into its head muscle while his exposed lower body was destroyed. He concludes that his new lower body completely absorbed rather than merely fused with dragon cells.
- Dragon-blood X-cell absorption causes Chu Xuan and Bawang to face death within twelve hours; Wang Xia's stronger body may last until the following afternoon. Their tissue attacks itself like an accelerated cancer.
- Because the museum eggs have been made into specimens, Chu requires three fresh T. rex eggs. Zheng must trade the schematics to the military, personally confirm a T. rex nest, recover the Spear of Judgment, inspect the dragon nest for an egg, and leave Wang Xia's armed gravity mine untouched.
- Chu will fax half the schematics to the Pentagon and withhold the remainder until Zheng returns safely. Zheng offers the unrecoverable gravity mine as additional leverage; its special casing cannot be opened without releasing its field and crushing everything within several hundred meters. The military agrees to negotiate helicopter transport and help locating the nest.
- Zheng's current inventory is stated as one A rank, two C ranks, one D rank, and over 7,000 points before the scenario's 1,000-point completion award. He declines personal risk over the D-rank gravity mine.
- Post-X-cell Zheng has more than doubled internal energy, vampire energy, and physical strength. A fingertip wound stops bleeding in seconds, scabs, and shows new pale-red skin within three to four minutes, suggesting regeneration at least three times his previous rate. Without the genetic lock, neural reactions lag behind his new speed.
- Zheng projects up to 600 Anubis warriors, about thirty seconds of Explosion, and about ten seconds of Destruction, though these durations remain untested. His doubled internal energy improves lightness-technique speed by only twenty to thirty percent.
- The military sends several attack helicopters, two transports, and ten-man marine squads. Zheng maps the armed gravity mine for the accompanying general, then recovers the Spear of Judgment half embedded in a tree and receives a radio report of a nest containing two adult T. rexes.
- Before heading to the reported T. rex nest, Zheng diverts to inspect the dragon's branch-and-leaf sleeping nest and hears doglike barking.
- The dragon nest contains an eyes-closed, newly hatched black dragon only half Zheng's forearm long, with wrinkled skin, patches of scales, tiny wings, and doglike behavior. It eagerly eats one compressed ration and one solid-water ration, bonds immediately with Zheng, and falls asleep in his arms.
- A roughly ten-second Explosion test consumes slightly more than one third of Zheng's two energies and causes essentially no tissue damage, confirming it as a repeatable combat skill at his new baseline.
- Marines kill two adult T. rexes and recover five fresh eggs in pellet-filled protective cases. The military successfully excavates the armed gravity mine and verifies gravitational distortion beyond its apparent mass.
- When the general considers seizing the hatchling, Zheng briefly uses Destruction and Tempest Kick to sever dozens of trees across ten meters of forest, then takes the general hostage for the return. The military transports all five eggs.
- In the city, Chu Xuan and Bawang are massively swollen and leaking yellow pus, with Wang Xia somewhat better; only a little over three hours remain. Zheng shields himself behind the general against rooftop snipers, and all seven reincarnators plus the eggs cross into the warehouse as the Lord God announces side-plot completion and begins transfer.
- On return, Luo Gandao, Wang Ai'ai, and Wang Xue are gone, confirming erasure. The Lord God cures the three veterans' X-cell backlash while preserving their four +100 dragon-blood gains, an enhancement value of 4,000 points each.
- The hatchling crosses into the Lord God's Dimension without a character wristwatch. Lori names it **Ugly** (`丑丑`); it still has unopened eyes and incomplete milk teeth, cries visible tears, and behaves like a puppy.
- Bawang creates an unnamed petite blond companion modeled on a famous real-world actress and reputed wealthy heiress. Wang Xia declines to create a companion because the woman he loves remains alive and he wants to face her honorably after returning to reality.
- Wang Xia preserved the Book of Amun-Ra and Book of the Dead in a concealed seam. At the end of the reunion, Chu Xuan remains beneath the Lord God and gives Zheng a rare personal acknowledgment: “You've been through a great deal.”
- Known post-scenario totals: Chu Xuan and Wang Xia each have slightly over 8,000 points and one B rank; Bawang has slightly over 9,000 due to extra dinosaur kills; Zheng has over 14,000 points, one A rank, two C ranks, and one D rank. The third side plot and main mission each awarded 1,000 points.
- The team agrees to share resurrection costs and enter *The Mummy* together. Provisional contributions for two veterans: every person gives two C ranks; Chu Xuan, Bawang, and Wang Xia each give 4,000 points, Zheng 6,000. Expected remainder is two D ranks and 4,000+ points for each other veteran, and A+D plus 8,000 points for Zheng, enabling an A-rank internal art.
- Resurrection priority is **Zero first, then Zhan Lan**. Chu rejects Zhang Heng for now because psychological instability can disrupt plans; restoring Zero first yields an estimated seventy-percent chance of protecting Zhan Lan, whose Psychic Scan and Mind Chain are strategically critical.
- Chu divides the dragon crystal into twenty equal pendants mounted on self-repairing **Gundam Z Alloy** (`高达Z合金`). All twenty cost slightly over 100 points to manufacture; charging one from the Lord God costs 150 points. He begins a live Gauss-pistol test against his own temple.
- The charged dragon-crystal pendant automatically forms a translucent barrier one or two centimeters from its wearer against bullets and energy attacks. A 150-point full charge is compared to a double-C defensive item and would require about twenty minutes of continuous Gauss-pistol fire to exhaust, though sufficiently intense instantaneous force may penetrate.
- Zheng angrily breaks Chu Xuan's jaw over the suicidal-looking test and pays for repair. Chu says he tested progressively from his hand the previous night, then unexpectedly apologizes for recklessness after Zheng invokes his ambition to become a true human.
- One pendant fragment requires about fifteen percent of Zheng's current internal-energy reserve to fill, or slightly less than fifteen percent of his smaller but denser vampire reserve. Both energy types convert safely into the crystal's stored energy. Zheng fills pendants for Chu Xuan, Bawang, and Wang Xia.
- Chu infers the adult dragon could continuously recharge its crystal, explaining prolonged immunity and mine detection. Combined with Zheng as a mobile charger, the necklaces may let the ranged specialists return fire safely and sharply reduce future casualties.
- Zheng's post-fusion estimates stabilize at thirty to forty seconds for Explosion and nearly ten seconds for Destruction. A fresh bite wound closes, scabs, and mostly heals within several dozen seconds; he now believes he could fight the dragon directly.
- Luo Gandao is added to the resurrection list but deferred until the team can equip him with a pilotable combat machine. The immediate resurrection order remains Zero, then Zhan Lan.
