- **Foundation Establishment Pill** (`筑基丹`): double-B rank. Its description says it gives a constitution suited to rapid Foundation Establishment; even an ordinary person gains more than three hundred years of life and immunity to disease. Wang Xia intends to take one home to his mother.
- Chu estimates every current veteran's lifespan at no less than 150 years and Zheng's at no less than 300. He postpones choosing a post-cycle ambition until he unlocks the genetic lock's fourth stage.
- The four veterans each spend a D-rank side story to unlock *The Mummy*, then 500 points for ten days there. They return outside the small hotel and travel toward Hamunaptra on the Sky Stick to resurrect their comrades.
- The guardian clan has built three hidden military bases, sent several thousand youths to study in America, and adopted **Egyptian National Revival Association** (`埃及兴国会`) after rejecting jokingly ominous names. Its goal is to free and unify Egypt while Western tensions rise.
- Zheng gives the clan twenty cubic meters of gold; Chu supplies designs for an improved AK-family weapon using standard 5.56-millimeter ammunition, manufacturable with contemporary industry but roughly thirty years ahead of the era.
- O'Connell, Evelyn, Jonathan, and Evelyn's young son have temporarily moved from Britain to America. A letter sent about twelve days earlier says they found a Maya golden mask built with ancient divine technology to “evolve” humans.
- Blood activates needles inside the incomplete golden mask, transforming a wearer into a superhuman, rapidly regenerating blood-drinker who can create vampire offspring vulnerable to sunlight and silver. O'Connell's party holds the missing gold fragment that prevents reuse and is fleeing east toward New York while pursued; Chu classifies this as a likely plot-item side plot.
- Chu identifies the golden mask with the opening artifact of *JoJo's Bizarre Adventure*: it produces ageless vampires sustained by human blood and destroyed by sunlight or silver. He withholds a further inference until after the resurrections.
- Shared resurrection payment is confirmed possible. For Zero and Zhan Lan together, all four veterans contribute 4,000 points each; Bawang and Wang Xia each contribute one C rank, while Zheng and Chu each contribute two C ranks.
- Resurrection transmits the target's defining life memories to the person invoking the book. Zero's past includes his father's murder, his mother's betrayal, his training as an assassin, and a tragic younger-brother relationship; he renews his promise to fight beside Zheng “until death.”
- Zhan Lan entered after her fiancé was murdered while stopping a handbag thief and justice was corrupted against him. She fell for Zheng partly because he resembled that lost love but could protect others and survive. Zheng recognizes her feelings yet cannot accept their emotional weight.
- Zero and Zhan Lan are successfully restored at Hamunaptra. Zheng publicly vows to reassemble the fallen companions and grow strong enough to defeat the Devil Samsara Team.
- Zhan Lan's established skill pair remains **Psychic Scan** (`精神力扫描`) and **Mind Chain** (`心灵锁链`); do not use “Soul Link.” Chu calls the combination potentially decisive for a team's survival.
- Chu theorizes that plot items derive from ancient systematic technologies rather than inexplicable magic. Runes and diagrams may function like integrated circuits or a programming language encoding fine-grained energy flows, potentially allowing legendary magical artifacts to be manufactured if their notation is decoded.
- Cultivation analogy: Foundation Establishment corresponds to early primary school, Golden Core to approaching junior-high graduation, and Nascent Soul to high school. Artifact refinement is the associated manufacturing discipline.
- **Heaven-Guarding Ring** (`御天环`): B-rank rechargeable cultivation defense item said to withstand a tactical nuclear missile without damage. Legendary magical weapons at B rank or above may nevertheless break equivalent defenses through sustained attack.
- Chu gives greater than fifty-percent odds of recovering systematic legendary-magical knowledge from the Maya excavation. The expedition needs at least forty days: twelve thousand points and two D ranks in aggregate for six people's access/time, with Zheng sponsoring Zhan Lan's 2,000-point forty-day stay and Chu sponsoring Zero's.
- Transatlantic travel requires a second Sky Stick, costing one D rank, because one board carrying six people is too slow and power-hungry; the party also needs airsickness medicine, storm monitoring through Psychic Scan, and island rest stops.
- During the nine-day Cairo break, Zero reveals considerable angling expertise and the old nickname “Prince of Fishing,” but Chu's nearly supernatural catch rate immediately challenges him.
- Zhan Lan tearfully tells Zheng that at death she feared only never seeing, hearing, or mentally connecting with him again. Zheng suddenly embraces her and may kiss her; the narration deliberately leaves the kiss uncertain from her dazed perspective.
- Defense forecast: sub-B defensive items do not yet neutralize technology, but widespread B-rank defenses will shift team combat toward close quarters and ranged legendary magic. Dragon-crystal pendants resist technological projectiles and explosions much better than direct physical strikes.
- Planned paths: Chu—orthodox Gun Kata, then a high-survivability bloodline such as Troll; Zheng—internal energy, Vampire Bloodline, and eventually a high-grade legendary magical weapon; Bawang—legendary magical suppression weapon and possibly Werewolf; Wang Xia—internal energy plus a portable legendary magical cannon; Zhan Lan—broader psychic abilities including telekinesis or mind control.
- **Omen** (`预兆`): A-rank ability Zero considers the ideal sniper skill, first noticed when he obtained Permanent Druid Hawk-Eye. He also seeks a high-grade legendary magical sniper rifle.
- **Instantaneous Destruction** is Zheng's proposed method of entering Destruction only for the instant required by Shave, Moonwalk, Tempest Kick, or impact, then disengaging it so dragon-cell regeneration can repair each brief injury. The present bottleneck is moving both energies to the heart and canceling their contact fast enough.
- When resurrected Zero returns to the Lord God's Dimension, the shy, androgynously pretty little girl he created also reappears. The source has previously called the corresponding child his younger brother in resurrection memory but calls this created child his younger sister; preserve the discrepancy unless later text resolves it.
- A companion erased together with a dead creator returns upon that creator's resurrection; a companion who died earlier does not. This explains Zero's created girl's return and Arnold's continued absence.
- Chu concludes that A-rank and higher methods, abilities, and perhaps bloodlines require talent, training, and adaptation rather than granting full power immediately. He prioritizes Zheng's internal art before the faster-adapting Vampire Bloodline so Zheng can establish their balance.
- **Primordial Unity Art** (`混元一气功`): A-rank internal cultivation method costing 5,500 points and one A rank; it draws external qi through 108 greater circuits and may produce a Golden Core after more than two hundred years at perfection. Refining Zheng's current energy should make its quality three to five times that of his former advanced internal energy.
- **Limitless Chaos Formula** (`无极混沌诀`): double-A-rank internal method also suited to Zheng's need for deep, renewable, body-protecting energy, but currently unaffordable.
- Chu directs Bawang to purchase Zero a Gauss sniper rifle, ten standard rounds, and ten anti-spirit rounds; Wang Xia contributes 2,000 points to Zhan Lan's psychic power and purchases the second Sky Stick's materials and energy system. Only Chu, Zheng, and Zhan Lan are presently judged suitable pilots because they have unlocked the genetic lock.
- Before the forty-day reentry, Zheng has only slightly over 500 points left: his A rank bought the Primordial Unity Art, two C ranks funded resurrection, and his last D rank unlocked *The Mummy* for Zhan Lan.
- For the Atlantic crossing, each Sky Stick tows a circular three-meter gondola made of reinforcing bars and high-strength polymer line, nominally seating three to five while weighing under one kilogram. In practice, one pilot and two basket passengers travel per board.
- Complete conversion to Primordial Unity energy requires roughly thirty days. The refined power answers thought like part of Zheng's body; both quality and quantity should rise three- to fivefold, after which automatic circulation multiplies recovery and growth rates. Physical strength and speed improve only gradually during refinement.
- Zheng deliberately jumps over the Atlantic and uses full Destruction plus several Moonwalk steps to enter the gondola. Zhan Lan bites him in fear and forces him to recognize that certainty of success does not excuse frightening companions—the same lesson he imposed on Chu after the pistol test.
- Zhan Lan's hummed song is titled **“Sky”** (`天空`); do not supply lyrics or identify a performer unless later text does so.
- Psychic Scan cannot identify O'Connell's family without previously learned psychic signatures. Chu's substitute is a lavish three-day New York touring exhibition of the Book of the Dead and Book of Amun-Ra, designed to draw Evelyn to authentic artifacts or provoke her to expose counterfeits.
- O'Connell and Evelyn's young son has been abducted by the golden-mask host to force return of the missing fragment. Jonathan swears he mailed the fragment and plea to Cairo a month earlier, conflicting with the guardian's earlier estimate of about twelve days; preserve both reports.
- Imhotep and his beautiful companion unexpectedly meet O'Connell's family aboard their eastbound train. He says he independently came to America as previously announced and asks about the monster and fragment.
- Team China reserves one cubic meter of emergency gold in New York. Zheng prioritizes physical protection of both ancient books—especially the resurrection-enabling Book of Amun-Ra—above any side-plot reward. The masked host carrying the kidnapped boy passes the team unnoticed.
- The lavish exhibition expands into an opening-night reception for public figures and ordinary New Yorkers. Zhan Lan's restored Mind Chain again gives the scattered team shared vision, direct speech, and unified tactical awareness across the city.
- Chu's hidden second objective is to attract Imhotep through his instinctive connection to the two ancient books and recruit him using the consenting-film-character wristwatch. Imhotep could add an immortal life-draining sorcerer able to summon up to ten giant sphinxes.
- Chu assigns equal odds to Imhotep seeking power for world domination or seeking it solely to protect himself and Anck-su-namun; he likewise estimates a fifty-percent recruitment chance. The three objectives justifying 10,000+ points are recruitment, ancient Maya knowledge, and the golden-mask side plot.
- The unmasked host is an exceptionally handsome brown-haired man with superhuman vision across several thousand meters. Fine tubes retract from drained men into his body; he orders them to avoid sunlight as their bodies swell into nearly two-meter giants.
- Exhibition compromise: only the Book of the Dead appears initially; the Book of Amun-Ra is announced for the final day because Zheng will not risk the team's resurrection hope sooner.
- Zhan Lan can continuously monitor fewer than fifty designated people and notice accidents or strong psychic fluctuations. Team protocol now keeps every member locked except during private matters; members mentally repeat her name for contact, and she may initiate links as needed.
- The mask host's body behaves as independently intelligent tissue: blood vessels become automobile-piercing needles, hair moves and feeds like snakes, physical strength and speed are immense, and a sudden extreme temperature drop freezes anyone touching him until they shatter. Victims become powerful undead able to lift cars or punch through houses.
- **Ultimate Life-Form Evolution Mask** (`最终生物进化面具`): Imhotep's ancient texts say it was intended to create an advanced life-form one step below nature-controlling ultimate life-forms. A manufacturing flaw leaves its product incomplete and fatally vulnerable to sunlight. The host was a local man in his sixties transformed into an apparent eighteen-to-twenty-two-year-old.
- Lock the host's name as **Didafo** (`缔达夫`) unless a later explicit romanization supersedes it. His hair converts victims into distorted near-two-meter zombies with roughly three to four times ordinary strength; neither he nor they produce a detectable psychic signature.
- Hidden side plot: kill advanced life-form Didafo while keeping **Alex**, O'Connell and Evelyn's roughly one-year-old son, alive and uninfected. Killer reward: one B rank plus 5,000 points; every other Team China member receives one C rank plus 4,000 points. Alex's death or conversion costs every member 10,000 points; no time limit.
- Didafo can instantly freeze one of his own giant zombies into a brittle statue through one-handed contact. He detects the missing golden fragment by scent and initially offers to spare Team China's companions in exchange for it.
- Zero's first anti-spirit Gauss sniper shot after resurrection completely pulverizes Didafo's head, continues through a streetlamp, and craters the ground. No completion prompt occurs before the part ends.
- Didafo survives decapitation by having scattered flesh and bone crawl back into place within several dozen seconds. Detached veins serve as high-speed piercing weapons; newly mauled corpses also rise as weaker zombies.
- His freezing grip numbs Zheng's neck within about one second. Before adapting, Didafo has roughly seven to eight times ordinary human strength; within a little over ten seconds he repairs fractures, grows scale-like horny skin, and triples output to at least twentyfold strength.
- Didafo's conscious genetic adaptation resembles the fourth-stage genetic lock: an efficient weak resting body can rapidly assume a combat-optimal form. Zheng treats him as an imperfect preview of the true fourth stage reached by Clone Zheng.
- During Explosion, Zheng crushes Didafo's face inward, takes Alex safely from his arms, and kicks him dozens of meters into a streetlamp. This is not yet confirmed as a kill.
- Didafo claims Zheng's blood contains an advanced life-form essence and that one taste restarts his stalled evolution. His body repairs fatal-looking facial, chest, and spinal damage within seconds and adapts its strength, speed, scales, and spinal protrusions toward Zheng's Explosion-level performance.
- Alex's frailty prevents Zheng from using Explosion freely because acceleration alone may kill him. Team response through Mind Chain: Zheng holds Didafo still; Zero destroys one thigh; Bawang shreds him from 100+ meters; Wang Xia supplies a five-second ultra-high-explosive bomb; Zheng catches it, shields Alex, and uses Destruction plus Shave to escape.
- The bomb leaves an approximately ten-meter crater and flattens the plaza center. No side-plot completion prompt follows. Because Didafo and numerous zombies lack psychic signatures, environmental Psychic Scan cannot distinguish an escaping Didafo or moving fragments from the remaining undead.
- The Maya site resembles an ancient workshop or factory rather than a tomb: corroded bronze tools whose wooden handles decayed, plus a pit of deformed humanoid and nonhuman skeletons misidentified as sacrificial victims. The archaeological team has since become zombies, but the ruins remain intact.
- Maya murals show apelike humans threatened by monsters until groups of **Saints** (`圣人`) arose, defeated the monsters, and guided human survival. As Saints aged and died, monster scarcity prevented new ones from emerging naturally.
- One Saint faction created an enormous realm that repeatedly placed humans among monsters to force new Saints to arise—strongly implying the origin of the Lord God's Dimension.
- Alternative successor projects failed: robed, tall-hatted Saints made golden pills whose mountain-dwelling recipients became cruel and exterminated each other; cloaked Saints surgically created sterile bat-winged and feather-winged humans who warred to extinction; masked Saints created golden-mask “perfect humans” who became cannibalistic monsters fatally vulnerable to sunlight.
- The last Saint dies with only the enormous realm surviving. Evelyn says Didafo is still an **incomplete creation** (`残缺品`) and has not obtained the full intended power of the mask-produced being.
- Mask flaws: loss of emotional restraint, constant dependence on human blood to sustain life's essence, and absolute fatal vulnerability to even weak sunlight. Restoring the missing fragment is only step one toward completion.
- Further evolution requires Jonathan's flawless diamond from the Scorpion King's pyramid—described as two footballs in size—to focus sunlight into the mask's brow. The transformation may elevate Didafo to Saint level. Didafo learned of the diamond because Jonathan boasted during a card game.
- Didafo's fingertip-sized flesh sample is a complete independent organism. Cow blood regrows a one-third excision and produces eyes, tentacles, and sensory organs; concentrated sulfuric acid destroys it, but diluted acid or strong alkali provokes a sacrificial insulating membrane, while saline produces a nearly transparent, air-breathing jellyfish-like sheet.
- Chu concludes the mask reversed its intended control: instead of the wearer controlling genes, autonomous genes/cells control the wearer. Every cell behaves like an amoeba and evolves independently under threat; Didafo must be considered alive while even one cell survives.
- Scattered tissue normally seeks reunion rather than independent conflict, so the captured sample may track Didafo. Chu first asks Imhotep to remove all water and test whether the cells can adapt beyond dependence on water.
- Imhotep wants the completed golden mask for Anck-su-namun so she can share his immortality. Evelyn warns the diamond-and-sunlight procedure is unverified and potentially fatal; Zheng forces Imhotep to distinguish love from selfishly risking her transformation. They provisionally agree to test another organism first.
- Imhotep's sand-form arm extracts all water and immobilizes the sample, but saline restores it and merges it with the living sheet. Confirmed rapid cell-destruction methods: concentrated acid or alkali, fire, and sunlight. Silver only poisons cells and temporarily stops division.
- Initial predawn plan: Zheng and Imhotep engage at close range; Zero disables Didafo's legs if he flees; Bawang clears zombies and supports with fire; Imhotep dehydrates every fragment; dawn supplies sunlight. Wang Xia, Chu, and O'Connell guard the hotel group while Zhan Lan relays both fronts. Tracking test was scheduled for about 4 a.m.
- Chu revises the causal chain: Didafo deliberately allowed Jonathan to steal the mask so O'Connell's family would lead him to the diamond. After they mailed the fragment to Cairo, he abducted Alex as leverage. Didafo has already implanted cells in Alex to track him and gradually assimilate him into a zombie; the hotel attack begins immediately after this deduction.
- Hotel infiltration uses long cords of living flesh: one extracts Alex down the exterior wall while two ceiling strands attack Zheng and his parents. Red Flame burns the attacking cords to ash in just over one second; Zhan Lan can lock onto Alex's living psychic signature even though Didafo's tissue is invisible to her scan.
- Zheng rejects Chu's fifty-fifty option of allowing the abduction until the planned predawn ambush and pursues immediately. The carrier is a fused multi-human zombie with seven or eight legs and multiple arms shielding Alex.
- Primordial Unity internal energy multiplies Zheng's impact resistance severalfold. The energized Spear of Judgment has a special effect on zombies: direct hits erase them, while nearby golden radiance causes corrosion and burning.
- Didafo's rooftop feeding site holds 100+ corpses and over a dozen mutilated living captives; paired zombies wring blood from victims while his feet become tendrils that absorb the pooled blood. Zheng recovers Alex before confronting him and ends the part by hurling a fully charged Spear of Judgment.
- Half of Zheng's enlarged internal and vampire reserves powers the Spear of Judgment like his former full reserves: its miniature-sun radiance burns zombies, erases Didafo's body, punches through the entire modern building, and leaves a three-meter-plus hole. Didafo reconstructs from cells in nearby corpses and zombies.
- Every zombie Didafo creates directly contains his cells; their bitten descendants inherit them. He claims tens of thousands or more now exist throughout New York, any survivor preserves him, and improvements learned by one distant body propagate to all cells and bodies.
- Alex's implanted cellular buds are dormant but will activate after ten days. Didafo offers to remove the threat only in exchange for both the diamond and mask fragment.
- Zheng's blood accelerates Didafo's bat-wing development. Five identical winged bodies pursue Zheng, while Didafo warns that killing zombies around the other team members can assemble additional advanced bodies. Imhotep arrives in sandstorm form as Zheng retreats.
- Imhotep naturally counters Didafo: he has no flesh or blood to parasitize, invading cells dehydrate inside his sand body, and he can mummify a captured body in several dozen seconds. Didafo's strength can still shatter Imhotep whenever he materializes. Anti-spirit bullets have no special effect.
- If infection reaches tens of thousands or millions, simultaneous sunlight exposure becomes impossible; one surviving seed preserves all. With synchronized evolution, no lasting distinction remains between Didafo's strongest and weakest bodies.
- Zheng and Chu accept an exact fifty-fifty life-or-death wager based on a shortcut principle from the Scorpion King forest. Their hypothesis: Didafo's apparently flawless autonomous evolution is itself the fatal flaw explaining why mask beings never ruled after the Saints.
- At a little after 1 a.m., eight bodies already pursue Zheng and he projects 100+ before dawn. He gives Alex to Imhotep, orders the defenders to dehydrate rather than dismember zombies, and lands alone to force Didafo's evolution toward the hypothesized failure point.
- Didafo confirms synchronized improvement arises through shared genetic/life-essence reconstruction rather than the energy gained by feeding; feeding mainly increases cell count, though time and further absorption also perfect evolution.
- Zheng forces every body to adapt first to Explosion, reaching its full physical speed and strength without Zheng's unlocked combat instincts. He then reveals the flaw: autonomous evolution cannot be controlled or reversed, while maintaining Explosion/Destruction performance from cellular energy alone is fatal.
- Destruction sequence: Fist Gun and Tempest Kick destroy bodies; several hundred maximum-force punches within seconds force global adaptation; after about five seconds the others reach one-third Destruction speed, then nearly one-half during Zheng's Moonwalk ascent. Zheng drives the target several meters underground and exits Destruction as his vessels rupture.
- Once Zheng's artificial external-energy state ends, Didafo's involuntarily over-evolved cells exhaust themselves. A charging body rots and melts into a collapsing skeleton before reaching him, validating the fatal-flaw hypothesis at least locally.
- Zheng explains that food normally powers cellular work, while his internal and vampire reserves externally power Explosion/Destruction. Didafo's globally synchronized cells must sustain comparable output from cellular energy alone; forcing Destruction-level adaptation exhausts every cell—including Alex's implants—within roughly ten seconds and kills them simultaneously.
- Chu protects the hotel party by reaching the roof and having Wang Xia demolish the stairwell; climbing giants are shot down until Imhotep returns. O'Connell's dislocated arm is the defenders' worst injury. Alex's dead chest implant drains as pus before it can infect him.
- Evelyn introduces a possible **Holy Grail** in America whose liquid grants eternal youth, based on a lead her professor heard from **Indiana Jones**. Team China declines another advanced-life-form hunt, but Imhotep chooses to investigate for Anck-su-namun.
- Imhotep postpones Lord's Dimension recruitment for no more than three years while testing safer immortality options. Since one scenario corresponds to more than one and nearly two years in this world, Zheng expects an answer after at most two further scenarios.
- **Evolution Mask** (`进化面具`): completed golden-mask plot item. A fourth-stage genetic-lock user who wears it gets one opportunity to experience the fifth stage; anyone below stage four suffers genetic loss of control. The appraisal also says “no usage limit”; preserve the distinction between a wearer's one opportunity and the item's unrestricted use.
- The mask's surface symbols visibly convert white internal energy into golden light. Chu models runes as bodily-energy integrated circuits; mastering their conversion coefficients and combined systems would amount to perhaps one tenth of a Saint's capability.
- Chu says the Spear of Judgment converts internal/vampire energy into atomic- and molecular-level matter disintegration. Decoding it could enable Judgment rifles/cannons; analogous rune systems could produce flying swords using antigravity, psychic control, destructive force, or matter replication, and artifacts governing ice, fire, space, or gravity.
- After three hospital days, the eleven adults excluding Alex fly to the ancient Maya site: Zheng and Chu pilot two Sky Sticks, four passengers ride each gondola, and Imhotep travels as sand. The earthquake-exposed stepped pyramid is dated to roughly five thousand years ago, millennia before the recognized Maya pyramid period.
- Didafo was formerly a gentle village elder on a Native American reservation. He transformed the whole village after wearing the mask, including his eight-year-old granddaughter.
- The dead village zombies decay for about four days and are individually cremated with measured gunpowder to prevent disease. A pressed exterior stone automatically opens the stepped pyramid entrance.
- Imhotep says stepped pyramids originated in a pre-dynastic civilization as ritual structures and later inspired pharaonic tomb pyramids. His high-priest inheritance preserves scripts from multiple ancient regions, including Maya and Shang-era Chinese pictographs, but not the lost rules for combining magical symbols.
- Inscribed chamber map: archive, demon-remains chamber, observatory, energy-storage chamber/control room, and fabrication chamber. A maker holding tools plus a membrane-wrapped user together signify “fabricating a tool,” supporting Chu's legendary-weapon-factory hypothesis.
- The energy/control chamber's powered door fails. Zheng avoids the Spear to protect possible records and vibrates through the wall with Primordial Unity energy in one or two minutes, then opens it with several punches using roughly tenfold human strength.
- Surviving apparatus consists of a smooth central pillar and several rune-covered platforms with adult handprints; the rear chamber is collapsed. Zheng accepts Chu's fifty-fifty activation gamble. His white internal energy becomes gold through the inscriptions, flows like liquid into the pillar, lights the whole pyramid, and makes it tremble.
- Post-Saint continental history: the Central Continent's **Chan Sect** (`阐教`) and **Jie Sect** (`截教`) destroy each other over the last golden pills, leaving myths of “immortals”; Babylon's sterile black- and white-winged races perish at the Tower of Babel, becoming “demons” and “angels”; African and Indian human-animal hybrids become “gods” before unleashed monster essence destroys their civilizations; North Continent mask beings ruin ancient Maya civilization and then vanish through uncontrollable evolution.
- Chu learns meanings for 100+ hieroglyphs. A vast black explanatory stele is found shattered in the fabrication chamber; recruiting laborers and archaeologists and arranging logistics consumes seven days.
- Restored knowledge: elementary rules for **43 symbols/inscriptions** and only three combination rules—**basic energy storage**, **energy-to-basic alternating-current conversion**, and **basic antigravity conversion**. Maya civilization reportedly had 17 such steles, some in underground stepped pyramids.
- Basic energy storage can retain portions of electricity, heat, light, internal energy, vampire energy, psychic energy, telekinetic power, magic, and other elementary energies, but not advanced/large-scale energy such as cultivation true essence or immortal spiritual energy.
- Fully depleted, Zheng requires about three hours to restore internal energy and five hours for vampire energy. A storage device able to return both reserves would nearly double his combat endurance; Chu says the AC-conversion rule can also enable Wang Xia's legendary magical mines.
- Luo Gandao says his unusually strong driving intuition lets him beat more technically skilled arcade opponents; his experience covers simulated motorcycles, sports/racing cars, and probably helicopters, but no real vehicles. Chu Xuan assigns a seventy-percent probability that the right technological path could make him a combatant comparable to Zheng.
- Provisional mecha exchange ladder: C-rank **miniature combat robot** (`战斗微缩机器人`), a 1.8–1.9-meter pilotable upright chibi-bear unit; B-rank **Macross three-form variable robot** (`超时空要塞三形态机器人`); A-rank **prototype Gundam** (`高达原型机`). None has unlimited energy, weapons may cost separately, and a mentally weak pilot remains vulnerable to psychic control and nonphysical horror threats.
- The next scenario is *The Lost World: Jurassic Park* (`侏罗纪公园二`), judged a lower-difficulty creature-combat film. Team China considers exploiting its dinosaur population for points if kills are rewarded.
- Lock Bawang's weapon as an unlimited-ammunition **tubular pulse accelerator** (`管状脉冲弹射枪`): one C rank plus 2,000 points. It pulse-accelerates physical rounds, with nearly twice the power of comparable Gauss weapons; the ordinary model must change magazines after three seconds of fire.
- Wang Xia receives fifty ultra-high-explosive mines, ten timed ultra-high-explosive plastic charges, and three **high-yield gravity mines** (`重力高威地雷`; three cost one D rank plus 500 points), plus 100 points each to muscle density and neural reaction speed through Zheng's once-per-scenario assisted enhancement. Chu Xuan receives two unlimited-ammunition Gauss pistols at one D rank plus 1,500 points each.
- Total stated expenditure is more than 9,000 points, two C ranks, and two D ranks, leaving Zheng slightly over 1,000 points, two B ranks, and one D rank. Preserve these source totals even if component arithmetic later appears approximate.
- The spear's golden light dissolves the Scorpion King completely. Completion awards 10,000 points and two B ranks and unseals the Bracelet of Anubis; all revoked power returns. The pyramid then begins collapsing on a thirty-second timer as Anubis's magic fades and the site turns back into quicksand.
- Imhotep carries Anck-su-namun and Zheng out as a sandstorm, honoring the bargain. The Green Goblin glider tows the whole party clear by foot-loop ropes. Jonathan retrieves the summit diamond, which is roughly one quarter smaller than a football. The guardian accepts Imhotep's departure if he abandons Egypt and agrees to preserve the Hamunaptra altar for future resurrections.
- Zheng reports at least 10,000 points and side stories aggregating to one A rank after the mission, plus both plot items. Chu Xuan asks him to test the unsealed bracelet at full power for future planning.
- Zheng's canyon plan buries more than ten cubic meters of TNT, enough by his estimate to kill over ten thousand targets. Fast fuses burn for two to three seconds; at ordinary lightness-technique speed he runs 100 meters in about six seconds, while Explosion should cover the roughly 300-meter escape in 1.8 seconds.
- The Hamunaptra chasm is confirmed at about twenty meters deep. Twenty-four hundred workers excavate it in three shifts and expect to uncover the Book of Amun-Ra within two days. Zheng plans to retreat for the book if the pyramid remains unreachable within three days.
- The pyramid beam activates above roughly three meters over the canopy. Zheng lures at least four to five thousand increasingly well-armed jungle guardians, including antitank-rocket users, with the unlimited-ammunition rotary autocannon. Sustained gunfire destroys one protective jade pendant and cracks the second before he reaches the canyon, where Imhotep's gunmen are holding his companions hostage.
- Excavation leadership requires two months to reach the deep chasm without explosives. Zheng offers all workers tenfold wages and threatens death if rough methods damage the Book of Amun-Ra. The guardian clan secretly imports high-grade TNT from Europe for the Scorpion King expedition.
- Zheng concludes that revisiting earlier horror films exists to let stronger teams pursue previously inaccessible side plots and plot items, while maximum difficulty supplies a corresponding risk. He identifies the T-virus prototype in *Resident Evil 2* as the only other known retrieval target and sees Chu Xuan's ability to locate hidden side plots as indispensable.
- Fastening the Bracelet of Anubis to Zheng's right wrist formally triggers the advance *Mummy Returns* mission at maximum difficulty: kill the Scorpion King and obtain his army for 10,000 points and two B ranks, with erasure on failure. The Lord God reports forty-seven days remaining; preserve this figure even though the nearby narration loosely says almost one month has passed and two months remain.
- If Zheng dies as captain, another Guide will appear among future newcomers and test an eligible genetic-lock user; passing the test preserves Team China's captaincy succession. Zheng orders the others to continue to the escape pods while he remains with Luo Li.
- The group enters the world of *Ju-On: The Grudge* at a Hawaiian beach for its vacation. They must return to the arrival point after five days, but unlike a temporary return to their original reality, this trip carries no stated ban on revealing the Lord God's Dimension.
- After the sutra's destruction, Zheng Zha revises team admission: outsiders may receive limited help, but only candidates with useful ability, sound character, proof under pressure, and approval from at least half the existing team may become companions. Team safety takes priority over rescuing outsiders.
- Zheng Zha reframes the grudge as a dangerous but potentially killable monster analogous to an invisible Xenomorph. The immediate question becomes whether anti-spirit ammunition can injure it, rather than whether an allegedly irresistible movie curse can be escaped.
- Ancient rule devices are material-specific: the recovered conversion platform uses obsidian, while energy storage uses mithril. A rule works only with compatible substances, which Chu Xuan expects the Lord God to sell at a moderate premium.
- Chu Xuan's provisional flying-sword model requires basic antigravity plus remote energy input, an energy password that prevents seizure, and attribute attack that converts the user's energy into offensive effects; further rules remain unknown.
- Zhao's resurrection is deferred so the six current survivors can fund enhancements, weapons, experimental apparatus, and materials before the next scenario.
- O'Connell, Evelyn, and Jonathan remain at the Maya ruins to continue excavation. Imhotep and Anck-su-namun leave to seek the Holy Grail, retaining their agreement to meet Team China at Hamunaptra within three years if no other path to her immortality is found.
- Chu Xuan's first energy-storage prototype is a six-sided cube with thumb-sized inscriptions and cheap mithril plating. It fills with Zheng's internal energy in one second and holds little; storage capacity depends on the number of complete rule arrays rather than device volume. Chu proposes two pure-mithril rings bearing thousands or tens of thousands of nanoscale arrays.
- The next scenario is *Starship Troopers*, an extremely difficult science-fiction war film and a team battle. Team China will enter later, meaning the Lord God currently judges it stronger than the opposing team.
- Lock **charged mithril ring** (`充能秘银戒`): each of Zheng's two rings contains 846 energy-storage arrays, costs 1,000 points, takes roughly two days to manufacture (at best at least a day and a half with greater proficiency), and can hold essentially all of his current internal energy or several times his vampire energy without transfer loss.
- With equal internal- and vampire-energy reserves supplied by the rings, Zheng estimates Explosion can last roughly seventy to eighty seconds. Destruction remains limited to ten seconds by physical collapse rather than available energy.
- Chu Xuan's prototype legendary-magical time bomb uses black crystal to convert roughly one tenth of Zheng's internal energy into electricity and a costly mithril storage component. After a delayed reaction it produces dozens of rotating blue plasma spheres that melt or vaporize even the alloy floor for over ten seconds.
- Before *Starship Troopers*, Chu plans to add antigravity units and energy chargers to the Green Goblin gliders, resurrect Luo Gandao at no reward cost, acquire a C-rank bear-shaped pilotable robot, and modify it for high-output electrical power and attacks.
- Chu's modified bear-shaped C-rank robot combines an auxiliary electrically propelled jet engine with antigravity, raising ground speed to 200 km/h and flight speed to nearly 600 km/h. The same modification path can later apply to the B-rank Macross three-form variable robot and A-rank prototype Gundam.
- Baseline mecha endurance: C rank one hour before battery replacement, B rank thirty minutes, A rank ten minutes. A full internal- or vampire-energy store plus electricity converter should extend operation to at least four or five hours; the overall modified C-rank machine is projected at two to three times its original performance.
- Zero exchanges two D ranks and 1,000 points for two **microfusion Gauss bullets** (`微聚合高斯子弹`), each producing a 250-meter-radius blast comparable in area to a high-yield gravity mine, and retains 3,000 points.
- Bawang acquires the D-rank **elementary mutant werewolf bloodline** (`初级狼人变异血统`), which initially produces only slight muscle growth and thicker body hair. He retains his other D rank and spare points after paying one D rank to exempt his created companion from future scenarios; Zero must pay the same exemption cost.
- Lock Zhan Lan's proposed double-C psychic attack as **Mind Lash** (`心灵鞭挞`): it retaliates against an enemy probing or controlling an ally and can later upgrade into **Psychic Shock** (`心灵震撼`) as her psychic power grows.
- Chu contributes his C-rank side story for Zhan Lan's Mind Lash in exchange for all of her remaining points, which he needs for manufacturing equipment and materials. Zheng hopes an A rank earned later can fund a mind-control ability for her.
- Zheng hypothesizes that exchanged skills other than bloodlines/genetic modifications can be taught. He offers to teach Wang Xia the Primordial Unity Art and, if Wang Xia succeeds, later teach the whole team. Wang Xia's C rank funds Luo Gandao's bear-shaped robot instead of an elementary internal art or redundant mines.
- Lock **Tiger Soul** (`虎魄`; current form `无白虎魂魄`): B-rank legendary magical great saber, rating 93, costing one B rank plus 4,000 points. A wielder's balanced and harmonious internal energy suppresses its vicious aura and generates saber aura whose length and resilience depend on energy quantity and quality. The A-rank form contains half the White Tiger's soul; the double-A ultimate form contains the whole soul.
- Tiger Soul is a bloodred crystal saber internally supported by a row of bones, approximately 1.6–1.7 meters long and 90–100 kilograms. Without internal energy it is only an exceptionally sharp physical weapon; Zheng's internal energy neutralizes its initial chill and makes it glow bloodred.
- Luo Gandao's C-rank cartoon-bear robot is over two meters tall, entered through a concealed rear hatch, and voiceprint-locks itself to its first pilot. Its inseparable voice modulator converts all speech to “Moni”; disabling it crashes the machine. Luo grasps the basic controls within ten minutes.
- **Dragon-Blood Enhancement Program**: every newcomer is to receive dragon blood in the Lord God's Dimension, then undergo full restoration, indirectly gaining attributes worth 4,000 points. **T-virus prototype program**: the prototype can confer first-stage genetic-lock access, but Chu rates the *Resident Evil 2* side plot at least double-B difficulty and opposes attempting it before the team is stronger.
- Chu watched *Starship Troopers* twenty-one times. He targets two locally abundant weapons roughly equivalent to D-rank exchanges: shoulder-fired miniature nuclear warheads and a late-film heavy explosive weapon whose shot destroys roughly fifty square meters.
- Team China's three immediate *Starship Troopers* objectives are: finish without casualties even if the opposing team largely survives; find and, where possible, complete side plots; and purchase both target weapons in bulk, especially miniature nuclear warheads. For a later return, Chu considers an understanding with the government and even purchasing a small space warship.
- Zero, Zhan Lan, and Luo Gandao receive dragon-blood injections and Lord God restoration; Zero confirms +100 to each of the last four physical attributes. Zhan Lan pays Luo's small restoration cost because the newly resurrected newcomer has zero points.
- Luo Gandao must master basic robot movement within three days before live-fire and post-modification training. The robot shields him from direct baton pain but transmits strong electrical shocks; his post-dragon-blood body is twice ordinary-human strength.
- Tiger Soul can influence Zheng toward grief and desolation; circulating the Primordial Unity Art restores emotional control. More than seventy percent of his internal energy initially creates saber aura extending from half a meter to nearly one meter beyond the blade.
- Tiger Soul's visible aura understates its true reach: one light swing silently bisects both a five-meter-thick alloy-steel wall directly ahead and an eight-meter-thick wall several meters beyond it, leaving mirror-smooth cuts.
- Tiger Soul's effective attack range increases roughly three meters per ten percent of Zheng's current internal-energy reserve; seventy percent yields about twenty-one meters. Its aura can change length and shape, including a hammer form that leaves a four-to-five-meter-wide, three-to-four-meter-deep crater. Weaknesses are immense energy use and the time required to charge.
- Internal arts can be taught, but the Lord God exchange derives value from opening precisely the needed meridians, implanting the route, and supplying initial energy. Wang Xia's atrophied meridians consume Zheng's transferred energy; unaided progress may require ten years, while daily assistance would still take months to half a year. A first-stage genetic unlock or stronger body—potentially from the T-virus prototype—may help.
- Luo Gandao's cheerful arcade-prodigy account is a facade. Born and abused in an orphanage, he escaped with an older childhood friend and survived through begging, theft, gambling, and lethal illegal street racing. The friend died in a racing crash, leaving over 100,000 yuan and the injunction to live; Luo later survived sabotage by driving into a crowd, after which a young victim's eyes made him question why he lived.
- Resurrection exposes the restored person's memories and past to the resurrector. Zheng knows Luo will not entrust survival to others and waits in the plaza for him to investigate the Lord God's catalog privately.
- Zheng privately confronts Luo Gandao's resentment over his prior death and recognizes their shared hunger for survival, but warns that betraying Team China will bring immediate execution. Zheng promises that the team will never attack Luo from behind.
- Luo's modified robot replaces batteries with internal-energy-to-electricity conversion. Ten percent of Zheng's current reserve provides seventeen hours of uninterrupted operation; the storage system holds about 130 percent, enough under ordinary conditions for a full scenario. Zheng initially charges it with twenty percent.
- The propulsion modification uses two rear housings that emit twenty-centimeter blue electrical jets with limited directional control. It drives the robot at two to three times Zheng's normal lightness-technique speed and enables a nearly ten-meter leap, but consumes energy rapidly and exceeds Luo's current reactions and control; first-stage genetic-lock access may make it manageable.
- The robot's third modification produces an omnidirectional electrical field extending five meters because Team China lacks an energy-control rule. Outer fur insulates the machine, but strong discharge still paralyzes Luo. The cockpit provides assisted targeting and miniature radar that tracks biological activity within one kilometer.
- Bawang's D-rank werewolf form is estimated at five times ordinary-human physical ability. Wang Xia still cannot generate internal energy, but daily meridian stimulation may be improving his physique.
- Plasma weapon parameters: a grenade stores ten percent of Zheng's internal energy, creates a roughly ten-square-meter plasma reaction, and attacks within about twenty meters; a mine stores twenty percent and has twice the power, with time bombs comparable to mines. Dragon-crystal barriers easily stop both plasma and residual heat.
- Chu's modified Green Goblin gliders have larger energy stores and electrical jet engines exceeding 3,000 km/h. Wind pressure makes the speed usable only by Zheng until the team obtains a wind-negation rule.
- Lock new team names as **South Flame Team** (`南炎洲队`) and **Forest Team** (`森洲队`), which entered *Starship Troopers* before Team China. Chu provisionally associates them with southern Africa and South America/Brazil respectively; treat this as his inference, not confirmed geography.
- Three-team scoring: killing an opposing member without a genetic unlock yields 2,000 points plus one C rank; killing an unlocked member yields 7,000 points plus one B rank. Each allied death is −1, each enemy kill +1, and the final score multiplied by 2,000 is awarded to each surviving member.
- Main mission: survive twenty days. Rewards are one point per 100 ordinary Arachnids, one point per ten flying bugs, ten points per laser bug, and 100 points per fire-spitting bug. No Brain Bug reward is listed, leading the team to infer a side plot.
- Team China has seven members and no newcomers. By a six-to-one vote, with Luo Gandao dissenting, it chooses the Arachnid planet over remaining aboard for the safe 1,000-point completion.
- Combat roles: Bawang sweeps ordinary swarms; Zero targets flyers and large castes; Wang Xia demolishes tunnels and concentrations and lays mines; Zhan Lan scans underground and watches for rival teams; Chu uses a glider for reinforcement and protects Zhan Lan; Luo intercepts close threats, supports Bawang at range, draws swarms, and uses electrical discharge; Zheng leads.
- The team is assigned to the **Fourth Mobile Infantry**, ordered to Hall Three for squad organization and deployment to Klendathu in fourteen hours. Johnny Rico belongs to the Sixth Mobile Infantry. The entry point is the Federation's first Klendathu landing, where the source states 100,000 soldiers die.
- All seven are registered as Federation privates; Chu is appointed squad leader over them and three ordinary soldiers. An operations map places the Fourth, Fifth, and Sixth Mobile Infantry battle zones in a triangle, with the Sixth close enough to reach using occasional glider travel.
- Chu identifies two rival-entry possibilities: nearly equal teams may have arrived only minutes apart, or the earlier teams may have entered at recruitment and risen into senior command or military intelligence. A rival psychic officer could locate Team China, trap it aboard a doomed ship, and order its destruction if it fled.
- To conceal their nonstandard weapons, Chu proposes killing all witnesses in their roughly 300-soldier landing group, fighting to the surviving Sixth Mobile Infantry plot characters, stowing the weapons, and returning with them. Zheng accepts: survival may stain their hands but not their hearts, and sacrificing a teammate as bait remains forbidden.
- After eight hours' sleep and roughly three hours following breakfast, Team China boards a dropship with the three ordinary squad members and descends toward Klendathu.
- On Klendathu, the thick cloud layer leaves the landing zone as dim as early morning. Most Federation troops are inexperienced recruits from a long-peace unified government; Chu characterizes the Mobile Infantry as closer to an extraterrestrial colonial corps than a battle-tested army.
- The robot's embedded dragon-crystal necklace draws power directly from its internal-energy store, making its barrier the team's strongest after Zheng's. Luo initially hesitates to fire on humans; Zheng warns that no further energy will be supplied during the scenario, forcing him to preserve himself by fighting.
- Bawang's first burst with the tubular pulse accelerator kills a formation of more than fifty soldiers. Zero destroys two live-broadcast reporters, their equipment, and a half-meter section of dropship hull with an ordinary close-range Gauss shot.
- A Federation shoulder launcher fires a red-tipped miniature nuclear warhead capable of flattening several hundred square meters. Luo's rotary autocannon can tear apart human targets within one kilometer and kills the launcher crew before they fire.
- Zheng uses Tiger Soul's aura to kill targets three meters beyond the physical blade and bisects a dropship hovering two meters above the ground. He orders all ships and witnesses destroyed within one minute while Zhan Lan monitors survivors and the approaching Arachnid swarm.
- Destroying a Federation dropship awards 50 points; killing ten soldiers awards one point. Zero downs seven dropships, while the tubular pulse accelerator remains lethal beyond 3–4 km and penetrates transport armor easily within 1 km.
- Ordinary ground Arachnids are over two meters tall and three meters long, move at roughly twice ordinary-human speed, and have carapaces tough enough that Bawang's tubular pulse accelerator penetrates only two or three in succession. Luo's rotary autocannon requires almost one second of sustained fire per bug.
- A hostile broad psychic scan detects Team China's general presence but not individual minds. Zhan Lan suspends Psychic Scan and Mind Chain for thirty seconds and uses Mind Lash to screen the team.
- Chu's two-Gauss-pistol technique kills every Arachnid within three meters for over ten seconds but rapidly consumes stamina. Zheng coordinates through Mind Chain, enters Explosion in under one second, and carries Chu from the four-meter-deep collapse.
- Wang Xia's four-second plasma time bomb lands atop four recovered miniature nuclear warheads, producing multiple explosions and a small mushroom cloud nearly 100 meters behind the team.
- The first Klendathu landing's estimated casualties rise from 100,000 ground troops to more than 300,000 including the fleet. Blue laser spheres fired from the surface destroy most ships in orbit.
- If both rival teams wait beside the protagonists, Chu estimates Team China's direct-combat victory chance at 70 percent because the Lord God's rating excludes Zheng's self-created Explosion and Destruction. Their absence would imply they acquired strategic influence intended to balance inferior personal strength.
- Team China plans to approach the Sixth Mobile Infantry, then—once rescue ships appear—use only Federation weapons to save and blend into a surviving unit, avoiding questions about how seven people crossed the battlefield.
- Flying Arachnids appear in the hundreds, with roughly three-meter bodies, wingspans over six meters, and speed exceeding a 700-km/h Green Goblin glider. They spread into an encircling net after the leading dozen are shot down.
- With under forty seconds before interception, Wang Xia drops a plasma time bomb from roughly twenty meters so it detonates on impact, collapses the ground, and exposes underground tunnels; both glider groups dive inside only dozens of meters ahead of the swarm.
- A giant fire-spitting bug will burrow into the team's tunnel in roughly five minutes. Wang Xia sets the escape demolition for 4:30 and adds mines; after preparations consume thirty seconds, Zheng announces four minutes remain to clear the flyers.
- Lock Chu Xuan's omnidirectional two-pistol technique as **Gun Kata** (`枪斗术`). With the genetic lock active and a Green Goblin glider, it prevents flying bugs from approaching within ten meters.
- Chu reveals three concealed plasma bombs inside Luo Gandao's robot: they detonate if Luo moves 50 km away from Chu or thirty seconds after Chu's heart stops. This forces Luo to join the aerial attack.
- The cartoon-bear robot's shell and frame use **Gundanium Alloy** (`高达尼姆合金`). Flying-bug wings break against the armor, leaving only white scratches, but the joints remain vulnerable. When the swarm severs Luo's autocannon and begins pulling the robot apart, his survival drive triggers the first stage of the genetic lock.
- Luo's electrical discharge covers roughly five to six meters for one second and carbonizes more than twenty flying bugs. It causes two seconds of pilot paralysis and requires a five-second recharge; the genetic lock lets Luo master electrical-jet acceleration and precise inertial turns immediately.
- Zhan Lan eventually connects Luo to Bawang, allowing Luo to align his pursuers with the tunnel while Bawang fires on his command. Zheng considers mutual contribution—not verbal acceptance alone—necessary for Luo to join the team.
- Dragon-crystal barriers do not stop living creatures in close combat. Flying-bug wings repeatedly wound Zheng through Explosion, including a nearly bone-deep right-chest gash, though his regeneration rapidly scabs and stops the bleeding.
- With 137 flyers remaining and unable to sustain Explosion for another minute, Zheng summons three-meter Army of Anubis warriors, empties his vampire energy, then feeds all personal and one ring's stored internal energy into Tiger Soul. Its at-least-twenty-meter circular sweep kills most of nearly 100 attacking flyers and leaves him completely drained.
- After the flyer battle, Zheng recovers roughly 20% internal and over 30% vampire energy from the rings. Luo suffers his first genetic-lock backlash and cannot move or breathe; Chu and Bawang spend over twenty seconds carrying the heavy robot seven or eight meters rather than abandoning him.
- Fire-spitting bugs are giant red beetles roughly 6–7 meters tall and over 10 meters long, projecting flame 15–16 meters high. One plasma time bomb plus several mines fills about 100 square meters with plasma and creates lethal heat across 200–300 meters; three fire-spitters and many ground bugs yield Wang Xia several hundred to nearly 1,000 points.
- Mobile Infantry status after the opening battle: First annihilated, followed by Second and Third; fewer than 1,000–2,000 Fourth survivors hold a hill; Fifth is nearly destroyed but a several-hundred-person unit gathers stragglers toward the Sixth; the Sixth remains largest and retreats toward Team China under pursuit.
- Chu concludes the hostile scanner belongs to a reincarnation team assigned to the Arachnid faction, analogous to Team India aiding Imhotep. It likely cannot command troops directly, only relay scans to the Brain Bug through Mind Chain; otherwise tens of thousands rather than hundreds of flyers would have attacked.
- Two questions remain: the location and alignment of the third team, and the concealed event absent from the film that Chu believes will force all three teams into desperate conflict during the twenty-day survival mission.
- Team China lands unseen on a steep slope behind a roughly 1,000-person survivor column, stows its equipment, and uses Federation rifles. Those rifles are ineffective against carapace except at the eyes, mouth, and teeth; Chu secretly retains his Gauss pistols.
- Lock the film unit **Roughnecks** (`硬汉部队`). Its metal-armed middle-aged officer reports 70% casualties and refuses to divert from joining the Sixth to rescue the Fifth. Chu estimates an 80% chance of safe extraction with this plot-surviving unit if it contains no rival reincarnators.
- The column includes over 300 walking wounded, more than ten gravely wounded, and 100–200 female soldiers guarding or treating them. Zhan Lan finds no psychic scanner or mental screen among them.
- Chu advises Zheng to display only the maximum plausible ordinary-human ability, lead soldiers, and seek promotion to NCO or commissioned-officer rank. Before this can develop, a fire-spitting beetle appears inside the formation despite Zhan Lan's scan.
- The fire-spitting beetle inside the Roughnecks was psychically screened, proving a rival team waited until Team China stowed its weapons and blended into the formation before directing or enabling the ambush. Zhan Lan begins screening the whole unit in response.
- Chu proposes reproducing the film protagonist's kill: climb the beetle with standard equipment, shoot a hole through its back, and drop ordinary grenades inside, earning Zheng an NCO or sergeant-major position. With ground bugs multiplying, he estimates 70% death risk after two minutes unless Zheng reveals Explosion and Tiger Soul.
- Zheng uses 30% internal energy and the third-stage genetic lock to imitate Zhao Yingkong's footwork while restricting his roughly tenfold-human strength. It takes over twenty seconds to cross several dozen meters and make a plausible three-meter leap.
- The metal-armed Roughnecks lieutenant recognizes Zheng's intent and orders an elite firing corridor. Zheng climbs with a standard knife in three stages, breaks the carapace with concentrated rifle fire, drops several grenades inside, then lands upon a ground bug whose teeth pierce his right arm.
- Lock the metal-armed Roughnecks commander as **Lieutenant Rasczak** (`铁手中尉赖奇`). He promotes Zheng to corporal and acting sergeant major after the beetle kill, then confirms the sergeant-major appointment after Chu's reorganization improves the march and rear guard.
- Zheng's right upper-arm bone is pierced and comminuted; full natural recovery requires at least one day, and it must be set within twelve hours to prevent his accelerated healing from fusing it incorrectly.
- The Sixth Mobile Infantry has 8,600 survivors, but over 3,000 at the rear are pinned; at most 5,000, mostly wounded or demoralized deserters, can rendezvous. Another swarm without glider escape gives the team over 80% death risk.
- The rival psychic screen is twenty kilometers away around a region roughly 500 meters underground, with Arachnids converging outside it. Zheng rejects attacking under his current wounds and these conditions.
- Chu reorganizes the 1,000-plus Roughnecks and orders at least 100 panicked, weaponless Sixth soldiers shot to halt the rout. Luo fears Chu, while Zheng stresses that true teammates share life and death and warns that survival through universal abandonment and betrayal produces loneliness and exhaustion.
- The Roughnecks merge with slightly over 5,000 Sixth survivors: only 40% (about 2,000) remain armed and combat-capable, while 60% are wounded or weaponless. Johnny Rico is unconscious from blood loss after a thigh wound and will likely die without treatment within twelve hours.
- With the senior major semiconscious after losing an arm, Rasczak assumes overall command. Zheng is promoted from corporal to sergeant while retaining sergeant-major authority over the combined force; Chu, Wang Xia, and Bawang become corporals.
- Fleet contact takes over twenty minutes, followed by another ten-minute wait. With ground losses above 80%, the Federation sends rescue landers and bombers due in seven hours.
- Chu's defense plan keeps all troops and wounded together, marches roughly one hour to a broad summit with one narrow approach, and assigns anti-flyer units. Rasczak expects 1,000–2,000 marching deaths, but fixed open-ground defense would permit endless underground ambushes; concentrated fire from 2,000-plus soldiers can stop surface attackers.
- The Third World War nearly destroyed Earth centuries earlier. The resulting Federation suppressed war triggers for centuries, allowing civilian technology to advance while military technology, theory, organization, and training deteriorated. Chu identifies restoring Federation military competence and technology as a potential double-B or A-rank side plot.
- Wang Xia's dozen-plus scouts use Psychic Scan to avoid massed ambushes; Bawang leads heavy gunners at the rear; Chu directs the army; Zhan Lan functions as its concealed communications network. Their prominence intentionally exposes four Team China members while leaving the other three and their abilities unknown.
- New rival strategist: **Neos** (`尼奥斯`), a predatory-eyed blond man who eats chocolate while reasoning. He is one of nine men and women aboard the reinforcement fleet traveling from Earth's solar system and has identified four Team China members.
- Both Chu and Neos independently infer that South Flame Team likely serves the Arachnids and that a spacefaring **Arachnid warship** must connect Klendathu with the other major battlefield, **Tango Urilla** (`探戈幽灵星`), forcing all three teams to meet.
- The true scenario mission has not yet appeared beyond the twenty-day survival condition. Both strategists expect an Arachnid space attack to trigger it; afterward Team China and Neos's group will compete to identify every hidden rival before deciding between battle and intelligence exchange.
- The final march to the summit costs over 800 dead and 300 additional wounded, still less than half the projected losses without Team China's coordination. Miniature nuclear launchers have an effective range of roughly 5 km.
- The summit consists of granite-hard black rock, with snow and temperatures over ten degrees below Earth's. Standard Federation uniforms provide personal climate control. Five hundred soldiers guard the single approach in twenty-minute shifts followed by ninety minutes' rest.
- Remaining inventory: 42 miniature nuclear warheads and roughly 13,000 magazines, only two or three per soldier. Heavy fire is projected to last slightly over four hours of the six remaining; revealing Team China's weapons risks conflict with the entire Federation, which Chu rates unwinnable.
- Millions of Arachnids surround the mountain. A newly observed, unlisted **tank-shaped pincer bug** has thick armor and enormous forelimb pincers and is rarer than a fire-spitter, marking it as a hidden high-threat caste.
- For the final defense, irregular one-to-two-meter boulders rolled down the steep road can kill ordinary bugs for dozens of minutes; the 42 nuclear warheads are reserved for fire-spitters and tank bugs. Bawang controls ammunition discipline, Wang Xia upgrades collected grenades, Chu coordinates, Zheng intercepts breakthroughs, Zero snipes large-caste eyes with Federation rifles, Zhan Lan maintains scan/links, and Luo protects and evacuates her.
- With 5:42 remaining before rescue, hundreds of thousands of Arachnids begin forcing their way up the mountain road. Bawang uses summary execution to impose aimed, controlled bursts by ten-person sector teams. A screened presence—rival team or Brain Bug—lies roughly five kilometers from the summit and deep underground.
- Modified fire-spitters carry more than a dozen armored tumor-like projectiles that launch as living artillery. Wang Xia's altered miniature nuclear timer can detonate after three seconds, allowing an aerial interception; surviving projectiles unfold into six-legged spider bugs whose close-combat strength and speed exceed ordinary ground Arachnids.
- With his right arm unusable, Zheng deliberately lets a spider bug's spear-like leg pass through the gaps between his organs, drives his left arm through its eye, and uses concealed **Red Flame** inside its head to kill it.
- Each spider bug is worth 100 points. Zheng tears a roughly one-meter spear limb from the first carcass and, aided by Zero's precisely timed shots overturning the target, impales the second through its head.
- The tank-shaped pincer bug is seven to eight meters tall, resembles an enormous black longhorn beetle, and is deliberately screened by the hostile psychic. Ordinary Arachnids shelter behind it as it advances.
- Federation grenades contain thumb-sized white explosive crystals surrounded by black stabilizer. Wang Xia combines ten crystals in one casing, producing a dangerously unstable enhanced grenade; Zheng stores several in his storage ring. Forty-one miniature nuclear warheads remain at this point.
- The tank bug's lifted black plates emit a blue vapor-like substance that forms an energy shield capable of stopping one miniature nuclear warhead; its ordinary shell alone deflects Federation bullets. The shield appears only immediately before an incoming heavy attack reaches it.
- Zheng repeats his *Alien* Spiral-Spear tactic by binding an enhanced grenade to a severed Arachnid claw. He throws it through the shield boundary before Wang Xia's warhead triggers the barrier roughly ten meters from the bug; the grenade then destroys it from inside. The kill awards 1,000 points and one rank-D side-plot reward.
- Three hours into the defense, spider bugs have killed over 1,300 soldiers; only dozens are wounded. Killing their carriers beyond 500 meters leaves them harmless, while troops unable to do so retreat more than 30 meters and shoot the spiders after they unfurl.
- With at least ninety minutes remaining, the army has slightly over 2,000 magazines, 11 miniature nuclear warheads, and over 400 grenades. A thousand defenders temporarily stop the Arachnids' all-out wave attack, but several thousand flyers crawl up the surrounding cliffs, repeatedly flying back after the wind tears them away.
- Zheng explicitly compares the defense with the heroes of **Shangganling** (`上甘岭`, the Battle of Triangle Hill), noting his force faces neither air strikes nor long-range artillery and holds a firepower advantage.
- With one hour remaining, the army has 1,000 magazines; flyers climbing for dozens of minutes crest the cliffs but cannot rise three meters without being swept away. The rescue fleet later estimates atmospheric entry in 30 minutes and boarding in 30–45 minutes.
- Arachnid remains form an almost-20-meter embankment roughly 500 meters from the position, with a 500-meter hollow behind it. The altered slope improves downhill gunfire but limits rolling boulders to a 500-meter path until Wang Xia collapses the embankment with two warheads.
- Ammunition then falls through roughly 200 magazines to under 100; five miniature nuclear warheads remain. Zheng reserves 50 magazines and sends the rest to anti-flyer patrols, while rolling boulders drive the swarm 700–800 meters away.
- Chu joins the line with visually inconspicuous Gauss pistols. His last-resort plan gives under a 30% chance: attack the rival team and kill, capture, or gravely wound members so that hostages may deter the Arachnids.
- With roughly 10–20 minutes left, Zheng kicks a boulder over four meters tall downhill. Dense black boulders slightly over one meter across require three soldiers to lift; after over twenty hours of combat, the thousand-plus workers begin slowing.
- Bawang, only two to three times ordinary strength from dragon-blood enhancement, collapses after commanding for six to seven hours and breaks his lower leg beneath a boulder. Zheng sends him to the center with the wounded.
- After patrols kill several thousand flyers, more than 200 remain and all ammunition is exhausted except 50 reserved magazines. Dozens of rescue ships enter the atmosphere, about ten approaching the summit; each appears able to carry over 1,000 people.
- Chu assigns the final 50 magazines to 50 soldiers policing the landing zone. When the first three ships land and over 40 defenders flee while a tank bug is only 60–70 meters away, Zheng kills dozens—including more than ten attackers—to force the survivors back into line; the fourth ship then lands.
- Each rescue ship brings 100 fresh troops and can carry over 1,000. Chu's force kills nearly 100 people to establish boarding order, then takes command of the reinforcements and surviving lieutenants.
- The last two miniature nuclear warheads isolate the tank bug and cut the main swarm's route. Zheng holds off more than ten ground Arachnids for Wang Xia, then turns his back and entrusts his retreat to Zero's precision fire.
- Team China's seven members evacuate aboard three rescue ships: one on Ship One, four on Ship Two, and two on Ship Three. As they lift off, the tank bug's blue shield expands around one-quarter of Ship Four.
- The tank bug's blue shield also acts as a cutting field: after roughly half a second, it cleanly severs the enclosed quarter of Ship Four, which crashes and is overwhelmed by Arachnids.
- Federation medical tanks immerse patients in liquid, supply air through nasal tubes, and use instruments to repair major injuries such as Zheng's shattered right arm. The rescue ships are about 20 minutes from the reinforcement fleet when he wakes.
- On the fleet, Team China must cease Mind Chain and Psychic Scan to avoid detection by an unknown hostile psychic. Confirmed exposed members are Zheng, Chu, Bawang, and Wang Xia; Zero may be exposed; Zhan Lan and Luo remain hidden. Their existence as at least two unknown members deters an unequal confrontation.
- Both strategists avoid direct combat while rosters remain unknown. Neos intends to identify Team China's complete membership before closing the net; Chu exploits the Lord God's designation of Team China as strongest, which causes rivals to avoid direct confrontation while Team China searches openly.
- Chu's **building prestige** strategy is to let the Federation elevate the summit survivors as heroes. Other fleet crews fled, while their force held seven hours and earned the surviving veterans' loyalty; government-backed heroic status can outweigh the hidden team's institutional advantage.
- Team China halts psychic communication five minutes before reaching the fleet. Chu teaches Maya hieroglyphic code from *The Mummy* to Zheng, Zhan Lan, and Luo, whose unlocked states permit rapid learning. Common words include use, hide, psychic power, fight, flee, and screen; decoys end in “hand over,” “do not,” or “take out,” while a genuine message requiring one of those endings repeats it.
- The reinforcement fleet contains 82 first-class space warships, 17 special-class warships with orbital bombardment, and many auxiliaries. The Federation publicizes the seven-hour stand as killing over 300,000 Arachnids after more than 100,000 human troops died in little over an hour.
- New Forest Team psychic: **Xue Ling'er** (`雪铃儿`), nicknamed **Xue'er** (`雪儿`), an eleven- or twelve-year-old girl who finds the loss of Psychic Scan like blindness. **Aya** (`阿雅`) is a long-haired girl in Neos's group. Neos explicitly recognizes Team China's actions as a deliberate prestige-building strategy.
- Fewer than 5,000 of the 200,000-person expeditionary force survive, all from Zheng's command. The media calls Zheng, Chu, Bawang, and Wang Xia the **Miracle Four**; the other survivors receive one or two combat medals, with several hundred promoted to NCO.
- Neos's new **Three-Eyed Race bloodline** (`三眼族血统`) initially improves calculation only; combat-relevant telekinesis requires rank A or double-A. Besides the three confidants around him, his other five teammates split between the lovers **Manini** (`玛尼尼`) and **Luosi** (`箩丝`), and three led by **Hofel** (`霍菲尔`).
- **Ocean Team** (`海洋洲队`) previously mentioned two ancient books. Neos says his own team's discord is a liability and manipulates Hofel's faction into testing Team China as expendable pawns; Chu independently deduces this plan after no mid-ranking officer accesses Team China's records for two days.
- Complete survivor rosters require lieutenant-colonel rank; the rival team's likely ceiling is colonel, not general. Chu's countermeasure is to kill any member who directly provokes Team China, using the resulting score penalty and anxiety to force the hidden strategist into mistakes.
- A reincarnator's death imposes a 2,000-point penalty and shifts the team-battle score by one point. Chu tells Zheng to respond normally in surveilled officers' lounges, training areas, or armories, but use maximum power in unmonitored, empty spaces and kill any attacker instantly.
- Chu's intimidation logic: because the Lord God only states that Team China is stronger without quantifying the margin, Zheng's overwhelming response can make the rival team infer that one member surpasses their combined force, triggering fear, factional discord, and exploitable mistakes.
- In the third genetic-lock stage, Zheng simulates **Xiao Honglu's** reasoning and suspects both strategists are willing to sacrifice discordant teammates. He concludes Chu may be leaving Luo Gandao conspicuous as bait: Luo's death removes a liability, while survival after Team China's rescue could restore trust and expose rival members.
- Zhan Lan can pass as a female soldier and Zero adapts naturally to military surroundings; Luo Gandao's civilian bearing makes him the easiest hidden member to identify. Before Zheng can decide whether to warn him, an unseen corridor attack tears a large piece from Zheng's calf.
- The corridor attacker is a young Black man with a twisted oak staff. His **Wind Blade** forms one meter behind a target; he also knows **Fireball**. Zheng senses killing intent within ten meters, infers the remote strike, then uses Explosion to cross nearly 100 meters in one second and shatter a Wind Blade with fist pressure.
- The attacker has roughly twice ordinary physical attributes and has not unlocked the genetic lock. Zheng kills him within two to three seconds, earning the normal non-unlocked-member reward and raising Team China's battle score by one while the rival loses 2,000 points.
- **Hofel** is a muscular white man and nominal team captain. A muscular Black accomplice survives as remote observer. Zheng's display makes both fear Team China may resemble Devil or Celestial Team, and Hofel breaks Neos's concealment protocol to seek his analysis.
- **Captain Luosi** (`箩丝`) is an exo-Arachnid biologist who reported remains found inside a human soldier's brain on a moon of Pluto, proposing a parasite that controls human behavior. Chu identifies her as a rival reincarnator and sees the claim as a future pretext for erasing Team China through Federation authority.
- Chu promises Zheng he will find the rival team before it harms Luo, then privately admits he lied: Luo remains bait. Senior Federation command is due within several days, imposing a deadline before Team China's heroic prestige converts into institutional power.
- Neos correctly identifies Explosion as short-lived burst power and Zheng's display as deliberate intimidation. Yet Hofel's frightened return exposes their faction: inter-warship shuttle surveillance and the dead attacker's ship let Chu localize Neos's group to one vessel.
- That rival vessel carries over 3,200 people. Neos simultaneously exploits Team China's weakness by locating Luo Gandao through surveillance footage.
- Two-person inter-warship shuttles can survive atmospheric passage as escape craft but fly only in space. Captain Luosi is 21, holds a doctorate in exo-Arachnid biology, and was first to propose the Brain Bug theory.
- Zheng, simulating Xiao Honglu, realizes each murdered rival officer strengthens Luosi's future claim: the parasite-controlled heroes are supposedly killing those who discovered proof. Less than ten minutes after the first death, the military attributes it to a brawl-triggered heart attack and opens an investigation.
- Chu's deeper counterplot is inferred, not yet confirmed: let Neos believe the parasite accusation is ready, then preemptively accuse certain officers of Arachnid control. Team China's heroic prestige can split the troops, seize warships, and potentially earn major side-plot rewards by sinking them. Zheng estimates Chu already places success above 50%.
- New South Flame fighter: **Richard Robias** (`李查德·罗比亚斯`), a northern-European-looking major over six feet tall and the team's most trusted strongest member. His self-created ability is **Berserker** (`狂战士`). Merely passing Zheng makes him abandon Neos's intended counter-intimidation.
- **Manini** has unlocked genetic-lock stage one. South Flame's first captain transferred to Celestial Team after Guide recognition; because he remained alive, the replacement captain gained information-query authority but neither stage two nor a Celestial invitation, leaving the team weak and divided.
- South Flame's first team battle occurred in *The Lord of the Rings* against another team similarly abandoned by its captain. They cooperated peacefully and devised the Balrog's death in the dwarven passage; Gandalf rewarded each with a **rune stone**, a plot item preventing Lord God erasure for a negative team-battle score three times.
- Luosi and Manini avoid factional combat and claim an unusual ability capable of taking several strong enemies with them in death. Zheng guarantees nonaggression and begins reciprocating their rune-stone intelligence with information from *The Mummy*.
- Zheng trades details of undertaking the Scorpion King quest early, which multiplies both difficulty and rewards, plus the two books from *The Mummy*. Manini adds that large, lore-rich worlds such as *The Mummy*, *The Lord of the Rings*, and *Transformers* are especially likely to host team battles and abundant side plots.
- South Flame's factions are Hofel's newly recognized captaincy versus Neos's group; the captain has stage one and ranks below only Richard in combat. Luosi and Manini plan to leave for Earth with senior command's escort in 3–5 days.
- Richard's comparative scale rates himself at 5 normally, 50 with Berserker, the coordinated team at 100, and Zheng alone at a conservative 70–80. Neos nevertheless sends Aya—whose ability favors ambush—to capture Luo as bait for the parasite accusation.
- With South Flame's current vessel fully screened by Xue Ling'er, Chu still watches Aya trail Luo through ordinary surveillance and mobilizes more than ten devoted Federation squad leaders for his counterplan.
