- Luo disappears overnight but remains alive because Team China receives no negative-score death notice. Chu confirms that the capture was anticipated and estimates his full plan gives every member over 70% survival; abandoning it for direct pursuit gives over 50% probability of total annihilation.
- Revealing the plan to everyone would create a 100% leak risk: a captured Luo may face mind control or memory search, and even without them is expected to trade Team China names gradually over 3–5 days to preserve his bargaining value.
- Chu's counter-game uses the same heroic record that enables suspicion. South Flame will accuse the summit heroes through senior command; Team China will persuade the rank and file that infected senior officers are slandering heroes, controlling generals, and preparing to kill the soldiers. Luo's capture is intended to make South Flame certain it has won.
- On the second night of captivity, Luo is covered in bee-sting-like swellings and deprived of sight and sound. South Flame offers freedom and a rune stone for Team China's full roster, appearances, enhancements, and abilities, while giving him one more day before the generals arrive.
- Aya practices African tribal **witchcraft**: bee spirits create poison, tree spirits are also available, and imperfect ghost spirits can extract incomplete information from a victim's soul after death. This limitation keeps South Flame from killing Luo immediately.
- Neos identifies three anomalies: Zheng's deliberate burst-power display, his inexplicable visit to Luosi without killing or recruiting her, and Luo's excessively easy capture. Despite sensing a trap, he refuses retreat and assigns Xue Ling'er to judge the truth of Luo's eventual answers psychically.
- Final operation assignments: Wang Xia and Bawang each seize a warship with loyal soldiers and may fire first on any vessel lacking the identification code; Chu, Zero, and Zhan Lan rescue Luo; Zheng intercepts South Flame members aboard the flagship, sparing incapacitated enemies so kill rewards can be distributed across Team China.
- Senior command arrives with over 80 warships, 100,000 recruits, ammunition, and supplies. Zheng is promoted to colonel; Chu, Bawang, and Wang Xia become lieutenant colonels, with a live Earth-wide ceremony scheduled for the next day.
- Luo receives Chu's repeating Maya-script message through a television ticker: reveal everyone except Zhan Lan, describe the last hidden member only as a noncombatant female psychic, and retain her name and location as insurance for safety.
- Luo truthfully identifies Zero and his Gauss sniper rifle, then lists Zhan Lan's Mind Lash, Psychic Scan, Mind Chain, several-times-human physique, and unlocked status without naming her. Xue Ling'er verifies each statement as true; Neos then asks who instructed Luo and when.
- Luo defeats psychic lie detection by conflating when Chu taught everyone the Maya script with when the television displayed his specific orders. Every statement is true but incomplete, sending Neos down the false branch of interpreting the orders as a generic capture contingency.
- New South Flame member: **Milia** (`米里亚`), assigned with Xue Ling'er to guard Luo in Gundanium Alloy restraints. Richard and Hofel deploy to the flagship; Neos and Aya take two information-network ships; Xue links them all by Mind Chain.
- Neos's broadcast names Zheng, Chu, Bawang, Wang Xia, and Zero as parasite-controlled flesh-slaves and authorizes immediate arrest, cranial surgery, and lethal force upon resistance. Chu hijacks the fleetwide transmission before Neos finishes.
- Chu reframes Luosi's parasite theory around telepathy. Surveillance shows Richard apparently communicating without speech, while timestamped recordings show Neos and Aya delivering identical words, movements, and tones simultaneously through Mind Chain; this becomes public proof that six South Flame members are infected.
- Chu's loyal squad leaders are publicly shown disarmed beside senior command, supporting his accusation that infected officers seized the generals. He calls upon the summit veterans to fight out of the conspiracy as they fought through the Arachnid sea.
- Neos finally reconstructs the entire trap: Zheng's intimidation forced psychic screening; Richard's visit supplied surveillance evidence; Luo's truth omitted the delivery method; and Mind Chain enabled synchronized incriminating broadcasts. The shock causes Neos to vomit blood and die in his chair.
- Two seized warships open fire on senior-command reinforcements; one target evades, while another takes a missile through its port wing. Chu orders every vessel to transmit unconditional surrender, treats silence as parasite control, isolates flagship officers, and forbids any ship from returning to Earth.
- New volume marker: **Volume IV: Starship Troopers (III)** (`第四集 铁血星河（三）`). More than 80% of veteran NCOs accept Chu's orders; Zhan Lan restores Team China's Psychic Scan and Mind Chain while using Mind Lash to suppress South Flame's psychic.
- Sinking a small warship gives every relevant Team China member 1,000 points; Chu expects a capital warship to yield at least twice as much. Bawang makes the first confirmed warship kill with a missile.
- Fifteen of the twenty survival days have elapsed, leaving under five. Chu predicts spaceborne Arachnids will transport the planet-bound rival team toward the second campaign at Tango Urilla and attack the divided Federation fleet during its mutiny.
- Roughly one-quarter of the flagship is initially in contact with Zheng's loyalists. Standard ballistic armor stops submachine-gun fire but fails against sniper rifles or two to three hits on one point; Zheng requests two intact suits to breach overlapping fire outside the central conference room.
- Federation ballistic armor is light and self-sizing but weak. Zheng uses two suits as shields to cross a 20-meter corridor, then subdues roughly a dozen soldiers and officers without killing them to preserve confidence in his humanity.
- Richard and Hofel have killed the flagship's defenders, reporters, crew, and several senior officers. A field officer yields 300 points and a general 1,000; Hofel claims over 5,000–6,000 points from one attack.
- Richard has genetic-lock stage one and offers his life—and to neutralize Hofel—in exchange for sparing Xue Ling'er. He describes her as an innocent dying child sold into exploitation by parents with AIDS before entering reincarnation, then invokes Berserker when he believes Zheng refuses.
- Hofel has three-to-five-times-human attributes, genetic-lock stage one, and the **Rumble-Rumble Fruit** (`响雷果实`), not yet at its final double-A enhancement. He controls electricity and can absorb a warship's energy but cannot become lightning; overabsorption will make him explode.
- Richard's giant double-bladed magical axe is over twice Tiger Soul's size and has an ice attribute. **Berserker** floods him with golden light, painfully convulses his muscles, removes apparent pain and restraint, and sacrifices his body for power slightly above Explosion but far below Destruction; injury and blood loss further increase his speed and strength.
- Tiger Soul's invisible saber aura severs Hofel's arm despite a clean dodge and later opens Richard's chest and lung. Richard lands dozens of axe blows within seconds, drives Zheng through an alloy wall, freezes his wounded arm and saber, and forces Zheng to activate Explosion.
- Seven or eight ships initially fight amid spreading fleet chaos. Team China has received notices only for destroying two warships; Chu delays South Flame deaths because repeated loss notices could prompt the survivors to kill hostage Luo.
- Aya's spirit bees are one to two times normal bee size, approximately two fingers thick, nearly transparent before attack, and formed purely from psychic energy. Stings cause fever and unconsciousness; multiple stings can kill. Zhan Lan's Mind Lash disperses them effectively.
- After Neos's death, a young South Flame woman negotiates. She offers Luo plus one unlocked and one ordinary teammate, with both neutralized as threats. Chu rejects all leverage, has Bawang and Wang Xia strike the vessel non-fatally, and demands unconditional surrender within five minutes.
- Aya can sacrifice her life to place a curse expected to kill one target before return. She, Xue Ling'er, and an unconscious Black man remain aboard; Richard and Hofel are on the flagship. Zero's intervention persuades Chu to preserve Luo.
- Surrender terms: Xue Ling'er enters Team China's protection as guarantee; surviving South Flame members must fight Forest Team; Luo and Xue emerge within 24 hours; the remaining spared flagship member returns to the vessel. Aya accepts, hoping to resurrect Neos through *The Mummy*.
- Chu admits Luo was deliberately tested: estimated 50% death risk, 55% chance of seeing the televised code, and over 90% chance Zheng would stop the plan if warned. Luo passes the survival and ability tests through will, intelligence, strength, and luck; Chu now recognizes him as Team China.
- Charged mithril rings greatly extend Zheng's Explosion duration while preserving internal and vampire energy for Destruction. Berserker grants Richard stage-two-equivalent reactions and combat intuition despite much lower base attributes, but its bodily burden exceeds Explosion and would kill him within roughly one minute.
- In dozens of seconds, Tiger Soul removes half Richard's palm and two fingers while both fighters accumulate wounds; its near-ten-meter aura can split the corridor. Berserker amplifies Richard as injury worsens, letting him forcibly reset a dislocated elbow and drive an axe crater roughly one meter wide into alloy.
- Hofel returns after absorbing ship energy, fully wreathed in lightning with spiked hair and scorching footsteps. Zheng severs both legs with saber aura, knocks him unconscious, then activates Destruction against Richard.
- Richard survives Destruction and enters emergency treatment. Team China and five surviving/condemned South Flame members regroup; Bawang and Wang Xia have destroyed three warships, producing 3,000 points for each Team China member.
- Space Arachnids approach from three directions at a minimum five-to-one numerical advantage, leaving the Tango Urilla direction open as a deliberate escape route. The Federation fleet forms around the flagship and plans a coordinated planetary landing.
- The common space form is a legless, crystalline centipede roughly 20 meters long. Behind it travels a bronze-crystalline fleshy sphere the size of a small moon, propelled by blue plasma streams from upright organic tubes. Chu classifies the swarm as a single **superorganism** whose physical evolution surpasses Federation technology.
- Fortified Federation bases exist on Tango Urilla. Chu expects the remaining travel, landing, and combat to leave Team China 24–48 hours on the planet, during which the true mission will force contact with Forest Team.
- Chu calculates a direct fleet assault on the moon-sized sphere at 20% success, potentially rewarding an A or double-A side plot. A Freedom Gundam or prototype Gundam could decisively affect space combat, but Zheng vetoes the gamble.
- Tango Urilla has seven major bases on one continent, each able to support 50,000 soldiers; total available troops with the fleet exceed 150,000. Ten small fast ships remain as rear guard while the fleet advances.
- Zhan Lan and Wang Xia execute the condemned South Flame members so the psychic and explosives specialist receive the kill rewards. The source says Team China obtains two rune stones, then calls all three stones unused; preserve this unresolved count discrepancy. Zheng holds them by team custom.
- Space bugs fire slow plasma spheres 2–3 meters wide. The first several thousand miss, but a combined second volley of roughly 100,000—including tens of thousands from the giant sphere—annihilates all ten rear-guard ships.
- Chu observes that the plasma spheres lack a mucus coating, orders the full fleet to descend, and assigns ships X3–X7 beneath the formation for barrage fire when the bugs enter range.
- Uncoated plasma spheres disperse through atmospheric friction. Ground plasma bugs use mucus coatings to preserve their shots, but space temperatures prevent the space caste from doing so; once landed, the fleet need face only ground Arachnids and Forest Team.
- Tango Urilla's seven fortresses have unjammable high-powered particle-beam communicators. The fleet centralizes around **Storm Fortress**, using grounded warships as layered defenses while warning Earth and drawing the Arachnids away from it.
- True mission: protect seven human bases for three days. Each base lost gives every team member −1,000 points; each held gives +1,000 after three days; losing all seven erases the entire team. Had they fled into deep space, Chu estimates over 70% probability of being unable to return and suffering erasure.
- Landing organization: one sergeant major per squad, one lieutenant per ten squads, one field officer per ten lieutenant-led units. Richard and Aya command Storm Fortress.
- Chu offers Zheng two strategies: defend/support all seven bases for maximum survival reward, or **Operation: One of Seven**, abandon six while Storm Fortress draws the swarm, and use Team China to hunt Forest Team plus the Brain Bug side plot.
- Ground **laser bugs** fire mucus-coated plasma through atmosphere. The fleet flies about 300 meters above the plain and avoids them while moving to Storm Fortress. Reinforcements include 100 prototype armored vehicles, each armed mainly with one rotary autocannon.
- Storm Fortress rises 50 meters above the plain and uses X-metal capable of resisting miniature nuclear missiles, but Arachnids can build corpse ramps over it. Forty-seven grounded warships form concentric defensive layers, with upper-deck fire and rooftop transports for fallback.
- Stable plan: defend three days with over 70% probability of no Team China deaths. Dangerous plan: defend two days and nights, then use the final 12 hours to annihilate Forest Team and kill or capture the Brain Bug, accepting possible team annihilation for resurrection resources and power against Devil Team.
- Zheng chooses the dangerous path because repeated helplessness—including his clone's trauma—convinces him that the team must gain enough power to protect themselves and those they love.
- Team China's seven members preserve all strength while other bases fall; under Chu, Storm Fortress loses only a dozen-plus of its 47 warships in one day and night, leaving over 20 plus the fortress. They deploy with Xue Ling'er when 12 hours remain.
- Dual-screen deception: Zhan Lan screens Team China while Xue screens Aya and Richard, creating two moving blind zones to confuse Forest Team. The strike force totals eight people aboard two Green Goblin gliders with passenger baskets.
- After two days and nights, at least one million Arachnids lie dead outside Storm Fortress. Octopus-like transport bugs descend from space; ground, flying, fire-spitting, tank, and laser castes converge from underground and afar.
- With the swarm concentrated upon Storm Fortress, Bawang, Wang Xia, and Zero begin unrestricted point farming using the tubular pulse accelerator, plasma bombs, and Gauss sniper rifle.
- Chu calls Neos the limit of ordinary human intelligence and describes Richard as South Flame's matching warrior. He defines himself as Team China's brain and Zheng as its heart, and declares that Team China will surpass both Devil Team and Celestial Team.
- A silver ranged strike destroys one Green Goblin glider without either psychic detecting an attacker. Zheng uses the glider's plasma modification and second-stage genetic lock to rescue Chu, Bawang, and Zero; all eight people plus Luo's cartoon-bear robot land aboard the remaining glider.
- The southeastern terrain has comparatively few Arachnids, but an enormous fire-spitter and masses of ground bugs emerge from underground over two kilometers away. Chu revises his estimate: Forest Team may be weak overall while one or two veterans grow powerful by treating newcomers as slaves and consuming their points, allowing the Lord God to retain a low team rating.
- Forest Team veterans **Rost** (`罗斯特`) and **Niga Yabin** (`尼迦亚宾`) travel with two cowering brown-skinned newcomers they call “meat pigs.” Niga Yabin wields an enormous silver metal bow and fired the earlier silver shot; he says he is ineffective in close combat. Rost smokes a cigar and intends to redeem the double-A ability **Mirror Materialization** (`镜像实体化`).
- Rost and Niga Yabin enforce a one-item-per-newcomer redemption arrangement, kill newcomers rather than let them self-enhance, and discuss resurrecting a woman Rost abused. Niga Yabin can connect information to the Brain Bug and uses Mind Chain, indicating coordination with both a psychic and the Arachnids.
- Team China counters the enemy's surface trap by entering an Arachnid tunnel. Xue Ling'er screens the group and scans within ten kilometers. They descend more than 50 meters while several thousand flyers approach; Wang Xia plants three timed plasma mines to collapse the route behind them. Both psychics then lose all sense of nearby Arachnids, as if the bugs themselves have been screened.
- Chu infers Forest Team's psychic may create an undetectable wide-area screen around Team China while possibly scanning inside it. The screen hides all Arachnid distributions but not the physical tunnel terrain; Team China therefore restricts all conversation to Mind Chain.
- Every member except Zheng faces a greater-than-50% estimated death probability. Team China divides: Zheng and Zero form a two-person elite unit intended to capture and interrogate a Forest veteran or psychic, while the larger group acts as bait. Forest Team mistakes the pair for a weak detachment seeking surface reinforcements and chooses to pursue them first.
- The main group's secondary objective is to appear helpless after encirclement and lure out the brain-eating Brain Bug, with Chu estimating over 70% appearance probability. Failure modes include death to the swarm, the Brain Bug never appearing, or a member being killed when it extracts their brain.
- Chu estimates over 90% probability that livestock-team veterans lack self-created abilities. His model: self-created abilities deliver 100% of their power, while Lord God redemptions are limited by genetic-lock development; every isolated strength also has a fatal environmental or tactical weakness.
- Zero retains both **microfusion Gauss rounds**, each capable of flattening everything within several hundred meters and exceeding a miniature nuclear warhead in destructive power. Zheng expects to escape such a blast with Destruction if Zero must fire one against Forest Team.
- Niga Yabin's silver bow attack annihilates matter rather than burning or shattering it. He and Rost carry rechargeable double-B universal defensive cultivation artifacts; Niga Yabin's manifests as a golden ancient-bronze-bell barrier and completely stops an ordinary Gauss sniper round. They claim it also blocks high technology, legendary magical weapons, and Tiger Soul.
- Rost and Niga Yabin wager on separate kills: regroup in five minutes, with the loser paying the winner 1,000 points in physical enhancements for every minute late. Rost pursues Zheng without Psychic Scan support and still exceeds Zheng's lightness-art speed; Niga Yabin pursues Zero.
- Rost is Forest Team's captain, has unlocked genetic-lock stage two, and was copied into Devil Team long ago. His invisible A-rank **Sword of Victory and Oath** (`胜利与誓约之剑`), also called the **Sword in the Stone** (`石中剑`), is an incomplete double-A legendary magical weapon and can shatter Tiger Soul's saber aura in a direct clash.
- Niga Yabin threatens **Silver Flame** (`银火`) and uses a silvery coin with his bow to transform one shot into thousands or tens of thousands of silver rays covering roughly 100 meters. The barrage is slower than a single arrow; Zero evades it by unlocking the genetic lock for the first time.
- Zero fires one of his two microfusion Gauss rounds from several hundred meters. Niga Yabin's bronze-bell barrier protects his body from the explosion, but not the surrounding terrain: the blast throws him dozens of meters into the air and buries him at least several meters underground. Zero retreats to endure his first genetic-lock backlash and intends to resume fighting if Niga Yabin survives.
- Under Explosion, Zheng lands at least 100 Tiger Soul blows on Rost in under ten seconds, visibly dimming the double-B bronze-bell barrier. Rost's protection prevents direct wounds but not shock from repeated impacts and ground slams.
- Rost's mirror technique creates several identical bodies or afterimages. In genetic-lock stage two he uses **Mirror: Dimensional Slash** (`镜像，次元斩`), suspending Zheng more than one meter above ground while space fractures like glass within a one-meter region. The first fracture cleanly severs fingers from Zheng's left hand.
- A shallow wound from the invisible Sword in the Stone reduces Zheng's internal and vampire energy by roughly 10%, suggesting the weapon absorbs or annihilates energy; this also explains its ability to break Tiger Soul's saber aura. Zheng enters Destruction to escape the dimensional fracture.
- Rost's full **EXCALIBUR** attack reveals the Sword of Victory and Oath and releases a laser-like curtain that planes uneven ground mirror-flat. His mirror binding immobilizes roughly ten cubic meters of space and makes him bleed from the nose and mouth.
- Zheng's present body can sustain Destruction for about ten seconds. After spending one second earlier, he uses Moonwalk, repeated Tempest Kicks, Fist Gun, and Tiger Soul to split Rost's bronze-bell barrier and sever all four limbs within another 6–7 seconds. The broken bell is permanently ruined; Excalibur is covenant-bound so that no one but Rost can use it.
- Zheng stores Excalibur in the Na ring but suffers ruptured capillaries and muscle damage on leaving Destruction. Even after drawing energy from his charged mithril ring and beginning to heal, his normal-state combat power is reduced to roughly 30%.
- Rost identifies Zheng's survival from Devil Team as the work of the Resurrection Cross. He learns that clone Zheng killed Devil Team's former white faction and became its captain; the revelation drives him from defiance into despair.
- Zero continues evading Niga Yabin's silver volleys after his first genetic-lock backlash begins and answers every attack with an ordinary Gauss round, progressively exhausting the double-B bell. Zheng arrives under Explosion, shatters the second barrier with Tiger Soul after Zero's final shot nearly penetrates it, and severs all four of Niga Yabin's limbs. Rost and Niga Yabin remain alive as captives.
- Both Forest weapons are owner-bonded; the destroyed bells cannot be recovered, and Forest Team's other items are held in a storage belt that Team China cannot yet access. Chu asks that both captives remain alive temporarily.
- New Arachnid caste: an extremely durable **black ground caste** repeatedly forces Chu's group far back through tunnels before dying. Chu gives Zheng 5–10 minutes to reach them and predicts one team member will die for every minute beyond that window.
- Rost and Niga Yabin can move through ordinary Arachnids without being attacked. This protection ends within 300 meters of the Brain Bug or upon any action that may threaten it. Forest Team entered with five newcomers, several of whom died before the veterans learned this rule. The Brain Bug's honor guard moving against Team China indicates it intends to extract their brains.
- Chu hypothesizes that killing the Brain Bug may remove planetary coordination and make Arachnids attack one another. Plan: Zheng drives it toward Zero's open firing ground, Zero kills it with his remaining microfusion Gauss round, Luo uses the cartoon-bear robot's **electric-jet system** to extract Zero, and the team collapses both tunnel approaches while the swarm fights itself.
- Luo accepts the lethal rescue assignment in exchange for ending all prior grievances and recognition as an ordinary Team China member. Zheng agrees; successful rescue also grants Luo one of the two unlocked Forest captives as a kill reward.
- Wang Xia loses one arm and Bawang suffers a severe abdominal wound while holding the narrow passage. Brain Bug honor guards have blue-black, black-metal-like chitin that neither Tiger Soul's blade nor its saber aura can sever; Bawang's tubular pulse accelerator also requires sustained fire to kill one.
- Zero loads his final microfusion Gauss round and waits among ordinary Arachnids with Luo and captive Niga Yabin. He tells Luo that he fears losing companions or a future—an excuse to keep living—more than death.
- The Brain Bug's entire one-kilometer perimeter is occupied by black honor guards. Zheng must cross three units of 3–4 guards while still over 10 kilometers from the target. Each unit is intelligent enough to remain still among restless warriors; honor guards also possess small wings and can leap to within roughly two meters of Zheng's 5–6-meter airborne height.
- Zheng has less than five seconds of Destruction remaining, with only 2–3 seconds considered safe before post-state helplessness. More than half his dual-energy reserves have recovered through natural regeneration and energy stored in two rings, permitting short uses of Explosion.
- Honor-guard weak points are the eyes and flexible joints. Zheng tears off one pair of pincers, kills a guard through its eye and neural center, stores both the pincers and corpse in the dimensional bag, and receives 1,000 points for the kill. He notes that even Gundanium Alloy cannot match the black chitin's resistance to Tiger Soul's aura.
- Several thousand honor guards surround the Brain Bug, which is over four meters tall and moves atop many small cockroach-like creatures. The target halts suspiciously at the plaza entrance; Zheng uses Moonwalk to accelerate in midair past the final blocking unit.
- **Instantaneous Destruction** works by placing two charged mithril rings on one finger so internal and vampire energy emerge together, collide while moving toward the heart, and vanish after only the consciously metered instant. It permits one Shave, Moonwalk, Tempest Kick, or similar action with reduced burden, but injures the finger-to-heart meridian and cannot be repeated freely.
- Zheng uses roughly one second's equivalent of Instantaneous Destruction plus about two seconds of full Destruction, leaving only 1–2 seconds. Tempest Kick crosses several hundred meters in one second, launches more than ten oversized honor guards into the Brain Bug, and drives it into Zero's line of fire.
- Zero's final microfusion Gauss round hits the center of the Brain Bug's head as half its body exits the tunnel. The resulting flash engulfs the tunnel and a 200-meter area; the outcome is confirmed in the next part.
- Killing the Brain Bug awards one B-rank side story with no stated points or side-mission completion notice. Chu's hypothesis is confirmed: both ground and flying Arachnids briefly freeze, then attack any nearby bug indiscriminately.
- The microfusion blast leaves a crater 10 meters deep and over 30 meters across. Luo's electric-jet cartoon bear extracts Zero and both limbless Forest captives; Zheng uses repeated Instantaneous Destruction techniques, Shave, and Moonwalk to reach them and collapses nearly 10 meters of tunnel behind the group.
- Return remains 2 hours 27 minutes away. Bawang's tubular pulse accelerator overheats after hours of continuous infinite-ammunition fire; its barrel explodes and a metal fragment punctures a major artery near his heart. Hemostatic spray stops the bleeding, but massive blood loss leaves him near death. Zheng discovers that internal energy can temporarily warm him and substitute for lost blood function, then requests the energy-storage components from Wang Xia's plasma bombs.
- Each plasma-grenade internal-energy reservoir holds roughly 20% of Zheng's Hunyuan Yi Qi Art capacity, enough for almost two seconds of Destruction. Wang Xia dismantles every plasma grenade, mine, and timed bomb, tearing back all ten fingernails to extract the reservoirs quickly.
- With 7 minutes 42 seconds remaining and Bawang facing death within 20 seconds without more energy, Zheng tears the remaining reservoir from Luo's cartoon-bear robot. He assigns one Forest captive kill to Luo and the other to Chu, while explicitly demanding that Chu spare Xue Ling'er.
- Bawang survives to return to the Lord God's Dimension. His recreated woman is present below his healing sphere, confirming his continued survival at the part's end.
- New volume marker: **Volume V: Surprise Attack on Resident Evil (I)** (`第五集 偷袭生化（一）`). Bawang's physical trauma heals before Zero's and Luo's genetic instability; all Team China members survive the scenario.
- Chu memorizes and reproduces complete Federation miniature-nuclear-warhead schematics. Specialized fabrication equipment for the detonator and energy reservoir costs an estimated 7,000–8,000 points; working alone would require 50–60 days. He proposes later returning to *Starship Troopers* to test how identities/home-team status persist and to acquire warheads plus a small space warship; a sufficiently large storage bracelet costs a double-B side story.
- Zheng's total reward is one each of D-, C-, and B-rank side stories plus over 12,000 points. The two killed Forest veterans possessed hidden Resurrection Crosses and returned upon death, so their killers received individual rewards but Team China gained no +2 battle score, costing each member roughly 4,000 expected points.
- Zheng's stated accounting: 2,000 points+C for one non-unlocked South Flame member; 1,000+D for a tank bug; B for the Brain Bug; 3,000 points from warships; net −5,000 from preserving one of seven bases; and roughly 4,000 from ordinary/honor-guard Arachnid kills.
- Rost and Niga Yabin concealed Resurrection Crosses internally and coordinated by Mind Chain plus undetectable screening. Rost's fearless behavior and Niga Yabin's terror/storage-belt disclosure were a deception to divert searches from their bodies; both survive in their own Lord God's Dimension and may seek revenge.
- Zero receives two B-rank side stories and no points for killing the Brain Bug; Chu concludes that *Starship Troopers* monsters and human machinery do not create formal side-plot missions, only target-specific rewards. Zhan Lan's unlocked-member kill should fund 1–2 strong new psychic abilities.
- Recovered spoils: Niga Yabin's silver bow, Rost's transparent two-handed Excalibur, two broken double-B bell artifacts, pincer/shell pieces, and one intact honor-guard carapace. The weapons are covenant-bound. Chu prioritizes analyzing the carapace for close-fitting flexible armor resistant to legendary magical attacks, potentially including Tiger Soul's aura.
- Zero holds over 7,000 points plus two B ranks; Wang Xia holds over 10,000 points plus one C and one B. Zheng chooses to seek saber techniques capable of drawing out Tiger Soul's power rather than another raw enhancement.
- Team China currently has seven members. Chu and Luo each hold over 13,000 points and two B ranks; Zhan Lan trails them by a little over 1,000 points. Planned resurrections are Zhao Yingkong, Zhang Heng, and Cheng Xiao, initially funded by 4,000 points from each of six members other than Zero plus one B rank each from Chu, Zhan Lan, and Luo.
- Each member plans to buy ten days in *The Mummy* for 500 points. A first-time entrant must additionally pay one D rank. Because ordinary transfers are limited to one per giver per scenario, Luo instead agrees to spend one B rank and 8,000 points resurrecting one person alone.
- Luo plans to redeem a three-mode **Robotech craft** (`太空堡垒三用型机体`) for one B rank plus 5,000 points; Zheng agrees to purchase its robot-scale machine gun and air-launched missiles afterward. Chu deliberately makes Luo invest heavily in resurrection so responsibility and sunk effort bind him more closely to Team China.
- Team China returns to *The Mummy* as WWII begins. The Pharaoh's Guardians, strengthened by Zheng's gold, receive intelligence from Europe three times daily, negotiate for armored equipment, and prepare to reclaim Egypt. Their bearded chief is in America pursuing arms purchases.
- O'Connell and Evelyn live in America; Jonathan is in China and involved in a Shanghai affair concerning a lightning-emitting Buddha head; Imhotep still searches for the Holy Grail but has discovered several gold mines managed by O'Connell.
- The Guardians remove or bury Hamunaptra's visible columns and statues, concealing the ruins under sand and ordinary stone so aerial observers and wartime bombing will not threaten the resurrection altar. A nearby military camp is staffed by guards who recognize Team China.
- Zheng alone conducts the three resurrections because the ritual exposes each subject's experiences and inner life; the others transfer the required rewards to him at the altar so their privacy remains limited to the captain.
- Zhao Yingkong's final memory: while fighting a handsome, smiling but cold-eyed man at near-Destruction speed, she pursues him around an alley corner and is decapitated by silver wire. Her childhood involved brutal assassin conditioning from age 2–3, survival competition at 5–6, and trusted companions later killed by the older-brother figure she once loved. She returns with her rage buried but intact.
- Zhang Heng's final memory: he and a winged woman deliberately exchange nonlethal arrows; his ancient bow is called **Shooting Sirius** (`射天狼`). His arrow passes her and penetrates a distant building/person, while hers destroys his heart. His childhood memories confirm maternal death in a car crash, his archer father's permanently shattered arm and subsequent abuse, and his later flight from the gang threatening his reunited childhood sweetheart.
- Cheng Xiao's final memory shows him wielding the Zaku Heat Hawk and protecting a girl from multiple attackers while unlocked, even after an impassive dual-pistol fighter breaks his arm and aims at his forehead. Cheng comes from intertwined martial-arts and military families and endured medicinal baths plus staff conditioning from childhood.
- Cheng's dissolute persona began after a landslide killed the plain village girl he intended to marry, breaking his vow to protect her while he lived. Beneath the persona, he retains a soldier's resolve. After resurrection, Zheng directs him to choose a battlefield-surgery/life-support method and offers team funding to replace the lost Immortal-Energy Golden Needles.
- Zhang Heng refuses immediate resurrection of Ming Yanwei, asking for time to gain the strength to protect her and the courage to ask for forgiveness or death. Team China's next major objective is the original T-virus prototype in *Resident Evil*, intended to grant every member first-stage genetic-lock access and possibly trigger a side plot.
- Team China spends the remaining ten days in *The Mummy* safely. Zheng asks the curator to preserve Imhotep's correspondence or keep him in Cairo near the agreed decision date; possible future side plots include the lightning/laser-emitting Buddha head and the Holy Grail.
- Luo redeems a silver three-mode Robotech machine over four meters tall with humanoid, fighter, and hybrid-glider forms, a sealed space-capable cockpit, and only 15 minutes of electric endurance. Chu agrees to add internal-energy storage and energy-to-electricity conversion, requiring all of Luo's remaining points in rare metals including mithril.
- Zhan Lan redeems double-C **Thought Control** (`思维控制`), which affects weak-willed, low-psychic, non-unlocked ordinary targets and advances into double-B **Mind Manipulation** (`操纵思想`) capable of controlling any being with lower psychic power. Her initial ranking is Zheng/Luo, then Chu, Zhao Yingkong, Zero, Wang Xia, Bawang, and Zhang Heng weakest.
- Bawang plans the C-rank werewolf bloodline, leaving two C ranks, and needs a replacement for the destroyed tubular pulse accelerator. Zheng recommends an unlimited-ammunition **shoulder-mounted miniature rapid-fire cannon** (`肩抗式小型连射炮`) to avoid another barrel failure.
- Bawang's C-rank werewolf form approaches three meters in height and emphasizes raw physique rather than a special energy. His paired shoulder cannons weigh over 80 jin (40+ kg) before ammunition, fire up to 15 antitank-missile-equivalent shells per minute beyond 14 km, accept miniature nuclear warheads, and require a rear loader for specialized ammunition. He also redeems an unlimited-ammunition heavy machine gun for short/medium range.
- Wang Xia redeems C-rank **Bomb Dominator** (`炸弹支配者`), gaining demonic energy that materializes imagined bomb life-forms. Its progression is C/B/double-B/A/double-A; the highest level reportedly produces miniature-nuclear-scale beings. At C rank he can only create weak, old-fashioned mining-dynamite bundles; plasma bombs remain his interim primary weapon.
- Zheng directs Zero to retain the Gauss sniper rifle and redeem the B-rank **Point-Line Mystic Eyes** (`点线魔眼`). Mechanics follow in the next part.
- **Point-Line Mystic Eyes** reveal structural points/lines for 0.5 seconds, require an unlocked state and 20-minute cooldown, can find weak points even in defensive barriers, and risk brain death under excess use. Progression: B-rank Point-Line Mystic Eyes → A-rank **Square Mystic Eyes** (`方正魔眼`) → double-A **Mystic Eyes of Death Perception** (`直死魔眼`). Zero completes the B-rank enhancement.
- Chu holds over 9,000 points plus one B rank. He contributes the B toward ten D-rank *Resident Evil* entry fees (one B plus one D total), plans Luo's power conversion within five days, another pair of charged mithril rings for Zheng, weapons for the resurrected three, and a team training battle. Zheng identifies A-rank **Lambda Driver Body Configuration** (`λ-drive身体形态`) as a future willpower-to-attack enhancement for Chu.
- Zheng finds **One Kill in Ten Steps** (`十步一杀`), rating 197, suited to extreme speed/reflexes and therefore Explosion/Destruction, but it costs two A ranks plus 10,000 points. He postpones saber training.
- Zhao Yingkong receives Rost's transparent **Sword of Victory and Oath / Excalibur**. She cannot use its covenant-bound active ability, but the blade remains invisible and retains its energy-annihilation property.
- The three resurrected members receive the dragon-blood enhancement. Cheng Xiao replaces his lost needles with ordinary-point surgical equipment, poisonous creatures, insects, and herbs. His studied **beneficial Gu** can reduce bleeding, preserve severed limbs for reattachment, and accelerate recovery; he claims not to have studied lethal Gu. Wang Xia confirms that Cheng is an exceptional field medic whose medical value exceeds his combat value.
- Forest Team's silver bow cannot produce its former energy arrows, while Excalibur's active power remains unavailable. The two broken double-B defensive artifacts cannot be repaired by the Lord God, which replenishes item energy but does not restore physical damage.
- Chu assesses at least a 50% probability that the superior team in the coming *Lord of the Rings* team battle will be Devil Team or Celestial Team. Because no last-entry notice was given, Team China expects an earlier entry and a stronger opponent. Except for Zheng, the current members have no remaining resurrection chance.
- Chu manufactures two more charged mithril rings, bringing Zheng's total to four. They do not extend **Destruction**, whose limit is his physical resilience, but their larger energy reserve substantially extends **Explosion**. After several days of team sparring and Luo Gandao's robot modifications, all ten members return to *Resident Evil* for twenty days.
- The team enters an ordinary city alley outside active scenario conditions. Chu considers fifteen days ideal despite their twenty-day purchase. Their objective is to infiltrate Umbrella's headquarters, obtain the original T-virus, return to the alley without casualties, and escape pursuit that may escalate to a nuclear strike; Chu rates it at least a **team B-rank side plot**.
- About ten days (±5) have passed since *Resident Evil 2*; Umbrella disguises Raccoon City's nuclear destruction as a meteorite impact. Umbrella's public headquarters is a city-center skyscraper about thirty minutes away by car, with a freely accessible outer tower and a vast restricted research complex behind it.
- Lock **network extractor**: a palm-sized, 500-point future device installed on Umbrella's internal network. It requires 10–20 minutes; full green lights mean successful extraction, yellow means no password, and red means detection. Chu cannot remotely defeat the near-national-nuclear-network-grade firewall using hotel hardware.
- Luo Gandao's Robotech machine uses **EOT extraterrestrial-alloy armor**, judged only slightly softer than an Arachnid honor guard's shell, and has flight, humanoid-combat, and hybrid flying-motorcycle modes. Zheng assigns Luo to counter large groups of contemporary armored vehicles, fighters, and attack helicopters.
- Zheng infers Chu's concealed reward strategy: use the original T-virus on members of opposing teams before killing them, so each death counts as an unlocked-member kill and yields the much larger genetic-lock reward. This is an inference from Zheng's Xiao Honglu simulation, not yet a direct admission from Chu.
- Umbrella security is ordinary below floor 30, increases above it, and replaces the elevator between floors 39–40 with a bulletproof-glass passage backed by heavy weapons. Floors above 40 contain weapons laboratories and human-sized robots. Chu's covert alternative is Instantaneous Destruction plus Moonwalk outside the tower.
- Zhan Lan's current Thought Control lasts only several dozen minutes on ordinary weak-willed targets. She can inspect accessible memories, but not deeply buried or forgotten material. Using a section chief's documents and glasses, Zheng reaches floor 30 after the extractor shows full yellow lights below it.
- Umbrella covertly recognizes Zheng on surveillance as a **G-virus carrier**, a former corporate employee from the town, after a retired-mercenary guard detects the combat training hidden beneath his office-worker disguise.
- The extractor shows half green/half yellow on floor 30, then full green after Zheng uses Instantaneous Destruction and Moonwalk to enter floor 41. Higher floors study advanced weapons, special materials, and robotic combinations. Carlos is secretly downloading Umbrella leadership files on floor 57.
- New mission: **Prelude side plot for Resident Evil 3**. Rescue the awakened Alice within 20 days. Three members each receive one B-rank side story and 5,000 points (three B ranks and 15,000 total); failure or Alice's death randomly erases three team members. Learning *Resident Evil 3* plot information from plot characters costs a random 100–1,000 points per sentence.
- Carlos and the others survived the helicopter crash, but Alice died and is undergoing roughly ten days or more of restoration inside an Umbrella facility. Carlos warns that Umbrella captures anyone who knows of the Raccoon City outbreak for brainwashing or T-virus injection, and estimates that the facility contains over one hundred **Nemesis units**.
- Zheng infers that Chu deliberately kept Zhao Yingkong on computer work and sent Zheng to intercept the password so Umbrella surveillance would identify the self-declared G-virus carrier, capture him alive, and carry him into the research base as an infiltration route. At the end of part 396 this remains Zheng's inference; Chu has not yet confirmed it.
- Chu confirms the exposure was deliberate but estimated only a 50% discovery probability. Zheng will approach Umbrella as an **avenger**, allow capture as the supposed G-virus carrier, locate the original T-virus data and Alice from within, then coordinate with the outside assault team. Chu calls Zheng not a game piece but “an important companion.”
- Jill estimates Alice's bodily restoration will finish in about 15 days. Umbrella's leadership is divided over whether the G-virus can transform one organism into a superhuman within one generation; existing original-T-virus experiments show limited aging delay through cellular activation but harm weakened bodies. Believing Zheng may offer immortality or rejuvenation, the directors order him captured at any cost.
- After five days of deliberate exposure, Umbrella ambushes Zheng with tranquilizer sniper rounds and a green **high-intensity cellular-weakening serum**. The serum would cause apparent death and fatal cellular failure in ordinary people at one-tenth the dose, but keeps Zheng unconscious only a little over three hours. Umbrella removes his Na ring, fits an explosive/injection collar, and holds him behind electrified steel.
- Umbrella misidentifies Zheng's vampire/dragon-enhanced cells as another G-virus evolution. The cells exceed ordinary muscle/tissue/cellular strength by 10× or more and promise at least doubled attributes when injected, but their vitality consumes laboratory mice through cellular backlash.
- New volume marker: **Volume V: Surprise Attack on Resident Evil (II)** (`第五集 偷袭生化（二）`). Zheng uses Explosion and Destruction to escape containment, survives the collar's injection mechanism, and issues a 30-minute nonlethal ultimatum while demonstrating that Umbrella cannot safely kill him without losing its research prize.
- Umbrella board member and facility deputy director **Richard** (`李察德`) accepts Zheng's demands for status, safety, and T-virus data, recruits him as a company member, and gives him monitored luxury quarters. Zheng infers that the Lord God blocks Psychic Scan within the research facility to preserve side-plot difficulty.
- During cooperative research, Umbrella prepares to inject Zheng's cells into new human test subjects and a Licker inside a supermagnetic-alloy, multilayer-ballistic-glass chamber. Alice remains in critical restoration and unavailable to him; T-virus reports are promised that evening.
- Eight unconscious human subjects across age groups receive Zheng's cells plus catalysts and are sealed with the injected Licker; two further living subjects are selected for vivisection. Zheng postpones intervention to preserve the Alice mission but vows to kill everyone responsible and recovers the Na ring containing Tiger Soul that evening.
- The psychic screen weakens for only about five minutes each night, briefly permitting Mind Chain but still blocking Psychic Scan behind black fog. Forcing psychic energy through the screen dissipates it and risks unconsciousness. The original T-virus data, cultivation records, and samples are in the adjacent **weapons-testing base**, not Zheng's research base; together the facilities hold roughly 7,000 guards plus T-virus bio-organic weapons and possible Nemesis units.
- Chu's plan: when Alice awakens in about 14 days, Jill's group extracts her while Zheng holds Richard hostage to secure passage. After the side plot is safe, Zheng destroys the research base and kills its personnel, drawing security and bio-organic weapons away while Team China raids the weapons-testing base.
- Umbrella rumor places its true headquarters somewhere in a desert; the present complex is only the largest publicly known base. Injecting Zheng's cells raises ordinary physical attributes to at most roughly 2×—comparable to dragon blood—despite his own 10×-plus performance, frustrating and costing researchers.
- Zheng sees an eight- or nine-year-old child specimen transformed below the waist with gene-modified Licker cells and reaffirms that, whatever his altered genes or hardened behavior, his **heart remains human**. Umbrella footage shows clone Zheng casually annihilating mutated zombies and Lickers with fan-shaped black fire, bat wings, and a two-handed greatsword; his apparent speed slightly exceeds Explosion and makes Zheng doubt whether Destruction can defeat him.
- On Alice's awakening day, Zheng kills 27 personnel before reaching Richard, uses Tiger Soul's saber aura and the Dragonshard Necklace to eliminate dozens of armored guards, and forces Richard to release Alice, Jill, and Carlos through the checkpoint. After demonstrating that the necklace blocks firearms, Zheng kills Richard with Tiger Soul because the barrier protects only against high-tech weapons.
- Zheng's current full vampire-energy reserve summons **more than 1,000 Anubis warriors**, exceeding the earlier provisional 600 projection. They can run on walls/ceilings, regenerate any sand-body damage while the head survives, and overwhelm fortified firing lines. Preserve this later explicit count rather than retroactively rewriting earlier estimates.
- Umbrella unseals **Nemesis Units Three through Seven** and warns that loss of research-base power would thaw hundreds of evolved Lickers. The Anubis assault draws thousands of reserve guards away from the adjacent weapons-testing base, leaving only slightly over 100 guards plus semi-humanoid defensive machines; Chu then orders Luo Gandao to destroy its outer perimeter.
- Luo Gandao's modified machine transforms in under one second, uses twin electric-thrust streams, miniature missiles, and a roughly three-meter power saw, and carries a Dragonshard Necklace embedded in its shell. Chu rates it comparable to double-B or even A-rank combat robots, limited mainly by finite ammunition; until its power ends, it is Team China's high-tech trump card.
- The Dragonshard barrier blocks missile flames, shrapnel, and bullets but does **not** absorb transmitted kinetic shock; four miniature missiles leave Zheng vomiting blood despite no direct penetration. Four smaller-than-Matthew Nemesis units carry one antitank missile plus one autocannon each, wear alloy armor capable of resisting Tempest Kick, and fight alongside about 500 guards.
- The Book of the Dead's **stone-control spell** also affects alloy flooring, raising three-to-four-meter stairlike steel walls and breaking the central-hall firing formation. Chu's team secures a vial of viscous original T-virus plus extensive records, but the two-base self-destruct leaves seven minutes to escape.
- Zero's fourth Point-Line Mystic Eyes shot destroys a helicopter through a fatal line. In actual moving-target combat he estimates only a 1/4–1/5 success rate, cannot reliably hit moving points, and suffers blindness, bleeding from eyes/mouth/nose/ears, convulsions, and impending unconsciousness from overload.
- Two roughly seven-meter evolved Lickers with tendrils can tear up several square meters of alloy flooring; Zhao Yingkong judges that she could kill one with Excalibur only at the cost of severe injury and would then die to the other. Fleeing them, she reaches the central hall and finds Zheng standing amid at least 100 giant Licker corpses.
- Zheng saves Zhao from the two pursuing evolved Lickers, bisecting one with Instantaneous Destruction/Shave and physically tearing the other apart under Explosion. Zhao identifies his new equilibrium as a **heart for slaughter** (`杀伐之心`): the ability to kill without fear, guilt, numb brutality, or loss of self; she contrasts it with Arot's corruption by murder.
- Shining may exceed Zheng's sustained Explosion speed, but it does not increase Zhao's reaction speed and therefore risks a fatal collision. Carrying her, Zheng exits the base in roughly 50 seconds, reaches the Green Goblin gliders, and escapes before both facilities detonate into two mushroom clouds.
- Cheng Xiao performs field brain surgery on Zero in under five hours, with Zhao Yingkong assisting. His golden needles plus crushed multicolored arthropod material provide hemostasis, anesthesia, and antisepsis; a circulation-slowing Gu—later called a **five-colored spider** despite the source first depicting small centipedes—keeps Zero unconscious because fissures in major cerebral arteries exceed the field kit's capability.
- Zero's safe Point-Line Mystic Eyes activation limit remains unknown. The observed overload exceeds Explosion's burden but remains below Destruction's; the team must test a safe maximum after restoration.
- Original-T-virus data indicate that created life-forms can be converted into Nemesis units. Chu expects to manufacture **80 doses of Nemesis conversion agent** before *The Lord of the Rings* and proposes using them on Orc soldiers acquired inside the scenario, avoiding the Lord God's 20-column entrant limit and an estimated 3,000-point equipment cost per strong created Nemesis.
- Escape route: Umbrella is intentionally allowed to track the team to a forest at night, deterring attack after the helicopter losses; at 5 a.m. they enter a 30-plus-year-abandoned gold mine, traverse a geological fissure using over ten prepared oxygen cylinders, cut through roughly 100 meters of rock, and hide in the city sewer system until the twentieth evening.
- Shortly before return, Zheng sees a black sedan carrying two women, two men, and a young girl—implicitly Alice's rescued group—and promises, “We will return... Without fail.” Preserve the sighting as implication rather than naming every occupant in narration.
- Alice-rescue rewards are randomly assigned to Zhao Yingkong, Cheng Xiao, and Luo Gandao. Zheng receives 50 points per Nemesis and 25 per giant Licker, totaling 3,000 kill points. Team China's overall strength depends on coordination among sharply specialized members; losing any one materially weakens the whole.
- New support items: ten **Three Pure Ones Talismans** (`三清道符`) at 500 points each can reconnect severed limbs and restore 70% combat power within one hour unless the wound is absolutely fatal. Five **Ice-Congealing Pills** (`冰凝丹`) at 1,000 points plus one D rank each induce sleep and multiply metabolism, immunity, vitality, etc. 100× until a fatal-level injury recedes.
- Additional supplies include high-strength bandages, hemostatic spray, and D-rank golden needles for Cheng Xiao. Chu completes 80 Nemesis-conversion doses despite several failed cultures infecting him, and injects the original T-virus into every still-locked teammate; internal remodeling requires at least seven days before first-stage access.
- Chu gives Zheng original-T-virus doses for 40 people, explicitly confirming the plan to inject opposing teams before killing them for multiplied unlocked-member rewards.
- New volume marker: **Volume VI: War of the Ring (I)** (`第六集 指环战争（一）`).
- Five-team entry order: South Flame and **Northern Ice Team** (`北冰洲队`) enter before Team China; **East America Team** (`东美洲队`) enters a random location after 3 days; Celestial Team enters a random location after 10 days. Team China infers it ranks third by entry timing.
- Team-battle rewards: kill a locked opposing member for 2,000 points + C rank; kill an unlocked opposing member for 7,000 + B rank; each enemy kill is +1 and each allied death −1, with final score ×2,000 awarded to every survivor. Mission: all five teams reach the **city of Mordor** (preserve the novel's wording), then return after 30 minutes. High-tech weapons cannot affect Middle-earth natives but still affect reincarnation-team members.
- Five newcomers: high-school hacker **Hao Tian** (`昊天`); private bodyguard **Li Shi** (`李世`); corporate secretary **Xie Zhen** (`谢贞`); students **Ai Nana** (`艾娜娜`) and **Zhang Guangyan / Xiao Zhang** (`张光焰` / `小张`). Team China intends to preserve all five because deaths cost score and Nemesis conversion makes them a hidden reserve.
- Northern Ice captain **Gungnir** (`岗尼尔`) is a likable baby-faced man whose severe killing intent makes Zheng ready Instantaneous Destruction. His A-rank **Frost-Frost Fruit** (`冰霜果实`) remains below its double-A maximum; he considers himself stronger than South Flame's Rumble-Rumble Fruit user and estimates equal odds against Zheng unless the self-created ability proves excessive.
- South Flame entered the North Marshes northwest of Hobbiton 10 days earlier; Northern Ice appeared at the marsh edge 5 days later. A 50-plus-meter python immune to firearms forced cooperation. Each team lost one newcomer, leaving 43 people across the new three-team alliance.
- Stronger teams experience harder but less frequent scenarios; South Flame completed four films, including *The Mummy*, during Team China's interval after *Starship Troopers*. Alliance terms lock each team's independence: intelligence/support may be shared, but no two-team majority can command the third.
- South Flame's earlier participants all died and its captaincy was selected anew, resetting the team's completed-scenario list. After *Starship Troopers*, its artificially low rating led through *The Perfect Storm* to *The Mummy*, where Neos resurrected Aya, Richard Robias, and Xue Ling'er; the Guide then recognized Neos as the team's true captain.
- Gandalf pays the forty-three allied reincarnators one gold coin each to protect Frodo until the following evening. The Lord God's expanded escort mission awards or deducts 1,000 points per member for each designated plot character who survives or dies before the fortress of Mordor, and awards or deducts 3,000 per member for every Human-Elven Alliance victory or defeat against Mordor.
- Chu and Neos infer that the accelerating three-team then five-team battles mean someone in Celestial or Devil Team is nearing genetic-lock stage five. Their current hypothesis is that once anyone reaches it, the Lord God will place every team into one final melee and only the last surviving team will be permitted to live; preserve this as analysis, not confirmed system truth.
- Northern Ice's disclosed combat core includes two close-range fighters (spear and Druid shapeshifting), two ranged fighters (frost magic and a legendary-magic crossbow), Gungnir at either range, a psychic controller, and a novice magical healer who expends magic-stone energy.
- South Flame's disclosed core consists of Richard/Berserker and Hofel/Rumble-Rumble Fruit in close combat; Aya's psychic venomous bees and Luosi's **Spider Arrow Heart** (`蜘蛛箭心`) at range; and Manini's still-secret special attack. Spider Arrow Heart guarantees a heart strike but leaves Luosi asleep until full Lord God restoration.
- Gungnir's marsh-python **serpent pearl** is a cold-aspected energy pearl roughly equivalent to a C-rank energy stone and raises his combat power at least fivefold while available. The allies agree to pursue side plots and unknown monsters, distributing gains by each team's overall contribution, including strategy.
- South Flame's formation uses five differently aspected energy stones at fixed points plus inscribed symbols to multiply bodily and personal-energy recovery dozens of times. Northern Ice's rune pattern converts electrical energy into mental energy. Team China trades its internal-energy-storage rune combination for both bodies of knowledge.
- The recovery formation requires metal-, water-, fire-, wood-, and earth-aspected stones. Five C-rank stones sustain it roughly three days and nights; five D-rank stones sustain it only about five hours. If supplied cheaply, it could reduce Zheng's full post-Destruction recovery from hours to tens of minutes.
- Gandalf commissions the alliance to escort Frodo and **Samwise Gamgee / Sam** (`山姆·卫斯·詹吉`) to the **Prancing Pony** (`跃马旅店`) in **Bree** (`布理村`). Zhan Lan's Endurance Mantra supports the march; local **green-star grass** (`绿星草`) cools abraded feet and removes pain and fatigue.
- A Nazgûl appears in the fields several hours outside Hobbiton earlier than the film sequence predicts and immediately beheads a newcomer. This establishes that Middle-earth plot characters and enemies are substantially enhanced beyond their film portrayals.
- The enhanced Nazgûl's smoky two-handed sword is poisonous and corrosive, can regenerate after being broken, and strikes with force comparable to Zheng's Explosion. Its translucent plot-character barrier stops Zero's Gauss round, while Zhang Heng's +3 enchanted arrow and Northern Ice's green bolt can break the blade itself.
- Under dense black smoke, the Nazgûl's horse transforms into a skeletal steed with bloodred eyes, flaming hooves, and gravity-defying movement; its rider beheads Northern Ice's frost-spear user, then rides up a tree and leaps toward Frodo from above.
- Gungnir's full-power **Ice Age** (`冰霜时代`) requires crushing a C-equivalent frost pearl because his stage-two lock and incomplete Frost-Frost Fruit cannot otherwise sustain it. He creates and controls more than ten cubic meters of ice, sealing the Nazgûl and mount inside a giant hand; he claims the kill yields one D rank and 2,000 points.
- Northern Ice crossbow user **Mavis** (`玛维斯`) retrieves fallen frost-spear user **Elikya** (`艾力克亚`)'s legendary-magic spear. The spear can channel through Gungnir's ice without resistance and disperses the Nazgûl into black smoke, but its skeletal mount remains intact after the rider's armor crumbles.
- The alliance estimates major plot characters at five times film strength or higher. Technological equipment becomes inert when used directly on protected natives—the Green Goblin glider cannot lift Sam—but works normally once he steps off.
- Nazgûl are invisible to Psychic Scan, though their approach can be inferred from rapidly withering vegetation. Chu and Neos plan to lure all eight remaining riders into the passing elven procession, then send Frodo, Sam, **Merry** (`梅里`), and **Pippin** (`皮聘`) across the ferry while the teams use technology to reach Bree separately.
- Frodo briefly wears the One Ring as bait and perceives eight mounted ghosts approaching at extreme speed. Preserve the ethical cost explicitly present in Zheng's thoughts: the passing elves are innocent, but the alliance uses them because it sees no survivable alternative.
- The elven procession numbers roughly 200–300, including about 100 elite longbowmen. Its golden-haired leader invokes the ancient human-elven alliance, accepts the defense of the Ring-bearer, and uses a two-arrow **Burst Arrow** (`爆裂箭`) technique that detonates a trailing arrow against the first to multiply its speed and power.
- Zhang Heng reproduces Burst Arrow with greater speed and force, destroying one enhanced Nazgûl's head. The elven leader warns that every slain Nazgûl transfers strength to those remaining; each subsequent rider gains speed and heavier equipment.
- Escape assignments: the newcomers hide in the woods for later aircraft pickup; Chu, Zhao Yingkong, Richard, Cheng Xiao, and Northern Ice's Druid shapeshifter escort the four Hobbits to **Bucklebury Ferry** (`巴寇伯理渡口`); remaining veterans assist the elves in holding all Nazgûl behind.
- Nazgûl share linked life and power: each death transfers strength into every survivor. The final survivor would become the **true Ringwraith**, Sauron's strongest commander. Their shields and swords enlarge and harden after each transfer; during absorption they briefly stand motionless.
- Zhang Heng's single-arrow **Arrow of Wind** (`风之矢`) crosses the battlefield too fast to see or hear, enters beneath a skeletal mount, exits through the rider's neck, and disintegrates the Nazgûl in midair. The attack leaves Zhang Heng completely spent.
- Neos prevents an allied retreat by noting that loss of the One Ring would fully resurrect Sauron at Mordor, potentially creating a threat worse than the combined nine Nazgûl and making the five-team mission impossible.
- Nazgûl prioritize the individual posing the greatest immediate threat. After another strength transfer, a rider's black blade aura extends beyond three meters, shatters Gungnir's ice almost five meters deep, and the rider beheads Hofel after tearing apart both frost and lightning defenses.
- The elves can pool **forest life force** as green threads into their leader. An arrow saturated with this energy spreads living green light across a Nazgûl's shield, causes extreme pain, and forces it to retreat to Mordor to purge the effect, preventing further pursuit without killing and strengthening the remaining Ringwraiths.
- At enhanced levels, killing a **level-six Nazgûl** awards 5,000 points + two C ranks; killing a **level-seven Nazgûl** awards 5,000 points + one B rank. Zheng spends roughly five seconds in Destruction to kill both, losing two-thirds of his left arm to the first rider's final stroke and suppressing ruptured capillaries afterward with internal energy.
- Acquired plot creature: **Skeletal Nightmare** (`骷髅梦魇`), a magically created life-form capable of gravity-defying movement. It feeds on non-yang-aligned energies including vampire energy, mental energy, magic power, and cultivation essence; ownership is recognized when Zheng takes its reins.
- The Skeletal Nightmare is built from glossy black bones and armored plates engraved with rune combinations. Roughly 20% of Zheng's vampire energy activates it, producing bloodred flames/smoke; it then reaches speed between level-six and level-seven Nazgûl, makes its rider experience the surface beneath it as “down,” and conceals both mount and rider from Psychic Scan.
- During the escort fight, Northern Ice's wolf-form Druid is killed. Chu cycles the four Hobbits through wearing the One Ring for only seconds at a time to redirect the Nazgûl from Richard, while Zhao Yingkong ambushes from the shadows and ultimately uses an unnamed high-speed technique to dismember both rider and mount before Chu is struck.
- Zhao Yingkong's self-created **Shining Vacuum Wave** (`闪灵真空波`) combines Shining with a technique modeled on Zheng's Destruction-state Tempest Kick. Shining accelerates her body roughly 10× and arms roughly 50–100× without matching reaction acceleration; sweeping Excalibur turns a linear vacuum wave into a plane that dismembers the rider and mount. It requires stage-two muscle control, leaves her arms near tearing, and yields 5,000 points + two B ranks for the kill.
- The elves award three C-rank wood-aspected **life stones** after the battle; Northern Ice and South Flame divide them because of their losses. Richard's death-force-contaminated cardiac wound requires golden-needle stabilization, wood-stone healing, Zheng's internal energy, and reconstructive surgery within twelve hours.
- Team China kills five Nazgûl without losing a member; South Flame loses Hofel and nearly loses Richard, while Northern Ice loses several newcomers and two close-range specialists. The final surviving Nazgûl carries the combined power of all nine back to Mordor.
- At Bree, Richard's cardiac reconstruction plus a Three Pure Ones Talisman is expected to restore full combat ability within days. South Flame would become the alliance's absolute weakest member if he died; Northern Ice carries enough negative score from newcomer deaths to threaten several veterans with erasure.
- **Aragorn** arrives in Bree after searching between the Shire and Bree for the missing Hobbits. He identifies himself as the Ranger expected by Gandalf and offers to guide the alliance to **Rivendell**, whose life force deters Nazgûl, while warning that the final ninefold rider may be powerful enough to force entry.
- Chu infers that the Lord God will not send the ninefold Nazgûl to Bree before Aragorn or Gandalf arrives because the early-entry teams have already lost strength rather than gained an advantage, which would make the later team battle structurally unfair. Preserve this as his balance analysis.
- Aragorn identifies the Skeletal Nightmare as a lost Second-Age battle-mage creation; Sauron learned the method and made nine for the Nazgûl after losing his physical body. The mount is traditionally usable only by a battle mage, though Zheng's vampire energy also activates it.
- East America enters with 15 members on a snowbound cliff. New captain **Zhao Zhuikong** (`赵缀空`) murders the team's newly arrived dual-sword psychic and orders the other two veterans to kill all 11 newcomers, deliberately accepting a team score of −11 and planning to recover through enemy-team kills.
- East America's two other surviving veterans are an unnamed large white spearman and an unnamed Black youth with a circular blade. Both fear Zhao Zhuikong, who strictly obeys rules once he states them, seeks amusement in watching opponents mature before killing them, and became more unstable after fighting Devil Team and a stronger counterpart of himself.
- New volume marker: **Volume VI: War of the Ring (II)** (`第六集 指环战争（二）`). The alliance travels seven days under maximum Psychic Scan because East America's location is unknown.
- Rivendell retains several thousand regular troops despite the elven withdrawal from Middle-earth; Aragorn estimates at least 1,000 longbowmen can fire green arrows. Enhanced Aragorn's speed and force are comparable to a level-six or level-seven Nazgûl, and white energy channeled into his ordinary greatsword gives it power comparable to Tiger Soul's blade aura.
- **Arwen** initially mistakes Zheng's Skeletal Nightmare for a Nazgûl and uses magic to trigger a rockslide. She reports that Saruman has betrayed the alliance, imprisoned Gandalf, and joined Sauron; Gandalf has escaped and is traveling to Rivendell.
- The Council of Elrond grants each allied reincarnation team one representative because their demonstrated strength earns both honored-guest treatment and political standing. Chu refuses Team China's seat because he is privately investigating Hao Tian.
- The exposed One Ring projects intense temptation—limitless power, authority, immortality, and godhood—causing nearly everyone except Elrond, Gandalf, and the four Hobbits to rise unconsciously. Zheng's Hunyuan Yi Qi internal energy restores clarity; Gandalf's spoken Black Speech and magical pressure suppress the effect.
- Lock established names: **Elrond** (`爱隆`), **Boromir** (`波罗莫`), **Legolas** (`勒苟拉斯`), **Arathorn** (`亚拉松`), **Isildur** (`埃西择`, novel transliteration), **Gondor** (`刚择/钢择`). Use official English forms while preserving the novel's events.
- Gandalf's payment is a pouch of more than a dozen D-to-C energy stones: only two wood-aspected stones and roughly four of every other attribute. Elrond supplies every allied member an Orc-warning elven sword (at least D-rank legendary-magic quality) and lightweight mithril-reinforced chain mail.
- Celestial Team enters with six members in a desert on a distant continent east of Mordor. Named members: blond strategist **Adam** (`亚当`), blonde **Linaia** (`琳娜亚`), and brown-haired mixed-ancestry Chinese fighter **Song Tian** (`宋天`). Their priority is finding and killing the original body of a “demon” before his clone becomes overwhelmingly powerful.
- An unnamed black-haired Chinese Celestial member flies on a green-edged sword faster than a Green Goblin glider and carries a newly refined artifact: **Demon-Subduing Formation—Tushita Eight Trigrams Furnace** (`降妖伏魔阵——兜率八卦炉`).
- The reincarnators split before Caradhras. Hao Tian leads the newcomers toward a Rohan rendezvous with Northern Ice's one assigned veteran, South Flame's Manini/Luosi, and Team China's Zero, Wang Xia, Bawang, and Luo Gandao; those four are chosen because their technological weapons remain useful against opposing teams but not Middle-earth natives.
- Fourteen veterans remain with the Fellowship: four Northern Ice, four South Flame, and six Team China. Gungnir proposes harvesting Moria's mithril and **Eloges** (`艾罗格斯`) metal, plus crystalline monster cores from the lake creature and Balrog.
- Gungnir's complete Ice Age requires three frost-energy stones to unseal; he holds six and claims the full version could challenge Celestial Team. Zheng and Gungnir agree to split all Moria minerals evenly, while the killer keeps each monster's points, side stories, and energy core.
- Twelve East America corpses are found frozen on Caradhras: eleven newcomers killed by either a circular blade or spear, plus the decapitated psychic. Neos correctly infers that the team's surviving veterans intentionally killed them; because the Lord God still rated the resulting three-person team highly, he considers all three unfathomably dangerous.
- Saruman chants remotely across Caradhras and blankets several kilometers of summit and slope with meter-thick explosive lightning. Gandalf identifies him as the stronger wizard, order chief, and a lesser divine being; Gandalf's translucent barrier barely blocks direct strikes and debris, while the ascent route is completely destroyed.
- The West-gate entrance to Moria is made of **Ithildin** (`伊西尔丁`), a magical metal reflecting moonlight/starlight and responding to remembered speech. The **Doors of Durin** bear the riddle “Speak, friend, and enter”; Gungnir prevents an early solution so the lake encounter proceeds naturally.
- The lake creature outside Moria is enhanced to roughly 50 meters long. Zheng and Gungnir reaffirm that both attack on emergence, protect their allies, and let the killer keep its energy core plus automatic system rewards.
- The enhanced lake creature can levitate and fire water spikes. Gungnir freezes one quarter of its body and receives 3,000 points + one C rank plus a C-rank water-aspected energy core for the kill.
- Tiger Soul's blade aura can be reshaped by Zheng's will into a full-body invisible shell. Deployment currently takes just over one second and consumes roughly 20% internal energy that cannot be recovered before the aura dissipates in under ten minutes. It blocks Chu's Gauss-pistol burst; Chu estimates it at least doubles combat power during that window if deployment can be reduced toward 0.5 seconds.
- **Eloges metal** is roughly 1,000 times rarer than mithril and is the best known metal for holding mental imprints. Tiny quantities let doors recognize spells or weapons recognize an owner; Gandalf agrees to attempt removing the prior ownership from Zhao Yingkong and Zhang Heng's two A-rank weapons after Moria.
- Legolas calls two-arrow Burst Arrow **Double-Blade Arrow** and can fire it up to 20 times while healing with life force; his **Triple-Blade Arrow** limit is three. Zhang Heng connects the progression to the still-theoretical **Nine Arrows Shooting Down the Sun** (`九箭射日`).
- Aragorn and Boromir wield explosive **battle aura**: less enduring and non-healing compared with internal energy, but more explosive. Aragorn with battle aura loses to Zheng's Explosion but would be instantly overwhelmed by Destruction; both Aragorn and Boromir display genetic-lock stage two.
- A mapped vault beside the Bridge of Khazad-dûm contains **1 kilogram of refined mithril** and **2 ounces of Eloges**. If Zheng retrieves it, he receives one half and Northern Ice/South Flame one quarter each; both promise to protect Team China during his absence, and any Balrog fire core goes to Zheng regardless of who lands the kill.
- Zheng can extend the full-body Tiger Soul shell around the Skeletal Nightmare, turning a charge through weak enemies into a lethal cutting surface. A wide circuit through enhanced Orcs kills hundreds but consumes over half his internal energy; the Orcs have roughly 3–5× human strength, while a four-meter cave troll is estimated near 20×.
- Chu solves the high-tech restriction by having Rivendell engrave life-energy runes on Gauss-pistol magazines. Bullets carrying magical life energy affect Middle-earth natives normally, restoring his gun kata; the same principle could make Zero's Gauss sniper rifle a plot-character-killing trump card.
- The Balrog awakens before the Orc encirclement closes. Its fire sweeps dozens of square meters and instantly incinerates more than 1,000 Orcs, producing heat perceptible across the immense hall.
- The Lord God identifies Balrogs as ancient human predators once fought and exterminated by the Saints: ambient-energy absorbers reproducing once per century, viviparous with one offspring, females consuming 5× body weight daily during reproduction, preferring human flesh, and normally exhaling highly toxic smoke.
- Current Balrog is explicitly non-toxic and at 30% normal strength. Kill reward: 8,000 points + A rank; escape penalty: −3,000 points per member. Its adult blue-green fire exceeds 1,000°C; an elder's white fire ignites living things within 100 meters unless deliberately suppressed.
- Zheng successfully recovers the mapped **1 kg refined mithril ingot** and **2 oz bottled Eloges sand** before returning to confront the Balrog.
- Balrog flesh regenerates directly from flame; ordinary/mortal weapons cannot kill it, while frost can injure but cannot finish it unless its fire is exhausted. Gandalf's **Flame of Anor** manifests as silver fire beyond his barrier and temporarily absorbs the Balrog's blue-green flame.
- Combined opening: Zhao's Shining Vacuum Wave erases roughly half the Balrog's chest; Legolas's Triple-Blade Arrow and Zhang Heng's Arrow of Wind simultaneously destroy the remaining torso connections, but fire alone supports and begins rebuilding the severed body.
- Complete Ice Age consumes three C-rank frost stones, requires long preparation, lasts at most one minute, transforms Gungnir into frost, and freezes thousands of square meters within seconds except the 7–8 meters surrounding the Balrog. Gungnir cannot reenter Gandalf's one-way barrier and dies if the Balrog melts his frost form; his simplified one-stone version trades vast power for near-instant activation and longer duration.
- The Balrog's final spell is **Roar of the Fire God** (`火神咆哮`), cast through blue-green fiery runes and a pure-white pearl while its flames approach incandescent white. Zheng counters with Red Flame and six to seven seconds of Destruction, quarters the Balrog with Tiger Soul, and lets Gungnir freeze the separated pieces.
- The Lord God's reward notice confirms the Balrog's death before its fire-aspected core begins detonating and rebuilding a body. Gandalf binds the core with the Flame of Anor, orders Zheng to protect Frodo, and carries it into the abyss; the blast collapses the bridge and both adjacent cliff faces.
- In the collapse, **Pippin** and Northern Ice's unnamed crossbow user die in the lingering fire. Zheng uses the Skeletal Nightmare, Moonwalk, Instantaneous Destruction, and finally Destruction to save Aragorn, Legolas, Zhao Yingkong, unconscious Zhang Heng, and Neos; Frodo and Merry are also saved. Neos notices Zheng's hesitation but swears to repay the life debt before the scenario ends.
- The Lord God credits both Zheng and Gungnir with the Balrog kill, granting each the full 8,000 points and A-rank side story. The survivors leave Moria through its true main gate and hurry to **Lothlórien** (`罗斯洛立安`) before Orcs can raid them after dark.
- Lock established Middle-earth forms: **Galadriel** (`凯兰崔尔`), **Caras Galadhon** (`卡拉斯加拉顿`), **Thranduil** (`瑟兰督伊`), and **Dúnedain** (`登丹人`). An unnamed Lothlórien officer initially bars the Fellowship because he senses the One Ring's powerful evil, then allows them to rest pending Galadriel's judgment.
- Caras Galadhon is built through a skyscraper-sized union of great trees and illuminated by thousands of D-rank fire-aspected energy stones. The Moria treasure is divided as agreed: Team China receives half the mithril and one ounce of Eloges, while Northern Ice and South Flame each receive one quarter of the mithril and half an ounce of Eloges.
- Galadriel possesses psyche-force abilities powerful enough to probe or influence the Fellowship; the three teams' psyche-force users detect her contact and shield their own members. She perceives Gandalf's fall before Legolas explains his sacrifice.
