- Chu rejects stealing or requesting the elves' energy stones. His strategic principle is to gain **local strategic backing** (`势`) from Lothlórien and allied plot characters, whose military and political support against East America and Celestial Team is worth far more than short-term loot.
- Teams may later spend points to reenter scenarios they have completed without encountering other reincarnation teams. Chu therefore treats Lothlórien as a future trade reserve for energy stones, mithril, and other unique materials rather than loot to be seized now.
- Five days after the detached twenty-four-person group leaves Rivendell for the Gap of Rohan, Bawang and Team China's other three veterans experience brief fevers from Chu's prototype T-virus and unlock genetic-constraint stage one, with modest strength and reaction improvements.
- Hao Tian identifies himself as the “Immortal” half of the **Immortal-and-Mortal Thieves** (`神仙二人盗贼团`), a real-world pair who stole any sufficiently valuable and difficult target, including technology and people. Chu knows from National Security Bureau Section Nine records that Hao Tian is a second-generation genetically enhanced human and the only one among twenty-odd survivors without adverse modification effects.
- Chu identifies himself as the sole surviving first-generation genetically engineered human. Hao Tian's second-generation modifications target intelligence and psychokinesis; China created him for deniable theft missions and kept his sole official record in Section Nine. His partner was **Adam**, who vanished from a sealed room after clicking a mysterious “YES” prompt. Hao Tian entered while tracing him, and Chu explicitly intends to kill Adam as an intolerable danger.
- Chu supplies Zero a high-explosive Gauss sniper round engraved with life-energy runes so it can strike Saruman from beyond ten kilometers; if Saruman raises a barrier, Zero must combine the shot with the Point-Line Mystic Eyes.
- Hao Tian defeats Zero, Bawang, Wang Xia, and Luo Gandao together while allowing their weapons and forbidding himself from killing or crippling them. He then replaces Chu's reinforcement plan with a proposal to absorb Saruman's Orcs and mass-produce armored **Pursuers**: eighty doses are finished, several hundred T-virus cultures will mature within thirty days, and at least five hundred conversions should be possible. Chu predicts this exact rebellion and intends Hao Tian to use the dispute to establish authority and win the four men's loyalty.
- New volume marker: **Volume VI: War of the Ring (III)** (`第六集 指环战争（三）`).
- Galadriel attempts to read Zheng and Chu and can induce only affection, not access their thoughts; Chu is wholly unaffected and breaks the influence on Zheng by shooting an ornament from her hair. Her specialty is mental magic, and she cannot remove the ownership bonds from Zhao Yingkong and Zhang Heng's weapons.
- Lothlórien's locked trade offer: one ingot of refined steel for ten low-quality fire-aspected energy stones or five low-quality stones of another attribute, with the elves also seeking medicine for their migration. Their Three Rings will fade whether Sauron reclaims the One Ring or it is destroyed, forcing them toward the Undying Lands to escape unending spiritual weariness.
- Chu proposes carrying the One Ring into the Lord God's Dimension as a plot item. His theory: an unexchanged plot character entering that dimension is erased, so Sauron would die while the Ring survived. This is a proposal, not yet a confirmed mechanic or decision.
- Chu estimates a 60% chance that taking the Ring into the Dimension would erase Sauron without destroying it, but less than a 10% chance that Team China could keep the Ring because Celestial Team will seek it and all five teams must spend thirty minutes together in Mordor before return.
- Lothlórien's departure gifts: every reincarnator receives a scan-concealing, color-matching **elven cloak** and a blood-bound, strength-enhancing short sword forged with mithril and Eloges, worth roughly two D ranks or one C rank. Zheng receives instead a **World Tree leaf**, whose vitality substitutes for his own and perfectly protects his life until the leaf fully dissolves. Chu receives Galadriel's magically delayed blessing for safety, which weakens her for several days.
- Lock **lembas** (`兰巴斯`), **Falls of Rauros** (`拉洛斯瀑布`), and **Uruk-hai** (`强兽人`) to established Middle-earth forms. Saruman's Uruk-hai are powerful, tolerate sunlight and harsh environments, and pursue the Fellowship along the river.
- Zhang Heng receives ten **Galadhrim arrows** (`凯兰崔姆箭矢`) that store mental energy and accept minute in-flight course corrections from its source. With Zhan Lan correcting aim, they can turn his already-mastered but inaccurate Triple-Blade Arrow into a reliable signature attack.
- Lock established Middle-earth forms: **Minas Tirith** (`米那斯提力斯`), **Argonath** (`亚苟拉斯巨像`), **Emyn Muil** (`爱明莫尔`), and the **Dead Marshes** (described rather than named in this source passage). Aragorn rejects Boromir's proposal to recover at Minas Tirith and plans to cross after dark, traverse Emyn Muil and the marshes, and enter Mordor from the north.
- At Rauros, all three psyche-force users scan the approaching Uruk-hai and share their locations through Mental Link. Zheng assigns Zhang Heng and Chu to guard the psychics, plus one veteran each from Northern Ice and South Flame, while the remaining veterans prepare to intercept the attackers.
- An unidentified handsome, long-haired man with a friendly smile and cold eyes approaches Aragorn and Frodo without detection, speaks of destroying beautiful virtues, and disappears before Zheng arrives. Do not identify him in narration until the source does, despite the strong implication that he is Zhao Zhuikong.
- Saruman's Uruk-hai number more than one thousand, are described in-story as transformed elves, possess crude innate battle aura, and include longbow users. Their battle aura can resist Tiger Soul's invisible aura and halt the Skeletal Nightmare's protected charge, preventing Zheng from simply riding through the entire formation.
- Zhao Zhuikong reveals himself to Zhao Yingkong at the cliff above Rauros after luring her with an instinctive psychological pull. He repeats the nearly invisible wire trap used in her previous death, says his clone told him how she died, refuses to fight because she can still mature and has exhausted her resurrection chance, then jumps more than one hundred meters toward the river. Zhao Yingkong vows to kill him for her dead companions, childhood, and buried past. Explicit sexualization of the underage Zhao Yingkong is omitted under project policy while coercive conduct and its consequences are preserved neutrally.
- The Uruk-hai captain can use two-arrow Burst Arrow strongly enough to penetrate Tiger Soul's weapon aura and shatter one layer of Zheng's body shell; it shoots through both sides of Boromir's chest, pins him to a tree, wounds and enables the capture of Aya, and helps enable Merry's capture. Zheng evades a later shot with Instantaneous Destruction plus Moonwalk, kills the captain at close range, then uses full Explosion to annihilate the remaining two-to-three hundred Uruk-hai.
- Boromir is preserved by an Ice-Congealing Pill. Cheng Xiao can replace both damaged lungs with century-rated bioelectric artificial lungs and repair the injured aorta with bioengineered vessels, but surgery requires a clean room, compatible donor blood, and a Blood Gu to prevent rejection. The group therefore carries frozen Boromir toward Rohan on the Skeletal Nightmare.
- The three allied teams divide along the film's routes: Northern Ice's three members accompany Frodo and Sam toward Mordor; South Flame pursues the captured Merry and Aya, seeking the Ent/Rohan/Gondor network of local backing; Team China remains with Aragorn, Legolas, and Gimli and accepts responsibility for the main war front.
- Zhao Zhuikong blocks Celestial Team's unnamed black-haired, sword-flying cultivator from examining the three allied teams. The cultivator is a third-stage unlocker who claims not to have lost against Zhao's clone; after their skirmish wounds both men, he deploys eight five-meter elemental banners and invokes **Demon-Subduing Formation—Tushita Eight Trigrams Furnace** against Zhao.
- The cultivator is **Luo Yinglong** (`罗应龙`). His formation uses the eight trigrams Qian/Kun/Li/Kan/Zhen/Xun/Gen/Dui, a taijitu, and Tushita violet fire; Zhao Zhuikong interrupts the incantation by severing one arm, after which a deliberately comic phone ringtone breaks Luo's concentration and causes backlash.
- Luo Yinglong's intact **true essence** can return to Adam's **Qiankun Mirror** (`乾坤镜`) in the Lord God's Dimension and resurrect him. Adam threatens total war if Zhao kills Luo. Zhao bargains that Celestial Team must not kill Zheng before the battle for Gondor's capital ends because Zheng sustains Team China and Zhao Yingkong is within it; Adam agrees and asks Zhao to tell “that man” about his presence on Celestial Team.
- Team China and Aragorn's party enter **Rohan**, characterized as the human kingdom second only to Gondor in military strength and famed for elite cavalry. They encounter roughly five hundred disciplined heavy riders plus spare mounts after four days and nights of pursuit.
- Saruman has mentally controlled **King Théoden** (`希优顿`) and exiled the riders who remain loyal to Rohan. Their unnamed silver-armored commander encountered and destroyed most of the fleeing Uruk-hai, then gives Aragorn's party seven mounts left by fallen soldiers.
- Chu's battlefield autopsy estimates that 70–80% of Rohan's cavalry can use battle aura; such riders can match two to three times their number of Uruk-hai. Zheng proposes combining one thousand elite riders with twenty Pursuers transformed from Burst-Arrow-capable Uruk-hai commanders, but Chu warns that this cannot account for the ninefold Ringwraith, Sauron, or rival fighters comparable to clone Zheng, then predicts he may die during the team battle.
- Gandalf returns as **Gandalf the White** after the Balrog detonates its life essence and both pass through a turbulence of time and space. He says divine contact resurrected him with a mission and revealed Team China's otherworldly origin.
- Gandalf's prophecy: the One Ring will leave Middle-earth intact—carried away either by Team China or another group—while Sauron vanishes and the Three Elven Rings retain their power. A hostile group from Team China's world may instead help Sauron reclaim it; Gandalf must defeat them. Until Mordor's allied armies are defeated, the Ring itself is prophesied to remain safe.
- Lock **Edoras** (`伊多拉斯`) as Rohan's capital. Gandalf directs the party there for its first battle against Saruman's army. Chu interprets divine prophecy as deduction enabled by near-total observation and infers that East America or Celestial Team has already approached one of Team China's groups with hostile intent.
- Chu infers that Zheng is deliberately concealing a power manifested while killing hundreds of enlarged Lickers: Zheng's light injuries there and lack of visible growth after two major scenarios cannot fit Chu's estimate of his potential. Zheng asks for time and promises the hidden power will not harm his companions.
- Lock **Ents** (`恩特树人`), **Mearas** (`米亚拉斯`), and **Shadowfax** (`暗疾`) to established Middle-earth forms. Several hundred Ents once destroyed ten thousand Orc troops in the wounded forest but normally refuse war unless directly provoked. Shadowfax, as lord of horses, matches the Skeletal Nightmare's speed and size.
- Freed from Saruman, Théoden physically reverts from an apparent octogenarian to a powerful middle-aged knight; within two or three days his battle aura surpasses Aragorn's. Cheng Xiao completes Boromir's cardiopulmonary surgery in roughly ten hours using blood from thirty compatible strong men, but Boromir needs months of recovery and cannot fight in the current war.
- Gandalf spends many energy stones and several days of strength to erase the prior ownership of Excalibur and the silver bow. Zhao Yingkong and Zhang Heng bind them with blood; unbound Excalibur appears as a plain two-handed greatsword with two runes, while the bow displays dense ancient runes, diagrams, and flowing silver light.
- Lock **Helm's Deep** (`圣盔谷`) and **Éomer** (`伊欧墨`). Théoden plans to evacuate Edoras before more than ten thousand Uruk-hai arrive; Éomer's two thousand loyal riders are roughly 1,500 km away. Chu proposes a hundred battle-aura mounted archers conduct systematic guerrilla warfare—scout killing, flank/rear raids, supply burning, camp attacks, and destruction of isolated pursuers—to protect the civilian column and exhaust the enemy.
- Chu converts the hundred elites into unarmored light cavalry with two horses per rider, three days of food, one heavy sword, abundant arrows, and ten black-dyed **night arrows** each. With maximum Psychic Scan, they annihilate a camp of 300-plus Uruk-hai and 300-plus Wargs without loss, then destroy a 1,500-strong raiding force through repeated shoot-and-retreat cycles at the cost of four riders.
- After three days and nights, the guerrillas earn the Uruk-hai name **Ghost Hoof** (`幽灵之蹄`), with forty-odd survivors after killing over four thousand enemies. All survivors use battle aura; seven or eight unlock genetic-constraint stage one, and more than ten learn Burst Arrow from Zhang Heng. The remaining 15,000-plus Uruk-hai consolidate into one army and cease scouting or raiding.
- Luo Yinglong is Celestial Team's second-strongest member and a disciple of the **Shushan Sword Sect**. He previously lost completely to Devil Team's “demon” despite his cultivation, then failed to kill Zhao Zhuikong with the Tushita furnace; he approaches Helm's Deep before the negotiated deadline to compare Chinese cultivation against Zheng's still-unawakened Saint-derived genetic constraint.
- Luo Yinglong's disclosed defenses include a master-refined **Ninefold Golden Protective Armor** beneath his clothes and a **Blueheart Jade Pendant** that raises a blue barrier against fatal attacks. Zhang Heng's readied shot forces Luo to admit it would consume one or two artifacts; Adam then recalls him by phone before the duel begins.
- At Isengard, Wang Xia captures Uruk-hai for Hao Tian's Pursuer program. Each receives transformation fluid plus a **consciousness-guidance device**, which exploits the newly transformed Pursuer's near-blank consciousness for hypnosis and conditioning; Hao predicts a battle-aura Uruk-hai Pursuer will be ten times stronger than a human one.
- Neos and South Flame attempt to recruit the Ents as their local strategic backing by arguing Mordor will ultimately burn their forest. Meanwhile, Zhao Zhuikong stands amid a field of corpses strongly implied to be Northern Ice, possesses the One Ring, and declares his intent to give it to Sauron for amusement. Preserve the implication until later confirmation identifies the dead and explains how he obtained it.
- Rivendell sends 300 **Night Hawks** (`夜鹰`), half of its royal guard, to defend Helm's Deep. Chu rejects summoning 1,000-plus Anubis warriors because Zheng cannot direct them in a siege and reserves the bracelet for Gondor; instead, he identifies a Qi Tengyi-translated Book of the Dead spell that alters terrain.
- Before the Battle of Helm's Deep, Chu instructs Zheng to recall Hao Tian's group, Zero and the other three veterans, and allied Burst-Arrow archers for the coming team battle at Gondor. His language again resembles final instructions; Zheng reminds him that he must survive to reach the fourth stage and recover human emotion.
- At Helm's Deep, an Uruk-hai carries a torch into the drainage culvert and detonates an enhanced charge whose force rivals a modern high explosive, breaching six or seven meters of wall. Zheng's previously concealed fourth-stage-like power manifests involuntarily: tiny hand motions tear nearby Uruk-hai apart, and in his rage he kills roughly two to three thousand attackers at the breach.
- Chu secretly bargained with Galadriel before leaving Lothlórien: if Team China returns and delivers the One Ring after Sauron's soul has been erased, she is to provide an item or spell capable of one resurrection or one feigned death. Legolas keeps both the meeting and the implied contingency item secret from Zheng.
- Zheng administers his World Tree leaf after Chu's heart is shattered, while Cheng Xiao attempts emergency needling and suturing and Zhao Yingkong tries the Ice-Congealing Pill. Chu cannot swallow the pill and has no carotid pulse; Zhao pronounces him dead. A parenthetical telepathic statement confirms Chu's death to **Adam**, but the speaker is not yet identified and must remain unattributed.
- Immediately after Chu's apparent death, Zheng catches an unseen-speed Burst Arrow barehanded before charging back into the Uruk-hai army.
- **Genetic Constraint stage four** is explicitly divided into three levels. The initial level awakens dormant evolutionary genes, optimizes the user's body, and permits abilities to reach their full power; the intermediate level actively evolves and rewrites genes beyond that optimum and can create abilities from genetic information; the advanced level controls bodily energy at the genetic level and lies one step below stage five.
- Zheng reached the initial fourth stage during his enlarged-Licker battle after returning to *Resident Evil*. It lets him tune individual gene pairs, physical attributes, and the efficiency of Explosion/Destruction in real time, but also awakens primordial rage and bloodlust that he cannot yet control. While berserk he considers killing allies as well as enemies and experiences an alien imperative to kill the Saints' descendants and exterminate humanity; that thought shocks him back to himself.
- Zheng's fourth-stage rampage outside Helm's Deep lasts more than two hours and drives the Uruk-hai army into retreat. He unconsciously moves over one hundred meters away from the fortress, likely protecting his allies from himself, then collapses after confirming Chu's apparent death and saying farewell to him as a brother.
- New volume marker: **Volume VI: War of the Ring (IV)** (`第六集 指环战争（四）`).
- Team China hears the one-point deduction confirming Chu's apparent death and initially accepts it as genuine. Zheng publicly appoints Hao Tian to replace Chu as strategist, relying on Chu's prior judgment, and resolves to reunite with the detached group at Isengard after Gandalf's two thousand-plus riders arrive at Helm's Deep.
- Hao Tian's group has created more than 300 Uruk-hai Pursuers, all able to use battle aura and more than fifty able to use Burst Arrow. Each is almost four meters tall and is estimated capable of fighting fifty ordinary Uruk-hai.
- Before the groups separated, Chu gave Zero a folding metal communicator that transmits voices directly through consciousness. A recording or remote voice unmistakably belonging to Chu contacts Zero after Chu's apparent death, assigns him to leave Hao Tian and travel to Gondor's capital for a future battle involving Zheng, and states that Galadriel's arrangement leaves Chu twenty days to live. The exact survival mechanism and Zero's final task remain intentionally unrevealed.
- Each use of Zheng's initial fourth stage leaves behind some permanent genetic optimization, steadily increasing his strength but also altering his mind and intensifying murderous impulses. The Golden Mask monster is identified as an intermediate fourth-stage user whose unrestricted transformations ignored genetic balance and bodily limits, causing its defeat through attrition.
- Gandalf's storage cloth can wrap a human body into a tiny portable bundle; Legolas carries Chu's body so it will not be destroyed during Gandalf's confrontation with Saruman.
- Neos persuades more than two hundred Ents to march east through the forest toward Gondor's capital. The Ents have already flooded and destroyed Isengard's Uruk-hai forces, but Merry reports no prior attack by Hao Tian's detached group.
- Saruman remains alive in Orthanc and displays a red-black seeing-stone image in which Sauron's flaming eye becomes an armored humanoid wearing the One Ring. Treat this as Saruman's claimed evidence that the Ring has returned to Sauron, pending independent confirmation.
- Celestial Team consists of former team captains and had six survivors after its second encounter with Devil Team. Devil Team's winged Chinese captain fought seven Celestial members alone, destroyed Luo Yinglong's body, mortally wounded **Song Tian** (`宋天`), and killed two others; Devil lost only two weaker members because its own strategist countered Adam.
- Lock **Linaia** (`琳娜亚`) and **Song Tian** (`宋天`). Linaia is Luo Yinglong's girlfriend. Song Tian is Celestial Team's strongest fighter, with mixed Han Chinese ancestry; Luo is second strongest and can resurrect if his true essence survives.
- Adam deliberately sent real-world information that led Hao Tian into the reincarnation world. He suspects Chu recognized Hao Tian as Adam's hidden agent and may have used genuine death plus a resurrection method as an open stratagem to obscure whether he remains active.
- Adam's current deployment keeps Song Tian and Linaia with him, sends Luo Yinglong plus two other Celestial members to kill Zheng during the battle for Gondor, and treats the Mordor-bound high-technology group—including Zero—as already trapped. He concedes that Chu's apparent death has forced Celestial Team to withhold its full strength at Gondor and cost him half a strategic step.
- Song Tian has reached the initial fourth stage and forcibly suppresses its murderous impulses. Zhao Zhuikong considers him less interesting than an immature target but draws a nearly transparent edge-on dagger that makes Song assume a full defensive stance with an ancient cold-radiating great saber.
- Adam bargains for Zhao Zhuikong to leave Mordor until its final battle ends by promising to spare or surrender Zhao Yingkong. Zhao accepts and asks that several newcomers from the three other teams be left for him; Adam's test confirms that Zhao is not Chu's hidden reversal.
- Lock Celestial members **Anovia** (`阿挪维亚`), a burly white man, and **Liara** (`历阿拉`), a black man. Adam treats them as expendable and orders them to join Luo Yinglong at Gondor, kill Zheng, and damage the human alliance while Song Tian and Linaia remain with him.
- An unidentified naked, weakened man emerges alive from ice beneath a marsh and invokes **Ice Age**, vowing revenge on Celestial or East America for an ambush. The ability strongly implies Gungnir, but narration must not name him before the source confirms it.
- Neos concludes that South Flame must accompany its 200-plus Ent allies to Gondor: victory can supply enough local power to suppress Mordor, while Team China's destruction would remove South Flame's only survival advantage. Zheng, unable to simulate Chu and able to reproduce only 50–60% of Xiao Honglu's reasoning, falls back on following the film route and defending Gondor.
- The maddened Saruman fails to raise any barrier and is killed by Legolas's Triple-Blade Burst Arrow plus Zhang Heng's Arrow of Wind. Orthanc collapses because Saruman's magic had sustained it, burying a potentially kingdom-scale collection of energy stones and magical items that Zheng intends to excavate on a future return.
- Rohan assembles 7,000 riders within a week, including roughly 5,000 fit elite cavalry. Théoden refuses to aid Gondor unless it lights the ancient beacons and formally asks Rohan to shed blood, preserving Rohan's dignity after Gondor failed to reinforce Helm's Deep.
- Team China divides again: Zheng and Zhan Lan accompany Gandalf and Merry directly to Minas Tirith to secure victory and deter rival teams; Zhao Yingkong, Zhang Heng, and Cheng Xiao accompany Aragorn to obtain Gondor's undead army. Gandalf leaves Legolas carrying the storage cloth containing Chu's body.
- Unsealed **Excalibur** ignores and annihilates battle aura on bodies or weapons, though Zhao Yingkong has not yet discovered its supreme single technique. A slender, unarmored rider in black secretly leaves Rohan; Legolas, visibly awkward, calls the rider a messenger carrying news of victory. Preserve the rider's identity until revealed.
- Gondor's guards mistake Zheng's Skeletal Nightmare for a pursuing Ringwraith and credit Gandalf with its defeat; the mount is temporarily surrendered to the city soldiers. Gondor's Steward refuses to light the beacons because he fears Aragorn's restoration would end his rule, so Gandalf covertly uses Merry to initiate the beacon chain.
- The black-haired Celestial cultivator watching the beacons vows to finish condensing his **golden core** and then kill Zhao Zhuikong, Zhao's clone, and the “demon.” The context strongly indicates Luo Yinglong, but preserve the source's delayed naming where applicable.
- Mordor fields an estimated 100,000 Orcs, Uruk-hai, and trolls against Rohan's 5,000 fit cavalry. Elrond brings Aragorn the two-handed sword reforged from the shards of **Narsil**, which continuously radiates silver light and proves the wielder's Gondorian royal blood.
- The **Men of the Mountains** are an undead army cursed by Gondor's royal line for abandoning their oath in its greatest need. Only Aragorn can command them, and only fulfilling their oath and receiving royal forgiveness can grant them rest; Elrond identifies them as the only force able to close the numerical gap with Mordor.
- Zhang Heng's unsealed silver metal bow can concentrate life energy along its string. When Legolas uses it, one shot produces more than ten life-energy filaments around the arrow, expanding the affected area by over tenfold into a shotgun-like spread.
- The entrance to the undead refuge bears skulls and a warning that the numberless dead guard it and living entrants will die. Inside, supernatural illusions separate the travelers and guide them toward unseen cliffs.
- Zhao Yingkong's illusion presents a gentle childhood Zhao Zhuikong inviting her back to their training companions. She rejects the path while asking whether he will always remain the brother she remembers, then bites her tongue awake one step from an abyss.
- Zhang Heng's illusion recreates the assault in which he abandoned Ming Yanwei, merging the attackers with memories of his abusive father. This time he refuses to retreat toward the cliff, declaring that another flight would destroy their future and that he will not lose her again even in an illusion.
- The undead refuge's mental trial materializes each intruder's strongest current desire or dread and guides them toward a cliff. Aragorn is protected by reforged Narsil. Zhao Yingkong and Zhang Heng awaken and save Legolas and Gimli; Cheng Xiao rejects his own temptation without assistance.
- The ghost leader initially appears to refuse Aragorn and floods the passage with bones, then reappears outside and pledges thousands of gravity-defying, intangible green spirits to the last King of Gondor. Their first task is to annihilate the pirate fleet of more than ten black ships on the river.
- Gondor's Steward orders more than 1,000 cavalry to charge Mordor's 100,000 despite Gandalf's protests. Only three return, and **Faramir** dies in this altered plot. Zhan Lan's Psychic Scan confirms a fully equipped siege army of Orcs, Uruk-hai, and large trolls with catapults, scaling ladders, and battering rams.
- Minas Tirith has only a little over 3,000 defenders because the Steward never mobilized Gondor's regional armies. Captain-class troops on both sides can use battle aura, and a smaller number can use Burst Arrow; Zheng judges a massed volley of roughly one hundred Burst Arrows too dangerous to receive directly.
- Celestial's Luo Yinglong and two unidentified teammates—one man and one woman—openly observe the siege from nearby mountains. Luo restrains them from attacking until Zheng and the ninefold Nazgûl exhaust each other.
- The ninefold Nazgûl rides a roughly ten-meter winged drake whose soul-piercing shriek terrifies human defenders but leaves Mordor's host unaffected. Gandalf manifests a city-scale translucent barrier that kills the drake on impact, but the rider emerges unharmed with a black longsword and huge flail powerful enough to pulverize adjacent buildings.
- Under Explosion, Zheng remains slower and physically weaker than the ninefold Nazgûl. He therefore combines Destruction with initial stage four and demonstrates **microscopic control** (`入微`): redirecting the flail through a minute strike at its chain and surface while automatically calculating injury, healing time, and safe Destruction duration.
- Luo Yinglong identifies Zheng's precision as microscopic control, delays Celestial's assault until the Nazgûl weakens him, then kills the rider with green sword energy and claims its points/side-story reward before challenging Zheng. Luo says his realm is only stage three but his actual strength exceeds Zheng's.
- Source-description discrepancy: Adam previously described Anovia and Liara as a burly white man and a black man, while the two unnamed partners beside Luo at Gondor are described here as a white woman and a burly black man. Do not silently equate or rename them until the source resolves their identities.
- Clone Zheng once spared Luo Yinglong after using terrifying black flame to erase the soul of another true-essence user, explaining that too few strong Chinese survive in the reincarnation world. Luo repays that mercy by advising original Zheng before their duel.
- Adam's theory: initial stage four awakens more than 90% of dormant genes and completes the active genome with the strongest inherited material, amplifying abilities, attributes, and enhancements. The dominant genes derive either from the Saints or from ancient humanoid enemies of humanity through **grand atavistic inheritance**; Zheng carries the enemy lineage, while Song Tian carries authentic Saint genes.
- Luo warns that failure to master the ancient enemies' slaughter instinct before intermediate stage four allows the inherited consciousness to take control, citing Zhao Zhuikong as unable to return. Luo rejects Adam and Song Tian's belief that all inheritors of enemy genes must be exterminated: genes are tools, and personal will determines their use.
- Luo deliberately performs the role of a guileless fool to prevent Adam and Song Tian from eliminating his unlimited cultivation potential. He spends most points purchasing return time in Shushan, suspects cultivation contributed to the ancient Saints' deaths, and speaks freely only after destroying his phone on Adam's orders.
- **Microscopic control** is both internal (awakening and directing genes) and external (perceiving and controlling minute force vectors). It can turn one measure of applied force into the effect of one hundred. Luo judges Zheng's realm sufficient but raw power and self-created techniques inadequate.
- Zheng immediately uses microscopic control to invent a double-stamp movement technique whose resonating ground forces more than double his forward speed.
- Luo's burly black teammate is a stage-two unlocker enhanced with four A-rank animal spirits—black bear, panther, tiger, and elephant—and the A-rank **One-Hundred-Percent Toguro Younger Muscle Enhancement**. The spirits respectively alter power, agility, offense, and defense; Luo ranks his melee strength/destruction below only Song Tian. The white female newcomer provides rapid healing and **Light Binding**.
- Under uncontrolled stage four, Zheng develops small black bat wings and fully loses human reason, bites flesh from the black fighter, pierces his abdomen, and tears him in half. Luo begins **Five Elements Transposition** with a Five Elements/Eight Trigrams diagram, raising the ground within one hundred meters while voluntarily giving up flight.
- Luo's cultivation equipment includes a golden protective barrier and trigram techniques: **Thunder Zhen** leaves an electrical seal powerful enough to kill an elephant; **Spiral Li** redirects direct force; and **Thousand-Weight Gen** accelerates his downward fall. Zheng uses microscopic control to neutralize the electricity and reverse Spiral Li.
- By genetically optimizing three gene pairs and drawing energy from charged rings, Zheng can sustain full-output Destruction for twenty-seven seconds. The resulting supersonic barrage displaces Luo's golden defense, fractures and destroys the raised hundred-meter platform, and brings Zheng's combat power temporarily level with Luo's.
- Luo escalates to **Demon-Subduing Formation—Tushita Eight Trigrams Furnace**, originally prepared to counter Zhao Zhuikong's extreme speed.
- Luo's eight-banner furnace artifact requires eighty-one days and many rare materials to refine; full activation requires the Nascent Soul stage. The Qian/Kun/Li/Kan/Zhen/Xun/Gen/Dui banners multiply gravity, while a rotating taijitu drops persistent violet fire comparable to the Balrog's white-hot flame.
- Zero, now a stage-one unlocker, destroys Luo's golden barrier and body with Chu's prepared long-range shot at the instant before Zheng dies. Luo's surviving true-essence light flees westward, preserving the established possibility of Qiankun-Mirror resurrection.
- Chu is revealed alive and physically whole beside Hao Tian and several others. He explains that Zero's trained shot had a baseline success probability above 60% but judges Zero's decisive performance effectively 100%; he explicitly calls his companions people rather than data.
- Hao Tian confesses that Adam entered the reincarnation world first, independently discovered Chu's real-world information-transfer method, and provided Hao Tian with team/captain intelligence plus a same-dimensional-space phone usable across Earth. Hao used self-hypnosis to enter Team China, intending to gain trust, help Celestial wipe it out, become its captain, transfer to Celestial, and ultimately help Adam steal the entire Lord God's Dimension.
- Chu genuinely died. Galadriel's secret soul-preservation rite and blessing keep his soul intact for twenty days, while Zheng's World Tree leaf animates and preserves his corpse so the soul can inhabit it and act. Chu therefore does not deceive the Lord God's death accounting.
- Hao Tian voluntarily wears the Pursuer hypnosis circlet to command the army after Chu's death; it is only an auxiliary unit. Chu holds the primary controller, which can direct or destroy Hao's brain with powerful current. The Pursuer program was bait designed to make Hao wear it.
- Zhao Zhuikong supplied Chu with Celestial's roster, strength levels, and Adam's location in exchange for arranging an uninterrupted duel with Song Tian. Chu dispatches all Pursuers plus Bawang, Wang Xia, and Luo Gandao to annihilate Celestial Team while Zhao occupies Song.
- Song Tian's microscopic-control specialty is **saber momentum**: saber force forms a fully controlled hurricane and wind blades. Zhao identifies weapon mastery stages as striking, killing, techniques, then momentum; Song has perfected the final realm and needs only condense more power.
- With Luo dead and the furnace destabilizing, Zheng retains self-awareness inside stage four and directly perceives categories of his own genes, allowing conscious selection for heat resistance, toughness, energy control, healing, explosive force, endurance, and body shape.
- Zheng survives more than 95% deep burns and one seared eye by reconstructing his body from newly optimized genes, internal/vampire energy, dragon genes, and his dominant ancient lineage. This high-energy body exists only during stage four until he reaches stage five.
- Zero's Point-Line Mystic Eyes locate an artifact or body's line of destruction, allowing a Gauss sniper round to reduce even Luo and his golden defense to powder; the shot apparently leaves Zero unconscious.
- Rohan's roughly 5,000 fully armored cavalry arrive against more than 80,000 surviving Mordor troops. Théoden assigns Éomer left, Gamling center behind him, and **Gamod** (`加默德`) right, then leads the “Death” charge. Several hundred riders fall to rare Burst Arrows, but the charge kills or tramples more than 30,000 enemies.
- Mordor deploys roughly 100 giant war elephants dozens of meters tall, far more than the film. Before one can trample the immobilized Zheng, Neos's several hundred Ents emerge and begin bombarding them with boulders.
- South Flame finds the incapacitated Zheng; despite Zheng's fear of betrayal, Neos repays his Moria life debt and orders Richard to carry him into Minas Tirith. Aragorn's undead host then arrives from the harbor and overwhelms both the remaining Orc army and giant elephants, securing humanity's battlefield victory.
- Song Tian's known saber-momentum elements are wind, earth, fire, water, and strongest metal. **Tornado Earth-Flow Wind** combines earth and wind into a stone-laden tornado; Zhao Zhuikong's extreme-speed collision triggers mountain-scale collapse.
- Hao Tian's psychokinetic defense manifests as visible air distortions and stops Song's mountain-splitting remote saber force. Zhao exploits the distraction to sever Song's non-weapon arm, then withdraws before more than 100 Pursuers can fire paired Burst Arrows from three-meter bows.
- Adam offers peaceful coexistence after conceding Chu's victory. Chu refuses because he considers Adam more dangerous than Devil Team and prefers total Celestial annihilation to prevent resurrection. Adam reveals an armed **continental-shelf oscillator** with a dead-man switch connected to his heart, threatening mutual death if Chu kills him.
- Chu compromises under the dead-man threat: he spares Adam in exchange for a storage ring containing energy stones and miscellaneous items worth roughly one S-rank side story. He infers Zhao Zhuikong will exploit the survivors, and Zhao then kills four Team China newcomers, producing four Lord God death notifications.
- Aragorn removes Gondor's Steward after the undead confirm his bloodline and orders a united assault on Mordor. The undead dissolve after fulfilling their oath. The alliance can assemble roughly 15,000 troops in two days, while Gandalf conservatively estimates at least 200,000 remain in Mordor.
- The Ents will accompany the alliance to the Black Gate, bombard it open, and combine life energy into a protective layer, but cannot enter Mordor's fiery interior. Zheng proposes channeling their collective life energy through his stage-four control and the **Bracelet of Anubis** to summon an army despite his catastrophic injuries.
- Microscopic control lets Zheng meter transferred Ent life energy into the Bracelet of Anubis rather than expend an entire reserve per summoning. He estimates 10,000 roughly three-meter, gravity-defying Anubis warriors—nearly indestructible unless beheaded—could breach the Black Gate and contest battle-aura Uruk-hai.
- Cheng Xiao treats Zheng's catastrophic burns with a crushed mixture of green leaves and giant translucent caterpillars. Zhang Heng's gaze and resolve have steadied after his illusion; Zhao Yingkong's sword draw becomes slightly slower but subtler, while her hatred appears gone or deeply hidden. A sexualized observation involving the underage Zhao is omitted under project policy.
- Neos secures the Ents' march through South Flame's future promise to restore Isengard's forest within ten years using Lord God resources. He immediately deduces Team China did not kill the newcomers and identifies either deliberate blame-shifting or a cross-team newcomer massacre.
- Lock **Mouth of Sauron** for the fully black-armored emissary with only a huge exposed mouth. Aragorn beheads him after taunts about eating and torturing the Hobbits, then challenges Sauron with reforged Narsil as the Black Gate opens onto an apparently endless Orc host.
- The full transfer of Ent life energy lets Zheng summon more than **40,000 Anubis warriors**, not the projected 10,000. Their sand bodies ignore ordinary arrows and gravity; only head destruction, such as Burst Arrow impacts, disperses them. They break Mordor's front formation and open the way for Aragorn and Théoden's combined charge.
- Zhan Lan locates Chu's group and Sauron through Psychic Scan. Zhao Yingkong volunteers to join Chu because she needs to meet Zhao Zhuikong; Zheng permits her to confront and, if possible, end that past. Chu meanwhile surrounds two remaining East America members with hundreds of Pursuers, intending to recover the four points lost when Zhao killed Team China's newcomers.
- Zhao Yingkong suppresses her killing intent when meeting Zhao Zhuikong and appeals to the genuinely protective older brother she remembers. A sexualized coercive act involving the underage Zhao is omitted under project policy, while her tears, appeal, and Zhao's momentary vulnerability remain. He spares Team China for her sake but still promises to return when she is stronger.
- Gungnir confirms Zhao Zhuikong ambushed Northern Ice, flayed his teammates before him, castrated him, and buried him alive in a swamp. Gungnir expends every hidden frost energy stone to force Ice Age toward the Nature Fruit's theoretical fifth-stage energy-control power.
- Zhao Zhuikong uses microscopic control and more than one hundred resonant stamps to fracture volcanic bedrock and release superheated groundwater and steam. This forces Gungnir out of elemental frost form, after which Zhao instantly beheads him. Northern Ice captain Gungnir is now confirmed dead.
- Chu states that the return countdown begins when all five teams gather in Mordor; with four present and Northern Ice presumed annihilated, Team China must secure the One Ring during the final interval and should claim Sauron's kill reward if possible.
- Sauron manifests as seven-to-eight-meter black armor wielding a five-meter-plus hammer, wearing the One Ring on his left hand, and radiating corrosive mist. One black hurricane erases several thousand Anubis warriors and even surrounding corpses; he subsequently destroys the remaining summoned army.
- Reforged Narsil disperses Sauron's corrosion, while Gandalf's barrier provides brief additional protection but cannot withstand direct strikes. Sauron incapacitates Aragorn and Gimli within seconds, survives Legolas's helmet-piercing Triple-Blade Burst Arrow, and apparently kills Théoden despite a battle-aura lance piercing through Sauron's torso.
- Unable to fight, Zheng commands the finishing team assault through Zhan Lan's Psychic Scan: Zhang Heng readies Arrow of Wind with the silver bow while Zhao Yingkong silently approaches behind Sauron with Excalibur.
- Adam concludes Team China's mutually supporting specialists give it greater long-term potential than Devil Team, despite only Zheng and Chu currently lacking fatal shortcomings. He identifies Zero as extreme high-tech offense plus personal weakness mitigated by Zheng, and Zhang Heng as even more destructive at short-to-medium range.
- Linaia retains Luo Yinglong's malleable true-essence light for later resurrection. Celestial Team's principal fighters survive, but Adam says its loss of strategic momentum prevents it from opposing Devil Team until it later defeats Team China; Luo may eventually surpass Song Tian through attaining the Dao by overwhelming force rather than insight.
- Zhao Zhuikong personally kills one of East America's last two members, leaving himself and one terrified survivor, then watches Zhao Yingkong from afar and appears to weep while urging her continued growth.
- Gandalf's barriers mutually annihilate Sauron's defensive arrays. Legolas's Triple-Blade Burst Arrow strips much of the black mist from Sauron's Ring arm, allowing Zhang Heng's Arrow of Wind to sever it two-thirds through; the shot leaves Zhang Heng unable to fight.
- Sauron's One Ring expands corrosive black mist across a roughly one-kilometer circle. Zhao Yingkong uses **Shining Vacuum Wave** to exclude the mist, cuts through the final connection of Sauron's arm, and seizes it despite corrosion destroying her left arm. Taking possession of the Ring-bearing limb triggers the team's return to the Lord God's Dimension.
- New volume marker: **Volume VII: Transformers Doomsday (I)** (`第七集 变形末日（一）`).
- Immediately after the return, Chu destroys Hao Tian's head before Zheng can request full repair, preventing any possibility of his restoration. The Lord God completely restores Zhao Yingkong's corroded arm and Zheng's burns; Chu's reconstruction takes more than five minutes.
- Zheng physically confronts Chu for treating companions like expendable pieces, insisting Team China fights as partners rather than machines or parasites. Chu's inability to feel pain renders the beating meaningless except for its repair-point cost.
- Chu estimated Hao Tian's betrayal probability above 50%; if even part of the plan leaked to Adam, he estimated Team China's probability of total annihilation above 70%. Hao Tian's unexpectedly easy acceptance of a subordinate role in the Isengard mission was a key warning sign.
- The device given to Hao Tian was both hypnotic equipment and a consciousness controller. If Hao betrayed the team, Chu could control him; otherwise it would control the Pursuers. Chu expected Hao to put it on once Chu's death seemed certain because Hao would never leave hundreds of Pursuers under Zero's control.
- Galadriel's blessing gives Chu's consciousness temporary elven properties after genuine bodily death, keeping it concentrated rather than dispersed. Chu hypothesizes a form of electrostatic resonance in which consciousness persists as wave/electrical information carried by particles, though he explicitly labels this only a personal scientific conjecture.
- The **World Tree leaf** is a high-order energy aggregate capable of granting dead flesh one final action when a surviving consciousness directs it. Galadriel gives it to Zheng rather than Chu because the entire plan assumes hostile surveillance: two gifts to Chu, a concealed cache, or Chu retrieving the leaf himself would expose the deception. Zheng administering it unknowingly is the true beginning of the plan.
- Chu ranks complete Celestial Team at roughly twice Team China's current upper-limit strength, making Celestial second among reincarnation teams, Devil Team first, and Team China somewhere between third and fifth.
- Chu kills Hao Tian before full repair because the Lord God might otherwise restore the controlled portion of Hao's consciousness. Hao Tian's accumulated rewards are lost with him.
- Locked reward calculation for Zheng after *The Lord of the Rings*: 12,000 points from mission/battle completion, 8,000 plus one A-rank side story for the Balrog, 7,000 plus one B-rank for an unlocked Celestial member, and 10,000 plus one B-rank and two C-ranks for two Ringwraiths, less 6,000 in team-death penalties. Net: **31,000 points, one A-rank, two B-ranks, and two C-ranks**.
- Zhao Yingkong receives **two B-rank and two A-rank side stories**. Chu infers that bringing the One Ring back without exchanging Sauron caused the Lord God to erase him as an illegal entrant and credit Zhao with the Sauron kill. The One Ring itself remains in Team China's possession.
- The One Ring's identified function is **energy conversion**. Chu proposes ordinary applications including refining internal/vampire energy into higher-quality forms and storing it in the body or Ring, plus extreme compression of laser output for a team superweapon he names the **Magic Cannon** (`魔动炮`).
- Cheng Xiao recovers Luo Yinglong's eight fireproof red banners from the Tushita Eight Trigrams Furnace formation. They appear mundane apart from extreme durability; their usable mechanism remains unknown.
- Chu states that sufficiently concentrated internal energy allegedly becomes a cultivator's **True Essence**. Zheng's first attempt to compress internal energy through the Ring explodes, destroying both hands and injuring Cheng Xiao, demonstrating that this requires microscopic control.
- Stage-five Saints control energy; through mass–energy equivalence, perfect energy control transcends biological limits. Microscopic control at stage four is only the entry point. Chu prescribes repeatedly reducing the blast radius until Zheng can compress large amounts of energy, producing immense striking power even if he cannot control the eight banners.
- Planned revivals: **Qi Tengyi** and **Xiao Honglu** are definite, with Qi's ancient-script expertise intended to uncover side stories during returns to prior scenarios. Zheng offers to help revive **Ming Yanwei**, but Zhang Heng insists on waiting until he can personally protect and revive her.
- The juvenile black dragon from *Jurassic Park* is now larger than an ordinary puppy and can spit small sparks/flames after only a few dozen days. Natural maturation may require a century or longer; adult power could deter an ordinary fourth-stage expert.
- On returning to *The Mummy*, Team China learns the O'Connells now own several American gold mines; Imhotep and Anck-su-namun have joined Jonathan in China seeking an ancient emperor's immortality elixir. Jonathan possesses a purported map, is being pursued by warlords, and offers Zheng only thirty percent of any gains. With no mission prompt or location and a ten-person cost of 500 points per day, the team postpones the lead.
- **Xiao Honglu** is revived at Hamunaptra for 8,000 points and one B-rank side story. His revival vision shows Chu killing him in *Resident Evil* and reveals his central desire to escape isolation and life as an experimental subject. On recognizing Chu, he immediately proposes a wager in the next scenario.
- In three days of training, Zheng uses microscopic control to stabilize small compressed-energy deposits inside adapted regions of his body. He suspects clone Zheng may permanently maintain fourth stage because the clone's bat wings appeared continuously during their *Resident Evil* encounter.
- Zhao Yingkong spends one A-rank side story and 8,000 points to give Chu the **Lambda Driver (λ-Drive)**, an A-rank device structurally integrated into brain cells that converts conviction into attack or defense.
- Chu uses a visual hypnosis device to create absolute belief that his first shot will destroy a thirty-meter steel wall. The Lambda-amplified Gauss-pistol round produces a crater over twenty meters deep and fifty meters across. The hypnosis ends once that first conviction is fulfilled, leaving Chu unable to reproduce the output at will.
- Full Lambda Driver activation requires total certainty and heavily drains psychic energy. Team China has rune arrays converting internal energy → electricity → psychic energy and ample mithril. Chu plans two or three rechargeable mithril rings plus miniature conversion arrays to sustain the device; the remaining central limitation is generating conviction.
- Chu personally uses the Book of Amun-Ra to revive **Qi Tengyi**, thereby experiencing Qi's life and emotions. Qi Tengyi is now permanently restored but has no further revival available. **Zhang Jie** alone remains impossible to revive because his half-Guide constitution denied that right from the beginning.
- Pre-*Transformers* enhancement plan: Zero saves side stories for Square Mystic Eyes while buying stronger Gauss ammunition/attributes; Zhang Heng advances Wind Archer to **Lightning Archer** for one B-rank side story and helps Qi Tengyi; Zhao Yingkong may advance Shining; Xiao Honglu advances his magic-network access and purchases spells; Zheng spends one A-rank side story advancing Tiger Soul.
- Zheng identifies the **AllSpark** as a possible limitless-energy plot item and a priority target, while dangerously assuming *Transformers* may be an easy scenario and that he can defeat Megatron.
- A-rank Tiger Soul gains living white mist and a white-tiger spirit but is still an incomplete form. Contact fills Zheng with boundless hatred, triggers Explosion-level violence, and projects cutting force beyond the blade; his first loss of control nearly kills Bawang, Luo Gandao, Zhang Heng, Cheng Xiao, and others before Xiao Honglu orders full repair.
- Qi Tengyi reads the new oracle-bone inscription as the **Seven Great Hates**: youth dies too soon; time cannot return; worldly affairs are impermanent; the human heart is unknowable; life holds nothing worth loving; death grants no peace; Heaven and Earth possess no benevolence. Loyalty and righteousness yield only these seven hates.
- Three days of microscopic compression transform a small amount of Zheng's internal energy from gas → liquid/near-solid → a lighter, life-like gas. It loses ordinary internal-energy functions but assimilates unlimited internal or vampire energy without increasing in volume. Wrapped around his hand, it blocks Tiger Soul's mind control and links him to the hatred of the murdered white tiger spirit.
- The refined gas is confirmed as **True Essence**. Tiger Soul's creator used it while refining a strange stone; when the loyal white tiger swallowed the hostile molten stone, the creator tore out its spine to form the hilt/core, used the molten stone as the blade, and sealed the tiger's hatred into the weapon.
- A-rank Tiger Soul is now classified as a cultivation artifact/demonic artifact rather than an ordinary legendary magic weapon. It consumes True Essence slowly; without True Essence, contact drives the wielder mad. Zheng can now wield it safely while his reserve lasts.
- Tiger Soul's creator and other cultivators prepared weapons to fight stage-five Saints. Zheng and Xiao Honglu hypothesize that cultivators may have been the Saints' enemies and created many of the Lord God's diverse enhancements; otherwise powerful fourth-stage beings would have little reason to create weak bloodlines and abilities seemingly designed for later reincarnation teams.
- Zheng plans to use True Essence to activate Luo Yinglong's eight furnace banners. His initial three-day reserve was mostly consumed taming Tiger Soul, so he intends to buy extended time in another scenario and refine a much larger supply.
- Zheng privately buys ninety days in Middle-earth, taking several cubic meters of advanced medicine and luxury jewelry. Because Sauron dies without the One Ring being destroyed, the Three Elven Rings endure and most elves remain, prolonging the Age of the Elves in this altered continuity.
- Gandalf gives Zheng 100-plus D-to-C-rank energy stones and a three-cubic-meter storage pouch requiring no energy. Galadriel asks that the One Ring never return to Middle-earth, then trades 3,000-plus D-rank stones for Zheng's medicines and jewelry.
- Zheng pays 1,000 D-rank stones plus remaining medicine for Aragorn to hire 5,000-plus workers to excavate Saruman's tower, now 100–200 meters underground; the work is expected to require seven to nine months.
- During the ninety-day stay, Zheng refines one wisp of True Essence every several days under full fourth-stage microscopic control. It cannot enter rechargeable mithril rings, so he stores it in his dantian alongside internal energy. He does not risk refining vampire energy because failure would likely be fatal.
- Final *Transformers* preparations: Qi Tengyi receives physical attributes, dragon blood, T-virus prototype, a Dragonshard Necklace, and heavy-machine-gun practice but no special ability due to contribution limits. Zero, Zhao Yingkong, and Zhang Heng train to injury and repeatedly use full repair.
- Zhao Yingkong develops an unnamed attack she judges countless times stronger than Shining Vacuum Wave and difficult for either clone Zheng or Zhao Zhuikong to evade. She reads the in-universe novel *Terror Infinity* during her final rest day.
- Zhang Heng can feed a D-rank energy stone into the silver bow to release 100-plus energy arrows, then apply Lightning Arrow to the entire volley. The test creates a roughly one-kilometer crater and pulverizes one arm while dislocating the other.
- Wang Xia's Bomb Dominator/demonic energy now produces a thrown invisible/faint sphere and a stronger underground homing explosive creature; he can use only two before depletion and plans to test a winged homing bomb.
- Chu's completed glasses integrate internal-energy storage, internal energy → electricity → psychic energy conversion, and visual hypnosis, supporting sustained Lambda Driver use. Zheng charges them after entering *Transformers*.
- *Transformers* mission: **possess the AllSpark for one hour or destroy it**, then return. There are no newcomers, and the difficulty is calibrated for twelve people or fewer.
- The protected starting point in a sewer, massed mutilated corpses, and absence of an early-film location lead Chu/Xiao Honglu to infer an alternate post-film timeline in which the Decepticons obtained the AllSpark and began exterminating humanity.
- Xiao Honglu can summon a rideable **Celestial Hound** through first-level magic-network access.
- The devastated city contains 100-plus robots ranging from roughly six to eighteen meters. A human armored unit covering civilian evacuation is reduced to a handful of tanks because ordinary fire cannot harm the machines and even antitank weapons have limited effect.
- Luo Gandao's three-mode Robotech machine is smaller than every Transformer but uses a much larger internal-energy reserve to power its Dragonshard shield. Locked forms: **shuttle**, **Battloid**, and **Guardian**. Its four-meter **high-magnetic vibration blade** can rip open a Transformer's chest energy core.
- Luo destroys more than ten robots with missiles and one large black robot in melee, but concentrated missiles and laser pellets rapidly drain roughly protected reserves; the Decepticons learn to evade his missiles. Zero's arriving Gauss shot destroys another without requiring the Point-Line Mystic Eyes.
- A first-generation blue Decepticon leading the newly created machines has armor that resists Luo Gandao's vibration blade. Luo defeats it by severing its grip with heel thrusters, mounting it, and firing a point-blank full barrage; Bawang's unlimited-ammunition shoulder cannon then finishes the surrounding group.
- One battle against a few dozen robots consumes **80% of Luo's mecha energy reserve**, despite that reserve exceeding those of individual Dragonshard Necklaces. Ammunition can be replenished by the U.S. military, but only Zheng can replenish its internal energy.
- Lieutenant Colonel **André Roosevelt** leads roughly 1,000 surviving troops toward **Shelter Base Seven**. He reports Megatron seized the AllSpark about one week earlier; New York then produced hundreds of thousands of robots, radiation expanded outward, and modern aircraft/tanks transformed while obsolete tanks remained unaffected. Nearly every modern city worldwide is now devastated or infested, and the Autobots' fate is unknown.
- **Shelter Base Seven** is a refugee valley defended by 10,000-plus troops and 70-plus obsolete tanks, with no aircraft or precision electronics. Team China claims to be a twelve-person special-operations unit sent from 2077 after decades of human–machine war.
- After Zheng demonstrates strength and the team displays future equipment, surviving U.S. leadership orders contact and transfer to **Shelter Base One**, its most intact military installation. Team China rejects a thousands-strong escort because it would attract robot armies and rejects researchers' attempts to retain or inspect its gliders/mecha/weapons.
- Major irradiated cities now contain only hundreds of robots despite their potential to create 100,000-plus large machines and tens of millions of small ones. Chu and Xiao infer the missing armies are massed around the AllSpark, whose location is concealed by destroyed/blockaded surveillance.
- Chu and Xiao independently plan to steal U.S. nuclear launch control at Shelter Base One, then use roughly ten hydrogen bombs to flatten the suspected AllSpark city before entering. They expect the Decepticons to possess nuclear countermeasures around the AllSpark, so bombardment alone will not complete the mission.
- Search plan: Zheng and Zhan Lan use Psychic Scan city by city while Luo Gandao provokes each urban robot population with his mecha, then escapes by electrical propulsion. The critical danger is AllSpark radiation transforming Luo's piloted machine with him trapped inside.
- **Shelter Base One** is a modern mountain installation with intact aircraft, Apache helicopters, main battle tanks, research towers, and roughly two million civilians. America retains only about 13 million people and 1.2 million soldiers across nine shelters; computer projections place global human extinction roughly one year away.
- Acting U.S. President **Aslant Roosevelt** (`阿斯兰特·罗斯福`), formerly vice president, leads Base One. X-ray scans confirm all twelve Team China members are human and show even the weakest exceeds elite soldiers by one-to-two times in physical strength.
- U.S. forces debate nuclear use. Bases Three–Nine have fewer than 100,000 poorly equipped troops each; Bases One and Two retain limited air/armor because they guard humanity's remaining “hope.” Seawater blocks AllSpark radiation, leaving nuclear submarines and many global warheads intact.
- An unidentified nonhuman voice at Base One states its civilization derives from the AllSpark and has no capacity to control genetic life, while Megatron would only destroy humans. Base One's secret “hope” both blocks AllSpark radiation and may provide the only means to locate it and protect Team China's equipment.
- Chu deliberately builds fatal flaws into Team China's future-soldier cover so U.S. surveillance will discover a staged attempt to steal/change nuclear launch codes. Officials will then verify the genuine codes at a secret site, whose location and contents Zhan Lan can capture through Psychic Scan; Team China's indispensable combat value makes exposure of the cover survivable.
- Base One's underground complex contains the badly damaged but living **Optimus Prime** and **Bumblebee**; a third surviving Autobot is at Base Two. Spark energy in their chests functions as a miniature AllSpark field that protects nearby machinery from transformation.
- Optimus Prime, missing a foot and near death, offers to have his Spark energy removed and used to irradiate Team China's weapons with protective Autobot energy. He invokes the dead human boy's words, **“No sacrifice, no victory,”** and asks humanity to destroy the AllSpark rather than bear the robots' mistake.
- Autobot and Decepticon Spark radiation are opposing signatures that can sense one another at close range even through transformed disguises. Autobot protection would therefore prevent equipment transformation but expose any stealth assault, requiring a decoy to draw away over 70% of the defenders within one hour after the nuclear strike.
- Zheng can use the One Ring to convert internal energy into sampled Autobot Spark radiation, preserving Optimus Prime while irradiating Luo Gandao's mecha. Luo and Zheng/Zhan Lan begin separate five-day nationwide searches; Zheng must return to Base One on day three regardless of success.
- Xiao Honglu commands the decoy operation and may select three members other than Zheng, Zero, or Zhan Lan. Chu uses this as the first deliberate test of Team China's two-squad command structure.
- Chu identifies the One Ring, golden mask, Spear of Osiris, and Bracelet of Anubis as incomplete fifth-stage Saint artifacts centered on energy control. He questions whether the Lord God's Dimension is actually the Saints' final refuge/testament rather than a training ground designed to create new Saints.
- Chu gives Base One a fragment of **Gundanium alloy** from Luo Gandao's mecha to intensify surveillance. Nuclear codes are dynamic and single-use, so his real target is the internal code-generation process at the instant an authorized user opens its computer case with a nuclear key; Zhan Lan and Zhao Yingkong begin the operation.
- U.S. surveillance detects Chu's deliberately staged approach to the nuclear-code firewall. Researchers assess his smaller devices as within fifty years of current technology, Gundanium beyond even Transformer metallurgy, and mature spatial storage as 300–500 years ahead, preserving uncertainty around the future-soldier cover.
- Acting President Roosevelt's physical key opens a concealed **black case** containing the nuclear code-generation computer. After a false report claims Chu has breached it, Roosevelt privately unlocks the room, case, long password, and menus to verify no code was generated.
- Under Zhan Lan's mental control, Zhao Yingkong emerges from concealment, copies the black computer's internal data using a small optical disc, then remains hidden until guard rotation. Zhan Lan erases Roosevelt's memory afterward. Chu now possesses the U.S. nuclear launch-code system/data.
- The AllSpark is at **St. Louis**, rebuilt as a smaller steel fortress with metal ground/buildings and four perimeter towers several hundred meters tall. Its defenders include overwhelming ordinary robots and hundreds of helicopters, fighters, and shuttle-form machines firing explosive basketball-sized light-particle rounds; Luo escapes with only 10–20% mecha energy.
- Xiao Honglu theorizes AllSpark exposure drives robot evolution: prolonged irradiation improves power and eventually grants transformation/advanced weapons, explaining why hundreds of elite machines have emerged. Delaying months or years would produce millions of transforming robots and vastly stronger core Decepticons.
- Assault deployment: Xiao Honglu, Bawang, and Zhao Yingkong wait west as the decoy team; Chu's assault group waits 100 km east. After nuclear-code rotation, three hydrogen bombs strike successively. The decoy must probe within six hours, carrying Three Pure Ones talismans against fallout, then draw defenders west.
- Xiao secretly leaves a note and signal for the U.S. president, expecting military encirclement within a week and intending to exploit a “back door” in Chu's earlier plan so Team China can finish without casualties.
- Zhan Lan cannot implant delayed hypnotic instructions, making Zhao Yingkong's stealth essential to the black-case operation. Zhao secretly allows Xiao Honglu to append a second program to Chu's disc, remaining neutral in the strategists' contest because Xiao's plan presents virtually no downside to Team China.
- Xiao's appended **Trojan** activates within three hours after nuclear launch. Chu's main virus first removes the firewall/network protections; Xiao's code then issues an apparently authentic Base One order commanding U.S. forces around St. Louis to assault the fortress ruins, after which Chu's virus destroys the central computer so the command cannot be revoked.
- Xiao's cover message tells Roosevelt that future humanity possesses a rapid-rusting anti-robot bacterium and that Xiao, supposedly a future American citizen, wants the U.S. to inherit advanced technology and the AllSpark. This induces hundreds of thousands of surviving troops to surround St. Louis before the fifth-day dawn, ostensibly to detain Team China after victory but actually to become Xiao's decoy force.
- Chu's virus forces Base One's central computer into fatal computational overload, seals its power room behind automated defenses, and threatens base self-destruction if attacked. It automatically generates and confirms codes for three hydrogen bombs launched from Nuclear Submarines Three and Six/other surviving platforms.
- Xiao's Trojan orders the encircling U.S. army into St. Louis immediately after the third strike. Chu has anticipated and countered the plan: a fourth hydrogen bomb is scheduled for **three hours after the third**, precisely when the American forces will reach the city center.
- Before the first ICBM impacts, Chu asks Zheng to confront his defining principle—hands may be stained for survival, but not the heart—and asks whether he would still choose to live if his heart had already been stained.
- The first three hydrogen bombs are airbursts that erase the steel fortress's outer/middle layers but leave its deepest shield intact. Xiao's U.S. forces enter afterward and draw surviving robots out; Chu's fourth warhead is an earth-penetrating bunker buster that detonates amid the battle, killing hundreds of thousands of soldiers and the exposed machines while crushing hardened defenses.
- Zhao Yingkong confirms Chu anticipated Xiao's military-decoy plan from the beginning and instructed her to prevent Xiao entering until all U.S. troops were inside. Xiao realizes his scheme supplied the precise final step Chu needed for mass destruction.
- Zheng rejects Chu's method but accepts that insufficient personal strength made the sacrifice strategically necessary. He reaffirms his captain's duty to lead/protect the team, while warning he will oppose any future plan that kills comparable numbers of innocents.
- After cooling, the blast zone contains vitrified ground and a crater several kilometers across and nearly one kilometer deep. A translucent shield still protects the underground core, confirming Decepticon force-field technology.
- Assault formation: Zero takes high ground and snipes through the Soul Link, protected by Zhang Heng; Zhan Lan supplies Psychic Scan. Zheng, Wang Xia, Luo Gandao, and Chu enter the core. Tiger Soul plus True Essence cuts through the shield; Wang Xia is ordered to plant plasma bombs while Luo performs maximum structural destruction.
- Inside the core, Wang Xia stays behind to saturate the entry corridor with maximum-yield plasma bombs, mines, and timers, then plans to retreat by Green Goblin glider. Zheng will sever/detonate the passage after Luo escapes carrying the AllSpark; Luo is ordered to deliver it to Zero's group even if mortally wounded.
- Zheng enters a controlled, blade-sharp fourth-stage condition distinct from his former berserk state. He uses **Shave under Instantaneous Destruction**, Tiger Soul's white blade-light, and explosive fragmentation to annihilate advanced robots; black bat wings over three meters across emerge deliberately. Chu tentatively identifies this as the **intermediate fourth stage**.
- Zheng consciously yields difficult moral decisions to the “demon” encoded in his genes while retaining one final strand of reason. The state feels like complete bodily/mental freedom and reproduces the power that previously slaughtered hundreds of evolved Lickers.
- The inner hall contains **2,000–3,000 robots** and the AllSpark: a floating Rubik's-Cube-like block roughly one to two cubic meters, linked to the chamber by a pillar of intense electricity.
- **Starscream** has newly evolved **phase-shift armor** powered by the AllSpark; it resists Zheng's claw and converts energy into a powerful shockwave. **Megatron**, fourteen to fifteen meters tall and covered in superior multicolored energy, stands closest to the AllSpark and has grown vastly stronger through sustained exposure.
- Under massed fire Zheng is temporarily pinned. Chu activates the integrated functions of his glasses, his gaze sharpens under hypnosis/conviction, and he moves into the plaza too quickly for Luo Gandao to see.
- The transition toward intermediate fourth stage produces an **inner-demon threshold** analogous to cultivation: ancient awakened genes increasingly influence consciousness. Overcoming the resulting illusion/compulsion permanently strengthens will; failure can cause madness, personality loss, energy disorder, bodily explosion, or dissolution of consciousness into false supreme freedom.
- Zheng's autonomous combat consciousness makes constant microscopic genetic adjustments and destroys over one thousand advanced robots without Explosion, but risks permanently erasing his conscious self. His bat wings generate Blood Flame darker and denser than his learned form, extending outside the Dragonshard shield.
- Chu's glasses sustain five minutes of brainwave hypnosis into absolute confidence. Through the Lambda Driver, conviction overrides causality: he moves at Shave-like speed, uses Gun Kata with amplified Gauss needles, steps on an immaterial energy beam, leaps over twenty meters, disperses opposing beams, and drills through **Thundercracker's** phase-shift armor.
- Luo Gandao commits to a direct charge through the plaza toward the AllSpark. Electrical propulsion lets him evade much of the fire, but repeated impacts rapidly drain his Dragonshard shield, and demonized Zheng nearly kills him before Chu intervenes.
- At the height of Zheng's loss of control, near-black flames cover his bat wings, black rune-like markings spread over his skin, small horns emerge, and the dark fire blocks projectiles after his Dragonshard Necklace is exhausted. Under Destruction, he tears loose a roughly seventy-square-meter steel plate and uses it to crush robots.
- Chu amplifies his voice through the Lambda Driver into a destructive shock wave and invokes Zheng's promise that everyone will survive together. This shatters Zheng's false state of supreme freedom before he can kill Luo Gandao.
- As Zheng regains control, the black markings become fine scales, his bat wings become scaled dragon wings, and the horns and near-black flames disappear. He throws Luo Gandao's mecha toward the AllSpark, then charges Megatron with Tiger Soul.
- Megatron's superior multicolored armor resists Tiger Soul, Instantaneous Destruction, dusky flame, and True Essence, and draws power directly from the AllSpark to restore damage. Metal shavings from his body can become palm-sized armed copies, while invisible shock waves can repel Zheng.
- Sustained Lambda Driver use drains Chu visibly: doubt causes his invisible barrier to crack, and his hair rapidly turns white. Zheng protects the collapsing Chu with the eight defensive flags made by Luo Yinglong.
- Luo Gandao removes the AllSpark from its lightning pillar. Zheng enters Destruction, tears through phase-shift armor by force, and hurls Luo's mecha into the escape corridor while the Decepticons prioritize recovering the AllSpark.
- True Essence transforms the Spear of Judgment by manifesting a savage, living-looking beast around it. Thrown at the plaza entrance, it smashes through robots and collapses the corridor, after which its stored energy is exhausted.
- During the escape, Luo Gandao breaks through to the **second stage of the Genetic Constraint**. He rapidly shifts among Guardian, shuttle, and Battloid modes to evade fire, preserve the nearly depleted Dragonshard reserve, carry the AllSpark, and cut down blocking robots.
- Outside the crater, Zero's Gauss sniper rifle can penetrate phase-shift armor but fires too slowly to stop hundreds of pursuers. Zhang Heng's broad energy-thread volley is inefficient against robots unless it hits critical systems, and Bawang's rapid-fire cannon can delay but not destroy the evolved machines.
- With his mecha effectively out of energy, Luo Gandao is seized by an advanced robot. Zhao Yingkong uses Excalibur to sever the arm carrying the AllSpark rather than the hand holding Luo; the robot crushes the cockpit and the mecha explodes, killing Luo. The team immediately destroys his killer but is shaken by its first veteran death since becoming much stronger.
- An aircraft-carrier-scale Transformer begins climbing from the kilometer-deep crater; its hand alone is roughly one hundred meters long. Xiao Honglu explicitly extends the AllSpark transformation rule to humanity's largest machines, including battleships, destroyers, and aircraft carriers.
- Wang Xia learns to use Bomb Dominator demonic energy to conceal plasma bombs, attach them to passing moving targets, manifest them remotely, and trigger them at chosen times and positions. Detonating dozens together creates a concentrated liquid-plasma field that destroys dozens of advanced robots, but remote control places extreme strain on his limited demonic energy.
- Xiao Honglu defines Team China's strength as coordinated specialization rather than solo power: Zero at extreme range, Zhang Heng at medium range, Bawang with heavy firepower, Wang Xia with prepared explosive fields, Chu in strategy, Zheng in direct combat, plus support roles. Wang Xia's prepared battlefield can approach Zheng's combat power, but requires bombs, space, time, concealment, and teammates.
- Roughly seventy of the strongest Decepticons remain below, including Megatron and phase-shift elites. Zheng's True Essence, vampire energy, and internal energy near exhaustion; Destruction would last only two or three more seconds, forcing him to rely primarily upon his intermediate-fourth-stage body while waiting out the mission timer.
- The aircraft-carrier Transformer stands more than two kilometers tall and carries over one hundred naval-gun-sized missile launchers. Zero uses the Point-Line Mystic Eyes and Gauss sniper rifle to strike a shifting death line beside a minor shoulder port; the entire machine disintegrates into powder without exploding, and the effort renders Zero unconscious.
- Excalibur can accept exactly five energy stones in its hilt, then release their energy as a laserlike band guided by Zhao Yingkong's consciousness. Combining this output with Shining Vacuum Wave creates an expanding semicircular oscillating light curtain that pulverizes most remaining robots and severely weakens phase-shift armor.
- The aircraft-carrier robot's step pulverizes Zhao Yingkong's legs below the thighs and crushes the AllSpark into inert-looking metal fragments. Team China collects every fragment because Xiao Honglu infers that the missing kill/side-plot rewards mean the AllSpark itself was the scenario's exceptional team-strengthening reward.
- Xiao Honglu identifies two escalating dangers: Team China's high Lord God evaluation will produce increasingly lethal scenarios while continued survival raises it further, and the team may receive fewer preparation worlds before being matched against exceptionally strong teams such as Celestial or Devil.
- After killing the remaining underground robots, Zheng finds Chu inside the Tushita Eight Trigrams Furnace barrier aged to roughly eighty or ninety by Lambda Driver use. Zheng's body is simultaneously collapsing at the genetic level. Both expect to survive only until return; Chu concludes Team China has no path except continuous growth toward power beyond the Lord God's finite difficulty escalation.
- Team China returns with eleven survivors; Luo Gandao's death is final because he had already used his resurrection. Zheng initially lashes out at the surface team, then reaffirms that everyone will live on carrying Luo's share of their collective promise.
- The next scenario is **A Nightmare on Elm Street 3**, reinforcing Chu and Xiao Honglu's prediction that Team China's high evaluation will generate increasingly lethal worlds. Xiao proposes sacrificing newcomers to lower or stabilize that evaluation, but Zheng refuses to address the proposal immediately.
- The Lord God identifies an AllSpark fragment as a **Spatial Conversion Energy Device fragment** and states that repairing it will let it provide energy again. Chu interprets the AllSpark as technology that converts space itself into energy, potentially granting effectively boundless energy and, at the extreme, the capacity to create worlds, life, or universes.
- Chu's quantum/relativity exposition is an in-world theoretical claim, not verified science. He uses causal concepts to classify the Lambda Driver and Point-Line Mystic Eyes as ultimately scientific phenomena and argues that the Lord God's Dimension represents science at its highest level.
