- Chu further theorizes that Lambda Driver selects a desired effect from possible causal lines, whereas the Point-Line Mystic Eyes sever causality and erase a target's worldly existence. These are explicitly in-world speculative explanations. Practical limits remain severe: Zero estimates only 70% success against stationary targets and roughly 10% against a full-speed Zheng; each unrepaired Lambda Driver use consumes Chu's lifespan.
- Team China receives only the fixed 1,000 points from *Transformers*. Zheng reserves more than 10,000 points, plans a ninety-day return to *The Lord of the Rings* to refine True Essence and inspect the mage tower, still intends to bring Imhotep from *The Mummy*, and orders the team to conserve resources before *A Nightmare on Elm Street 3*.
- The nearly completed **Magic Cannon** has a silver antique-cannon body and uses the unique skeletal warhorse's four gravity-defying legs as an all-terrain locomotion/aiming platform. It routes Zheng's internal and vampire energy through the circulation pattern of Destruction, then condenses the result through the One Ring; even Chu cannot estimate its output.
- Chu classifies *A Nightmare on Elm Street* as an “unsolvable” horror series and rushes the Magic Cannon in case physical destruction can erase the entire town. Chu and Xiao estimate Team China may encounter Devil Team again within four or five scenarios.
- Team China's Devil-Team readiness audit: Zheng cannot yet defeat his clone or overcome his inner demon; Zhang Heng cannot shoot his lover's clone; Zhao Yingkong cannot yet surpass Zhao Zhuikong; Zero/Bawang/Wang Xia are vulnerable when formation and range collapse; Zhan Lan may face two enemy psychics; Xiao Honglu must overcome fear of clone Chu. Chu defines the only viable long-term path as defeating Devil Team and becoming the strongest reincarnation team.
- During his ninety-day *Lord of the Rings* return, Zheng studies his controllable genetic remnants: vampire genes generate vampire energy; incomplete dragon genes strengthen physical attributes and regeneration; a third ancient-demon sequence spreads through barriers and dominates surrounding enhanced genes when energized. He remains trapped between early and intermediate fourth stage because he cannot safely overcome the inner demon or deliberately reproduce the extreme emotional catalyst behind previous breakthroughs.
- Gandalf plans an unprecedented Isengard mage tower built above the excavated sunken tower, combining aboveground and underground functions. He defines wizards primarily as seekers who investigate magic, society, history, and interspecies balance; towers are laboratories and archives rich in knowledge and magical materials rather than ordinary treasure.
- Tiger Soul, ignoring its corruptive danger and harsh use conditions, now surpasses ordinary A-rank legendary magical weapons and is identified as a cultivator-refined **demonic artifact**. It clears the collapsed tower floors, but Gandalf requires more than seventy days to dispel the ownerless magical restrictions and expose a floor of pictographic ancient research records.
- Gandalf senses Zheng's spiritual defenses are riddled with openings and warns he could become like Saruman. No external cure exists; Gandalf advises him to hold unwaveringly to the conviction he has always upheld.
- Qi Tengyi identifies the mage-tower records as a mature **pre-ancient civilization script**, older than pictographs yet grammatically fluent. His tentative translation names a year when the Ice Phoenix took flight, the fall of the Saint of the West, an alliance between Saints and a truth-seeking/cultivator collective, a pyrrhic victory that destroyed both groups, a sealed passage destined to reopen, and the dying Saint **Anubis** as recorder. Accuracy remains uncertain pending roughly a year of comparative translation.
- Team China postpones its return to *The Mummy*: Jonathan's earlier letter from China may herald a time-limited side plot, making a short solo visit dangerously inescapable. The future plan is for the full team to enter together after earning more points.
- Cheng Xiao receives the B-rank legendary magical gauntlets **Gale Fists**, permanently enchanted to harm incorporeal beings, accelerate punches like a gale, and permit both open-palm and closed-fist techniques.
- **Soul-Guiding Incense** instantly awakens anyone who smells it from a nightmare, even if the soul itself is trapped in the dream. Team China buys it, protective talismans, spirit ammunition, medicines, and minor physical enhancements for Xiao Honglu and Qi Tengyi, exhausting all point and ranked-reward reserves before *A Nightmare on Elm Street 3*.
- Cheng Xiao's hidden combat power comes partly from a **life-bound Gu**, a family-cultivated beneficial genetically mutated parasite housed in his dantian. Activating it causes severe abdominal pain but briefly raises his speed and strength to roughly Zheng's Explosion level.
- Cheng can also seal his own acupoints with golden needles to multiply strength, speed, reactions, and durability, using a breath-suspension technique to mitigate—but not eliminate—the severe bodily damage. His circular boxing chains more than one hundred vital-point strikes while preventing the target from being knocked out of range.
- The Magic Cannon is complete. Chu adds an energy-stone firing mode to preserve Zheng's internal energy; although weaker than the Destruction-powered mode, Xiao estimates it still matches an ordinary nuclear weapon. Team China enters *A Nightmare on Elm Street 3* immediately afterward.
- *A Nightmare on Elm Street 3* begins at **fifteen-person difficulty**: eleven veterans plus newcomers **Liu Yu** (`刘郁`), **Wang Fenlan** (`王芬兰`), **Mo Li** (`莫丽`), and **Ouyang Wei** (`欧阳微`), all students. Mission: survive thirty days; killing Freddy grants every team member 5,000 points and one B-rank side plot.
- Zheng accepts the four newcomers as provisional Team China members. He rejects deliberate sacrifice to reduce Lord God evaluation because violating his conscience may worsen his mental openings, and because newcomers can contribute specialized value even without obvious combat talent.
- Attempting to leave town by taxi forces every group into sleep and returns them to the starting town. Freddy appears as the driver and can leave apparent physical traces in the taxis. Soul-Guiding Incense awakens Liu Yu, but Chu reports sleeping without dreaming and believes his hypnosis would counter Freddy if the demon entered his mind.
- Xiao Honglu infers the town cannot be left and that the world may differ from their assumptions; Chu forbids him from voicing the full hypothesis aloud, stating not that he is unwilling but that he **cannot** explain it.
- Night arrangement: three adjoining rooms of five, each with a rotating watcher who must light Soul-Guiding Incense immediately if a sleeper shows nightmare signs. Women's room: Zhao Yingkong, Zhan Lan, and three female newcomers. Chu's room: Chu, Zero, Bawang, Zhang Heng, Cheng Xiao. Zheng's room: Zheng, Wang Xia, Xiao Honglu, Qi Tengyi, Liu Yu.
- Despite meditating deliberately, Zheng falls asleep without noticing. He witnesses newcomer Mo Li being dismembered and reduced to a skeleton by Freddy, then awakens in the inn after unconsciously activating Explosion and destroying his bed.
- After Mo Li's disappearance, every person except Zheng remembers only three newcomers—Wang Fenlan, Ouyang Wei, and Liu Yu—and insists Mo Li never existed. Zheng retains precise memories of her face and voice.
- Xiao Honglu hypothesizes that the Lord God placed Team China directly into a shared dream when the protective barrier vanished. Pain, usable energy, and apparent physical reality do not disprove the theory because the dreaming brain can generate all perceived experience.
- Proposed Freddy mechanism: the dream world is sustained by memory, including teammates' memories of one another. Freddy kills a person, erases everyone else's memory of that person, and makes the victim truly cease to exist once no one remembers them. Xiao warns every member never to forget anyone, or the cycle of killing, erasure, and fear will annihilate Team China.
- Xiao Honglu divides the scenario into three states: **waking reality**, the **safe dream world** belonging to the dreamer, and the **inescapable nightmare** Freddy controls. Freddy may observe speech, behavior, and inner thoughts in the safe dream but must first break its protective shell by exploiting fear, temptation, corrupted memories, or trusted relationships before he can kill.
- Revised defense: keep everyone in one house, allow sleep but maintain four simultaneous watchers, and use Zhan Lan's Soul Link to detect violent emotional surges that may indicate Freddy's attack. Plans should not be spoken unnecessarily because Freddy may be listening throughout the safe dream.
- Zhang Heng becomes separated from Chu and Zhao Yingkong in a crowded daylight street and is pulled into an inescapable nightmare built from his defining trauma: abandoning Ming Yanwei during an assault. His silver bow and +4 arrow initially function, but after he commits emotionally to repeating the rescue, both vanish; he is beaten and involuntarily retreats while Ming Yanwei condemns him, allowing Freddy to manifest beside him.
- Xiao Honglu hints that the shared nightmare possesses both a central **source** and a second way to make the dream disappear besides waking its dreamer. He cannot state the method while Freddy may be listening and explicitly entrusts the team's lives to Zheng.
- After Zhang Heng is erased, everyone except Zheng remembers Team China as ten veterans plus three newcomers, thirteen total. Zheng retains Zhang's memory and moves the survivors into one villa, requiring everyone to remain together or travel in pairs while deploying talismans, Soul-Guiding Incense, weapons, and the eight Tushita furnace banners.
- Xiao's probabilistic death-sight still functions in the dream and shows death aura on nearly every team member. His current escape theory has only a thirty-percent probability; testing it risks total annihilation.
- Freddy draws Xiao into a burning-building nightmare centered on a charred female skeleton and a traumatic staircase. Xiao knowingly follows on the Celestial Hound, using ice rays to advance. He reasons that mental engagement is reciprocal: if Freddy must expose Xiao's psychological opening, Freddy's own mind must also open, creating the key Zheng needs. Freddy then drags the weeping Xiao through the burned door.
- Under third-stage simulation of Xiao's thinking, Zheng infers that time need not flow equally between the safe dream and inescapable nightmares. He then discovers his memory of Mo Li has almost vanished, including her name and face.
- Xiao Honglu is erased next. Everyone except Zheng now remembers only twelve people—nine veterans and three newcomers—and denies Xiao ever existed. Zheng associates a sudden burned-plastic odor with Freddy, whose original death was by fire.
- To resist memory erasure, Zheng writes Zhang Heng, Xiao Honglu, and all twelve current members on a physical roster, includes his own name, and requires everyone to sign beside their entry. He orders even a sole eventual survivor to remember every listed person.
- By day eighteen, Zheng infers the shared dream most likely began during the taxi escape rather than at arrival, because the initial protective barrier, mission terms, and four newcomers were real. He recalls that Zhang Jie's former team was annihilated after its driver became Freddy, yet Zhang Jie performed some incomplete act sufficient to earn Lord God recognition as captain.
- At midnight, the two remaining female newcomers accompany Zhan Lan to the bathroom, vanish, and are immediately forgotten by her. Outside, Zero sees them beside Freddy and fires a spirit Gauss round; within his perception the targets become his dead mother and his younger brother, who was raised dressed as a girl, and the single shot pierces both.
- Freddy exploits Zero's guilt and taboo family memories, animating the apparent corpses like mother and doll until Zero's mental defenses collapse and he is dragged into darkness. Zheng witnesses Freddy take Zero and enters Destruction in pursuit.
- After leaving the bathroom, Zhan Lan forgets the two newcomer girls and hears a deeply familiar male voice ask whether she loves Zheng so much, marking the opening of her own targeted nightmare.
- Freddy uses the apparent warmth of Zhan Lan's dead fiancé, then accusations of betrayal and sexual degradation, to break her defenses and erase her. Wang Xia is drawn into his old home and broken by false news that his mother died alone after he left; his love and filial guilt are his sole major psychological opening.
- After Zero, Wang Xia, and Zhan Lan vanish, only seven people remain: Zheng, Bawang, Chu, Cheng Xiao, Qi Tengyi, Zhao Yingkong, and Liu Yu. Their written names disappear from Zheng's signed roster along with everyone else's memories, proving ordinary external records do not resist Freddy's erasure.
- Zheng develops a dangerous hypothesis: everyone else may be a construct within his personal dream, making his companions the nightmare's sustaining source. Killing them might remove that source, but whether they are real or false, attacking them would itself create the hesitation and guilt Freddy needs.
- Liu Yu observes that characters in the original film can imagine dream powers. Zheng infers that the shared world's extreme realism and **habitual thought** constrain imagination like simulated reality in *The Matrix*. He further infers Freddy selected him because of his unstable mind, his obsession with returning every teammate safely, and his detailed knowledge of the veterans' pasts and weaknesses.
- Bawang's nightmare uses a dead war comrade, half a bottle of cheap vodka, and their shared farewell song. Bawang accepts the drink before attacking, exposing the comradeship central to his identity. After the bathroom event, both Bawang and Zhao Yingkong are erased from the remaining group's memory; Zheng interprets this as further confirmation that the others are leaving his personal dream. 
- Zhao Yingkong's central weakness is no longer simple hatred of Zhao Zhuikong but contradiction: she hopes he had hidden reasons for killing their companions while fearing he truly murdered without emotion. Freddy exploits this hesitation through a lifelike Zhao Zhuikong who tortures the severed heads and remembered voices of her dead assassin friends; Zhao's defenses collapse when she mercy-kills a speaking skull and charges him.
- After Bawang and Zhao Yingkong are erased, only Zheng, Chu, Cheng Xiao, Qi Tengyi, and Liu Yu remain in Zheng's perceived dream. Zheng believes Freddy is waiting for the complete destruction of his obsession that every companion survive, but still lacks Xiao's final method for terminating the nightmare after its source is removed.
- Cheng Xiao's reflexive return to his usual personality despite his erased memories gives Zheng the final insight. Zheng concludes that Freddy built the shared dream from Zheng's consciousness because Zheng resurrected the veterans and therefore knows all their histories and psychological vulnerabilities. Freddy avoids attacking Zheng until the others fall because Zheng's mind sustains the dream; attacking him too soon could collapse it and awaken everyone.
- The veterans vanish because Freddy can exploit those known vulnerabilities and pull them into private nightmares, whereas the physically weaker newcomers can simply be killed in the safe dream, leaving visible corpses. Zheng now sees *A Nightmare on Elm Street 3* as a Lord God trial of his inner demon: success may carry him into the intermediate fourth stage, while failure means Team China's total annihilation.
- Xiao Honglu's final hint is resolved: besides awakening a dreamer, a nightmare ends when its dreamer dies. Zheng plans to destroy Freddy and then kill himself so that the shared nightmare will disappear. The sexualized description of sixteen-year-old Zhao Yingkong that precipitates Cheng Xiao's characteristic response is omitted in translation while its causal narrative function is retained.
- Cheng Xiao's psychological vulnerability is his childhood sweetheart. Freddy recreates her as an exploited immigrant sex worker and uses the bar patrons' degrading remarks to provoke Cheng into violence; Cheng recognizes the attack but pursues Freddy when the demon leads her away, and is erased. Team China is reduced to Zheng, the apparent Chu Xuan, Qi Tengyi, and Liu Yu.
- Zheng's plan depends on Qi Tengyi: Chu, not Zheng, resurrected him, so Zheng does not know his psychological vulnerability and Freddy cannot derive it from Zheng's mind. Zheng tests the apparent Chu's sensory fidelity by asking about the food, warns Qi to trust the strength granted by dragon-blood baptism and the first-stage Genetic Constraint, and then draws Tiger Soul on Chu.
- The “Chu Xuan” in Zheng's safe dream is Freddy's imitation, only superficially accurate: it lacks Chu's intellect, eats more than Chu would, cannot plan, and cannot use the Lambda Driver. The real Chu cannot be drawn into Freddy's world because his emotional incompleteness gives him no known psychological vulnerability, while the Lambda Driver could reshape the dream and destroy Freddy.
- Zheng attacks the false Chu to expose Freddy and entrusts the actual kill to Qi Tengyi. Freddy immediately casts Zheng into his own nightmare: the original Devil Team's abuse of Luo Li and beating of clone Zheng, the formative atrocity that drove the clone to become the strongest Reincarnation fighter. Explicit sexual violence against the child is omitted; its fact and traumatic consequences are retained non-graphically.
- Unable to ignore the reenacted atrocity, Zheng enters fourth-stage Genetic Constraint, Destruction, and the ancient-demon form together and kills the phantoms, knowingly sealing himself into the nightmare. Clone Zheng then appears, accusing the original's kindness of being hypocritical weakness.
- The false Chu attacks Qi Tengyi and Liu Yu with two Gauss pistols but initially lacks the real Chu's Genetic Constraint, Gun Kata, and Lambda Driver. Qi's Dragonshard Necklace blocks the shots while Liu Yu's arm is disabled; Qi enters the first-stage Genetic Constraint, carries Liu out, and keeps running because Liu realizes that motion and distance in the dream persist through the dreamer's own expectations.
- Freddy grows stronger from fear, rage, and despair and begins reproducing more of Chu's accuracy as Zheng's mind approaches collapse. Meanwhile, Zhang Heng remembers the courage he possessed as a child, rejects endless flight from his guilt toward Ming Yanwei, kills the Freddy inside his private nightmare, and awakens in the shared dream with unwavering resolve. He arrives with his silver bow as Freddy and false Chu pursue Qi.
- Zhang Heng's evolved B-rank elven bloodline transforms **Arrow of Wind** (`风之矢`) into **Lightning Arrow** (`电之矢`). Fired through his silver bow with a +4 enchanted arrow, it disintegrates one Freddy and collapses a distant building wall; its speed, accuracy, and power approach Zero's Point-Line Mystic Eyes but do not share that ability's defense-negating property. After his mental breakthrough, Zhang remains standing and can use it again after roughly five minutes.
- As Zheng's mind deteriorates, false Chu acquires the unlocked state and Gun Kata; incoming attacks such as Zhang's two-arrow Explosive Arrow are intercepted by Gauss rounds. Freddy can resurrect indefinitely and multiply while his true self remains in the shared dream, with his number and power increasing alongside Zheng's despair.
- Wang Xia begins overcoming his nightmare by reconciling filial devotion with military duty: although he missed his mother's death, he resolves to honor her with the technology, knowledge, and power he brings home from the Lord God's Dimension and to prove that her son fulfilled both her hopes and his duty.
- Wang Xia escapes his private nightmare and manifests numerous winged bomb creatures, each carrying a plasma grenade. A coordinated plasma barrage can excavate a crater more than twenty meters wide, but leaves him severely exhausted. He rescues the critically wounded Qi Tengyi and Liu Yu from another Freddy manifestation.
- Liu Yu identifies himself and Qi as the last two **anchors** maintaining the safe dream once the veterans have entered private nightmares. He theorizes that a dream needs both dreamers and dream-information: the survivors supply the former and Freddy/false Chu the latter. If both anchors die, the veterans will have nowhere to awaken and will remain trapped.
- False Chu reaches second-stage Genetic Constraint and can intercept Zhang Heng's two- and three-arrow Explosive Arrows with Gun Kata. Zhang's Lightning Arrow requires roughly five minutes between uses. Cheng Xiao defeats his private nightmare by recognizing that love exists through chosen trust; he refuses to let Freddy's illusion make him doubt or punish his childhood sweetheart.
- Cheng Xiao awakens directly between false Chu and Zhang Heng, shields Zhang with his Dragonshard Necklace, and uses **Hunyuan Taiji Fist** (`浑圆太极拳`), an advanced close-range circular boxing art, with his B-rank enchanted gloves. He drives false Chu out of the bar but gravely injures his fist against the imitation's nascent Lambda Driver barrier.
- False Chu's Lambda Driver begins manifesting as Zheng approaches mental collapse; a fluctuation from it collapses the entire bar. Zhang needs a little over two more minutes before Lightning Arrow can be used again. Freddy meanwhile produces three fast demolition vehicles carrying wrecking balls and bends the dream's geography to funnel Wang Xia, Qi Tengyi, and Liu Yu back toward the villa.
- Zhao Yingkong overcomes her private nightmare by rejecting the false Zhao Zhuikong's claim that companionship weakens an assassin. She defines assassination pragmatically—any weapon and method that kills the target qualifies—and recognizes that her companions preserved her life. Her eyes and killing intent take on the purity associated with Zheng's first fourth-stage awakening, and she instantly beheads the imitation despite its microscopic control.
- Zhao emerges into the shared dream as a clearly visible yet impossibly fast black shadow. She splits a descending wrecking ball in half, runs along its chain, kills the Freddy driver, and attacks the remaining vehicles, rescuing Wang Xia, Qi Tengyi, and Liu Yu. A sexualized taunt involving sixteen-year-old Zhao is omitted while its provocation and her response are preserved.
- The awakened Zhao Yingkong says her ability to “revive” in the dream proves she was not truly annihilated. She asks the survivors to tell real-world Chu about her strength and behavior and to devise a self-hypnosis method capable of recreating the extreme dream stimulus so she can revive completely. Her changed manner and self-reference are preserved without prematurely resolving their meaning.
- In his nightmare, Zheng fights the clone as remembered from *Resident Evil 2*. Destruction briefly lets him suppress the clone but cannot penetrate the clone's black flames. Facing exhaustion and death, Zheng knowingly enters the intermediate fourth stage despite the inner demon, taking on wings and horns as his ancient genes control his body.
- False Chu can now combine an incomplete Lambda Driver with Gauss fire, though using conviction still sharply reduces his Gun Kata accuracy. Lambda-amplified missed shots can nevertheless strike and threaten multistory buildings.
- Lambda-amplified Gauss rounds can sever a building at its base and carry destructive fluctuations hundreds of meters through an urban block. Conviction can act as either shield or spear according to the user's mental image; false Chu's accuracy improves continuously as Zheng's mind deteriorates.
- After overcoming his inner demon, Zhang Heng can finally use his full ability, placing him among Team China's five strongest members and arguably above Zero in overall strength. He theorizes that Arrow of Wind and Lightning Arrow concentrate all elven energy into one lethal burst; by feeding that energy gradually through his body instead, he begins developing an Explosion/Destruction-like state that enables gravity-defying wall running while preserving enough power to fight.
- Zhang's three-arrow Explosive Arrow with +4 enchanted shafts can penetrate one to two centimeters into false Chu's incomplete Lambda Driver barrier and drive him backward, but the barrier ultimately shatters the arrow. False Chu then blasts Zhang through a building and breaks both his legs.
- Zhang proposes a four-arrow Explosive Arrow, a technique he has practiced since upgrading his elven bloodline. Its successful-release rate was about five percent and its effective-hit rate about one percent during training; he now believes his healed mind will let him execute it. Each additional layer is geometrically harder and stronger, culminating theoretically in Nine Arrows Shooting Down the Sun.
- With Cheng Xiao deliberately holding position and throwing him upward, Zhang successfully performs four-arrow Explosive Arrow. It pierces false Chu's incomplete Lambda Driver and embeds more than halfway into his chest but does not kill him; the +4 enchanted shaft then corrodes the wound. Cheng and Zhang retreat toward the villa.
- The awakened Zhao identifies Zheng's deeper trap: his obsession is not merely a psychological wound but the imperative that he and all companions survive, which has sustained him since entering the horror worlds and now prevents the necessary solution.
- **Shining Wave** (`闪灵波动`) is Zhao Yingkong's self-created precursor/related technique to Shining Vacuum Wave: rapid sword motion under Shining produces a long-range distortion wave reaching dozens or hundreds of meters. In the dream, the awakened Zhao uses it to detonate an air-to-ground missile and push the explosion nearly one hundred meters away.
- The awakened Zhao estimates full-power Shining Vacuum Wave should affect at least five hundred meters, but her long-dead body's condition and return to the lowest fourth-stage level limit her. She can throw invisible Excalibur roughly one kilometer to destroy an attack helicopter; black tadpole-shaped runes briefly appear on her arm.
- Cheng Xiao's golden-needle treatment plus Zhang Heng's remaining elven energy temporarily restores Zhang's injured fingers. A demonstrated three-arrow Explosive Arrow severs a tree over one meter thick from one kilometer away and continues for several kilometers, but Gun Kata remains its natural counter by destroying the shafts before collision.
- The awakened Zhao believes she could defeat real-world Chu by surviving the Lambda Driver's five-minute limit, then killing him instantly. Her concern is that false Chu's dream-created conviction may have no such duration limit.
- Zheng sustains Destruction for more than two minutes inside the dream, exhausting internal and vampire energy and beginning bodily collapse while the clone remains unharmed behind living black flame. The state of supreme freedom suppresses all pain and self-preservation, so the inner demon will make Zheng maintain Destruction until he dies.
- False Chu survives his chest wound through conviction alone, floating half a meter above the ground because of severe blood loss. Four attack helicopters simultaneously surround the villa as he approaches. His Lambda Driver can excavate a trench over one hundred meters long and ten meters wide with a hand motion and blocks more than one hundred close-range stabs plus Shining Vacuum Wave.
- The awakened Zhao briefly considers abandoning the group, which begins to rouse the ordinary Zhao Yingkong consciousness. She nevertheless attacks false Chu, moving at Shining-level speed while retaining full reaction control and paradoxically remaining visually distinct. She possesses a stronger unnamed technique comparable to four-arrow Explosive Arrow but doubts the current body can withstand it.
- Zero overcomes his private nightmare by recognizing that Team China ended his lifelong solitude and gave him companions worth living and fighting beside. He awakens in the safe dream and uses the Point-Line Mystic Eyes with his Gauss sniper rifle to disintegrate false Chu and the surrounding Lambda Driver field, saving Zhao.
- All four helicopters launch missiles immediately afterward. With false Chu dead and the survivors exhausted, Zhao concludes their survival depends entirely on Zheng seeing through his obsession.
- At the brink of death, Zheng leaves the inner demon and empathizes with the clone's hatred, despair, and death wish. He realizes his supposedly selfless insistence that everyone survive also conceals his own need for enough companions and strength to live. He formulates the lesson of **舍得** as “to gain, one must first be willing to let go.”
- Zheng uses True Essence to activate Tiger Soul's white blade light and escape the clone's grip. He vows to meet the clone as an equal in reality and lead him out of suffering, then deliberately leaps into his own blade-light cloud—the final self-killing step intended to end the nightmare.
- The shared dream ends immediately before the missiles strike. Zhao Yingkong, Zero, Zhang Heng, Cheng Xiao, Wang Xia, Qi Tengyi, and Liu Yu awaken in the original taxis; the three female newcomers remain dead, Zhao and the previously absent veterans remain trapped asleep, and all injuries suffered in the dream carry into reality. Zheng's self-inflicted blade light reduces his body to fragments while leaving clothing and equipment intact.
- Real-world Chu confirms returning members by demanding visible dream injuries, then massacres more than one hundred nearby civilians on the highway. The Lord God immediately announces Freddy's death and awards every team member 5,000 points plus one B-rank side plot, establishing that the physical Freddy was among the people Chu killed. Wang Xia destroys the highway scene with plasma bombs after the group recovers Zheng's rings, scriptures, other equipment, and sleeping members.
- Team members still trapped by psychological contradictions do not awaken merely because the safe dream's source and Freddy are dead. Chu compares them to self-aware binary programs endlessly attempting to solve a question outside zero and one; apart from achieving their own breakthrough, the only solution is “formatting.”
- Chu concludes Freddy was a physical human with an ancient-demon dream ability, not an incorporeal immortal: he likely survived the neighborhood fire, hid for years, and later awakened powers resembling a Dementor or Nightmare. An exclusively dream-bound invincible entity would contradict the Lord God's ancient-enemy framework and humanity's continued existence.
- Chu identifies returning dreamers through injuries carried into reality, then kills every nearby candidate before Freddy can escape. Had the Lord God not confirmed Freddy's death among the civilians, Chu intended to test and potentially kill the conscious team members next.
- Zhao Yingkong's post-dream sleep and radically stronger alternate behavior lead Chu to infer that there is “another Zhao Yingkong,” the personality/identity that truly possessed the assassin clan's greatest-genius ability. Preserve this as an inference rather than fully resolved canon.
- Team China plans to awaken the sleepers through a psyche-force user's Mind Chain. Training a replacement to the necessary level would take at least four scenarios and duplicate Zhan Lan's role, so Chu instead plans to capture the next opposing team alive and coerce its psyche-force user if Zhan Lan has not awakened.
- Zheng's death sharply lowers the Lord God's evaluation of Team China. Chu estimates that overcoming his psychological vulnerability makes Zhang Heng three to four times stronger and calls Zhang, Zheng, the late Luo Gandao, and partly Cheng Xiao **emotional miracles**: people whose large vulnerabilities suppress ordinary performance but permit two- or threefold output after a breakthrough.
- Chu defines the **power to survive** as the full combination of force, will, intellect, knowledge, and equipment. A person able to dominate Freddy or resist inner demons need not die to break a survival obsession; an “unsolvable” scenario is therefore relative to the participant's capability. Wang Xia concludes that Zheng's excessive humanity and Chu's absence of it complement each other and together make Team China whole.
- During the final days of the scenario, Team China becomes socially and strategically hollow without Zheng. The veterans recognize that his obsession with everyone's survival, despite poor formal leadership, is what holds the team together; Chu alone cannot sustain it.
- Zheng previously died and returned via the Resurrection Cross in *Resident Evil 2*. The team does not know whether that death overlaps with the resurrection scripture's one-use restriction, creating uncertainty about whether he can be restored.
- Zhang Heng confirms he has unlocked second-stage Genetic Constraint. Cheng Xiao and Zero infer that Chu may secretly have reached the fourth stage and acquired emotions while concealing both strength and feeling; this is explicitly speculation, prompted by his abnormal reaction to Zheng's death.
- **Volume X: A Perilous Journey Through Myth** (`第十集 神话惊程`) begins after Team China's return from *A Nightmare on Elm Street*. Full restoration heals physical injuries but does not awaken Zhao Yingkong, Bawang, Zhan Lan, Xiao Honglu, or the other psychologically trapped members.
- Active members are Chu Xuan, Zero, Zhang Heng, Wang Xia, Qi Tengyi, Cheng Xiao, and Liu Yu. Each received 5,000 points and one B-rank side plot; everyone must reserve 2,000 points, while Liu additionally reserves one D-rank side plot.
- Next plan: return to *The Mummy*, resurrect Zheng, recruit Imhotep, investigate the flying Buddha statue side mission, and travel to China around 1940. The team buys at least forty days. **Solid-state coolant** costs 500 points each and will support several dozen Magic Cannon test shots using Zheng's low-grade energy stones.
- Wang Xia advances **Bomb Dominator** by one rank, roughly tripling demonic energy; he currently has about one hundred plasma bombs. Zhang Heng saves his B rank because the next bloodline tier, **Elven Dream Archer**, requires double-A, and he intends to equip the soon-to-be-resurrected Ming Yanwei.
- Qi Tengyi purchases B-rank **Words of Truth** (`真言之语`), which senses individual brain waves and can progress through A to double-A ranks toward suggestion, illusion, and direct mental control. Each mind requires linguistic decoding. Auxiliary **Instantaneous Celestial Intellect** (`瞬时天智`) accelerates thought more than tenfold but requires two or three days of sleep afterward. Chu denies both acquiring emotion and reaching fourth stage but leaves an unfinished qualification.
- Cheng Xiao purchases C-rank **Nanto Suicho Ken** (`南斗水鸟拳`), which can advance to A rank and synergizes with his martial-arts foundation. Zero saves resources and continues Point-Line Mystic Eyes training despite dizziness and suspected mental aftereffects that full physical restoration does not remove.
- Liu Yu purchases **Heart of Summoning** (`召唤之心`) with two C ranks as a prerequisite for future summon skills, hoping eventually to acquire double-A **Obelisk the Tormentor** (`奥伯里斯巨神兵`). He has received the T-virus prototype and spends 1,500 points on physical attributes.
- Zhang Heng uses the Book of Amun-Ra to resurrect Ming Yanwei and experiences her memories. Ming remains deeply hostile and vows to live until she witnesses his death; Zhang accepts her hatred and refuses to flee again. She joins the *Mummy* expedition because her selection for Devil Team confirms high potential.
- Ming Yanwei receives the C-rank evolvable enchanted **Divine Sun-Shooting Bow Kamis** (`射日神弓卡米丝`), which can advance through B, A, and double-A ranks.
- The Magic Cannon and Skeletal Nightmare are stored together in a dimensional bag because neither counts as a living creature. Chu brings all sleeping members into *The Mummy* to give them subjective time to awaken and include them in possible team side-mission rewards.
- Around 1940, Cairo is heavily damaged by the Second World War. Hamunaptra remains intact, while the black-robed guardians have developed a semipermanent base of more than 50,000 trainees, plentiful tanks/firearms, and over seventy aircraft. Their leader says the great powers' war has made Egyptian independence achievable.
- Jonathan has sent letters about an eastern tomb involving Chinese warlords and multiple countries, plus a small contested fragment. This is the new China side-mission lead.
- The Book of Amun-Ra successfully resurrects Zheng at Hamunaptra's altar despite his prior Resurrection Cross death. Zheng returns declaring that he can now oppose his clone and possesses the strength to make his convictions reality.
- Zheng confirms he has overcome the inner demon and stabilized at the intermediate fourth stage. He no longer fears any opponent but still judges himself slightly weaker than clone Zheng; his aim is not merely to defeat the clone but to free him from suffering.
- Chu leaves the sleepers in an abandoned Cairo inn under body-mounted proximity alarms because transporting unconscious people on Green Goblin gliders would create liabilities during aerial combat. The black-robed guardians may instead shelter them, but Rommel's Afrika Korps could discover Hamunaptra.
- The guardians' ultimate dream is Egypt's national revival. Chu proposes tracking and ambushing Rommel, then coordinating with the British while the guardians' mechanized army maintains contact and destroys the Afrika Korps, thereby securing Egypt's place among the victorious side. He distinguishes mere statehood from genuine military, cultural, economic, and civic revival.
- Chu's more radical alternative is a “strategy of darkness”: allow foreign armies to devastate Cairo, destroy both the Afrika Korps and Anglo-French forces afterward, impose a temporary military dictatorship, and use the crisis to awaken Egyptian national identity. Treat this as Chu's in-character authoritarian proposal, not narrator endorsement.
- Chu has reproduced roughly three hundred *Starship Troopers*-derived miniature nuclear missiles and offers fifty to the guardians, estimating that quantity can destroy both foreign armies in Egypt.
- Chu's primary China objective is establishing a long-term logistics base that can manufacture miniature nuclear missiles, plasma bombs, and other large-scale equipment without consuming Team China's Lord God-space training time. He plans to give early-twenty-first-century technology, selected advanced weapon designs, and post-founding Chinese history to the era's Communist leadership, then purchase future materiel with gold.
- Strategic geopolitical aim: a tripartite world with the Soviet Union, the United States, and a China-led Third World bloc. Egypt is intended as the first newly assertive Third World state and a catalyst for broader resistance.
- Jonathan and Imhotep report a Qin-dynasty tomb in Shanxi, built from levitating stone above a vast underground void. It allegedly holds Qin Shi Huang's elixir of immortality and a sealed Eastern-Saint biological weapon called a **God**, stronger than the American masked vampire creature. A levitating-stone Buddha head is the seal's key.
- Jonathan alone survives Yan Xishan's excavation and is pursued by the God, other warlords, Japanese spies, and foreign agents. Imhotep's Egyptian secret arts can only delay the pursuit. They are hiding in Shanghai and instruct Team China to place an evening-paper notice for “old friends from Egypt.”
- Terminology distinction: **Immortals** (`仙`) are human practitioners of cultivation, a development path comparable to fifth-stage Genetic Constraint. **Gods** (`神`) are Eastern-Saint biological weapons containing human souls, analogous to Western Angels/Demons and American masked vampires. Chu connects their appearance to the post-Investiture-War extinction of most Saints and Immortals.
- Zheng assesses his stabilized intermediate-fourth-stage power as above Zhao Zhuikong but still below clone Zheng. His analogy: initial fourth stage gives an unarmed fighter a gun, while overcoming the inner demon loads it with bullets. At full power, he claims he can annihilate either the Afrika Korps or Anglo-French forces within one hour.
- Chu has manufactured fifteen Green Goblin gliders. The remaining combat models average roughly 1,700 km/h and reach 4,000 km/h under an electric-jet system, but even a full internal-energy charge lasts only two hours. Ordinary models remain preferable for routine travel.
- Zheng demonstrates complete microscopic control by pulverizing a stone with internal energy, gathering every particle within several meters using True Essence, and reconstructing it. His optimal genetic balance currently resembles clone Zheng's form, but that favors compressed vampire magical power; Zheng intends to pursue dragon genes better suited to internal energy and True Essence.
- New Shanghai figures: **Ding Li** (`丁力`), leader of the Green Gang, and Japanese Lieutenant Colonel **Yasuo Kojiro** (`康雄小次郎`) of the Shanghai Garrison's Second Army. Kojiro offers one billion yen, equivalent gold, and foreign identity papers for the levitating-stone Buddha head. Ding Li refuses to sell his country despite openly acknowledging his criminal life.
- Ding Li is killed by unknown assailants soon after refusing Kojiro. The mark Imhotep placed on him disappears at his death. Jonathan and Imhotep are sheltering in Shanghai with Imhotep's unnamed companion, the Buddha head, and more than four hundred kilograms of gold whose carried weight is reduced to roughly five kilograms by an antigravitational object.
- Five recurring monsters dressed in yellow are pursuing Jonathan's group; Imhotep expects them to revive again and says they are capable of wounding even him. Team China reaches Shanghai before dawn with the sleeping members, where Chu authorizes killing anyone who threatens his plan and Cheng Xiao immediately decapitates a corrupt policeman before confronting Green Gang fighters.
- Chu intends Team China's public violence in Shanghai as a deliberate demonstration of force before approaching the Chinese Communist Party with world-changing technology. He argues that shared interests cannot produce a genuine alliance without comparable power; the Magic Cannon will serve both as a weapons test and a strategic deterrent.
- Cheng Xiao's rudimentary Nanto Suicho Ken cuts people and weapons apart with light hand sweeps. In the first street clash, Team China kills five policemen and forty-seven Green Gang members and captures four. Zheng declares that his Elm Street ordeal cured his indecision: while his companions remain asleep, he will completely eliminate any potential threat to them.
- Cheng Xiao, Zhang Heng, and Zero defeat roughly four hundred Green Gang members; circulated figures report sixty-nine dead, 143 gravely wounded, and the rest crippled. The scale of the incident attracts Shanghai's prominent Chinese and foreign interests. Cheng and Zhang then storm a police station, offering more than five kilograms of gold—or the death of everyone inside—to bypass newspaper publication controls and place Team China's notice.
- Chu's current translation of Team Celestial's documents says the Lord God's Dimension was jointly constructed by Saints and Immortals, combining Saint energy technology with Immortal runic technology. Other untranslated subjects include divisions among ancient Saints, their primordial enemy, cultivation developed by non-Saint humans, and the final battle.
- Imhotep's companion is explicitly named **Anck-su-namun** (`安苏娜`). The five yellow-clad monsters have been scattered six times; after each defeat they reform faster and become stronger (more than ten days after the first defeat, roughly ten after the second, and six or seven after the third). Their seventh attack manifests gigantic fists and four visible colossal figures around Imhotep's sand barrier before Zheng detects the disturbance.
- **Yellow Turban Strongmen** (`黄巾力士`) are cultivation artifacts formed from human souls bound inside yellow garments. They have metal, wood, water, fire, and earth attributes (their manifested combat forms are described as fire, ice, wind, earth, and metal); the first person to infuse them with True Essence can command them. They grow stronger by killing. Destroying the garment releases the souls but does not prevent later reformation; Tiger Soul charged with True Essence can annihilate both garment and souls.
- Zheng names his strongest current intermediate-fourth-stage form **Hidden Dragon Transformation** (`潜龙变`). Extreme microscopic control lets him bind internal energy into threads that sever a Strongman's soul-body without damaging its cloth, and coat detached sections in internal energy. He describes the principle beyond maximal microscopic control as **supreme skill appearing clumsy** (`大巧若拙`). The transformation leaves him temporarily over two meters tall with black dragon wings and two small horns.
- **Skyborne Pavilion** (`凌空悬阁`) is the hidden legacy of 476 cultivators of the Eastern Yellow lineage. Seeing Jonathan's restored map or any Buddha-statue component triggers a seven-day mission: enter the Pavilion and kill the primordial experimental “God” **Haoyi** (`昊一`), or every participant loses 10,000 points. Success grants every team member one B-rank side plot; Haoyi's killer receives two B-rank side plots.
- Messages signed by cultivator **Hongjun** (`鸿钧`) three days before the Lord God's completion and Saint **Fuxi** (`伏羲`) on the completion day say its construction began three hundred years earlier. Saints supplied energy technology and cultivators supplied runic technology as a contingency for humanity's survival after their anticipated deaths fighting an external enemy. The messages' racial hierarchy and factional hostility are explicitly partisan in-world ideology, not neutral narration.
- Chu identifies the Pavilion mission as a deliberate ancestral exploit: a B-rank task may yield S-rank-or-higher cultivation scriptures. He infers rival Saints scattered the Buddha statue and removed an original 10,000-point-per-member reward while possibly strengthening Haoyi. Team China chooses the riskier response—assemble a vast intelligence network, seize every statue component by force if necessary, and divide into a tomb group and a Shanghai group.
- First-group roster for the Shanxi tomb: Zheng, Jonathan, Imhotep, Anck-su-namun, Qi Tengyi, Liu Yu, the sleeping members, and initially Ming Yanwei. Ming insists on joining Zhang Heng in the second group; Zhang accepts responsibility for protecting her. Zheng reiterates that Team China does not abandon its companions.
- Chu provides silver communication plates capable of long-range normal signals and psyche-force contact; psyche-force communication consumes energy at five times the normal rate, with roughly one month stored. He gives Zheng duplicate plain glasses to place on sleeping Zhao Yingkong while connecting two electrodes to Zheng's temples whenever safe; the purpose remains undisclosed.
- At Shanxi, every Buddha-statue component is confirmed missing and more than 5,000 of Yan Xishan's troops guard the valley. Second-group assignments: Zero to Chongqing for possible contact with Chiang Kai-shek by any necessary means; Cheng Xiao to the Communist Shanxi-Chahar-Hebei base with a complete list of contemporary underground operatives as proof; Zhang Heng and Ming Yanwei to the Northeast with ordinary and enhanced gliders; Chu and Wang Xia return to Shanghai.
- Once recovered, a Buddha component must not be shattered into smaller pieces: although the original mission key cannot have been divided into arbitrarily tiny components, later fragmentation could make full reassembly permanently impossible and doom the team.
- As Chu approaches the fourth stage of the Genetic Constraint, he reports emotions and feelings drawing nearer while his inner demon begins to emerge. For one instant above Shanghai he feels the whole world beneath his feet, producing in Wang Xia the same instinctive danger once radiated by pre-breakthrough fourth-stage Zheng.
- With only six and a half days remaining, Chu rejects conventional agreements and intelligence exchange as too slow. He defines the limit of intellect as prediction enabled by near-total information, and chooses **overwhelming ingenuity with force** (`以力破巧`) because Team China's combat power is effectively invincible in the pre-nuclear 1940 setting.
- Chu orders Wang Xia to break every combat-capable Japanese formation centered on Shanghai and extending no farther than Nanjing. Wang Xia's improved Bomb Dominator demonic energy expands a plasma bomb's destructive radius from roughly a dozen meters to nearly one hundred, with lethal heat reaching about three times farther; ten initial bombs reduce a major camp sector to ruins before he targets ammunition depots.

## Open questions and correction log

- Decoder/manifest defect confirmed: `decode_novel.py` recognizes only `第…章` headings and omitted the two genuine prose sections `第九集 结局（一）` and `第九集 结局（二）` embedded in decoded batch 007. Working translations correctly preserve them as parts 351–352, so working sequence numbers are two greater than manifest numbers from that point onward. Do not rename or collapse those sections; account for the +2 offset in later merges, and fix/regenerate the manifest before deterministic publication.
- Decoded part 446, source line 1386, is textually corrupted: Hao Tian's sales pitch is spliced into fragments of an objection and wording duplicated later in the chapter. The translation preserves every intelligible claim, marks the abrupt source break inline, and does not invent the missing transition or speaker attribution.

- Batch 001 literary review is complete for parts 001–050. The Astra pass began but hit its usage limit; at the user's direction, the active Codex model completed the remaining bilingual review. See `translation/astra_review_batch_001.md`.
- Part 004's intervention yields 500 points plus attribute increases equivalent to 500 more points: 1,000 total value, not 1,500. Part 006's 半里 is approximately 250 meters, not half a mile.
- Part 018: only the flirting young man and woman leave at this point; the third office worker, Li Shuaixi, remains. Part 023 uses another steel bar, not the last of the original four.
- Preserve source-level discrepancies: Zhang Jie refers to both three and four scenarios; Luo Li's stated age, decade since her death, and Zheng Zha's age do not align cleanly. Do not invent an explanation. Part 017's 纸草文字 is retained as “papyrus script,” rather than silently identifying it as Egyptian hieroglyphs.
- Source claims about Facehuggers, human evolution, and toxic genetic-lock activation are in-world explanations, not verified scientific or film-canon facts. Preserve their content and uncertainty.
- Confirm all personal-name readings against their first clear introduction; do not infer readings solely from fandom memory.
- The official-versus-source naming policy is fidelity-first: retain an explicit name chosen by the novel (for example, Matthew Addison) and use official English only when the source clearly points to the same established name or entity without contradicting it.
- Expand this file before each newly encountered ability, bloodline, ranked reward, weapon, team, or borrowed franchise is translated.
